Chapter 301: It Will Certainly Sell Out

Although told it would occur in the morning, it in fact was almost noon when a territory official led the dragonman youngster to the city government palace.

The city government palace was inside the old island.

Because of how life worked on Two Eyes Island, it was very difficult to construct large installations.

If a castle was built, it would be too heavy on the ball cage treetop and would be in danger of sinking.

“Commander Long Fu, your good reputation precedes you.” Hun Tong sat on a thick sofa, his eyes half open and half closed. When he saw the dragonman youngster walk in, the corner of his mouth rose into a smile.

He had a dark yellow complexion, and when he smiled, wrinkles covered his face.

Adding on his white hair and dispirited nature, he looked somewhat senile.

“Lord Hun Tong, it is my honor to meet you!” The dragonman youngster put his hand to his chest and bowed in courtesy.

While he had been led in, he noticed that although the city government palace was inside the old island, he was very spacious, exquisite, and luxuriously decorated.

As the information said, Hun Tong was a hedonist, and he lived life extravagantly.

“This is a letter of introduction from Lord Tan Qiu.” The dragonman youngster took out a letter.

This was also one of Tan Qiu’s reimbursements to him.

With Tan Qiu’s endorsement, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could obtain the trust of other nobles and feudal lords.

Hun Tong glanced at the letter of introduction, then cracked another smile, “Sit. Take a drink of my special product – Fragrant Fish Gill Tea.”

The dragonman youngster sat upright and took a sip. The tea was potently fragrant, and after swallowing it, its fragrance lingered on endlessly in his belly.

“I heard my subordinate report that your Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps only plans to stay for 10 days?” Hun Tong asked.

The dragonman youngster replied, “We sought out the famous Two Eyes Island to participate in its auction.”

Hun Tong laughed, he was no longer faking a smile and decided to expose his true feelings.

He had built up Two Eyes Island, and the reason Two Eyes Island could have developed to its current state was by following his governing philosophy and realizing his concept of governance.

The dragonman youngster seeking to join the auction proved that Hun Tong was successful.

Hun Tong replied, “I look forward to the unique commodities you provide.”

“Perhaps, this place can become a long term venue for you.”

“If you want, I can provide you a store free of charge and allow you to sell some weapons and equipment.”

It was very clear that he didn’t believe the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ surface identity.

Like how Tan Qiu and the other higher ups in Snake Rat Island surmised, Hun Tong thought that the survivors were arms smugglers that Rou Zang’s pirate group had harmed.

The dragonman youngster’s eyes shined and he briefly sank into pondering.

He was thinking of a future where their redemption was successful, even if he no longer had the divine artifacts, he still had many blueprints. He would be able to build a weapons factory and an alchemy factory.

Manufactured munitions must be sold.

If he and Hun Tong opened a collaboration, Two Eyes Island’s lively marketplace would undoubtedly be a good place to have a store long term.

“I will remember Lord Hun Tong’s benevolence. If it is possible in the future, I will set up a store here.” The dragonman youngster sighed, “But I am weak, and my mercenary corps is new, with circumstances beyond my control for a while, I cannot even think of doing that right now.”

Hun Tong nodded, showing his understanding.

The waters of arms smugglers was very deep, even though he was a gold level fighter, not even Hun Tong wanted to get too involved with them.

To him it seemed that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were arms smugglers that had a big patron. The power behind this mercenary group was enormous, otherwise they wouldn’t be qualified to make arms smugglers. Due to working under such a formidable force, it was both normal and reasonable that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps weren’t in control of their situation.

“Are you selling your gold cannon?” Hun Tong then asked.

If the cannon appeared in the auction, it would certainly give the new island auction positive buzz.

The dragonman youngster apologized, the cannon already had an owner. They were just forced by circumstances to borrow it for their own use, nothing more.

Hun Tong showed his disappointment, and his interest in the conversation was dismissed greatly.

“Every time the old island sinks, it will draw in sharks and other predators. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps can receive many missions related to this from the Mercenary Guild; the rewards are good.”

“This year’s new island auction will have higher standards than previous ones and will have great things there!”

Hun Tong reminded the dragonman youngster before he left.

The young captain left the city government palace and returned to the ship at port.

The meeting didn’t leave him with a good impression of Hun Tong.

Hun Tong was a haughty gold level fighter; he looked down at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group and didn’t show any courtesy to them.

He only invited the dragonman youngster to see if the latter could increase Two Eyes Island’s weapons business. After learning that the gold level cannon couldn’t be sold, he lost interest.

Finally, his reminder to the dragonman youngster seemed to be done with mostly good intentions.

In fact, Hun Tong was still looking at the issues from his point of view.

Two Eyes Island would be the one to benefit the most if the predatory magic beast groups were eliminated.

“Anyways, it is normal for a gold level to look at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in that way.”

“On Snake Rat Island, due to Tan Qiu’s pressing need to deal with Rou Zang, he had vigorously beguiled all powers.”

“While on Two Eyes Island, all powers survive and compete in Hun Tong’s domain.”

The dragonman youngster could sense Hun Tong’s contempt for him and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

This was not to say that Hun Tong’s individual battle strength was greater than Rou Zang’s. Rather he was not like Rou Zang who fought alone, he had the Duo Mu Clan backing him. On Two Eyes Island, he could rely on a feudal lord’s prestige and mobilize the strength of five great merchant alliances among others.

He was the leader of Two Eyes Island; a place Tan Qiu’s Snake Rat Island couldn’t be compared to.

“Contempt towards us is a good thing.”

The dragonman youngster also wanted to keep a low profile.

At this time, they were waiting for the Bai Zhen Clan and participating in the auction here, all the while trying to improve their strength.

On a ship.

Zi Di and Fei She were immersed in reading and comparing all kinds of price lists.

The lists had the prices of Two Eyes Island goods, where they came from, how prices fluctuated in the past, and other such details.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had gotten war spoils, but they didn’t have much cash on hand.

Tan Qiu wanted to develop Snake Rat Island and needed capital; therefore, he tended to give the spoils of war as remuneration and rewards.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had scored their most money ever last night, when Zi Di returned with 300 gold coins from the Wisteria store.

“Soon, the auction will be held.”

“We have too little cash on hand, and even if we see something we want, we don’t have the ability to buy it.”

“We must make money as soon as possible, the more the better.”

“I have the capability…business opportunity, where is the business opportunity?”

Zi Di turned a price list in her hands.

Fei She did the same.

The two people researched until noon before they gained something.

Fei She said, “Lord, Lord President, we can make the Sweet Blood Potion. As the old island sinks, it will attract predatory magic beast groups that will devour the remnants left on the old island. During this cycle, the new island will also be affected and will often have small beasts and tide beasts.”

“Two Eyes Island’s market lacks effective magic beast lures,”

“The Sweet Blood Potion just so happens to make up for the deficiency in the market, it will certainly be well received and popular.”

“Right.” Zi Di nodded, “Apart from this, we can also refine healing medicine.”

“Two Eyes Island has a Wealth Goddess parish, and the Wealth Church’s clergymen are not good at healing.”

“Our medicinal recipes come from War Merchant and will be on par with similar goods.”

“Furthermore, I’ve seen it.”

“In the bargain market, there were large quantities of green hair turtle shells being dumped on the cheap.”

“This is the main ingredient for Green Hat Potions.”

“Although this potion has a side effect, drinking it will turn your hair green. But its healing effect is decent and is fast acting.”

Sweet Blood Potions and Green Hat Potions.

After a formal determination, Zi Di immediately notified the tower spirit on the Deep Sea Monster Fish with an alchemy device.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was disguised as a reef on the sea floor 5 nautical miles from Two Eyes Island.

In the alchemy cabin, after the tower spirit received the command, it immediately began to refine potions.

The relevant alchemy formation were already set up, and the remaining yellow crab puppet was standing next to a giant basin.

The giant basin was emitting a gold level aura.

It was Rou Zang’s magic medicine bathtub.

If medicine was used in it, then the medicine poured in would be then times as potent.

And if it was used to make medicine, then the ingredients could make ten times the medicine!

Rou Zang lacked any way of making medicine, because he didn’t have a qualified alchemy master.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn’t have one either.

But the alchemy ship had alchemy facilities War Merchant used and the tower spirit could also be regarded as half an alchemy master.

With the gold level alchemy artifact, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could refine large quantities of potions.

They had War Merchant’s recipes and Rou Zang’s medicinal recipes, and Zi Di’s barbarian race herbal medicine recipes…

If Zi Di wanted, within a short amount of time, she could refine lots of commonplace potions and ruthlessly attack Two Eyes Island’s medicinal market and become a hegemon among it.

But that was unnecessary.

It would offend too many people.

The five great merchant alliances wouldn’t sit still, and Hun Tong wouldn’t tolerate such a disturbance to the order of things.

Therefore, Zi Di and Fei She decided to search for a blank space in the market.

Zi Di had confidence in herself.

If the Sweet Blood Potions hit shelves, they would certainly sell out!

Chapter 302: Refining Medicine

Zi Di snuck back into the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

In the alchemy cabin, she closely inspected everything before her.

The main thing in her view was a large bathtub.

The bathtub was round and 3 meters in diameter. The bathtub seemed to be made of innumerable multicolored stones fused together and looked utterly gorgeous. Neither the outside nor the inside were smooth, instead it had countless bumps and holes in it.

It was the Magic Medicine Bathtub.

It once belonged to Rou Zang.

But its true designer was an ingenious alchemist.

They meticulously designed the bathtub’s surface with a creative bumpiness. After magic potions and water is mixed, they flow inside these bumps.

The bumps increased the contact area, inducing an enhancement of medicinal effects severalfold.

The Magic Medicine Bathtub was part of the magic ship, the Bath Tub, and consumed a large amount of magic power.

The alliance was victorious in the Snake Rat naval battle. After the war, Tan Qiu dredged up the Bath Tub and in order to reward merits with his tight financial situation, he was forced to reluctantly give the Magic Medicine Bathtub to the survivors.

After the survivors got it, the dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, and the others began to make use of it.

Bathing in it felt quite wonderful.

By sleeping in its warm water, one could enjoy the medicinal soak while also enjoying the natural massage the concave surface provided.

This was the Magic Medicine Bathtub’s most basic function.

In the survivors’ hands, the Magic Medicine Bathtub combined with alchemy formations could give the former a new use, turning the former into an effective manufacturing tool.

This was beyond Rou Zang’s abilities to accomplish.

It was also impossible for the survivors too.

The one who really accomplished this was the tower spirit.

The tower spirit cleverly reformed currently available alchemy formations in War Merchant’s database and had the gold level Magic Medicine Bathtub serve as the core of an alchemy formation.

“The Magic Medicine Bathtub’s most core alchemy technology is coordinated magic time extension. Manufactured potions or magic equipment is essentially about setting up a new magic equilibrium. Through the consumption of a large quantity of magic power to maintain a potion’s magic equilibrium even longer, it will increase its medicinal effect.”

“The formation we built just utilizes this technology.”

“By logical extension, any alchemy item is the same, if one fully grasps its alchemical secrets, they can use them in a broader fashion.”

“Rou Zang was only a fighter, a superficial person. Magicians are the controllers and beneficiaries of knowledge.”

Zi Di sighed to herself.

She was a magician.

Regardless of whether she was alive or dead, she was still one.

She had ample inquisitiveness and cravings for any knowledge.

Therefore, even though the tower spirit was the overseer, and the yellow crab alchemy puppet was the controller of the Magic Medicine Bathtub alchemy formation and didn’t need Zi Di to participate in the medicinal refinement, Zi Di would still come and watch.

She wouldn’t give up any chance to learn.

And this was a great opportunity, one she treasured.

Because she too had experienced the hardships of trying to make a living. During her studies at the College of Magic, she had a deep understanding of the value of knowledge.

The potion refinement had begun.

The remaining yellow crab alchemy puppet was facing the Magic Medicine Bathtub.

Its two crab legs had turned into drums.

From the drum shaped crab legs, a palm yellow translucent oil constantly poured out of them.

The oil had a rich sesame fragrance.

After the oil was poured in, it immediately layered into the bathtub.

When the layer became 10 centimeters thick, the crab legs morphed, stopping the flow.

Green copper lumps were taken out.

The yellow crab alchemy puppet processed it on the spot, using its crab leg, now morphed into a saw, to cut the copper lumps into smaller pieces.

The copper pieces were dumped into the bathtub.

Not long after, the alchemy formation activated. The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s magic power reservoir constantly imbued it with magic power, increasing the temperature.

In the center of the alchemy formation, the Magic Medicine Bathtub was baking red hot, the oil was boiling, and the copper was melting inside it.

The heat persisted throughout the alchemy refinement.

The yellow crab alchemy puppet took out a bamboo tube.

Inside the bamboo tube was a processed alchemy material with a very short shelf life.

The alchemy material looked like scarlet pollen.

The yellow crab alchemy puppet dumped all the “pollen” into the bathtub, instantly causing a waft of gray smoke.

The smoke was very irritating to people.

But Zi Di was indifferent to it,

She was already dead and was now a ghost girl.

After the smoke dispersed, the bathtub’s water level rose, almost 80% of it was filled.

Refinement successful!

Zi Di walked into the alchemy formation, carefully observing the Magic Medicine Bathtub from the side.

The Sweet Blood Potion was somewhat thick, and looked a dark red.

But when poured, the viscous potion would turn from a dark red to a bright scarlet.

If Zi Di could still smell, she would smell a rich and sweet fishy odor.

It was precisely this characteristic that the pharmacist who invented this recipe named it the Sweet Blood Potion.

The yellow crab alchemy puppet’s crab legs changed again.

The crab legs became ladles.

It used the ladles to rapidly and precisely scoop out the potion into medicinal jars.

These medicinal jars were not conventional thin, slender glass bottles.

Because the Sweet Blood Potion was not to be taken, it was to be thrown.

It was to disperse rapidly in water, create the smell of food, and attract groups of magic beasts, animals, and scavengers.

At first, the Sweet Blood Potion that the yellow crab alchemy puppet was scooping out was of ordinary quality.

Gradually though, when it reached the layer below, the Sweet Blood Potion rose to a bronze level quality.

In the most center layer, the Sweet Blood Potion became iron level.

Then in the layer below that, it reverted to a bronze level quality.

Finally, on the lowest layer, the potion was ordinary again.

“It’s clear that this resembles an egg. During the manufacturing process, the center of the potion is where the magic harmonizes the most, therefore it has the highest quality.”

“The Magic Medicine Bathtub is no regular brewing tool; it is just being commandeered as one for the time being. Its real application is still how Rou Zang used it.”

Zi Di continued to analyze.

“The Sweet Blood Potion’s quality limit is the iron level. If it was the silver level, then this temporarily assembled medicine making formation could have been refined up to that quality.”

“Reaching the gold level needs both luck and an alchemy master refining it.”

“The tower spirit and the yellow crab puppet can refine iron level things, barely refine silver level things, and are incapable of refining anything of the gold level, that exceeds the limits of their abilities.”

For now, the Sweet Blood Potion refinement has come to an end.

This was because before Zi Di had come, the tower spirit had already refined many batches already.

Having a tower spirit was quite convenient.

The yellow crab alchemy puppet began to tidy up the medicine refinement tools.

It was mainly cleaning the bath tub.

Its crab legs morphed into nozzles that sprayed yellow water from them.

The water washing across the bath tub quickly vaporized, sizzling across it and causing mist to rise.

Two crab legs then turned into large scrubbers, and as water flowed across, it was diligently brushing the bath tub down.

After cleaning it, a crab leg fired an electric current.

The electric current split into tiny currents that interweaved into a net that covered the bathtub both inside and out.

For a moment, the crackling of the tiny currents lingered on.

Magically, where the electric current lashed out, long purple and red hairs grew from the bath tub’s surface.

The long hairs emitted an ominous aura.

This was the accumulated residue from prior medicine refinement, now condensed into visibility by magic power.

If one had hair, one needed to trim it.

The crab legs turned into scissors.

Eight pairs of scissors clipped the hairs.

After being sheared, they were shaved again by the blades.

White form spurted, and the hair strands completely melted at last.

Afterwards, the crab continued to spray water.

Then scrubbed it again.

Then it used the electric current to stimulate the hair growth again.

By the second time, the hairs were five times as sparse and were much shorter.

After five rounds of washing, shearing, and removal, no more hairs grew, and the bath tub was finally considered clean.

This was also because the Magic Medicine Bathtub was not a true medicine refinement tool, otherwise cleaning it wouldn’t have been so troublesome.

After this, it was time to refine the Green Hat Healing Potion.

This time, Zi Di participated too.

She first took out a green hair turtle shell and began to process the alchemy material.

The outer layer of the green hair turtle shell looked like any ordinary turtle shell. But on the inside, “moss” was growing all over it.

If one touched it with their hands, they would find that the “moss” was actually small clumps of fine fur.

The green hair turtle shell’s most quintessential spot was that fur.

Zi Di took out needle tubes and injected them into the turtle shells.

Inside the needle tubes was prepared medicine, and due to their sharpness, they easily inserted themselves into the turtle shells.

After each turtle shell was injected with the medicine, they quickly softened into a pool of mud.

Zi Di started the alchemy formation, and it cleaned away the mud, leaving just the fur behind.

By the time she finished preparing this material, the yellow crab alchemy puppet on the other side of the bathtub had finished processing the other materials required.

The medicine bathtub was already heated up, and it was filled with a pink mixture.

Zi Di threw the green fur into the bath tub, then threw in the other dozen or so materials in too.

After these materials were added in, she controlled the magic formation so that the liquid in the Magic Medicine Bathtub began to automatically swirl into a whirlpool.

The liquid wildly spun, and the various alchemy materials mixed together in a wondrous harmonization of magic power.

When the liquid finally slowed down, it was a gelatinous thing.

It gave off the impression of elasticity.

This process lasted 30 minutes. Zi Di expended her spiritual power as she stood in front of the medicine bathtub.

She now began the final step.

The yellow crab alchemy puppet handed her a medical bottle.

She removed the stopper, which was also the dropper, and dripped the liquid into the bathtub.

The medicinal liquid was purple red, and when it hit the gelatinous mass, it looked like ink flowing across clear water.

The gelatinous mass changed color at the same time its nature changed too.

This was because Zi Di’s spirit had directed the magic power towards equilibrium.

Following the drops of liquid, the glue first became viscous, then began to clear. Finally, the milky white substance turned completely colorless, as if it were clean water.

“Drip, drip drip.”

“Forecasting attainment of the final stage.”

The tower spirit aptly warned.

Upon receiving the reminder, Zi Di dripped the other half down.

The clear and pure water reached the final step, and the new magic power equilibrium had been completely established, its quality jumping from the bronze level to the iron level!

The bath tub’s refinement of healing potions was done.

Chapter 303: Money Shortage

Zi Di coughed, most of her spirit was expended and she was quite exhausted.

She had a sincere feeling of satisfaction.

To refine so many iron level potions was once unprecedented for her.

“The final step’s mixture is very difficult; it’ll be ruined if even a little is off. Although I didn’t fail, the quality is only bronze level.”

“This set of alchemy equipment makes things much more convenient. It can remind me of crucial moments at the right time.

“Although the Magic Medicine Bathtub is not a normal medicine refinement tool, it is still a gold level tool. One refinement can produce ten finished goods.”

“Not only is the output improved, the spirit consumption, time, and other inputs by the refining pharmacist are also cut down. It saves on a great deal of labor and production costs.”

“As for material costs, it doesn’t seem that big on the surface.”

“Because during this process, the magic power consumed is 15 times higher than conventional refinements.

One of the rules of alchemy was equivalent exchange.

It was impossible for there to be a tenfold output to happen for no reason.

“Even so, refining medicine like this is more cost effective.”

“Because the Green Hat Potion doesn’t have many materials.”

“Materials cannot be reduced, but elemental crystals can be bought at bulk prices.”

“It’s all thanks to the Deep Sea Magic Fish’s magic power furnace. The magic power furnace is also a gold level component, and its performance surpasses the bathtub greatly.”

Having the tower spirit, the yellow crab alchemy puppet, the Magic Medicine Bathtub, the Deep Sea Monster Fish, and the alchemy formation was an extraordinary luxury indeed, amplifying Zi Di’s medicine refinement level by many times.

Zi Di finished ruminating, and began to rest, honing her strength for the second medicine refinement.

As Zi Di was refining medicine, Zong Ge was entering a church.

It was the Wealth Goddess’ church.

If one were to say the Sheng Ming Church radiated a light of reverence, then the Wealth Church had a style of extreme lavishness.

There were all kinds of golden decorations, expensive furniture, silver candlesticks, detailed ceiling frescos, and hanging crystal lamps.

Zong Ge emitted a silver level aura, and when he entered the church, he drew attention.

The clergy in the church respectfully lead him into the discussion room.

Zong Ge lifted his arm and immediately stated his reason for coming, “I need healing.”

“This arm was once severed.”

“Ordinary treatment methods can’t cure it.”

“It needs at least gold level healing.”

Tan Qiu had a gold level healing potion, but after the Snake Rat naval battle, Tan Qiu didn’t want to give his only bottle of it to an outsider.

He wanted to save it for himself.

Therefore, Zong Ge couldn’t exchange merits for it.

The clergy first examined Zong Ge’s injury, then said, “This kind of injury really is hard to deal with, even the priest present cannot fix it.”

“After all, the priest is only a silver level.”

“Lord Shi Qi, you have come at an inopportune time. The priest just left, for today he is going to the cemetery to preside over funeral ceremonies.

Zong Ge pursed his brows.

But at that moment, the clergy perked up with a smile, “But Lord Shi Qi, our Wealth Church stands out from the masses.”

“Even though the priest cannot do it, we can ask a bishop over to heal you.”

Zong Ge’s brows wrinkled tighter.

He knew a lot about the Wealth Church.

The pursuit of riches was the core doctrine of this church.

Thus, if one had the funds, they could have a gold level bishop travel across land and sea to Two Eyes Island to heal Zong Ge.

But that required enough money!

Firstly, gold level healing divine spells were very expensive. In addition, having a bishop rushing over here themselves was even more troublesome.

It wasn’t easy requesting the aid of a gold level character.

It took a long time for Tan Qiu to request Guang Ke’s aid.

If Zong Ge wanted to ask a bishop to heal his injury, he would have to spend much more than Tan Qiu did!

And the most conflicting thing was this; he was very poor.

Zong Ge left the church as Cang Xu visited the cemetery.

The guard at the gate let him through, thinking that Cang Xu was one of the guests participating in the funeral.

Cang Xu watched the burial rites being held from a distance.

The Wealth Church priest wore gold and silver, adorning himself in rich attire.

A coffin had just been lowered into a deep hole.

The priest prayed, “My dear friends, please don’t be sad.”

“Our Lord summons her believers away so that they can continue to strive for the accomplishment and enjoyment of acquiring riches.”

“My God, your grace will soon free the parted from the fetters of death and allow them to join you in your peaceful and radiant heaven, for because of your love, we can enjoy the happiness wealth brings.”

“A rich smell of money is assailing my face.” Cang Xu sighed to himself.

Then, he saw the priest use a divine spell.

The golden rays of light covered the coffin, then spread out to engulf that part of the cemetery.

Upon seeing that, Cang Xu’s expectations were reduced by a lot.

He came here to gather necromancy materials.

But the cemetery was obviously purified everywhere, the Wealth Goddess’ divine power had washed through here time and again.

After the priest’s prayer ended, the people attending the internment began to get up.

They lined up, and slowly walked around the edge of the pit, forcing a circle.

As they circled, they took out prepared gifts from their pockets. Most of it was coins, but there were also some gold bracelets, pearl necklaces, gemstone rings, and other adornments.

These were funerary objects, one of the Wealth Church’s teachings.

The believers believed that the dead would take these funerary objects with them, and when they started working in the Wealth Goddess’ divine domain, they would use these things as capital to make more profits.

“It seems the deceased’s status was not low.” Cang Xu thought to himself when he saw the rich funerary objects.

He continued to look around the cemetery, his heart now creating a new expectation.

“Perhaps, there are no necromancy materials in this cemetery.”

“Two Eyes Island has been developed for many years, and the Wealth Church has established a parish here for a long time too, thus basically everyone here is a believer in the Wealth Goddess.”

“Therefore, the cemetery funerary objects excite the people a little.”

What was grave robbing to a necromancer?

It was a routine scenario of their cultivation.

But Cang Xu wasn’t urgently going about it.

He had come this time to mainly scout out the spot.

Since there was a priest present, it was also inconvenient for him to do such a thing.

“This is the island’s biggest graveyard; it must have sufficient guards.”

“Everyone knows that Wealth Church believers value the doctrine of funerary objects.”

“I must plan it slowly.”

Zong Ge needed money.

Cang Xu also needed money.

Which magician didn’t need money?

Necromancers were no exception.

By the time Zong Ge had returned to the ship as night fell, he had already seen the first batch of medicine.

Altogether there were 600 jars of Sweet Blood Potion, as well as 50 cases of Green Hat Healing Potion.

Each jar wasn’t that big; they could be held in an adult human’s palm.

Each case of Green Hat Healing Potion had 80 glass bottles inside it.

Cang Xu carefully examined them.

He found that the Sweet Blood Potions were mostly bronze level while the healing potions were a step higher in quality, most of them were iron level.

Cang Xu wasn’t an expert in making medicine, thus when the team divided up work, he was in charge of looking for necromancy materials.

Upon seeing Zi Di’s triumphant success heaped before him, he felt very gratified. Besides some information, his trip to the cemetery was fruitless.

Zi Di returned to the ship. Although she wanted to keep on refining medicine, other work preoccupied her. Next up, she had to return to the Wisteria store herself.

“We will keep the iron level Sweet Blood Potions and the silver level healing potions.” Zi Di presented, “This first batch will be put onto the market, then refinement will continue.”

Of course, the best things would be kept for themselves.

The survivors’ struggle on Snake Rat Island helped them gain their footing. In the early stages of establishing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they most definitely had ordinary members and assets. Naturally, their financial situation was strained, but they had space to move about.

Cang Xu didn’t understand market analysis, but he believed Zi Di’s plan was dependable, “We should probe the market’s reaction, then amend the medicine refinement plan accordingly.”

Zi Di asked, “What have you gained on your side?”

The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s medication room was quite good; however, the survivors only had two magicians, both of which were necromancers.

During meditation, they needed to expend necromancy materials to build a meditation environment.

But they had already exhausted their supply of necromancy materials.

This caused Zi Di’s and Cang Xu’s meditations to be very inefficient.

They couldn’t cultivate in the cemetery. For during meditation, they would have intense magic power fluctuations. This was because magicians absorbed external elements and transformed them into their own magic power.

Cang Xu shook his head regretfully, “The cemetery’s defenses are very tight, because most of Two Eyes Island’s inhabitants are Wealth Goddess believers.”

Zi Di understood.

She was a merchant; it was impossible for her to not know of the Wealth Church’s teachings.

Cang Xun continued, “The priest here is very diligent, and with divine force washing off the cemetery innumerable times, it is very difficult for necromancy materials to form.”

“I’ve already placed my hopes on the bargain sale.”

“Moreover, the cemetery here actually has a distinguishing quality.

“You know? Because Two Eyes Island is a coral ball cage tree, it is constantly growing.

“Although Hun Tong seized Two Eyes Island, he is not a fishman and can’t control the growing method.”

“There are many graves that were gradually buried in the depths as the coral ball cage tree grew.”

“If it was a normal burial, it would be easy to discern the location. But the coral ball cage grows in all directions, it is spherical. This causes the coffins buried inside it to be caught amongst the growing branches, making it difficult to pinpoint.”

“Because of this, perhaps you can still excavate some fishman graves in the branches near the cemetery.”

Cang Xu said some interesting things.

At evening, the two left for the Wisteria store together.

The Wisteria store’s business was still cold.

But there was a carriage parked outside it, and the carriage was unexpectedly an iron level vehicle.

Zi Di and Cang Xu entered the store.

They didn’t need to flash the wisteria secret order, and the salesclerk respectfully received them.

“Lord elders, please come this way.”

“The store manager stopping by is from the Flint Merchant Alliance, they are here to discuss some business matters.” The salesclerk took Zi Di and Cang Xu to another room in the back.

“The Flint Merchant Alliance?” Zi Di was slightly astounded.

Chapter 304: Registration

The Flint Merchant Alliance is one of the great merchant alliances, they mainly engage in the mining and sale of natural materials.

What business dealings did they have with the Wisteria store?

Zi Di and Cang Xu sat down to have tea.

A conversation was taking place in a nearby room.

The Flint Merchant Alliance’s representative was threatening, “Yan Yi, this price is already appropriate. I advise that you stop while you’re ahead.”

Yan Yi bitterly laughed, then said, “My Lord, you are forcing me to accept it. I really don’t want to sell this store location. Store location is very important to all that do business.”

“The Wisteria store location on the island is already terrible and has greatly impacted my results.”

“I am hoping that on the new island, with the help of a better location, I can increase business and do better.”

The Flint Merchant Alliance representative sneered, “Our Flint Merchant Alliance is one of the great six; what kind of trash is your Wisteria Merchant Alliance?”

“It has already been broken up, only the name remains. Isn’t the current merchant alliance president some yellow haired girl? She is a bit stronger than her father, an iron level magician apprentice. Reportedly, she got shipwrecked and has been missing for a few months. What are you hoping the Wisteria Merchant Alliance can do for you?”

“Even if your shop has a prime location on the new island, what can you do? How much business can you give it? It is a good spot, yes, but can you defend it?”

“Your store turnover is too low. By next year, you won’t be able to keep it. According to island law, this location will be given to a more prosperous store.”

“We just want to purchase it ahead of time, that’s all.”

“Recognize reality, old chum!”

Yan Yi was silent for a good while, before replying with a gloomy and husky voice, “I must admit that you are speaking facts.”

“But why must I sell it to you?”

“I can sell this good location to other merchant alliances.”

The Flint Merchant Alliance had considered this issue earlier, and without hesitation, he answered, “Two Eyes Island has many merchant alliance stores.”

“Our Flint Merchant Alliance has only been here in recent times.”

“Our location compared to the other four great merchant alliances is inferior. Therefore, we’d like to purchase your store location.”

“We all do business. If you sell it to the other merchant alliances, can they beat my higher bid?”

“Would they buy your location a year earlier?”

“No?”

The Flint Merchant Alliance representative should have made an inquiry; thus, he spoke confidently and calmly.

Yan Yi deeply sighed, and he could only say, “Let me think it over again.”

The Flint Merchant Alliance representative was displeased, “Hmph, more thinking it over? I have come to negotiate sincerely with you over and over again, yet you act so perfunctory with me?”

“Three days.”

“At most I will give you three days to think things through.”

“I hope you have good news. Otherwise, don’t blame me for doing some small tricks.”

The conversation ended, and soon after, the sound of a door being slammed shut rang out.

Zi Di and Cang Xu had been silently listening throughout this.

Zi Di looked somewhat embarrassed.

Cang Xu was concerned, “It seems there is trouble?”

Zi Di faintly nodded, “The Wisteria Merchant Alliance established itself early on Two Eyes Island, thus, it has a good store location. Perhaps in recent years, due to Two Eyes Island growing even more, Hun Tong established this new rule, an understandable action.”

Cang Xu asked, “I’m a bit curious, what tricks will the Fling Merchant Alliance use?”

Zi Di muttered, “They won’t attack, everyone is a merchant alliance, thus, it will be lawful. After all, it only involves a store location, that’s all. The feudal lord’s system is the strongest on Two Eyes Island. The tricks on the surface won’t be too vile.”

“However, the Flint Merchant Alliance is one of the six great merchant alliances of humanity, they are extremely powerful. The Wisteria store on its own is not their opponent. He only needs some sales promotion tricks to seize the store’s customer flow. By wasting some funds, they can sell the same goods as the store but at a discounted price, thereby forcing Yan Yi into a tight spot.”

Business competition was cruel.

The Wisteria store’s vulnerable position was obvious.

Cang Xu’s interest was piqued, “If discounts promote sales, can’t we also do that? Since Yan Yi is a local bully and a member of the Duo Mu Clan, I don’t believe he doesn’t have some capital of his own.

“That is the problem.” Zi Di said, “Our Wisteria Merchant Alliance is a business coalition made up of countless small and medium sized merchant alliances, banding together for warmth. Therefore, don’t hope their loyalty and unity can even equal that of the six great merchant alliances.”

“Yan Yi is no die-hard member of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance; he won’t compromise his benefits and make unprofitable deals to boost his own performance. If the Wisteria Merchant Alliance wasn’t smashed, then perhaps it would have been possible.”

“I now suspect that he isn’t so unwilling to sell this store location.”

“He most likely sold it when his location on the old island changed. But the funds obtained from this transaction are definitely not on the store ledger. His dilemma is perhaps a way of raising the price.”

Zi Di calmly analyzed.

Cang Xu sighed, “What concerns me more is this, will this hinder our plan?”

Zi Di sighed to herself.

They wanted to unload the potions they made onto the market.

With the Wisteria store as a readily available channel, it not only was it convenient, but it also helped the Wisteria Merchant Alliance too.

After all, Zi Di was the merchant alliance’s master, a merchant alliance that had been meticulously cared for by her father.

But Zi Di hesitated a bit.

The conversation moments ago had worsened her impression of Yan Yi.

Should they really help the manager here?

On the one hand, Zi Di felt resentful and grieved.

The Wisteria Merchant Alliance had been targeted and divided by the six great merchant alliances. They could all be said to be her foes.

Zi Di too wanted this foe to taste defeat!

Soon after, Yan Yi came from next door to them.

“Two elders, you should’ve heard that. My life is hard.”

Yan Yi’s eyes watered, he wanted to get the Wisteria Merchant Alliance’s higherups’ support.

But Zi Di was in an unusual situation, it was fine if she was mistakenly believed to have been shipwrecked, but it would be disastrous if her time on Mystifying Monster Island was exposed.

Therefore, when she entered her own merchant alliance, she pretended to be an elder, and couldn’t expose her true identity.

For this reason, too, she also couldn’t transfer more merchant alliance strength to help Yan Yi.

Zi Di gently smiled, “Yan Yi, you are the merchant alliance representative here. The store location is not a big problem and doesn’t need me to get involved in it.”

“You bear full authority in dealing with this problem.”

“Moving on, you have another assignment.”

“There are two potions here, the Sweet Blood Potion and the Green Hat Healing Potion.”

“You will get me good sales. The price cannot be altered, and you will put forth your full effort in accomplishing this assignment.”

Zi Di handed over the potions while informing Yan Yi of how they were used.

“Starting tomorrow, you will prioritize advertising this at the Mercenary Guild.” Cang Xu suddenly reminded him before they left.

Yan Yi revealed his gratitude, “Two elders thank you for acting. These potions will have great sales, and we can save this store!”

But Zi Di waved her hand, “This is a minor attempt. Don’t hold your hopes too high. The store location business is still yours.”

Upon seeing the two elders’ casual attitude, Yan Yi showed hesitation, and his medicinal market prospects dampened somewhat.

The next morning, the dragonman youngster entered the Mercenary Guild with his head and chest held high.

He immediately attracted the eyes of almost everyone.

He was a dragonman, scarlet red dragon scales, deep dragon eyes, and a silver level aura made it abundantly clear that he had powerful battle strength.

The Bandage Mercenary Corps commander was also at the guild.

“It’s Long Fu…” He instantly recognized the dragonman youngster.

Despite being the first time meeting each other, the Bandage Mercenary Corps commander had already obtained pertinent information on the former.

As a fellow mercenary, he had followed the news of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps more than most.

Due to how large the Snake Rat naval battle was, along with Tan Qiu being unable to lock down news, information on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had spread profusely.

Therefore, Commander Beng Dai had a deeper understanding of the dragonman youngster.

He knew that the alliance had paid a disastrous cost during the Snake Rat naval battle. Silver level cultivators had died, most unfortunate of which was the Sheng Ming priest. During the battle, the only silver level that could face Rou Zang directly was the dragonman youngster.

“Long Fu’s bloodline must be impressive.”

“His dragon scales can compete with gold level armor and can resist gold level battle skills.”

“What a striking figure!”

When the Commander Beng Dai saw the dragonman youngster with his own eyes, he felt the latter’s ferocious and dauntless aura, while also sensing his staunchly determined and calm demeanor, causing his assessment of the dragonman youngster to skyrocket in his heart.

He decided to step forth and greet him, “Commander Long Fu, it’s great to see you here! I am the Bandage Mercenary Corps commander.”

The dragonman youngster grinned, “I am Long Fu, I know you, your group is one of the two great mercenary corps on Two Eyes Island, please look after me.”

“Now that you’re here, there are three great mercenary corps.” Commander Beng Dai laughed heartily, he seemed very forthright, but his words held a deep meaning to them.

The dragonman youngster shook his head and immediately said, “We are only staying here for a short time until the auction, then we’ll be leaving.”

Commander Beng Dai earnestly gazed into the dragonman youngster’s eyes, and he sensed the young man’s candidness. Upon realizing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wasn’t staying to compete in Two Eyes Island’s mercenary market, his smile grew more cordial.

He didn’t leave, rather he accompanied the dragonman youngster to the front desk.

“Hello, Lord Long Fu.” The staff member bowed respectfully, “What types of missions do you request, I can filter them for you.”

The dragonman youngster coughed, “Register me first.”

Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was only founded on words; they hadn’t registered with the Mercenary Guild yet. Only after registering it could they use the Mercenary Guild to accept normal missions and receive rewards upon competition.

It was like Snake Rat Island, where Zong Ge had to first register with the Hunting Guild to be able to receive missions and rewards.

Chapter 305: Army Training

The staff member softly gasped, he didn’t expect that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wasn’t registered yet.

But after reacting, he immediately helped the dragonman youngster fill in and submit the needed information.

A moment later, the staff member looked embarrassed, “Lord Long Fu, you didn’t fill out everything, even if your registration goes through, your group’s reputation won’t be high. Perhaps you are unaware that many missions not only require a certain mercenary corps level but also look for reputation too. When the level is the same, mission providers value the reputation of the mercenary corps more.”

“If it is convenient, please provide more information. So that during the verification process, your group will have a better reputation.” The staff member suggested.

The dragonman youngster shook his head with a smile, “Unnecessary, this will do.”

Thus, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was registered.

It was ranked one star, and its trust level was also one star, but the lowest level.

“Ridiculous.” The dragonman youngster stated to the Commander Beng Dai.

Commander Beng Dai smiled understandingly, he was more in the know than the staff member and was convinced more so that the dragonman youngster and his people were arms smugglers.

“Your group’s strength is here. Rank matters not. As long as you know success, all will realize that your group is at least four star level strength!” Commander Beng Dai said pleasantries while telling himself to be vigilant.

Mercenary work was an honest business.

But arms smugglers were different.

“These people are obviously criminals; we shouldn’t provoke them at all.”

After finishing registering, the dragonman youngster looked up at the magic panel above the front desk.

Like the Hunting Guild, the magic panel had rolling words that indicated all kinds of missions.

The dragonman youngster said to the staff, “When we paid a visit to the feudal lord, he let us know that in recent times, there have been some predator beast group cleansing missions.”

The clerk hurriedly replied, “That’s right, every year during the rising and falling of the islands, many predators are drawn in. If we don’t clean up these predators on time, if things go critical, a beast tide might be formed. Currently, there are quite a lot of these missions and the rewards for them are considerable.”

“I will let you take a look.”

The clerk manipulated the magic panel.

The panel’s script suddenly changed.

All the related missions appeared.

The service here was punctual.

Due to the dragonman youngster’s current strength and merits, such special treatment received no objections.

The dragonman youngster observed with rapt attention.

Commander Beng Dai smiled at the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster quickly decided to take a shark group elimination mission.

The rewards for this mission was the most generous of its type.

The clerk did the paperwork at once.

After seeing this, Commander Beng Dai felt more relieved.

The truly profitable mercenary missions on Two Eyes Island were all from the five great merchant alliances. Such as merchandise escorting, bodyguard duty, looking for stolen property, and so on.

“When the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps arrived at Two Eyes Island, Hun Tong summoned them first while none of the five great merchant alliances contacted them at all. They don’t have contacts here.” Commander Beng Dai considered to himself.

Outwardly however, he said to the dragonman youngster, “According to your group’s strength, this will be an easy mission.”

“It’s only an elimination mission and the reward is quite ample. That’s the new island.” Commander Beng Dai tossed out his experience in an expression of goodwill.

Before the new island rose, it had been submerged on the seafloor, and as it rose, there was many fish, magic beasts, and other animals still alive in it.

Generally, it was the feudal lord’s faction that would dispatch the army first to cull the remnants of these things on the new island. At the same time, it was an immense fish catch.

But if information on the new island was comparatively thorny, there was too much to do, or the feudal lord’s government was strained, it would issue pertinent missions to the Mercenary Guild.

The dragonman youngster chatted with Commander Beng Dai for a bit before leaving.

He treasured time.

Thus, he didn’t want to waste it chatting.

As he walked towards the door, Yan Yi approached him.

“Two commanders, do you wish to know what the two potions in my hands are?” Yan Yi took out a sample of both as he gave a cajoling smile.

Zi Di, as a level seven elder, had given him an order.

Yan Yi was forced to comply and came to the Mercenary Guild to peddle the potions.

But he hadn’t done well.

No one wanted to buy them.

Beng Dai indifferently and skeptically gazed at Yan Yi.

Yan Yi was just a bronze level and immediately felt the formidable pressure.

The dragonman youngster also waved his hand, gesturing to Yan Yi that he was leaving, then exited through the Hunting Guild’s door.

But Yan Yi didn’t give up, he clenched his teeth and ran after them, “Two commanders, these are no ordinary potions.”

“In my right hand is the Green Hat Healing Potion, it had a great medicinal effect while being quite cheap. A bottle of iron level potion is only 12 silver coins.”

“The Sweet Blood Potion here is even more useful. It can attract sea creatures and cause them to gather up, making it easy to catch them all at once. One jar only costs 20 copper coins, but it can save a lot on manpower.”

“Eh? That sounds a bit interesting.” The dragonman youngster stopped, “Give me two samples.”

“Here you go!” Yan Yi passed two corked samples to the dragonman youngster while also not forgetting about Commander Beng Dai too.

He took out another two samples and gave them to Commander Beng Dai.

But Commander Beng Dai refused them, “This is the Mercenary Guild, when was a peddler allowed in?”

His deliberately raised voice immediately attracted a staff member.

Yan Yi was rushed out past the dragonman youngster.

Commander Beng Dai kindly advised the young man, “Such a peddler won’t have good potions.”

“Don’t try potions wantonly.”

“Because of magic power harmonization, if you consume a potion that conflicts with your battle qi, you might jeopardize your life!”

“During the sale and the auction, all kinds of people will be on the island, a mingling of dragons and snakes.”

“If you want to buy potions, you’d better go to the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance store. There you can feel more confident in their goods and the quality is better there too.”

“As you say.” The dragonman youngster sighed to himself, but on the outside, he humbly accepted the suggestion. “I will go take a look through the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance store.”

Medicine needed to be verified by the market, after all, they went into one’s stomach, and it was normal for the user to be prudent about them.

Six of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships left port.

This act instantly attracted many information gatherers.

Some fishing boats seized the opportunity to covertly watch them from a distance.

Zong Ge was leading the six ships.

This time, the goal was the elimination of a shark group. It was the mission the dragonman youngster took from the Mercenary Guild.

In Zong Ge’s training plan, it was also a good chance to train these mercenaries in dealing with submerged enemies.

“These guys…” Zong Ge successively received news from his subordinates.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish, the bird scouts, and the snake scouts were also supplying Zong Ge with information too.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ coming to Two Eyes Island had caused many powers to pay attention to them.

Despite them getting a lot of information from Snake Rat Island, personally gathering firsthand information was still very important.

“Then take a good look.” Zong Ge smiled.

These intelligence gatherers were also one of his targets.

“They are nearby.” Zong Ge checked an ocean map after transmitting an order.

The six ships slowed and began to look for the shark group.

“I found a shark group!” A lookout reported not long after.

“Encirclement formation.” Zong Ge began to train the ships in formations.

“Throw Sweet Blood Potions.” He then ordered soon after.

Medicinal jars were opened.

The mercenaries stood on the side of the ship, each of them dropping a medicinal jar down.

The potions dropped into the seater and quickly dissolved.

A wide expanse of blood red began to spread.

Sharks smelled the potion’s sweet fishy odor, and one after the other, they swarmed.

They swam through the bloody seawater, gradually growing frenzied, causing the surface of the sea to splash and spray, churning endlessly like boiling water.

Aim at the potion water, and open fire!”

Following Zong Ge’s order, the already aimed cannons boomed.

Artillery shells poured down in torrents like a scattering flock of birds and smashed into the sea.

The sharks were exploded into two or lacerated by the bombardment.

The blood and severed flesh made the sharks more frenzied, and they started to tear each other apart.

The cannons were unending and deafening for fifteen minutes.

“Cease firing.” Using information transmitted by the Deep Sea Monster Fish, Zong Ge had precisely determined when the shark group was nearing total defeat.

This time, the mercenaries’ sailing training had been accomplished to Zong Ge’s satisfaction.

Previously, on Snake Rat Island, he had bitterly drilled this aspect.

Later during the Snake Rat naval battle, their exceptional display had successfully intercepted Rou Zang’s fleeing fleet.

Although after the war the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had taken in many people, these people are all excellent pirates and had sailing skills engraved in their skulls.

At the same time, Zong Ge still made out deficiencies.

“Not enough cannons.”

“Furthermore, these cannons are ordinary. We only have a gold level cannon, and lack bronze level, iron level, and silver level cannons.

“Bombardment battles are the foundation of conventional naval battles.

“Close quarters combat occurs far less than bombardment battles.”

“If the bombardment battle goes well, there will be no need for hand to hand combat, it can determine the war situation and beat the opponent into raising the white flag in surrender.”

Zong Ge was an outstanding officer that always wanted to get a good battle to damage ratio.

He continued to issue orders, and the ships headed to the bloody area the cannons had devastated.

“Deputy commander, there are still magic sharks wreaking havoc in it!” A lookout reported.

“That is what I want to use.” Zong Ge laughed, “Is Kuai Tui and the others ready?”

A subordinate promptly reported, “They are prepared!”

“Then have them deploy.” Said Zong Ge.

Kuai Tui was an iron level dogman.

A long rope connected to the mast was tied around his waist, and behind him, other mercenaries were tied with ropes too.

By Kuai Tui were other dogmen fighters like him.

After receiving Zong Ge’s order, they jumped from the ship and dove into the seawater.

In the bloody red water, line of sight was obstructed greatly.

Kuai Tui and the others dropped into the seawater and received the charging shark group.

Wielding cutlasses, they tussled with the sharks.

When they needed to take a breath or were in a dangerous situation, they would be yanked up by mercenaries onboard the ships.

Chapter 306: Necromancy Materials

The training continued for thirty minutes.

When Kuai Tui and the dogmen returned to the ships, none were unscathed.

Many were seriously injured; some even had their arms or thighs torn off and swallowed by sharks.

Kuai Tui’s arm was bitten, a bloody chuck missing.

A box of medicine was given to them.

Kuai Tui didn’t prioritize taking medicine, instead he first inspected how his fellows were doing.

The seriously wounded dogmen were either missing an arm or a leg.

Upon seeing Kuai Tui coming to console them, they endured their pain, “Clan elder, don’t worry!”

“Don’t worry, we’ll find your arms and legs.”

“You will also be sent to the church to be healed.”

“The healing divine spell will weaken your bodies for a time, but it will preserve your limbs intact.”

Kuai Tui comforted the severely injured dogmen before then inspecting the ones with minor injuries.

The lightly wounded dogmen took healing potions.

The potions alleviated their injuries and restored them to health.

Kuai Tui also took some medicine, and the bloody hole in him faded away. The wound was patched by a lump of pink skin, the hair once there needed time to regrow.

“This iron level healing potion is quite strong!”

“The residual effects are small too.”

Kuai Tui looked at his clansmen; each of their heads’ hair turned green, some were light green, some were straw green, and others had a dark green.

The intensity of green was based on how many potions one took.

“Most crucial is our battle qi…” Kuai gazed into the distance where Zong Ge was.

“Quick Knife battle qi…don’t tell me you want to establish a battle squad?” Kuai Tui felt that was the case somewhat but couldn’t verify it as so.

“Today’s training was very difficult, and different from before.”

“Was it his intention to use a blurry field of view to help train our quick knives?”

“This seems to be battle skill training?”

“We use the same battle qi, and if we use the same battle skills, we should be a battle squad. Could it be that Lord Shi Qi has a battle squad training method?”

Kuai Tui constantly guessed to himself.

Zong Ge closely followed how the dogmen were doing, both physically and mentally.

An outstanding officer not only trained soldiers’ bodies, but also their minds.

The half beastman placed great expectations on these dogmen.

They were one of his future chips.

If a Quick Knives battle squad was formed, not only could they fight in narrow ship holds and crowded decks during naval combat, but they could also fight on large battles on dry land displaying battle strength in all terrains.

The cannon problem could not be solved by Zong Ge, but in this training exercise, he could exhaust the limits of his capabilities.

“Kuai Tui and the rest should faintly sense it.”

“But it’s a pity, battle squad training needs a more secret environment.”

“Furthermore, we lack the relevant supplies.”

“If I had a barracks, abundant supplies, and a month of training, I could form the initial form of a battle squad.”

When Zong Ge thought of that, he looked towards the aft.

“The shark group mission has ended; they have seen what they should see.”

In fact, before Zong Ge’s six ships returned to port, information on them had already been delivered to the heads of every power.

After Commander Beng Dai looked at it, he sighed, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is newly established, but they have recruited many pirate elites. Most of their members are Rou Zang’s people.”

“Why can’t I have their kind of luck?”

Commander Beng Dai envied the quality of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ members.

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps was currently one of the three big mercenary corps on Two Eyes Island.

Commander Tie Bang was a middle aged man, with a tall and sturdy figure, and a solemn face.

He had been eating.

Even as he ate, he didn’t put down his weapon.

He always carried a two meter long iron club on his back, its tip covered with sharp spikes. The iron club emitted a silver level aura and was high quality.

After Commander Tie Bang looked at the information, the things he paid attention to were different from Commander Beng Dai.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps have beastmen?”

“It seems they are valued by Deputy Commander Shi Qi; he is attempting to train them hard.”

“They used potions?”

“One can gather fish schools, the other is a healing potion.”

“A good idea.”

“Investigate those two potions. Also investigate their food.”

“I heard dogmen have a unique flat bread that is cooked on stones.”

“I want to know whether they produce that food or not.”

Commander Tie Bang continued to issue scouting orders.

Hun Tong’s desk also had information related to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ actions.

“Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps control a gold level cannon, they lack basic cannons.”

“Their ranks have beastmen, and although not many, they are a hidden danger.”

Hun Tong glanced at it, then put it to the side.

He continued to deal with other things.

“This year, the beast group situation seemed to be much bigger than previous years.

“The auction cannot be compromised.”

“Have the patrols also cull some scavenger groups.”

Two Eyes Island was far bigger than Snake Rat Island, and a gold level like Hun Tong did not follow the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps too closely.

Wisteria store.

Bit by bit, people came to make discreet inquiries with the salesclerk about the Sweet Blood Potion and the Green hat Healing Potion.

The salesclerk threw up his hands and helplessly told the prospective buyers, “All of our stock was purchased by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. But there are still some samples left, honored customers, you can try them out first and come back later.”

The salesclerk found Yan Yi and informed him of this rarely seen market situation.

Yan Yi had already inquired the reason, “It is because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps used those two potions during their mission today. Since every power is watching them intently, they have become interested in those two potions.”

The salesclerk cheerfully replied, “Lord Yan Yi, in light of this, we should replenish our stock. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ representative has already come over to preorder much more. It seems other powers also desire to purchase them too.”

Yan Yi nodded, “I will go do that.”

But he was unsure.

Because he didn’t know where to find Zi Di and Cang Xu.

The two Wisteria elders were truly elusive, and not a tail of them had been seen, their whereabouts were a real mystery.

The impact the survivors had on Two Eyes Island was expanding.

The old island.

The magic light here didn’t illuminate the vendor stall very much.

Fei She was standing in front of the stall, and with two fat fingers, he twirled some fine powder in them. He felt it, then raised his fingers up to smell and taste it.

A strong stench of feces assaulted his nose, and it tasted somewhat rancid, as if it came from the rectum of a sick person that ate onions and then was left out overnight.

“Customer, what I have here is traditional fish excrement coral powder.” The stall owner said.

Fish excrement coral was fish manure that covered the surface of coral all year round, and in special environments, this process would gradually grow a type of coral.

After this coral was grinded to dust, it became a base alchemy material.

Two Eyes Island was a coral ball cage tree and had many unique types of coral growing on it.

Fei She was an ordinary person and didn’t have an alchemy device that could determine whether it was a genuine or fake good, thus, he looked at Cang Xu next to him.

The stall owner also cast a reverent gaze at Cang Xu.

Cang Xu emitted an iron level magician aura, and with both hands behind his back, he looked down at the fish excrement coral powder in the stall. Then, he slightly nodded.

Upon getting Cang Xu’s approval, Fei She said to the vendor owner, “Then, then how much, how much is it?”

The stall owner was mildly startled; he didn’t take Fei She as a stutterer.

He announced the price, but Fei She shook his head, “Expensive, too expensive. I believe the price is fair, fairer.”

He raised four fingers.

The vendor owner declined, “This is the freshest goods.”

Both sides wrangled and haggled.

The vendor owner didn’t think that Fei She had such an understanding of the market price of fish excrement coral powder and had no choice but to cut the price down.

“Do you have any other goods? Such as seafloor magic beast corpses and the like.” Stated Cang Xu.

The vendor owner stated that he himself didn’t but could show where such things were.

He knew some stores with the goods.

The vendor owner was right when he said these goods were the freshest.

Two Eyes Island’s new island hadn’t been rising for long, and inside it there were still magic beasts and fish schools that needed to be purged.

To refine magic potions, Zi Di needed raw materials. At the same time, from the team’s point of view, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Group also needed to stock up on materials for normal consumption.

The market price familiar Fei She and the magician Cang Xu joined together to form a two man procurement team.

It wasn’t unusual that they were a team.

Many shoppers were moving between stores and stalls.

These people not only came from Two Eyes Island, but also from surrounding waters.

The bargain sale covered the entire island’s market, and during this time, all kinds of goods were discounted.

It had been Hun Tong’s idea.

It was said that in the beginning, some merchants were very resentful.

But as the island expanded, and attracted more buyers every year, the merchants gradually tasted sweet benefits. In current times, all of them leap to join in and even shout their praise for it.

Cang Xu and Fei She followed the vendor owner to a family store.

After understating what was going on, the salesclerk there said, “Yes, we have the goods in storehouse 3.

The three people went to go find the merchandise.

A gigantic fish skeleton soon appeared before Cang Xu and Fei She.

The light inside the storehouse wasn’t good, however, the fish skeleton emitted a bright white light and was very conspicuous, for its iron level aura mixed with a strange gloominess and coldness.

When Cang Xu saw it, his eyes couldn’t help but contract somewhat.

It was necromancy materials!

His heart rejoiced.

Cang Xu craved necromancy materials.

But previously, he had gained nothing from the cemetery.

On the one hand, the cemetery was subjected to the Wealth Church’s divine spells, and on the other hand, even if there were old graves, due to the coral ball cage tree’s growth, the gravesites would shift and be very hard to locate. Thirdly, the cemetery was tightly guarded. Wealth Church believers had a social custom of reburying their dead, which attracted grave robbers; naturally after many hard lessons, their graveyards were rigorously guarded.

After Cang Xu reflected, he decided to try his luck on this sale.

Chapter 307: Ashen Death Mask

Cang Xu didn’t believe in the Luck Goddess, and as a magician, he always planned before moving, therefore despite this being a lucky stroke, he acted as he usually did.

He believed that every time Two Eyes Island sank and rose, the local ecology would be overturned and cause many underwater creatures to fight each other. It was only after the turmoil had long past did a new ecosystem establish itself.

The corpses of the turmoil’s dead would accumulate, and their souls would tangle together, likely forming into necromancy materials.

Therefore, Cang Xu had come with Fei She, firstly so that he could identify the materials and prevent the ordinary Fei She from being swindled, and because Cang Xu was searching for necromancy materials.

Sure enough, he ran into one.

“These are the bones of an ice blade fish. Look at its top fin, it is just like a knife, and its ice element is very dense. The problem is that the fish bones have a death aura winding around them and must be processed further.” The salesclerk presented.

The winding death aura was a characteristic of necromancy materials, and if processed, it would instead be destroyed.

As far as Cang Xu was concerned, that would cause its value to plummet.

Normally, these fish bones would have been sold as ice path alchemy materials.

“How much?” Cang Xu asked.

The salesclerk stated the price.

Cang Xu didn’t reply, instead he looked at Fei She.

The necromancer didn’t know the market price and wasn’t good at haggling.

Fei She opened his stammering mouth and began to bargain.

The salesclerk quickly began to sweat from the ensuing word duel.

At first because of Fei She’s stutter, he was somewhat frivolous, but he soon began to feel how shrewd Fei She was.

Although Fei She was an ordinary person, he was a Wisteria Merchant Alliance elder!

His business ability trounced the salesclerk by miles.

To him, haggling with the salesclerk was like using an ox knife to kill a chicken.

After a few minutes, the visibly pale salesclerk and Fei She had come to an agreement, the fish would cost 62 silver coins.

But just as it was about to be passed over, another salesclerk brought a new buyer in.

This purchaser was dressed from head to toe in a black gown and they wore a black mask that emitted a gold luster.

When the buyer saw the fish bones, they expressed their interest at once.

“This is ours; we already purchased it.” Fei She replied with displeasure.

The salesclerk that received them vigilantly agreed, “Correct.”

When the other salesclerk realized competition was afoot, they immediately said, “You haven’t received payment for the goods yet, it doesn’t count as a finished transaction. How much are you selling it for?”

The first salesclerk was distressed but was forced to say it.

Just as he stated the price, the black gowned buyer laughed, “I offer 80 silver coins!”

The salesclerk who received Fei She and Cang Xu grew unsightly, then he glanced at Cang Xu, showing their faint anticipation.

Fei She got the cue, “85 silver, silver coins.”

The black gowned buyer immediately replied, “90 silver.”

This price exceeded the market price, causing Fei She to look at Cang Xu.

The old magician was the decider.

Cang Xu muttered, “95 silver.”

The black gowned buyer sneered, “1 gold.”

Cang Xu watched the black gowned figure with interest as he increased the price by 10 silver.

The black gowned buyer raised his voice and shouted, “1 gold, 50 silver!”

As he spoke, an iron level aura emitted from him.

Shockingly, they were also a magician!

Cang Xu took a step back and held out a hand, “It’s yours.”

He conceded.

Soon after the black gowned buyer provided payment and put the fish bones into their storage bag.

He didn’t stay any further and left the shop.

Cang Xu told Fei She, “Continue to browse for products, I’ll be back.”

He left the store and quietly caught up with the black gowned buyer.

The two winded around vendor booths and passed through a narrow path.

The people around them gradually thinned out.

Suddenly, the black gowned buyer turned a street corner and disappeared into a dusky alley.

Cang Xu narrowed his eyes and stopped walking; he was at the port.

His cautious nature caused him to hesitate.

“It is unsuitable to act on Two Eyes Island.”

“Although I can use magic scrolls, using them will be unprofitable.”

“I don’t know anything about that person.”

“I don’t have any equipment on hand!”

Magicians needed time. If one had qualified magician equipment, they could cast faster and more easily handle suddenly occurring situations.

The more Cang Xu pondered, the less his impulses were.

Finally, he sighed and turned around to leave.

Not long after he left, the figure of the black gowned buyer emerged from the alley.

As the black gowned buyer watched Cang Xu leave, they coldly snorted, their ice-cold and hoarse voice echoing from their mask, “Smart guy.”

Cang Xu returned to the market and continued to buy needed supplies.

After spending half the day, he and Fei She returned to the ships with lots of materials.

Upon seeing Zi Di, Fei She spiritedly expressed that the discount sale was a great chance to stock up on materials.

Zi Di inventoried the materials and looked at the purchase prices, she too admired Hun Tong’s business strategy.

Cang Xu spoke, “I noticed that the most purchased material is green hair turtle shells. How much Green Hat Healing Potion do you plan to refine?”

Cang Xu was somewhat worried.

“I have faith.” Zi Di smiled, “The marketplace will have a favorable reaction.”

Fei She also comforted Cang Xu’s shoulder, “No, no mistake!”

The two were both brimming with self-confidence.

This kind of confidence occurred together with their continually deepening understanding of the market here.

Knowing who the two were, Cang Xu nodded and chose to trust them.

After inventorying the supplies, Cang Xu whispered to Zi Di, “I found something.”

He spoke of the black gowned buyer as well as his brief episode with them.

“I feel he is a necromancer!” Declared Cang Xu.

Zi Di was flabbergasted, “Based on what?”

She knew Cang Xu, he wouldn’t speak such things thoughtlessly.

Cang Xu changed the topic, “Did you know the Great Pain Temple’s battle qi cultivation has four aspects? Rou Zang had cultivated the flesh aspect; therefore, his physical nature was particularly developed. Gu Qiao was a monk of the bone aspect. Apart from those, there is also the blood aspect and the rarest, the heart aspect.”

“I only heard about that after the Snake Rat naval battle.” Zi Di did not understand why Cang Xu was bringing this up.

Cang Xu continued, “The Great Pain Temple is like that, and our necromancy school is the same, there are now three aspects that have been developed. They are the bone aspect, the corpse aspect, and the soul aspect. We are of the soul aspect, experts at making, storing, and controlling ghosts. The bone aspect gets their strength from undead skeletons.”

“That black gowned person’s mask seemed to be made of metal, but it shouldn’t be. I heard that it is a bone aspect necromancy equipment, called the ashen death mask. That mask can conceal one’s aura and can let a magician pretend to be an ordinary person. Furthermore, it also has a texture similar to metal.”

Zi Di sank into deep thought, and she muttered, “As Two Eyes Island grew to the present day, the sale and the auction have had a real impact on the people. Every time it happens, not only are people on Two Eyes Island interested but also those of nearby islands and surrounding seas. It isn’t strange a necromancy snuck in with them.”

Cang Xu replied, “I cannot be certain. It is just one possibility.”

“Even if it is just a possibility…” Zi Di’s eyes gradually shined, “We must grab hold of this thread, we cannot abandon it so easily!”

“The black gowned buyer purchased the ice blade fish bones at a premium, it is possible that they considered its worth as necromancy material. If they really are a necromancer, if we could capture him…”

Zi Di couldn’t help but be excited.

She lacked necromancy resources.

Cang Xu was fine, for he had already reached the limits of his gifts and unearthed all his potential.

While Zi Di hadn’t opened her magic power pool for long, and was still only a bronze level magician, she desperately wished that she could be promoted to the iron level.

But she lacked the resources.

Due to that shortfall of necromancy materials, they couldn’t build a suitable meditation environment.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s meditation room was high-level, but Zi Di couldn’t utilize it.

Meditating by herself was not very efficient.

Especially because her workload was heavy and her time scarce, thus Zi Di desperately needed necromancy path resources.

She and Cang Xu conversed, and the two decided that they would follow this trail and do a deep investigation.

Cang Xu was happy he and Zi Di could reach a consensus.

“But the problem is we don’t have time.” Cang Xu threw up his hands, “I plan to continue my deep research into the Bloodline Purification Formation as well as the memory modification device.”

Zi Di also shook her head.

Cang Xu was not good at refining medicine, thus while managing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ financial affairs, she also had to refine medicine, causing her to be very industrious for the last several days.

When Zi Di thought of that, she sighed and expressed her awkwardness.

“Investigation is not our field of expertise.”

“We had better entrust this to another.”

“Unfortunately, our group doesn’t have a person talented at that.”

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish and the scouting puppets are not appropriate to gather this kind of information.

“Speaking of which, we have recently lost several scouts we deployed into Two Eyes Island.”

Cang Xu muttered, “With the auction approaching, there are all kinds of people on the island, the situation has become complex, and the undercurrent is turbulent. I suggest we withdraw the scouts so as to avoid provoking unnecessary trouble.”

Zi Di thought about it and agreed.

Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps could hold off a gold level enemy, they were currently waiting for a reply and fighting to redeem themselves, thus, the less complications now, the better.

“Even though Two Eyes Island has a Thief Guild branch, since the matter concerns a necromancer, we cannot entrust it to others.”

“Then we better have Xu Ma do it.”

After thinking it through, they realized they had no one else.

Xu Ma was the most suitable person.

Even though he had just been stabbed.

After discussing that matter thoroughly, that afternoon, Zi Di and Cang Xu once again came to the Wisteria store.

When Yan Yi saw them, he excitedly gave them a thumbs-up and excessively fawned, “Lord Elders, your potions are quite outstanding.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps bought almost all of them.”

“Later on, there were many customers who wanted to buy them too.”

“But we don’t have any to sell.”

“It has genuinely made me anxious!”

Zi Di waved her wands to and fro, and took out a pile of medicine, “Take it and sell it. But don’t just sell it to a single party. At the same time, the price will also be adjusted as follows.”

After carefully giving instructions, she and Cang Xu left.

Chapter 308: Booming Sales

The Wisteria store quickly displayed a large shop sign and used scarlet words to indicate that the medicine shipment had arrived.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had tried it out in front of everyone, and soon after, the store provided samples. After all sorts of powers or individuals used the samples, they sensed their medicinal effects.

Therefore, when the Wisteria store displayed the sign, customers immediately rushed over to buy the potions.

Yan Yi rigorously followed Zi Di’s commands, and did not exclusively trade to one power, but did his best to expand the marketplace splash.

Very quickly, the two potions had lifted a minor craze in Two Eyes Island’s medicinal market.

“Your Lordship, these are the two potions.” In the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance shop, a clerk returned with the Sweet Blood Potion and the Green Hat Healing Potion.

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance was one of the six great merchant alliances and mainly engaged in the drug trade.
