They were also Zi Di’s greatest opponent in terms of medicine sales.

As the front-runner in this sphere, the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance had keenly sensed the moment this craze had occurred.

The store manager immediately assigned someone to purchase some for study.

“Current testing has determined that the Sweet Blood Potion’s ingredients are bat wings, snake teeth, and snow sugar. The quality ranges from bronze to iron level.

“The Green Hat Potion’s main ingredient is the green hair turtle shell; the other materials are unknown. The quality ranges from iron to silver level.

The Secret Medicine store’s people quickly got some results.

The merchant alliance’s manager’s brows were tightly wrinkled.

“How terrible.”

He was no alchemy master, but he had a deep understanding of the Secret Medicine’s marketplace.

By looking at the material analysis, they determined the production costs for these two potions weren’t high.

From the observed medicinal effects, both were stronger than and far surpassed similar potions.

“From the price, the advantage of these two potions is very clear.”

“It seems to justify their low cost.”

“Of course, we cannot eliminate the possibility of a deliberate discount to attack the market.”

“The Sweet Blood potion appeared when the market demanded a way to handle oceanic beast groups, thus, it filled a small gap in the market. The Green Hat Healing Potion is a greater threat. The Wealth Church is not good at healing divine spells and is the only source of healing on Two Eyes Island. Who wouldn’t stock up on healing potions before sailing out?”

The more the store manager mulled it over, the deeper their brows tightened.

He gradually took a seat.

They looked at the reporting subordinate, their tone somewhat fidgety, “Is this all you could find?”

“My Lord, I am only an alchemy apprentice.” The subordinate also looked anxious, “Perhaps we can ask Lord Hun Tong for help. He has a silver level alchemy master subordinate.”

The store manager thought about it, and shook their head, “Unnecessary. We will go to the Wisteria store and buy the potion recipe from them!”

The store manager and the alchemy apprentice quickly went to the Wisteria store.

He didn’t enter, rather he stood next to the store doorway.

He had forgotten all about this remote shop and tossed it into some corner of his mind.

To the six great merchant alliances, the Wisteria Merchant Alliance was just a name now. The ungraduated magician apprentice that was their merchant alliance president getting lost at sea was further proof of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance’s miserable situation.

But things had changed.

The Wisteria store’s most obvious change was the sign standing at its doorway.

The gigantic sign used the brightest paint to declare – Sweet Blood Potions and Green Hat Healing Potions being sold while supply lasts!

“Excellent.”

“Simple and crude, but straight to the heart.”

The store manager evaluated the line and surmised soon after that it was possible that the Wisteria store might have a custom-made alchemy device. A magic panel type signboard that could replace the current sign.

The signboard’s function seemed to be very clear.

As the store manager stood in the doorway, several customers pushed by them as they went into the store.

The store manager also went in.

The apprentice alchemist subordinate blindly followed suit.

When the two entered the store, they saw the potions spread across the shelves.

The variety was limited to two other potions besides the Sweet Blood Potion and the Green Hat Potion, nothing else was present.

Three clerks were busy.

One was presenting to customers, one was processing transactions, and another was moving merchandise.

Shelves emptied by sold goods were quickly filled.

The scene caused the store manager’s heart to thump, “It seems there are plenty of these potions.”

The store manager could already see that soon; Two Eyes Island’s medicinal market would be battered by the storm they could see before him.

His subordinate was more interested in the placards around the store.

Above the Sweet Blood Potion shelf, there was a sign that said: Sweet Blood Potion – driving both hunters and prey crazy.

The sign above the Green Hat Healing Potion over yonder said: As long as you can survive, there’s no harm having green hair!

“A pretty good saying.” The subordinate felt those two slogans were quite fitting.

The fretful store manager found a clerk and flashed his identity.

He was quickly led to the backroom and met with Yan Yi.

The store manager didn’t bother with pleasantries and instead elucidated his purpose in coming.

“You want to purchase the recipe for those two potions?” Yan Yi was stunned.

Soon after, his eyes flashed sharply.

In the wake of constant growth, Yan Yi gradually realized the value of those two potions. Now, he had suddenly discovered that he had still managed to greatly underestimate the true worth of these potions!

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance store manager went so far as to state his desire to purchase the recipes!!

He knew the other party was the largest front-runner in the drug market, a leviathan existence!

“So then, how much? I can pay twice, no, three times the market value!” The store manager’s rich and overbearing display showed his sincerity and great power.

Yan Yi bitterly laughed and shook his head, “So sorry, I am not the architect of this. These potions were all provided by the two elders.”

“Elders?” The store manager was stunned.

Yan Yi told the truth, “Several days ago, two elders came to the store with a level seven wisteria secret order. These two potions, along with the sales tactics were provided by the elders, I am only the executor of them.”

The store manager asked him to elaborate.

After a long time, his brows furrowed further.

“Wisteria Merchant Alliance elders? Where did they suddenly pop up from? What are they up to?” The store manager realized this was no simple affair, causing his heart to constantly sink.

“There are so many goods, they must have stockpiled a large quantity; it is impossible for so many to be manufactured in so short a time.”

“Do the ashes of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance burn once more?”

“Wasn’t their president lost at sea?’

“If they wanted to rise again, then Two Eyes Island…is indeed a good starting point.”

It was very difficult for the Wisteria Merchant Alliance to survive on the Sheng Ming Continent. If a merchant alliance wanted to revive itself, it was far more challenging to do than their initial rise. Because the vested interests in the shattered Wisteria Merchant Alliance all remembered how wonderful it was to glut themselves, and that the bigger the merchant alliance was, the more they salivated.

They would unwaveringly gaze at the Wisteria Merchant Alliance’s every move. Although greedy, they would also be scared that the Wisteria Merchant Alliance would seek vengeance as it expanded.

Two Eyes Island was different.

It was isolated in the sea, and the six great merchant alliances weren’t as strong here as they were on the Sheng Ming Continent.

At the same time, Two Eyes Island was the economic core of neighboring waters, it was the most developed commerce island, and its long disseminated reputation had a strong effect.

If the Wisteria Merchant Alliance could succeed on this island, then they would take a step towards revival!

“Since this is the case, then please pass on our purchase request. I want to invite the two elders to a comprehensive discussion.” The store manager replied.

Yan Yi promptly expressed that he would!

The store manager and his subordinate left the Wisteria store.

“Your Lordship, are you going to pay a visit to the feudal lord and ask if his silver level alchemy master can help us analyze the potions?” The subordinate suggested.

The store manager shook his head.

Leaving aside how complicated and expensive reverse engineering a recipe was, even if their research derived the correct recipe, it would already be too late.

The Wisteria store would use that time to successfully gain a foothold in the market.

“The trouble isn’t the potions or the recipes; they are just weapons. The trouble is the people behind this.”

The store manager sighed.

Staying here longer was useless, and he had no choice but to return and scheme.

Like a seed beginning to sprout in the soil, the craze over the two potions grew bigger.

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance’ store manager was a person of foresight. While the other islanders gradually detected that these two potions seemed to be trending in popularity.

Even if they did not know about the two potions, whenever they saw someone in the crowd with green hair they would think: what was going on?

The Green Hat Healing Potion had a systemic problem; it dyed people’s hair green.

As a result, that very thing was a striking way to promote it!

Those who used the potion, regardless of what they were doing, would provoke the attention of others.

Curious people would ask, “Brothers, what happened to your hair?”

Those asked would often explain the facts.

Upon hearing about the healing potion, the curious people would then ask, “Eh? How good is the healing effect?”

It was then said to be quite great.

Regardless of the recipe or tools used to refine them, the potions were first class quality goods.

Thus, through this dissemination, the Green Hat Potion’s reputation quickly circulated.

The Wisteria store’s sales figures were constantly rising.

In three short days, the second shipment was completely sold out.

But this time, the Wisteria store wasn’t worried. Because Zi Di had been following the market, and before the second shipment was gone, she had already delivered a third shipment to Yan Yi.

With the withdrawn capital, the ghost girl quickly had an abundance of money on hand.

She continued to buy large amounts of medicinal materials.

Turning them around into refining ever more potions.

Then they would sell, sell, sell!

Sales were booming.

“You’ve proven yourself, Zi Di.” When the dragonman youngster learned this, he was very happy.

Every profession had its merits; if it were him, he could only rely on honest mercenary work and make his living off the mission rewards.

Zi Di said to him, “We stand on War Merchant’s shoulders. All the medicine recipes in War Merchant’s database are outstanding; we just chose two of the most suitable from it. The magic medicine refinement tool is also high quality. The alchemy cabin and the Magic Medicine Bathtub cost ten thousand gold coins to build. This doesn’t include the yellow crab alchemy puppet or even the more valuable tower spirit.”

“The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance proposed purchasing the recipes, but we refused. Will trouble follow?” The dragonman youngster asked.

When Yan Yi received the third shipment, he passed on the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance’s purchase request.

Zi Di refused without the slightest hesitation.

Chapter 309: Dividing the Money

Zi Di nodded to the dragonman youngster, “There will be some trouble.”

“But it won’t be a big issue.”

“The reputation of our two potions is already widespread.”

“This is Two Eyes Island; Hun Tong is watching. Besides, the auction is coming, and even if the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance wanted to cause trouble, their hands are tied.

When Zi Di said that she then suggested, “Perhaps, we should leave some people here so the Sweet Blood Potion and the Green Hat Healing Potion can provide a stable income.”

“When we redeem ourselves in the future, we still need to continue living.”

“By grabbing hold of this business opportunity, our future lives will be more comfortable.”

When she saw her sweetheart, the ghost girl couldn’t help but think freely of how beautiful their future would be.

The dragonman youngster also nodded, “Indeed, we must consider our future.”

“We need solidarity in power, not just wealth, but we also need comrades.”

“But at present, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has expanded too much, the silver level is fine, however, our control over the iron levels and the bronze levels is quite frail.”

“We need Lan Zao, Bai Ya, and the rest to stand up and help.”

“We live and die together, and by the tribulation of Mystifying Monster Island, we trust each other.”

As commander, the dragonman youngster had a clear understanding of what state the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was in.

“At the moment, we need money to buy equipment for them. In the future when we have the Bloodline Purification Formation, we can change their aptitude and gifts. From that base, we can widen their limits. I believe that as long as the Bloodcore is preserved, along with ample time to grow and conduct business, our future will be unimaginable.” The dragonman youngster’s ambitions were bottomless.

Zi Di had convinced him.

“Then lend it to them.”

“In the future, we can also build a capital treasury for the mercenary corps and design a way to select the best talents. Through appropriated funds from the treasury, we can subsidize teams filled with loyal members.”

Zi Di’s line of thought gradually diverged.

If the Bloodline Purification Formation was built, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would complete a qualitative change.

They didn’t need to gather the exceptionally gifted, only those that were loyal. With bloodline alteration, they could manufacture talents by the batch and create a formidable force!

Through the captain room’s window, they could see the port.

The early morning port was tranquil.

The sun had just risen; its yellow light mixed with the ocean’s still coldness as it illuminated the side of the dragonman youngster’s face. To others, it seemed to look like the dragonman youngster was bathing in a layer of heart gladdening radiance.

Lan Zao walked in, “Your Lordship. You summoned me?”

He knelt to the floor and looked up at the dragonman youngster behind his desk, his gaze showing respect, love, adoration, as well as a trace of devotion.

Lan Zao’s experience on Mystifying Monster Island had always tormented him.

The dragonman youngster had saved his life.

He had not only saved Lan Zao in body but also in spirit.

Especially during the Snake Rat naval battle, when the dragonman youngster dragged him out of his ocean of regretful sorrow.

To Lan Zao who had nearly drowned spiritually, it had been no less than salvation for him.

From now on, he could face his sins.

So long as he followed the young man, Lan Zao believed firmly that he too could walk the path of righteousness.

Therefore, in Lan Zao’s eyes, no matter how much the dragonman youngster’s appearance changed, the latter’s nature was always the same.

The young man was the brilliant flag that guided him forward.

“Please give me your commands. No matter the mission, I will do anything to complete it!” Lan Zao’s sonorous voice and expression revealed his inhuman determination.

Upon seeing Lan Zao like this, the dragonman youngster couldn’t help but think, “If this comrade is unworthy of our help, who would be worthy then?”

A moment later, the dragonman youngster smiled, “Stand up, Lan Zao. No need to be so zealous.”

Lan Zao stood up perfectly straight, like a knight always waiting for their sovereign king’s command.

He would not hesitate to brave any danger if commanded!

“Recently, the situation hasn’t been well?” The dragonman’s eyes penetrated Lan Zao, keenly observing the purple bruises on the latter’s arm and chest.

For the last few days, Lan Zao and the pirates had fought on multiple occasions.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ immense expansion was overall stable due to the silver level leadership, the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge, suppressing those beneath.

But hidden in the lower levels, there was nevertheless chaos.

This was the world of the strong, and people worshiped the strong.

Lan Zao’s identity as a survivor was strictly kept secret, and the recently joined pirates only knew him as a mercenary that had joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps on Snake Rat Island.

As time went on, if they just joined later, why should he be valued more? Why was he placed as one of the bronze level leaders?

Lan Zao didn’t seem that strong!

“Don’t tell me I have to be under a weaker person?”

“Can’t I climb up?”

These kinds of thoughts lingered in the minds of the former pirates, spurring them on to find trouble with Lan Zao.

Lan Zao had encountered many confrontations and challenges.

Although drawn swords were unlikely, occasional fist fights were still painful.

Crucially, Lan Zao’s displayed battle strength wasn’t convincing enough.

When faced with the dragonman youngster’s question, Lan Zao tightened his lips and looked ashamed, “I am very sorry to have disappointed your Lordship.”

He thought that the dragonman youngster would excoriate him, but a moment later, he heard the young man laughing, “Hehehe. Relax, no need to be so anxious. I have always believed that you would assume personal responsibility. Lan Zao, we have fought side by side many times. I know that you have potential, that there is much you can improve on.”

But Lan Zao was dour, he treated those words as mere consoling, “My Lord, my bloodline is no good, I have already unearthed all of my potential and reached the peak of my life already.”

“No, you have not. You haven’t used the Bloodline Purification Formation yet.” The dragonman youngster said to himself.

Lan Zao was still a cultivator, even though his limit was the bronze level.

But as long as they found a better bloodline in Lan Zao, utilized the Bloodcore to absorb lifeforms that had it, then used the Bloodline Purification Formation on him, then they could improve Lan Zao’s bloodline concentration.

This was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ main goal.

Since the Deceptive Disguise Formation and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy Formation were already built, medicinal testing of bloodlines with and building the Bloodline Purification Formation were now the most important development goal.

Lan Zao didn’t have hope in himself, but the dragonman youngster had great expectations for him still.

Of course, since this plan concerned the Bloodcore, he couldn’t speak about it right now.

The dragonman youngster took out a bag of money, got up, and placed it in Lan Zao’s hands.

“Go to the island and buy some equipment.”

“Your Lordship!” Lan Zao’s heart shook.

He unconsciously wanted to refuse.

“I won’t permit your refusal!” The dragonman youngster solemnly displayed his might.

Lan Zao instantly stopped moving.

“Consider it a loan.” The dragonman youngster clapped Lan Zao’s shoulder and continued to console him, “You don’t need to feel any burden. After you become stronger, you will stabilize your position as the bronze level leader. This will be helpful for our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

“This subordinate understands!” Lan Zao took a deep breath; his eyes were red with dripping tears.

He really needed this money!

By the time he left the captain’s room, he had already wiped away his hot tears and cleaned up his face.

He clutched the pouch to his chest.

He didn’t want to show weakness; he would answer the young captain’s expectations and become the strongest bronze level!

The afternoon ocean surface reflected the sun’s sharp arrows.

The dry heat caused mercenaries to hide in the ship holds.

Anyways, on the port.

The morning training had exhausted many mercenaries, and after eating their lunch, all of them were sleeping heavily.

Zong Ge was studying his training manual in his captain’s room.

Tripleblade had come to visit, he didn’t look well, “My Lord, there is a matter I must inform you of at once. This morning, the commander over there distributed a lot of money.”

“Lan Zao, Fei She, Mu Ban, Que Shan, and even Bai Ya were given some.”

Zong Ge had been amending the training manual. When he heard this information, he didn’t raise his head and his pen didn’t stop, he continued to write as he said, “For the last few days, the potion sales have been prosperous, it seems they have obtained a lot of capital.”

“Previously, the mercenary corps’ funds were in short supply, now this problem has been greatly alleviated, this is a good thing.”

Tripleblade’s brows tightly wrinkled, “But we haven’t gotten any money.”

“The potion recipes come from War Merchant’s database, and the Magic Medicine Bathtub is our shared spoil of war. We deserve a portion of the profits from the produced potions.”

“Furthermore, all the others got some, why not us?”

Zong Ge shook his head, “There is an issue with your words. Indeed, the recipes and the Magic Medicine Bathtub belong to everyone.”

“But the raw materials for the potions were purchased with the 300 gold coins that came from Zi Di’s Wisteria Merchant Alliance.”

“She only used the recipes, the Magic Medicine Bathtub, and the alchemy cabin, that’s it.”

“It was her medicinal abilities and business methods that allowed her to be profitable.”

Although Zong Ge didn’t understand business, he had a broad scope on things. On Snake Rat Island, he experienced how amazing Zi Di’s business finesse was.

“Besides Zi Di, no other person could accomplish this.”

Tripleblade grinded his teeth, unreconciled, “But when she refined potions, she depleted much of the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s magic power.”

Zong Ge chuckled, still immersed in revising his training book, his brief words had silenced Tripleblade, “Do you really think that someone as shrewd as Zi Di would leave behind such obvious leverage? I believe that she has already replenished the magic power. After all, elemental crystals are very common, especially on Two Eyes Island, where they can be effortlessly purchased.”

Tripleblade did not reply.

Zong Ge then asked, “How was the money specifically given out?”

“Everyone has differing amounts. It is entirely divided up according to status. According to them, these are all loans that will be repaid in the future. Besides, if one wanted more, they could make a request.” Answered Tripleblade.

“Good.” When Zong Ge heard that, he sincerely exclaimed in admiration.

On the surface, it seemed that directly giving people money won over the people’s hearts.

But the detriments outweighed the benefits.

If an organization wanted to expand, their foundation was their laws and standards.

Relying on the kindness of charity to win over the people would inevitably make things murky. Humans had different emotions; how could one calculate how one would react to that?

It would end up with benefits and emotions entangled together. Envy, suspicion, unwillingness, jealousy, grievances, and all other sorts of feelings would form internal friction, and no organization could walk far with that present.

It was an incorporeal pitfall, but the young man and woman had stepped across it.

After crossing it, they could suddenly see far more.

Chapter 310: Pirate Booty

Tripleblade didn’t give up, “My Lord, perhaps we too can apply for some capital.”

“We are short on money!”

“Most crucially, with that money, your Lordship can heal your arm injury.”

“That injury has greatly encumbered you.”

“That wound prevents your Lordship from cultivating, stagnating your battle strength.

“You are but a step away from the gold level.”

“If you cross that step, your world will completely change.”

“Then, you shall be the mercenary corps’ sole gold level!”

Zong Ge stopped writing, he earnestly looked at Tripleblade, and solemnly asked, “Tripleblade, do you consider me a shameless person?”

“Your Lordship, I had no intention of offending you!” Tripleblade immediately knelt.

He wholeheartedly followed the half beastman.

Zong Ge sighed, “When I asked for a ship, Kuai Tui, and his people, I made my determination clear. In the future, we will seek our success in the Wilderness Continent and won’t be with these people any longer.”

“Since we are separate, I will maintain my independent nature.”

“This is the cost of that.”

“If we snuggle up when there are benefits and hold our independence when there are not, I will despise myself.”

When Zong Ge said that his ears seemed to ring with grumbles and complaints.

Back in the day, he had been a general.

He frequently had subordinates complaining and grumbling.

“My Lord, why are our supplies being replenished slower than other units?”

“It infuriates me! Today when army provisions were provided, not only were we last, but we were given less supplies than the others!”

“We are obviously the same rank and file, why do we get less?”

“This is unacceptable! Our merits have helped the most people, yet we lose the most!”

Zong Ge could only bitterly placate his subordinates.

What could he do?

He was a bastard, a half beastman.

He was General Da Shu, and sometimes his military successes had been claimed by others falsely.

He had been discriminated against and treated differently.

His heart concealed indignance and fury, yet his face was filled with apathy.

Back to reality.

To Zong Ge, who had been treated quite unfairly in the past, what was this to him?

Zong Ge was as indifferent as the clouds and wind.

But at that moment, Cang Xu came to his ship.

“Before you go out to morning training, the commander told me to bring you these.”

Cang Xu dropped two bags of money and left after saying his piece.

He was bogged down in work and valued his time; thus, he was direct and efficient.

The two looked at the money bags on the table.

Tripleblade reacted first, after he stepped forth to examine them, his expression became more complicated, “This money…we got the most.”

Zong Ge smiled, he put down his pen and took out some gold coins from the bags.

He sincerely exclaimed in admiration, “Our commander truly is magnanimous.”

He thought to himself, “If I was an ordinary half beastman, I would feel proud to be under his command.”

“Unfortunately, I am not.”

“More regretfully, this troop has no future.”

Zong Ge’s opinion on the survivor team never changed.

Even though the shell of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps covered them, the true core of it was the survivors.

The survivors had too many types of backgrounds, races, and beliefs mingling together.

“This can buy how many cannons? After you purchase the cannons, equip them first.” Zong Ge tossed his bag to Tripleblade.

Tripleblade caught it, “My Lord, healing your injury should take precedence. You recovering your full battle strength is more important than buying cannons.”

Zong Ge shook his head, “Even if you add your bag, there still isn’t enough money for that.”

“It is too expensive to ask a gold level bishop over here to heal my wound.”

“Even Feudal Lord Hun Tong would feel distressed at doing that.”

“I think that even if we pooled all of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ cash together, it still won’t be enough to ask for a gold level bishop’s help.”

“Give up on that unfeasible notion. I expect that a gold level healing potion will appear in the auction.”

Tripleblade had no choice but to concede to Zong Ge’s rationale.

Afterwards, he looked embarrassed, “But it is very difficult to buy cannons from the market.”

The old island’s sale was mostly peddling materials.

The five great merchant alliances were mainly selling cloth, foodstuff, magic potions, ore, and the like.

Not only were weapons and armor in short supply, but also military equipment too.

The new island’s auction would have large deals, but those were mostly in materials too. Cannons sales were very rare.

Two Eyes Island was a developed trading island; however, its manufacturing was not developed at all.

Therefore, Zong Ge and Tripleblade were confronted with an awkward situation.

They had some money.

Not enough to ask the Wealth Church for healing.

Based on market price, they could afford cannons, but they weren’t able to buy any.

Munition sales were mainly traded on the black market.

Large scale external sales of munitions was very taboo.

The empire did not allow this kind of business. Therefore, the six great merchant alliances did not involve themselves in that business.

Otherwise, how could there be arms smugglers?

When he heard Tripleblade’s explanation, Zong Ge exasperatedly thought to himself, “Then you had better buy bronze level blades first.”

His heart was still focused on the Quick Knife Battle Squad.

The old island.

Bai Ya looked at a store sign.

The store sign said: Dark Rainbow Store.

“This is the place.” Bai Ya muttered.

Don’t be fooled by the small shop front and how unremarkable it seemed; it was the old island’s biggest booty market.

Most pirates chose this place to dispose of their stolen goods.

Many people believed that this store was backed by Two Eyes Island’s feudal lord, Hun Tong.

Of course, more people believed that if a genuine investigation was conducted, the Feudal Lord would be completely clean and wouldn’t be implicated by this place one bit.

Bai Ya had heard that Tripleblade had purchased a dozen bronze level war blades from this place.

Afterwards, he heard Lan Zao say that he purchased bronze level equipment from this place.

Bai Ya clucked the coin purse Zi Di had lent to him and entered the small store.

Following the code taught to him, after he and the salesclerk exchanged a code word, he got to see all kinds of equipment.

Cutlasses, pirate hats, peglegs, fishing nets, and even a parrot shaped alchemy puppet…

Most of the equipment was ordinary level, some bronze level ones.

There were few iron level ones.

Bai Ya picked up a crossbow and a firearm and examined them.

He came from a family of hunters and was opinionated towards such long range weapons.

Bai Ya soon felt disappointed.

These weapons were far inferior to the ones the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had in their armory. The alchemy firearms and crossbows they distributed were produced by War Merchant’s arms factory and were a step above all others on the market in quality.

What’s more, Bai Ya now saw that these were defective goods pirates had dumped from their loot.

The real good stuff were kept by the pirates.

Who would sell the things that earn their keep? Unless it was under extraordinary circumstances or as a last resort, this wouldn’t happen.

“Most of the equipment needs battle qi. I am not even a bronze level.”

“My talent is too inferior.”

Bai Ya was depressed.

After escaping Mystifying Monster Island, the dragonman youngster gave the survivors battle qi techniques,

Bai Ya began to cultivate battle qi.

At first, he thought his talent was pretty good.

But later on, after talking to Xu Ma, he learned the cruel truth.

Bai Ya’s talent was terrible and his hope of ever becoming a cultivator was uncertain.

If nothing unexpected happened, he would be just like most people, an ordinary person for the rest of his life.

Bai Ya endured the blow, and didn’t give up, instead cultivating even harder.

After careful selection, he chose from the piled booty a wrist crossbow and a set of leather armor.

Just as he was about to pay, he felt nothing in his chest.

“The money is gone?!” Bai Ya was startled, and he turned around to look all over for it.

He saw a familiar person leaning against the corner of the store, smiling back at him as he held his purse.

“Senior Xu Ma!” Bai Ya was stunned.

He didn’t even realize that another person came into the store.

Xu Ma threw the purse at Bai Ya, “Boy, your vigilance is terrible, you didn’t even know your money was stolen.”

“Anyways, how did you get so much money?”

“I never saw you as a rich guy.”

Of course, this money had been loaned by Zi Di, but Bai Ya couldn’t say that.

Luckily, Xu Ma didn’t dig any deeper, instead he wobbled over to Bai Ya and pointed out, “This wrist crossbow has a problem, it is a machine construct. Don’t be fooled by its bright appearance, it’s obvious it has been tampered with. It might malfunction after a night.”

“The leather armor is actually good, but it doesn’t fit you. You are only an ordinary person, so you should choose this leather armor.”

Xu Ma presented.

He was far more experienced than Bai Ya.

The salesclerk hadn’t ever said a word.

Buying booty depends on having a discerning eye.

Bai Ya heeded Xu Ma’s words and bought another set of leather armor based on his suggestion.

When the two were about to leave the store, Bai Ya curiously asked, “Senior Xu Ma, why are you here? Did you also want to buy equipment?”

“Of course not, I don’t have as much money as you.” Xu Ma sighed.

He had received an order to investigate the black gowned magician.

But he hadn’t made any headway in this matter.

On the one hand, the suspected necromancer was very mysterious, they left behind few traces, and those limited clues were cut short.

On the other hand, Xu Ma didn’t want to do this. He had just been stabbed! Now he was to investigate a magician, an iron level one!

“I am just a small bronze.” Xu Ma shouted to himself, but he couldn’t disobey the order.

He chose to passively slack off. Then, while he was strolling in the discount market, he saw Bai Ya.

He then followed Bai Ya and played a small joke on him.

Xu Ma was a thief with long hands and legs, a natural advantage when it came to stealing.

His battle qi was the Spider Walk technique. Those who cultivated that battle qi had a distinct trait, they could move soundlessly, faster, and more nimbly.

Xu Ma had walked behind Bai Ya, and the instant the salesclerk looked away, he gently lifted the purse from Bai Ya’s chest.

“Since you have money, you might as well drink at the bar with me.” Xu Ma shook Bai Ya’s shoulders.

Bai Ya hesitated, thinking to himself: he still had most of the money, he should return it.

But after thinking again, he realized Xu Ma indeed had been looking after him a lot, and he nodded his agreement.

Chapter 311: Pharmacy Troubles

The two arrived at a pub.

Bai Ya expressed that he wasn’t drinking.

“Then your senior will be impolite, bring over five cups of beer!”

Xu Ma didn’t order the expensive Pine Wind beer, instead he made up for it in quantity.

After drinking the five cups, he drummed his belly with contentment.

After chatting for a bit, Bai Yai stated that he wanted to return to the ship.

But Xu Ma pulled his hand, “Where are we now?”

“Boy, haven’t you come to buy equipment? Then follow me.”

“First, you need a good pair of boots.”

Xu Ma dragged Bai Ya to a small roadside stall.

Bai Ya saw Xu Ma’s “good boots”.

The boots were in terrible condition, the left boot’s front was open, exposing the wearer’s toes. Both boots were so worn down the leather was peeling, and they emitted a foul foot order.

It assaulted the nose.

Bai Ya couldn’t help but guess: how long had these boots been left unwashed?

“Not bad. This is an alchemy item, with magic script on it. Look here, it can steady your gait.” Xu Ma understood a bit about magic script.

It wasn’t strange upon thinking about it, given that he was a thief.

After all, if one didn’t have discerning eyes, one couldn’t determine the true value of good things.

Upon seeing Bai Ya’s weird expression, Xu Ma explained, “You are a hunter, who spent all of their time on dry land, your time at sea has been too brief. With them on, you can maintain your balance during surging gales or during naval battles. It’ll be unlikely you’ll fall into the ocean.”

“Every year, large numbers of sailors and pirates die by falling overboard.”

“Although they are ugly and smell, they are good to wear. It is also cheap, if you find a cobbler in the future, perhaps they can sew up the seams for you.” Xu Ma recommended it enthusiastically.

Bai Ya couldn’t endure his persuasion and bought the boots, and so Xu Ma brought him to another residential area.

This was a cave for ordinary people and was very squalid. Despite Two Eyes Island having flourishing trade, it had no shortage of poor people and beggars.

“Boy, you still got the stuff?” Xu Ma hit a door.

It was a small shack.

Soon after, a dirty head popped out from inside it.

It was a small boy.

The boy’s wary gaze looked at Xu Ma, then replied, “Of course I still have the goods, have you returned to actually buy them?”

Xu Ma shook his head, “To be precise, I am not buying it.”

He pointed his thumb at Bai Ya behind him, “He is the actual buyer.”

The small boy looked at Bai Ya, his expression skeptical, “Can you spare 5 silver coins?”

Bai Ya was startled, and he thought to himself: what thing was so expensive?

Bai Ya unconsciously looked at Xu Ma.

Xu Ma nodded to him, then said impatiently to the small boy, “Bring it over so we can take a look at it now. With great difficulty, I brought over a customer. We don’t have much patience.”

The small boy groaned, “I won’t sell it for anything less than 5 silver coins!”

After saying that, he squeezed back into the shack, and when he reemerged, he had a small flower pot in his hand.

The flower pot had a green branch in it, and when Bai Ya peered into it, he thought it was thick grass.

“This is it.” Xu Ma’s eyes glinted.

After confirming it, he pulled Bai Ya in and urged him, “Take out the money.”

“Oh ho.” Xu Ma naturally reacted when Bai Ya unconsciously took out his purse and fished out 4 silver coins and 100 copper coins.

Both sides transferred money and goods.

“There are only 4 silvers here, 1 silver is missing.” The small boy shouted.

Xu Ma squeezed out a silver coin into his hand and coldly snorted, “Give me a break! I went through trials and tribulations to get you a customer, the transaction is completed, I take my cut!”

“Also, I advise you to not kick up a racket.” Xu Ma lowered his voice and looked down at the small boy, doing his best to oppress the latter.

“You don’t want others knowing you suddenly have money do you?”

The small boy was suppressed, and couldn’t help but take a step back, his red eyes rigidly staring at Xu Ma.

But he didn’t make a sound.

Bai Ya sympathized somewhat with the small boy.

Xu Ma and Bai Ya departed the slums.

“Don’t sympathize with him, young man.”

“How could that stinky little demon have such a good thing?”

“He didn’t gather it, he stole it. Or perhaps he swindled it or snatched it.”

“Don’t underestimate poor people. They have no bottom line as they struggle to survive.”

“Furthermore, we already paid enough!”

Bai Ya was inevitably curious, “What is this, why is it so expensive?”

“It was bought at market price; we didn’t pay a premium.” Xu Ma corrected, “It is known as Snake Grass. It can move, you see.”

As he said that, he pulled out the grass.

After being separated from the flower pot’s soil, the snake grass instantly changed; both ends stretched out and it became slender.

Then, the grass’ end tapered into a snake head and its roots turned into a snake tail.

The snake head opened its mouth; it wanted to bite; however, Xu Ma’s two fingers rigidly held it shut.

The snake grass could only whine and wind its snake body around its arm, squeezing it and wanting to snap Xu Ma’s bones.

Bai Ya jumped back in fear at this change.

“Snake grass is a plant, however, when it comes time to attack, it can briefly turn into a grass vine that strikingly resembles a snake. It can move and is aggressive.”

“This grass is cyan, a cyan snake grass. Apart from this, there is also white snake grass, gold snake grass, red snake grass, and so on.”

“It isn’t poisonous; however, its bite hurts dearly. Its strongest point is that it can take root. If it is thrown at someone, it can dig into the skin and flesh, and if not handled promptly, it can damage one’s heart, lungs, and other internal organs.”

Bai Ya turned pale when he heard this.

Then he saw that Xu Ma was emitting qi, it was obvious he had activated his battle qi.

The green snake grass was ordinary level, and when it was oppressed by a bronze life level’s might, it was subdued and no longer whined.

Xu Ma stuck it back into the flower pot, and it gradually turned back into the grass it once looked like.

But unlike before, the grass looked wilted and withered, it looked dehydrated and frozen, causing it to look quite pitiful to Bai Ya.

Xu Ma continued, “You are not a cultivator, and cannot rely on your life aura to intimidate it. But that doesn’t matter, you can use your blood to feed it.”

“You will need to mix your blood into the soil. The snake grass will absorb the nutrients from the bloody soil. In a day, it and your bloodline will be connected. With this level of deep control, when you are in danger, it will feel your pain and snake away at its own accord to help you strike back.”

“Also, with this level of control, even when in snake form, it won’t bite you and will regard you as a close friend.”

“If you can do that, during battle, you can toss it out.”

“It can threaten bronze levels!”

Xu Ma’s frank words caused Bai Ya to understand gradually.

“Senior Xu Ma, many thanks.” Bai Ya was moved upon realizing how sincerely Xu Ma had thought about him.

Xu Ma laughed loudly, “You were lucky to run into me!”

He was passively slacking and wasn’t willing to risk investigating the mysterious black gown magician.

Cang Xu hadn’t explained to him that the magician was a suspected necromancer.

But Xu Ma had to provide some information to report back about the situation.

Therefore, he strolled through the marketplace and constantly made discreet inquiries.

Information on the Dark Rainbow store had been brought back by him. Tripleblade and Lan Zao benefited as a result.

As a result, he could be considered the person more familiar with the old island’s marketplace in all of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“Someone died!”

“A dead person!”

“Where is your store manager? Come out for me! Your healing option killed my man!”

“Get out here, you must give us an explanation!!”

Noisy insults circulated around.

Bai Ya and Xu Ma were attracted by them and rushed over.

“That’s Miss Zi Di’s store!” Bai Ya’s heart thudded when he saw the store’s doorway was surrounded by people.

A group of people was causing trouble; their leader was a bronze level cultivator who was angrily shouting.

Along with Bai Ya and Xu Ma, other bystanders had come over to see what was going on.

Yan Yi came out and attempted to appeal to them, “It’s a misunderstanding, it must be a misunderstanding!”

“Consumption of our potions didn’t cause their deaths.”

Yan Yi constantly defended himself.

But the troublemakers didn’t relent and instead grew haughtier and more aggressive.

“Your potions must have an issue!”

“You must compensate us!”

“Yea, in order to sell at such a cheap price, you must have done a sloppy job?”

“This isn’t the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance; their potions are safe.”

“To consume this small store’s potions is simply playing with your life!”

Not only were the troublemakers suspicious, but the bystanders were also beginning to grow skeptical too.

When Bai Ya heard this, he secretly grew anxious.

He knew the mercenary corps only had money because of those two potions. Bai Ya sensed the strong odor of conspiracy when he saw what was happening.

“There must be a villain that wants to cut off our livelihood!” Bai Ya shouted to himself, “I have to think of something I can do.”

While he was restless with anxiety, Xu Ma was a crossed arm spectator, he was a lively viewer of this.

He didn’t know the Wisteria store and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were connected.

Yan Yi humbled himself and wanted to discuss things with the troublemakers alone, however, the troublemakers didn’t agree, instead they cried out, “Do you want to bribe me? No way! You must pay what we deserve, everyone is watching.”

Yan Yi’s head filled with sweat.

“I must do something!” Bai Ya was anxious too.

He was outside the encirclement, and at that moment, he noticed some onlookers leaving the crowd and exchanging glances with each other frequently.

Bai Ya clearly remembered that those same bystanders had been the ones who had been belittling and doubting the potions.

“They are all shills!”

“Truly despicable!”

“Someone wants to make trouble for us.”

When Bai Ya discovered this, he was unable to hold back.

He whispered to Xu Ma, “Those people are all working together!”

Xu Ma shrugged his shoulders, “It appears so. This group of people is particularly attentive; they even change the faces of the bystanders frequently.”

The longer one stayed in the crowd and shouted negative things, the bigger one’s flaws would be.

“We have to stop them, expose this conspiracy!” Bai Ya pinched his hands into fists.

“Why?” Xu Ma was flabbergasted, “You want to get benefits from the Wisteria Merchant Alliance? But if you do that, you will offend the troublemakers.”

“Don’t do that, we don’t need to meddle with this maelstrom. Alas, hold on!”

Bai Ya had already left the crowd and squeezed into an alley.

The spectators had been entering and exiting through that very alley.

There was no one in the alley.

As Bai Ya walked into it, Xu Ma grabbed him.

“Hey, justice boy, don’t be a meddler…” Suddenly, Xu Ma flexed his strength and yanked Bai Ya into a shadowy corner.

A group of people walked through the alley, and after passing where they were hiding, stood at the mouth of the alley.

A portion of them infiltrated the crowd and played the role of shill.

The remaining three people stood in place and gazed from afar.

“Leader, we haven’t drawn out the target person.”

The one called “leader” was a man with a lean stature.

He gazed for a moment, then commanded, “Continue creating a disturbance, then withdraw when the target person appears. I have a noble guest here.”

After giving his instructions, he turned around and left through the alley.

Not long after, the other left too.

The alley was once again devoid of people.

“Senior Xu Ma, I…” In the dark corner, Bai Ya opened his mouth and racked his brains on how to convince Xu Ma to get involved with this affair.

He heard Xu Ma fume with rage, “Investigate, I must investigate!”

“Finally, I found you. The guy who took advantage of me!”

Xu Ma’s eyes sprayed the flames of hatred.

It was because the male leader had been the thin person who had stabbed him.

“You are an ordinary person, stay nearby and be my lookout. I’ll be back!” Xu Ma told Bai Ya before running down the alley.

Chapter 312: Xu Ma Attacks

Xu Ma left Bai Ya elsewhere and had him keep watch while he rushed into the depths of the alley.

A strong wind assaulted his face, causing Xu Ma to narrow his eyes, his slits flickering with cold light.

Previously, he had been stabbed and forced to part with information on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Fortunately, he had just joined and didn’t know anything tantalizing about them.

The thin person who stabbed him knew this too, and didn’t kill Xu Ma, opting to release him instead.

But Xu Ma wasn’t happy, because he knew the other party had let him go to squeeze the last bit of value out of him. They had used him to give the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps a show of strength. It told the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps that it wasn’t easy to mingle into Two Eyes Island!

Xu Ma, with a dagger stuck inside him, had staggered back to the ship and immediately reported what had happened.

The news was unavoidably spread, and as Xu Ma recuperated, many mercenaries jeered at him.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was newly formed, and everyone was fresh faces. If they hadn’t been recruited on Snake Rat Island, they would have been captives from the Snake Rat naval battle.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ higher ups were suppressed by the dragonman captain and the half beastman.

However, the lower ranks didn’t have a clear leader, and so was in a chaotic state.

This world worshiped the strong.

Without a clear strongman, the bronze and iron level mercenaries were secretly competing with each other to decide who ranked where and who was more or less important from now on.

Why should I listen to you?

Why should you get those resources first before me?

Prove your strength!

These secret competitions were not started by the survivors, but rather an automatic refinement of a new organization.

As a bronze level, Lan Zao was valued, causing many other bronze levels to be frustrated and challenge him.

Xu Ma was a special case; he was injured.

The injury was his protective shell, yet at the same time, his weakness.

Xu Ma had been growing more and more worried these days.

After being stabbed and forced to sell information on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he had been put at a clear disadvantage in this secret competition.

That disadvantaged state was far worse than the one Lan Zao was in.

How did bronze level cultivators look at Xu Ma?

Someone unimportant, they would ridicule Xu Ma for being weak and incompetent. Even if they were to swap positions, they would perform worse.

They wanted to force Xu Ma underneath them and make the latter uncompetitive.

When this chaotic period ended, Xu Ma’s decidedly evaluated position would be a great vexation.

Not only would he be disparaged and teased, more importantly, it was how much benefits was allocated to him.

No one would give more to the weak, it is always the strong that benefits!

Xu Ma valued his face.

And this face also concerned his future earnings in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Xu Ma valued those earnings, because he viewed the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as having a good future.

A cultivator, even as the lowest bronze, was often welcomed into mercenary corps, pirate groups, and other such organizations.

However, not all powers or organizations resembled the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. They had two silver leaders that could vie with gold level cultivators!

Especially since the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was new, and Xu Ma had joined not too long ago either. He wanted to grab hold of this good opportunity, become a direct subordinate, and in the future, rely on the higher-ups’ approval to gain profits beyond his own strength.

Not only had Xu Ma gotten stabbed and was forced to sell information on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, but he was also severely punished.

However, the exact way and level the punishment was carried out had caused Xu Ma to regard the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps as having more prospects.

Because regardless of whether it was treatment or punishment, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ higher-ups had done both according to the group’s internal regulation system. Both existing laws and sufficient consideration were applied to Xu Ma’s difficult situation.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had given Xu Ma an order to investigate a mysterious magician.

Xu Ma passively slacked off on that task.

He joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to get a better life and more resources, not slave away.

This time, Xu Ma was doing his utmost to pursue and attack.

Actually, it was for the same reason.

He wanted a better life and resources, then he had to turn a new leaf and counterattack, forcing others to sit up and take notice of him.

What target was more suitable than the thin person who had stabbed him?

Climb back up where you fell.

Most crucially, although the thin person was a cultivator, he was the same as Xu Ma, a mere bronze level.

Battle skill – Slink.

Xu Ma sped up by 30% and his figure hugged closer into the darkness of the alley, masking him.

Most crucially, his life aura dwindled and was close to nonexistent.

Slink was a popular battle skill, but if Lan Zao used it, it wouldn’t be as good as Xu Ma’s.

Because Lan Zao cultivated the Water Grass battle qi while Xu Ma cultivated the Spider Walk battle qi. His Spider Walk battle qi was more compatible with the Slink battle skill.

“Amazing!” Bai Ya exclaimed in admiration from behind. In his view, Xu Ma’s figure suddenly became fuzzy as it sped up efficiently, vanishing after turning an alley corner.

The thin person was already missing after turning the corner.

Xu Ma had anticipated this and used another battle skill – Tracking.

Xu Ma gazed at the ground and found a series of jumbled and disorderly footprints.

Among these footprints, one group stood out the most.

It was the footprints the thin person had left behind.

Because they were from the person with the strongest life level that had passed through here recently.

Xu Ma duel used the Slink and Tracking battle skills to investigate and determine the thin person’s precise route along with reducing the distance between them.

After passing through two alleys and turning five or six times, Xu Ma slowed down as he arrived in front of a building.

“This is the place.” He was careful and didn’t advance prematurely.

Battle skill – Walk Climbing.

He stretched out his limbs and stepped onto the wall.

The battle skill allowed him to walk as if he was on level ground.

To not reveal himself, Xu Ma pressed his chest against the wall, with both hands and feet crawling up like a spider.

The Tracking battle skill had ceased already and couldn’t be used.

This was Xu Ma’s limit.

He could at most use two skills at a time, previously it was Slink and Tracking, now he had changed Tracking out for Walk Climbing.

Relying on his battle skills and experience, he cleverly avoided two sentries out in the open and passed through a gap in the line of sight of a hidden sentry post.

After circling about, he came to the back side of the building, then pressed his ear onto the wall.

Battle skill – Eavesdrop.

Very quickly, the rustling conversation transmitted into his ear.

Xu Ma frowned and changed his position until he found the correct room.

This time when he eavesdropped, the voices were much clearer.

“Lord Xi Dai Zi, I didn’t just mishear you?” The thin person’s stunned voice could be heard, “You’re making us your pirate group’s insiders that will help you attack Two Eyes Island? This is impossible, I am a human, I cannot betray the interests of humanity!”

A finely sharp voice rang out, “Hee hee, betraying the interest of humanity is something you’ve done before. Don’t play tricks with me. Just state your price.”

The thin person grumbled, “This is too great of a responsibility. Not only was this different from what we discussed previously, we only planned to sell information on Two Eyes Island’s defenses.”

“Hee hee.” Said Xi Dai Zi, “We want both information on defenses and insider knowledge!”

The thin person replied, “This…I can’t decide that.”

“Then let me speak to whoever can.” Xi Dai Zi expressed obvious displeasure, “How long will it take?”

“Now, at once.” Said the thin person.

Xi Dai Zi then asked, “Oh, that’s interesting, is that a gold level communications device?”

Xu Ma listened even more attentively and heard the clunk of an alchemy device being moved.

Soon after, a third voice was heard, “What?”

The thin person replied, “Lord Shadow, the situation has changed. The other party wants us to be insiders; they don’t just want to purchase defense charts.”

Xi Dai Zi followed up, “Our sincerity is sufficient, and we are always resolute. We have cooperated many times before, and don’t speak of betraying the interests of humanity. I haven’t struggled my way here to listen to such a corny joke.”

The third voice pondered for a bit, then laughed, “Your group wants to seize control of Two Eyes Island, hehe, interesting. I accept. My force can serve as your insiders, but we will need 100,000 gold! No matter the outcome of this, you will need to pay it all first, no down payments accepted!”

Xi Dai Zi screeched, “Bah! That is unacceptable, at most this is worth 80,000 gold, furthermore, I can only provide a downpayment of 40,000 gold. When we seize control of Two Eyes Island, we shall deliver you the other part.”

What proceeded was a spell of bargaining.

Finally, both sides agreed on a price of 94,000 gold coins. Xi Dai Zi would first pay a down payment of 50,000 gold coins.

Xu Ma didn’t dare move from his corner.

He had already canceled the Slink battle skill and was currently using Eavesdrop and Concealment.

The Concealment battle skill greatly diminished his life aura, even more so than Slink.

“This is a big play!” Xu Ma’s head filled with cold sweat.

He was a thief and attentively gathered information.

Many times, it was hard to imagine how valuable the information was.

Therefore, he knew more than an ordinary person, such as the person in this private discussion, Xi Dai Zi.

Xu Ma knew that Xi Dai Zi was a fishman shaman, a silver level, and the first mate of the Vinegar Jar Pirates.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates were a pirate group made up of fishmen, their commander, Er Gua Zi, was a gold level cultivator. Unlike Rou Zang’s pirate group, the Vinegar Jar Pirates roamed around and weren’t fixated in some sea area, they treated all waters as their domain.

Therefore, the Vinegar Jar Pirates weren’t overestimating themselves, rather it was very possible that they could take Two Eyes Island.

The actual situation was far more complex.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates had unexpectedly found a human insider!

“There was someone backing that dimmable thin person!”

“For what reason did this group of people dare become traitors?”

“Thery are bound to be a powerful force; otherwise, why would the Vinger Jar Pirates seek collaboration with them?”

“Damm it!”

“My situation is very dangerous; I cannot let them find me. If they notice me, I will meet a terrible end here…”

Chapter 313: The Mysterious Magician Appears Again

Xu Ma shrank into the corner, motionless as the dead.

Everything had greatly surpassed his expectations.

In his mind, he only had to deal with the thin bronze level person, the others were ordinary people. An ambush had a high chance of success.

But he no longer thought of doing that, now that he had run across Xi Dai Zi.

He was a fishman shaman, a silver level caster!

Xu Ma was dispatched to investigate an iron level masked magician; he had been skimping on that task greatly because he was worried that he would be killed for doing that. Now, he had encountered a far more formidable caster.

“What terrible luck, I have the worst luck!”

“Can’t let them find me, I mustn’t let them find me.”

“Great Shadow God, Thief God, bless and protect me from detection…”

Xu Ma was fully aware of how much danger he was in, and like walking across a tightrope between precipices, perhaps he would die at any moment.

He used his battle qi to diminish his aura as much as possible.

At the same time, he began to ponder how he was going to retreat.

But fate didn’t give him time.

As Xu Ma decided his retreat route and was about to move, the conversation between the two had concluded.

Xi Dai Zi got up, “It’s time, I’ll go first.”

The alchemy device transmitted the backer’s voice, “Xi Dai Zi, why are you worried? Leave tonight, let me entertain you.”

Xi Dai Zi sneered, “I’d rather entertain you after Two Eyes Island is under my group’s control.”

The backer was unconcerned by that and continued on, “Seizing Two Eyes Island is a big matter, how can this short conversation clear everything up about it? We’ll have a proper discussion, trust is the foundation, no?”

“Trust…we really do need more trust.” Xi Dai Zi muttered, “The best way your group can earn our trust is by doing your best to destroy Two Eyes Island’s defenses. The easier we can seize it, the more we will trust you.”

“Rather…” When Xi Dai Zi said that, he sneered, “There are two hidden stakeouts present at our meeting. They are two mere bronze levels. I wanted to kill my way out of here; Hun Tong couldn’t stop me. That wasn’t necessary. Are you looking down on me?”

The backer burst into laughter to dull the awkwardness.

But the thin person had a smile on his face.

During the conversation, Xi Dai Zi and the noble backer had reduced him to a tool.

But as he smiled, his eyes contracted to the size of needles.

The higher up backer was still laughing, but Xi Dai Zi, inside the room, was looking at the thin person’s changing face. That caused him to immediately purse his brows and felt a tinge of wrongness.

Xi Dai Zi glared at the thin person and asked, “What’s wrong?”

The thin person stammered, “Lord Xi Dai Zi, you. You are sure there are two bronze stakeouts? I only, I only had one set up.”

Xi Dai Zi was startled, then a moment later, his eyes flashed and without thinking, he smashed the wall next to him with his fist!

Ding!

The wall crumbled before his fist.

Xu Ma dodged in a panicked flurry.

The moment the two people’s conversation reached that point, Xu Ma realized things were bad and had accelerated his withdrawal ahead of schedule.

If he hesitated any more, he would have been hit by Xi Dai Zi’s fist that if it didn’t kill him, would have left him crippled!

“Run! Run! Run!” Xu Ma’s brain was filled by that one thought.

He rolled to discharge the shockwave of the blow and desperately ran away.

“Stop him!” The thin person glimpsed the back of Xu Ma, loudly screeching upon instantly realizing he wasn’t one of them.

When the bronze stakeout that was lying in ambush and the gate guards heard that, they attacked Xu Ma.

Xu Ma dodged left and right, and at that moment, all of his strength burst out.

Although his potential was nearly depleted, his long legs and feet combined well with the Spider Walk battle qi technique and various battle skills. For a moment, he dodged across walls, frantically ran away, and actually wriggled his way out of the encirclement.

The thin person was both anxious and furious.

“Useless people!” Xi Dai Zi coldly snorted as he acted again.

He took off his belt, and in a flash, turned it into a whip.

The whip had an unusual shape, it was thin, long, had a sleek appearance, and looked like a lengthened snake tail.

Crack.

Xi Dai Zi flung the long whip, and its tip snapped into the air.

Right away, a bolt of lightning burst out and shot at Xu Ma’s head. Before colliding with him, it turned into a net woven from small electric currents.

The large net was very wide, and Xu Ma had no time to evade it as it covered him.

The electric current ran through his body, throwing his battle qi into chaos, causing him to tremble all over, and then fall to the ground. Due to his inertia from running, he rolled across the ground/

Xu Ma stopped at the courtyard’s large gate.

“Kill him, leave no witnesses!” The thin person screeched again.

Soon after, guards rushed over, lifted their weapons, and aimed them at Xu Ma’s forehead, neck, heart, spleen, anus, and other places that would be harmed by stabs.

Xu Ma was fully paralyzed, including his tongue, and his mind was even more dizzy.

But as death approached, he was greatly stimulated!

His body contained the Dark Walking Spider bloodline, and although his concentration wasn’t high, he still had a fantastic ability. Whenever death approached, he would feel a chill.

And now, Xu Ma felt that chill, but not just one, but rather five or six.

It chilled especially violently in his buttocks.

“I’m going to die!”

Xu Ma’s heart thudded, and before death came, his eyes opened wide as he did his utmost to struggle free, however, this only resulted in his body shuddering more.

His muscles were still being shocked, and they couldn’t hear the commands of his brain.

Xu Ma’s eyes closed, showing his heavy hopelessness.

He lay on the ground and looked up at the courtyard gate. It was obviously only a few steps away, nevertheless it was the chasm between life and death.

Bang!

A fire ball suddenly flew out and crashed next to Xu Ma. The shockwave of the rolling flames launched both Xu Ma and the others into the air.

The unlucky people that were the closest screamed as they died.

Xu Ma was also injured, for after being thrown into the air, he crashed into the ground hard.

The pain caused him to grimace in pain, but his heart was nevertheless ecstatic, “I’m still alive, I’m actually still alive!”

Cough cough.

He spat out some blood. His body was lacerated and heavily burned; there was still flames burning on his left leg.

He turned over into a kneel, then extinguished the flames with a pat.

Acute pain in his back began to emerge and was growing more intense.

Xu Ma tenaciously grinded his teeth and glanced around him, discovering that the guards pursuing him were in a more miserable state than he, and for the moment, weren’t bothering him.

Xu Ma shifted his gaze beyond the courtyard.

From where the fireball had come from, stood a single person.

The person was dressed in all black and wore a mask that seemed to be made of black iron. The flickering flames reflected against the glossy metal.

Xu Ma blankly stared at them, and thought to himself, “Don’t tell me that’s the mysterious magician I was supposed to investigate?”

“Who came to the rescue?” Xi Dai Zi was rigidly fixed on the black gowned magician, then looked at the thin person.

The thin person wildly shook his head, “I don’t recognize him, hold on, don’t be mistaken. We are also victims here!”

The tin person gave an order to the surviving guards, “What are you doing, kill them both for me.”

But the guards groaned.

They had been too crowded together and were more severely injured than Xu Ma was.

Because Xu Ma had been lying down while they were standing up.

Despite hearing the order, none of the guards budged.

Xu Ma let out a breath and retreated back a few steps without a trace left behind.

The mysterious magician attracted the majority of attention, allowing Xu Ma to easily put distance between them all.

But he was also very perceptive and didn’t leave the scene.

If he did this, he was bound to provoke the others greatly and be the center of attention once more.

“Fight, stand and fight now.” Xu Ma shouted to himself. Only by fighting and muddying the situation could he fish himself out of troubled waters and have hope of fleeing this place alive.

“What kind of person are you?” Xi Dai Zi growled out a question.

The mysterious black gowned magician coldly snorted and had no intention of replying. He bent down his arm and took out a scroll from their spacious cuff.

As the scroll fell, the skilled magician pinched it and utilized gravity to unroll it.

A moment later, the magician’s spiritual power activated the scroll.

The scroll’s engraved magic script suddenly flashed, and soon after, the scroll’s pentagram formation leapt out and appeared in front of the magician.

Within a second, the formation and magic script lit up with eye dazzling light.

Magic power converged extremely quickly, like a gust of wind.

A fist sized fireball coagulated into being, and with a shout, launched towards Xi Dai Zi.

Xi Dai Zi was on guard, and when he saw the magic scroll being unrolled, he reached for his belt.

His belt was no ordinary thing; it was a silver level magic item.

The instant he took off his belt, it transformed back into the long whip.

The long whip cracked through the air, stretching 5 meters and accurately hitting the fireball.

The fireball had enormous explosive might, but after being struck by the whip, its magical might was instantly cut in half.

Although it still exploded, it didn’t achieve the ideal result.

“An insignificant iron magician…” Xi Dai Zi was resentfully offended.

He was a majestic silver level!

“What made you think I was so weak?” Xi Dai Zi turned his wrist, and like a snake, the long whip lashed out through the air, flying quickly towards the black gowned magician.

The black gown magician stood in place and did not move nor dodge.

He suddenly took out three scrolls from his spacious cuff.

The scrolls all opened at the same time, all of them Fireball spell scrolls.

Three formations condensed, and from magic script, three fireballs fired at Xi Dai Zi.

Xi Dai Zi’s face changed; he was both startled and furious.

The other party didn’t protect themselves, rather they attacked. It was extremely likely they had defensive equipment they were confident in.

Not only that, but the other party also wasn’t using their own spells, instead they were using magic scrolls.

Thus, they could cast far quicker than he.

Most of all, the other party was using offense in place of defense, prickling Xi Dai Zi!

If this was a naval battle, and Xi Dai Zi had pirate accomplices, he would have adopted an attacking formation. But he was on humanity’s Two Eyes Island, and if his identity was exposed, the consequence would be difficult to anticipate.

Chapter 314: Water Path vs Necromancy

Xi Dai Zi didn’t dare take risks and was forced to withdraw his long whip.

Controlled by his spirit power, the long while circled around Xi Dai Zi like a spiraling pillar to obstruct the three fireballs.

Bang, boom, bang.

The three fireballs struck the circling whip, and all exploded at nearly the same time.

For a moment, flames radiated all around, causing smoke and dust to billow up.

The thin person and the others dodged in a panic.

“Run away!”

Xu Ma had been looking for this chance and immediately turned to jump over a wall and run.

The thin person and the others had their hands full already, and as the building was teetering from more fireballs, Xi Dai Zi’s full attention was on the mysterious magician who had thrown them, no one cared about a nobody like Xu Ma.

Smoke and fire scattered to reveal Xi Dai Zi still standing there tall.

He had a layer of water covering his body. The light blue water protection reflected the light and was clear and crystalline. Its spherical shape was dented by the explosions.

Xi Dai Zi’s glamour was also damaged; his human appearance was flickering between that of a human and his true fishman features.

The black gowned magician responded with another quick and fierce fireball spell.

Xi Dai Zi had either used disguise magic or an alchemy device, either of which was now broken.

It was unlike the Deceptive Disguise spell the survivors used. That magic used Pearl Bubbles, a gold level casting material, and was the world’s greatest disguise magic. Even if one died, as long as the magic was still in effect, they would still be disguised.

Xi Dai Zi rigidly stared at the black gowned magician and began to chant.

For a moment, water in the water ball defense surged forth as Xi Dai Zi’s life aura constantly rose.

It was clear a powerful move was being prepared.

But at that moment, the black gowned magician used three more scrolls.

Bang, boom, bang!

An additional three Fireball spells.

The thin person and the others trembled: where in the world did this ferocious person come from? They were smashing them with spell scrolls!

Already, they had used 7 scrolls.

Although they were just iron level, they had already spent more than 10 gold. This amount of money was sufficient to buy a silver level weapon.

Blood leaked from the corner of Xi Dai Zi’s mouth.

During his casting process, a fireball explosion had interfered and almost caused his cast to fail.

The black gown magician suddenly retreated!

Xi Dai Zi howled internally, “Don’t you run away!”

It wasn’t that he couldn’t cast while moving, it was just that he couldn’t right now.

Because he still needed to maintain his water defense.

This caused him to look on helplessly as the black gown magician disappeared from his field of view.

“You guys aren’t pursuing?” Xi Dai Zi bellowed in his heart as he glared at the thin person and the others.

He was unable to voice that thought.

Because he still needed to chant.

The thin person and the others were very hesitant.

The strongest among them was only a bronze level, how could they stop the indiscriminate barrage of fireballs?

Magic spell – Three Headed Water Snake!

Xi Dai Zi’s cast finally finished, and a large stream of water rushed from his water sphere defense and turned into a three headed water snake that impatiently pounced onto the exit gate.

“Give chase!” Xi Dai Zi ordered the thin person and the others, then stepped onto the three headed water snake’s tail, and was the first to give chase.

At this time, the black gown magician wasn’t that far away.

Xi Dai Zi madly followed the trail in pursuit.

The three headed water snake was huge, and like a mammoth sized elephant, toppled all buildings and vendor booths that stood in its path, causing the streets to be filled with cries of fear as everyone fled in all directions.

“Where are you thinking of escaping to?” Xi Dai Zi sneered; he had finally blocked the black gowned magician in a dead end.

The black gown magician unexpectedly pulled out 6 scrolls.

Bang, boom…

Six fireballs volleyed at Xi Dai Zi.

But this time, Xi Dia Zi was in a far better situation.

He didn’t use his water ball defense, instead he moved into the three headed water snake.

Unlike humans, he was a fishman who could breathe in water.

The fireballs all detonated upon impacting the three headed water snake.

With a deafening explosion, large portions of water turned to steam and caused both water and fire to splatter and splash all over.

The impact waves formed also collided with each other in the alley.

Xi Dao Zo lost eyes on the black gowned magician; however, he was still grinning with killing intent.

“Today, I will kill you!” Xi Dai Zi’s spirit had already locked onto the black gowned magician.

He could instantly detect whenever the black gown magician acted.

However, a moment later, something strange occurred.

An immense ghost passed through a wall and ambushed Xi Dai Zi from behind.

Xi Dai Zi sensed the ghost’s aura, but it was already too late to protect himself.

The ghost had obviously been lying in wait and had attacked suddenly and violently.

It stretched out its hand and plunged it into the water snake.

Although its speed had been reduced, it still easily stuck its hand into Xi Dai Zi.

The translucent ghost hand penetrated Xi Dai Zi’s chest, grabbed it, then pulled its hand back.

Xi Dai Zi’s neck had a pendant around it.

At that moment, it exploded and emitted a divine light hued deep blue.

The divine light protected Xi Dai Zi and thrusted the ghost ruthlessly back.

The ghost withdrew.

Xi Dai Zi staggered to a kneel.

He unconsciously turned around, and the only thing he could see in the steam was the waning silhouette of the enormous ghost.

For a moment, Xi Dai Zi’s heart filled with fear.

“I fell into a trap!”

“I couldn’t see my mistake; that was a ghost, a silver level ghost!”

“The other person is actually a necromancer!!”

“No wonder they always used magic scrolls…”

“It’s only thanks to my sacred emblem that I remain alive. That ghost must also be heavily injured too.”

“Firstly, I must retreat first!”

Xi Dai Zi’s fighting spirit dropped completely.

His soul was wounded, his spirit was disorganized, his brain was in great pain, and he had already lost his lock on the black gowned magician.

In fact, even if his spirit was still locked on, Xi Dai Zi didn’t want to continue the pursuit.

Although the other person was an iron level, they were nevertheless a necromancer.

This magic school had just sprung up, and Xi Dai Zi lacked the skills to respond to it.

He had been taking refuge in the three headed water snake, whose defensive power far surpassed the water ball defense, however, the ghost ambush had slowed it, and it wasn’t able to defend him any longer.

Xi Dai Zi controlled the water snake, and he retreated.

“Ah…” As he retreated, he heard a few miserable shrieks.

The thin person and his people had suffered a vicious attack.

Xi Dai Zi once again sensed the fluctuation of necromancy and his heart couldn’t help but sink, “The other party still has energy to spare!”

At that moment, a fighter shouted, “The city guard is arresting thieves, all outsiders retreat!”

“They came so quickly?” Xi Dai Zi was actually faintly happy.

He took out a fish scale and used magic to cloud the scene.

Then, he revoked the water snake and used the fog to quickly evacuate.

When the fog dissipated, the city guard commander and their men were standing in front of the ruins with ugly looks.

The commander waved their hand, and their subordinates charged into the ruins and searched them.

There were no corpses.

Only the traces of a magical struggle.

“Fire path…this should be from a Fireball spell explosion.”

“Water path traces, it looks like a water snake, at least a two headed one. The water fog from before must have been casted by a silver level water path caster.”

“Hold on, this is necromantic aura?!”

When the scouts found that, one by one, their faces changed.

“Could it be that a necromancer participated in this fight?” The city guard commander’s look grew even more uglier.

A necromancer appearing and another faction’s magician appearing were two completely different things!

The city guard commander didn’t dare tarry and hastily dispatched a messenger to report to Feudal Lord Hun Tong.

Bai Ya was looking around a street corner.

Xu Ma quietly jumped over a wall.

He clapped Bai Ya’s shoulder.

Bai Ya jumped in fear, but when he saw Xu Ma, he shouted in pleasant surprise, “Senior, you’re back!”

“When I saw you had been gone a while, I called the city guard over.”

“Of course, I employed a beggar and had him notify them on my behalf.”

“Senior, you’re wounded?!” When Bai Ya examined him, he noticed that Xu Ma’s injuries weren’t light.

“Great job!” Xu Ma praised, then grunted through the pain, “But we need to leave now. Quickly, the city guard is likely to set up blockades nearby, we can’t stay here for long.”

Xu Ma was still in shock by the time they returned to the ships at port.

He really had walked the edge of death.

After recalling the pursuit, Xu Ma was filled with bewilderment.

Especially on one question.

“Why did that black gowned magician suddenly burst in?”

“What conflict did he have with the thin person and Xi Dai Zi?”

Xu Ma thought it through and felt that wasn’t right.

Whether it was the thin person, or Xi Dai Zi, both were unfamiliar with the mysterious black gowned magician.

An idea suddenly flashed through Xu Ma’s mind, “Don’t tell me the black gowned magician was following me?”

“Did he sense that I was investigating him?”

Although Xu Ma had made up his mind to skimp on this mission, on the surface, it seemed he had labored to get into position, and as a result, had a string of investigative inquiries into this.

At least, he had investigated the black gowned magician who had appeared in the store.

“If that is the case, wouldn’t I have the black gowned magician’s eyes on me?”

“It is precisely because I was being watched by him that he was attracted over.”

“Then why did he instead save my life?”

“No, all of this is merely guesswork, there is no evidence of anything.”

The more Xu Ma thought, the more chaotic his emotions became and the more his mind filled with cold sweat.

“No matter what, we can’t stay on Two Eyes Island any longer.”

“From now on, I will stay on the ship and won’t get off even if I die!”

Xu Ma made up his mind.

“That’s right.”

“My wounds are so severe; I must recuperate for a while.”

“This information should be enough to report this mission completed!”

The city palace.

The city guard commander, head covered in cold sweat, knelt towards Feudal Lord Hun Tong and reported the conclusion of the investigation,

“A necromancer?!” Hun Tong’s eyes widened, and he pressed his palms into his desk, the startling news almost caused him to stand up.

“Arte you certain?” Hun Tong asked.

The city guard commander took a deep breath, summoning the courage to reply, “It has been confirmed. The scene has a strong necromantic aura in it.”

Hun Tong sat back down and leaned back on the chair, revealing a powerless look, “A strong necromantic aura, it might not necessarily be a necromancer, perhaps it is an undead life form.”

The city guard commander shook his head, “But Feudal Lord, you also know that area. How could an undead life form suddenly appear from nowhere?”

“According to imperial law, if a necromancer is discovered, we must report it immediately.”
