“Report it?!” Hun Tong glowered at the city guard commander, the pitch of his voice rising, “And let those madmen from the Blood Light Punishment Institution in? What’s going to happen to my auction? My Two Eyes Island will be overturned to the heavens by them, who would dare trade here from then on?!”

The city guard silently bowed his head.

Hun Tong gasped a few times, then asked, “Who knows about this too?”

The city guard commander whispered out, “All of those that followed me in my investigation know.”

Hun Tong couldn’t help but clench his fist.

“Get lost!”

“Get out of my sight!!”

He threw an ink bottle at the city guard commander’s head.

The ink bottle smashed into the city guard commander’s face, dying him with black ink.

The city guard commander seemed to have been excused, and he hastily departed the study to get as far away as he could from his raging feudal lord.

Hun Tong was the only one in the study.

He let out a long sigh and took out an alchemy device from his drawer.

This was a communication device.

Hun Tong activated and said, “Report, what is going on?”

The alchemy device glimmered brightly, and slightly vibrated as a voice was transmitted, “You know, I wasn’t there. I don’t know much. I had finished negotiating with Xi Dai Zi and he was just about to leave. The final thing of the discussion was when he declared that there were two stakeouts.”

“One of the stakeouts wasn’t one of us. After noticing this, Xi Dai Zi and my men immediately pursued them.”

“Soon after, I heard an explosion.”

“Someone came to the rescue of the other person.”

“The one who came should be a necromancer. According to what we can infer from the marks at the scene, in the beginning of the fight, the necromancer used some magic scrolls. He and Xi Dai Zi confronted each other, then he feigned retreat.”

“Afterwards, in the second battlefield when they confronted each other again, Xi Dai Zi was ambushed, and it is unknown whether he is alive or dead.”

“All of my people are missing, everything about this bodes ill.”

The person reporting to Hun Tong was shockingly the thin person’s superior!

Chapter 315: Imperial Secret Spy

Who was the person, the high level backer who was negotiating with Xi Dai Zi and cooperated with the Vinegar Jar Pirates on many occasions?

Regardless of whether it was Xi Dai Zi or Hun Tong, the voice from the alchemy device had been altered and couldn’t be determined to be either male or female.

Hun Tong rubbed his brows, and sighed again, “It seems a third power is meddling. Our plan has been greatly disturbed.”

“The current trouble is quite big. We must find this power as soon as possible; it’s up to you to do this.”

The voice from the alchemy device retorted, “You are the feudal lord here.”

“But you are a member of the imperial secret spies.” Hun Ton replied back, “This is your specialty, no?”

The alchemy device was silent.

Why was an imperial secret spy repeatedly collaborating with a fishman pirate group, and why were they discussing how to seize control of Two Eyes Island?

Let’s bring it back to the beginning.

The Sheng Ming Empire attacking beastmen tribes in the Wilderness Continent was the present age’s main event.

So far, the imperial vanguard army that had landed on the Wilderness Continent had caught the beastmen off guard.

A portion of the Wilderness Continent’s coastline had been seized, and the local beastmen tribes were not expelled, but annihilated.

The humans wanted to gain a foothold, but was being raided by small groups of beastmen, a bothersome vexation.

Due to humanity being universally inferior to beastmen on the battlefield, the imperial higher ups decided to construct forts on a wide scale.

The empire’s bridgehead grew larger, and their situation grew better.

The Pirate Throne’s appearance was a major variable and cast a true shadow over the future of the empire’s storming of the Wilderness Continent.

At present, the empire hadn’t yet thrown in its main armies.

The beastmen also knew this and were currently organizing an alliance and preparing to breach these bridgeheads to either crush or expel the human vanguard.

A war was about to erupt and supporting roles such as slave traders were withdrawing and leaving the stage for the true protagonist.

The brewing war attracted the close attention of every large power in the world.

Because this war between the empire and the beastmen was the first real showdown between the two giants.

However, this battle did not only take place out in the open.

There were also many secret battles and strategies.

The Sheng Ming Empire’s Secret Spy Agency assumed this heavy responsibility.

The Imperial Secret Spy Agency not only collected information, but they also instigated things, infiltrated places, conducted guerrilla warfare, and other such destructive missions.

Their goal was to use every trick to clear away any obstructions and prepare well for the main imperial army’s future advance.

Although Two Eyes Island was not on the Wilderness Continent, its position was still quite crucial.

When the imperial army departed in the future, this place would become a transportation line.

Two years earlier, the Imperial Secret Spy Agency began to place agents here, their main mission was to purge the surrounding threats and guarantee that this transportation line was safe and unimpeded.

This was undoubtedly against Hun Tong’s operational policy.

The Duo Mu Clan’s family motto was turn a blind eye. Hun Tong was a direct descendant, and he had purposely cultivated the situation to make Two Eyes Island the most bustling trading island in its waters, even becoming the biggest place for pirates to dispose of their booty.

Due to Hun Tong’s control being lax, Two Eyes Island’s black market was very well known.

The pirates that disposed of their booty here were also its most magnanimous customers.

Merchants liked this place, and of the six great merchant alliances, five were already set up here.

Streams of people converged here, along with distribution of goods.

When the secret spy agents contacted Hun Tong and requested his cooperation, Hun Tong was quite unwilling to do so.

But what could he do if he was unwilling?

Against the Sheng Ming Empire, what could a gold level like Hun Tong do?

Not to mention that even the entirety of his Duo Mu Clan was nothing but an ant to the empire.

Hun Tong had to cooperate, for there was no other option.

Afterwards, Hun Tong also understood that within the basis of cooperation, he still had room to maneuver and be able to lessen the damage as much as possible.

It had to be said that the imperial secret spies didn’t deploy too many people here.

The empire’s target this time was too big, it was an entire continent. It goes without saying, the empire’s secret spy’s scale of operations was massive.

That they did transfer some personnel to Two Eyes Island meant they put stock in this place.

In order to truly purge the hidden threats to Two Eyes Island and beautifully accomplish their mission, when the imperial secret spies arrived at Two Eyes Island and recognized the circumstances, they didn’t take direct action.

They believed that it was already known far and wide for years how Hun Tong ran Two Eyes Island. Such an environment had to be eliminated, and that was his job too.

But that wasn’t a priority.

By exploiting this environment, they could construct a trap. If nothing else, every year during the rising of the island, the black market, discount market, and auction would attract many pirates. By taking advantage of this moment, they could cleanly capture many pirates.

Anyways, compared to pirates, the still hidden power nearby was more deserving of the secret spies’ attention.

It was the fishmen.

The fishmen’s racial potential and gifts couldn’t compare to humanity.

But there were a lot of them and had a different life structure than humanity – fish gills.

They could breathe underwater and swim through the sea unimpeded.

No one knew how many fishmen lived on the seabed, this was a tremendous hidden power!

For the empire to storm the Wilderness Continent, they had to establish a transportation route.

If the fishmen attacked this transportation route, the empire was bound to be hard-pressed. The tempo on the open battlefield would also be severely encumbered.

The empire had already taken note of these fishmen.

Instead of enticing them, the empire was trending towards attacking and annihilating the fishmen power.

If it was possible, the empire considered roping them in.

But the empire’s human gods and the fishmen’s gods had crossed blades many times before, and many fishmen gods saw their end due to that. The conflict between both sides had long been irreconcilable.

In contrast to the humans, the fishmen and the beastmen had always maintained good relationships.

It goes without saying that when humanity built the transportation route, the fishmen were sure to make a move.

And so, near Two Eyes Island, there was a hidden fishman clan.

Furthermore, this fishman clan was always waiting for an opportunity to seize Two Eyes Island.

Actually, seize is not the right word, to be more precise, the correct word is “reclaim”.

Because the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan was Two Eyes Island’s former master.

In the past, Hun Tong had led an army to claim Two Eyes Island but wasn’t powerful enough to exterminate the Blue Green Coral Fishmen Clan in one stroke. The fishmen who escaped submerged to the seafloor to recover, and after regaining their strength these few years, had gradually become Two Eyes Island’s greatest danger.

After analyzing the situation, the imperial secret spies had decided to prioritize dealing with this fishman clan.

With regards to this decision, Hun Tong lifted his hands in approval!

He was also very happy to do so.

The Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan was originally his quarry.

Hun Tong tried to do more for this.

He knew the fishmen clan knew the secret spell that could control the coral ball cage tree.

In fact, Two Eyes Island had been cultivated by the fishman tribe for many generations.

Hun Tong really wanted this secret spell.

With this secret spell, he could remodel Two Eyes Island and construct a large harbor!

Hun Tong had a new development plan for Two Eyes Island: since Two Eyes Island had to become a transportation throughline, its surroundings had to be secure.

Without pirates visiting, Two Eyes Island could only conduct regulated business.

Hun Tong made up his mind, before Two Eyes Island was attacked, and while business was the most prosperous, he wanted to build a large harbor and create a distribution center.

If that comes to be, when the army was dispatched and this transportation hub went into operation, then he would make far more profits than he was doing now!

But to do that, he had to have a large harbor.

This was the necessary prerequisite and foundation.

Without that, Hun Tong’s plan was a fantasy.

The essence of Two Eyes Island was the coral ball cage tree. It was a living being, constantly growing and recovering.

Therefore, it was very challenging to build a large harbor.

Normal development methods would destroy the coral ball cage tree, causing it to die. If the construction method was too gentle, the harbor would be consumed by coral growth and restored vegetation, washing away the investment.

The secret spies and Hun Tong reached an agreement, they would deal with the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan first.

The plan was good but actually carrying it out was nevertheless quite difficult.

Firstly, Hun Tong’s troops found it difficult to fight in water for long periods of time.

That’s not to say he didn’t have methods of going underwater.

There were many methods actually.

For example, underwater breathing scrolls, underwater magic battleships, or perhaps requesting the help of a gold level cultivator, among other ways.

The problem was cost.

The costs were too high, and Hun Tong could barely afford them.

Even if an invited gold level cultivator could fight underwater, when the fishmen clan saw things were going bad, they would retreat and hide.

In fact, their living area frequently moved.

A gold level cultivator could only be hired for a time, and the fishman clan could use delaying tactics to great effect.

Hun Tong himself was a gold level and could act alone. But according to inside information on the fishman clan, they also had a gold level cultivator too.

Fighting on the sea floor was very unfavorable to Hun Tong, and he would narrowly eat a big loss.

Luckily, on the water’s surface, the fishmen’s combat power declined in turn.

Due to how Hun Tong oversaw the island, it wasn’t difficult for them to get a foothold.

Underwater combat was out of the question, so the secret spies and Hun Tong plotted another method.

It was simple: induce the fishmen to launch a land attack.

“The Vinegar Jar Pirates have countless connections with the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan. This situation should be real.”

“Our people also infiltrated the Vinegar Jar Pirates and confirmed this intelligence.”

“This pirate group can be exploited!”

With help from their information superiority, the imperial secret spies proposed a plan to Hun Tong.

Hun Tong reflected for two days before agreeing to this plan.

This was the plan more or less: the imperial secret spies would pretend to be a third party force, and for their own benefit, would collaborate with the Vinegar Jar Pirates.

The imperial secret spies would give the pirates many true pieces of intel that would allow the Vinegar Jar Pirates to attack and rob some merchant ships.

From that, they could gain the Vinegar Jar Pirate’s trust.

While gaining a deeper cooperation between each other, the imperial secret spies would also penetrate deeper into the Vinegar Jar Pirates.

In due time, when they found an opportunity, Hun Tong would lead an army to besiege the Vinegar Jar Pirates and force the fishman clan to save them. Then with the help of a hired gold level cultivator, they would wipe out both groups in one go!

Chapter 316: Pale Wing Bone

Hun Tong and the imperial secret spies had a good plan, but in fact, before the plan bore fruit, a new change had arisen.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates suddenly asked the imperial secret spires to obtain a map of Two Eyes Island’s defenses.

Why would a pirate group want a map of the island’s defenses?

They wanted to attack Two Eyes Island!

Hun Tong and the secret spies were both angry and secretly happy.

The Vinegar Jar Pirates alone did not have the force to take Two Eyes Island. When the moment came, the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan was bound to act too.

This was a golden opportunity that appeared only once in a blue moon!

The secret spies’ plan had made a breakthrough.

This time, when Xi Dai Zi went incognito onto the island, besides trading for the map of the defenses, he also forced the imperial secret spies to serve as their inside man.

This made the imperial secret spies very happy.

He insincerely negotiated with Xi Dai Zi, and after some bargaining, determined their cooperation in this.

He also planned to entertain Xi Dai Zi to listen to the other’s plans, most crucially when it was happening.

But Xi Dai Zi was still suspicious of them and wanted to leave the island.

The secret spies knew haste was irrational here and decided to let him go. As a result, strange things occurred, not only was someone eavesdropping, but a necromancer also appeared too!

All kinds of indications indicated that a third party power existed.

The secret spies and Hun Tong were very disturbed!

News of the necromancer couldn’t be suppressed.

According to imperial law, those that knew must immediately notify the higher ups, and the Blood Light Punishment Institution would immediately dispatch a team to hunt down and kill the necromancer.

But Hun Tong didn’t want this to happen!

The necromancer’s residual life aura was only iron level; they didn’t have anything that could threaten him.

Even if he was attacked by the necromancer, couldn’t he just run away?

If the Blood Light Punishment Institution’s people came, the auction would be canceled.

The Blood Light Punishment Institution was a group of lunatics that wanted to use bombardment spells to flatten the area. If they didn’t dig up any clues, they might even destroy Two Eyes Island.

Hun Tong didn’t doubt they’d do this.

Because there were many proven accounts of this happening.

If the Blood Light Punishment Institution dispatched people here, Two Eyes Island’s future would be bleak!

Although the imperial secret spy also wanted to purge Two Eyes Island of threats, Hun Tong could discuss things with him and had a large amount of space to operate.

Unlike with the Blood Light Punishment Institution who would demand Hun Tong’s unconditional cooperation and give him no say in the matter.

Two Eyes Island was Hun Tong’s sole territory, the place he had settled down on.

The imperial secret spy didn’t want the Blood Light Punishment Institution to come here either.

His painstakingly arranged plan to bait in a larger fish had just made headway.

If a Blood Light Punishment Institution expert came, would the fishmen dare to attack the island?

His efforts would be wiped away, and his dream of merits and promotion would all be in vain.

Another thing, the Blood Light Punishment Institution’s cultivator was bound to conduct a comprehensive investigation. If the imperial secret spy’s plan was implicated and revealed that the secret spy was leaking merchant alliance shipping routes, the secret spy would be in big trouble!

However, just like before, Hun Tong was confronted with the imperial secret spy’s request. This was not a problem of willingness, but rather it was compulsory.

Imperial law regulated that if a necromancy was discovered, even if it was a small sign, it had to be reported immediately.

If information was concealed, when those who were responsible were investigated, they would be judged accomplices or a harborer of sinners and be consigned to eternal damnation.

Necromancers were enemies of the gods!

Those people played with life and death, controlled souls, and touched the gods’ forbidden domain.

As long as there were gods, there would be no tolerance for these magicians.

Regardless of whether it was the Sheng Ming Empire or the other continents, whether it was human, elf, beastman, or dwarf, all were like this.

The study fell into a deathly stillness.

Both Hun Tong and the imperial secret spy were pondering.

It went on for a long time.

A deep sigh transmitted from the secret spy through the alchemy communication device, “Since the information has already been leaked, we must report this.”

“But reporting it now won’t do, lest we squander our prior efforts!”

Hun Tong clenched his teeth, “If we delay our report, we will be held accountable, I don’t want to spend my remaining years in prison.”

“No one saw the necromancer; it is just suspicion.” The secret spy stressed, “We have reasonable justification. The Blood Light Punishment Institution is busy working, and giving thought to that, we wanted to first determine the actual situation. Therefore, we decided to do an investigation, then after clearing things up, we would then report it!”

Hun Tong coldly snorted, “That is a justification. But the Blood light Punishment Institution is insane, if they believed we were procrastinating, they wouldn’t listen to that justification.”

The imperial secret spy continued, “That is why we not only do an investigation, but we also find the necromancer!”

“That wretched guy!”

“By finding him and executing him, we won’t need the Blood Light Punishment Institution to come here, and we’ll be heroes.”

“There is also that third party power that suddenly popped up, we also need to investigate them thoroughly. The power that has the guts to dare ruin our plan must bleed for it!”

Hun Tong’s eyes narrowed, and he sank into pondering once again.

If he chose to report to his superiors now, his prospects would be ruined. Not only would Two Eyes Island be done for, his arduous and conscientious collaboration with the imperial secret spy would all be wasted efforts.

Choosing to delay would have two outcomes.

One was that they found the necromancer, eliminated him, and distinguished himself, thus, everything was all cheers and happiness.

The second outcome is that they couldn’t find the necromancer, but they were able to resolve the fishman problem, however, this outcome was unacceptable.

Because when the Blood Light Punishment Institution intervened, the investigation would determine that they had procrastinated and covered up suspicion, and thus, they would be punished for that. When that happened, the Blood Light Punishment Institution would still purge Two Eyes Island.

Two Eyes Island’s population would be decimated, and it would take more than ten years to recover.

Fighting meant an unclear future and great risks.

But if he didn’t fight, Hun Tong’s foundation would cease to exist.

This was unquestionably a difficult choice.

For a moment, Hun Tong strolled around his study.

He bowed his head in pondering, grinding his teeth from time to time, occasionally snarled, and sometimes looked onwards bewildered.

Finally, he swept away his hesitation and through gritted teeth, grumbled out, “Go investigate, go find them! We must do everything we can to find him, even if we have to dig ourselves under!”

“Grind that damned necromancer to boney ash!”

“I want my Two Eyes Island to continue to prosper. It is my foundation!”

The sunk costs were too high, and so, Hun Tong decided to take the risk.

If he didn’t fight, he would regret it for the rest of his years.

At the deepest level, Hun Tong had an adventurous spirit.

Otherwise at the time, he wouldn’t have given up his previous foundation, sailed into the boundless ocean, and commanded his troops to storm Two Eyes Island.

As a necromancer, Chi Yu was always careful.

He quickly sensed something was amiss.

The atmosphere changed.

Originally, it was a relaxed atmosphere that was now becoming faintly tense.

“What’s going on?”

“The information inquiry went very smoothly.”

First off, the act had been very big. The city guards who had done the investigation were many and news concerning the necromancer had already spread like the wind.

Two Eyes Island’s inhabitants were panicking for this very reason.

Necromancers were undoubtably an extremely sinister existence.

Chi Yu felt bewildered, “There is another necromancer on Two Eyes Island besides me?”

“What did he do?”

“He unexpectedly started a fight here!”

“The timing is terrible.”

Like Cang Xu, Chi Yu was also stressed about his lack of necromancy materials.

He had infiltrated Two Eyes Island to replenish his materials.

Magician cultivation need a lot of resources.

Sufficient materials could not only be used for casting, but they could also be used to make necromancy type magic items. If they were of no other use, the materials could still be dissolved to construct a meditation space suitable for necromancers.

This was not the first time Chi Yu had come here.

Because of his previous experience, his harvest was far greater than Cang Xu’s.

Cang Xu was a novice and still at the exploration stage, while he was on an easy drive across a familiar path.

Chi Yu had been preparing for this shopping for a while and still had abundant funds on hand.

“Should I stay to continue buying or should I leave the island?” Chi Yu hesitated.

He didn’t want to let this rare opportunity slip by, but since a necromancer’s trail had been exposed, Two Eyes Island was bound to conduct a large-scale search. It was even quite possible that the Blood Light Punishment Institution would be drawn here.

When he thought of the Blood Light Punishment Institution, Chi Yu immediately sobered up and cleaned away any thoughts of luck, “Leave, I have to leave as soon as possible.”

He put on an ashen death mask and skulked away.

He had refined this magic item with great difficulty; it could restrain his necromancy aura and suppress his iron level aura to the level of an ordinary person.

Along the way, he saw many patrols and a distinct increase in guards.

When he arrived at the port, he saw a congested crowd, then heard the bad news.

The port was sealed!

No one could go in now.

The reason the official gave caused Chi Yu’s heart to jump – they were checking for necromancers!

“Who is responsible for this?”

“If I run into them, I will make him pay!”

The implicated Chi Yu had no choice but to turn around and give up his plan of evacuating via ship.

Although he was an iron level magician, he didn’t have the means to travel across the sea and needed to ride on a ship.

Furthermore, in the current circumstances, Two Eyes Island’s coastline was surely being monitored too. Him diving into the ocean by himself was too conspicuous and was liable to be noticed by others.

Chi Yu was forced to return to where he had been hiding.

“If I cannot leave by ship, I have no choice but to depart by air.”

He took out a big piece of necromancy material from his storage bag.

It was the ice blade fish bones. He had gotten it after outbidding Cang Xu in the store warehouse.

He had purchased it at a premium because this material was very helpful for his plans.

“After processing these fish bones, I can obtain enough materials.”

“As long as I refine a pale wing bone, I will have the ability to fly.”

“If I succeed, I can fly far away from this troublesome spot.”

“No, if that wretched necromancer is caught, perhaps I can still continue shopping here.”

Chi Yu formulated a plan, dissipating the franticness in his heart.

He buried himself in work and began the initial processing of the ice blade fish bones.

Chapter 317: Fire Ring

Two Eyes Island’s port was locked down, however, on the seafloor under Two Eyes Island, the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s reef disguise was not compromised.

Eight consecutive refinements had been done in its alchemy cabin.

Zi Di was located on one side of the formation while the tower spirit was on the other.

The humming alchemy formation emitted a warm radiance and bathed the entire cabin in a resplendent red light.

The light dissipated, revealing a ring floating above the center of the alchemy formation; it had an iron level aura.

It was a fire ball ring, the joint work of the tower spirit and Zi Di.

Of course, the tower spirit was the main refiner while Zi Di was the assistant.

Even though it was seriously damaged, the tower spirit’s alchemy capabilities still greatly surpassed Zi Di’s, and obviously Cang Xu’s too.

It proved itself as one of War Merchant’s creations.

Zi Di waved, and she used her spiritual control to have the floating fireball ring fly to her hand.

Upon careful examination, the quality was determined to be excellent.

The fireball ring’s refinement method was very simple to understand; however, it was still an iron level magic item and beyond Zi Di’s abilities to refine.

The domain of alchemy was gigantic, and Zi Di had only dipped her toes into the field of medicinal refinement.

The fireball ring had only one function; after being activated, it would shoot a fireball. The ring could shoot three fireballs and is able to shoot a fireball every 30 seconds. After being depleted, it would need to be recharged before further use.

Zi Di tried the ring on her finger.

The size fits.

Naturally, the purpose of using this ring during normal combat was to disguise one’s identity.

Besides this fireball ring, Zi Di also refined a ring of fire ring, a flame jet ring, and a fire wall ring. The first ring discharged a defensive ring of fire, the second could cast the flame jet spell, and the third could cast the fire wall spell.

Along with Fireball spell, these were all standard fire magic.

“It’s a pity that all these fire spells cannot be centralized in one ring. Wearing all four rings at once makes me feel like old money.” Zi Di was somewhat regretful.

It was feasible for four fire magics to be burned into one piece of magic equipment. But that needed alchemy skill, a skill requirement that greatly exceeded the survivors’ current capabilities.

After the refinement was finished, the tower spirit began to clean up the work area and shut down the alchemy formation.

By the time Zi Di finished her study, the tower spirit had already activated the Deceptive Disguise Formation.

The Deceptive Disguise Formation looked a bit different than it did previously.

The formation’s outermost circle was two meters in diameter. Inside the circle was a square. A line stretched inwards from the square’s four points. Each line intersected with two other lines, flowing together until they formed a slanted small square inside the square.

This was no different from before.

The difference was that there was now a circle inside the small square.

This small addition reformed the Deceptive Disguise Formation.

Before, the Deceptive Disguise Formation could only be used on lifeforms, but after this remodeling, it could not work on things.

Zi Di placed all of the rings she had refined into the center of the formation.

There were eight rings altogether. Four for Zi Di and the other four for Cang Xu.

A moment later, the Deceptive Disguise Formation activated.

The eight rings were all covered by the Deceptive Disguise spell.

Zi Di was tranquil.

This had not been the first time she had succeeded.

Deceptive Disguise was always able to affect objects. The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s preinstalled set of formations had used it to disguise its hull.

Behind these eight rings was Cang Xu’s mask.

He customized this mask to give the pretense of being made of black iron, and it had a striking resemblance to what the black gown magician wore.

Originally, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had a serious lack of alchemy materials.

These alchemy materials all naturally came from Two Eyes Island’s market.

One had to say that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps coming to Two Eyes Island was the correct decision. Zi Di and Cang Xu were able to replenish a lot of materials.

Of course, the cost was considerable.

With the money earned from the drug business, that wasn’t a problem.

Zi Di and Cang Xu had made a massive windfall, thus they had the funds to purchase enough alchemy materials.

Zi Di left the alchemy cabin with the eight rings.

Before leaving, she ordered the tower spirit to keep on refining medicine.

It was to still mainly refine Sweet Blood Potions and Green Hat Potions; however, the scale of refinement was decreased greatly.

The trouble at the pharmacy and numerous rising rumors had cast serious doubts on the Green Hat Healing Potion, causing the sales volume of the potions to greatly drop.

Without a way to solve this problem, Zi Di decided to lower the amount refined.

After Zi Di left the alchemy cabin, she first went to the meditation room.

When she opened the meditation room’s door, she felt a strong energy hitting her.

The ghost girl immediately felt coziness emerging from her innermost self.

In this meditation environment, her cultivation efficiency had greatly improved.

But she didn’t do that.

Although Cang Xu had gathered some necromancy materials, they still had a shortfall, thus, they had to stringently calculate their usage.

A massive ghost floated in the meditation room.

He was fat, dark blue, and most attention grabbing of all, he had two heads.

It was the two headed ogre Lan Lu.

One of Lan Lu’s heads had been removed by Guang Ke, but his soul still had them both.

On Snake Rat Island, when Mo Kong caused havoc on the execution ground, Cang Xu and Zi Di had a chance to call back the soul of the dead Lan Lu.

By some fluke, Zi Di had succeeded, and since then, her battle formation’s strength had risen dramatically with the silver level ghost!

The ghost Lan Lu did not react when he saw Zi Di, and floated motionless in the air.

His body appeared translucent, and although his aura was silver level, it was very weak.

He was gravely wounded.

He had been wounded by divine power.

The Running Water God’s sacred emblem that Xi Dai Zi wore was the main culprit.

Currently, necromancy had the advantage of being a new path over other magic schools and alchemy.

The most effective way to deal with necromancy was divine spells.

There was only one reason – divine power was omnipotent.

Gods were omnipotent, so too was divine power.

But each god’s strong points were different, and there were distinct differences between every large religion.

After inspecting him, Zi Di lightly sighed.

The ghost Lan Lu had been recovering here at a slow rate; his injuries had not shown any clear improvements.

“If only I had a soul crystal.”

“That custom-made soul crystal is very pure. It would greatly help Lan Lu’s injuries.”

“It would also be effective in my cultivation.”

The ghost Lan Lu could eat souls.

This was the instinct of all ghosts and could maintain their existence.

However, devouring souls brought evil consequences. It would pollute a ghost with foreign memories and feelings. If too much of those accumulated, the ghost would likely dissolve into nothingness by itself.

As far as Zi Di was concerned, Lan Lu devouring souls would increase her burden of control.

She already found it difficult to control a silver level ghost.

After all, she was only a bronze level necromancer.

What truly was helping her was her iron level spiritual power.

Zi Di closed the meditation room’s door tightly, then went to another cabin to find Cang Xu.

Cang Xu was currently using the memory transformation device.

Zi Di had used this alchemy device to extract Zhen Jin’s memories, make a memory crystal, and used it to make the dragonman youngster think he was a templar knight for a time.

“Spare me, please spare my life. I am willing to defect!” A thin person struggled in a daze.

But he had already been drugged and lacked the energy to move.

Cang Xu used the most lukewarm tone he could, “Don’t be tense, if I wanted to kill you, I would have done so on Two Eyes Island.”

“As we agreed, you will relax as much as possible.”

“If you cooperate properly, there is hope of being returned alive.”

“I understand, I understand…” The thin person’s tears splattered, hope and pleading filled his face.

Upon seeing Zi Di arrive, Cang Xu nodded to her, then formally activated the alchemy device.

The thin person immediately screamed, “Hurts, it hurts!”

“My head hurts!”

“Relax, don’t resist. The more you resist, the worse you’ll feel!” Cang Xu grumbled, he looked serious.

The thin person’s eyes widened, and his limbs twitched like he was being electrocuted, “Let me go, let me go…”

Ding.

A moment later, his head seemed to explode like a watermelon smashing into the ground from a tall building.

The red blood and white brain matter splashed everywhere.

Zi Di dodged it and wasn’t touched. Cang Xu was standing too close and was covered in blood and brains.

Cang Xu was unconcerned and apathetic.

He took off his glasses, wiped them clean, then put them back on.

“Like I said, as long as the target doesn’t want it, you cannot use this alchemy device to extract their memories.” Said Zi Di.

Cang Xu faintly nodded, “To gain a deeper understanding, one must test it and see it with their own eyes.”

“I won’t elaborate. Since the body is still warm, I will recall their soul.”

Cang Xu then used the Soul Summoning spell.

The thin person’s soul was successfully summoned and became a bronze level ghost in Cang Xu’s troops.

“Although its intelligence is pretty good, its potential is lacking. It is possible to cultivate it to the iron level, but that would waste too many resources and not be worth the price.” Cang Xu examined it, then issued his verdict.

He continued to interrogate the ghostly thin person.

Because he had just died and was successfully summoned back, the ghostly thin person retained many of his memories and his mood as he died.

He was very scared of Cang Xu, and as long as he knew, he would tell it to Cang Xu.

However, he didn’t know much.

He wasn’t part of the Imperial Secret Spy Agency, rather he had been personally developed by the imperial secret spies after they arrived at Two Eyes Island.

To maintain secrecy, the imperial secret spies didn’t tell the thin person the truth.

The thin person was completely in the dark and really thought that they were selling out the interest of humanity and selling information to pirate groups.

“This loathsome power has been hiding in secret for many years, yet Feudal Lord Hun Tong hasn’t noticed them, they really cannot be looked down on.” Cang Xu sighed.

Zi Di followed up, “I’m more curious as to why this group of people caused an artificial commotion in front of the store and smeared our healing potions.”

While she said that, Zi Di gave the four fire path rings to Cang Xu.

As Cang Xu put on the rings, he said, “Hun Tong will help us investigate who these people are and what their goals are.”

Zi Di chucked, “I have to say, your improvised stratagem of acting as the black gowned magician really was a wonderful idea.”

Chapter 318: How Things Went

This is how things went.

Ever since Cang Xu encountered the black gowned magician and lost the ice blade fish bone material to him, he had surmised that the black gowned and masked magician as a necromancer.

He ordered Xu Ma to investigate.

Xu Ma was worried about his safety, and did it half-heartedly, but Cang Xu didn’t notice.

Xu Ma was savvy at stealth and evasion.

Cang Xu just believed Xu Ma’s abilities were limited and understood that he wasn’t making any headway.

Thus, Cang Xu changed his way of thinking. Since the search wasn’t working, then he would play the role of them, stalking the streets and using necromancy of his own accord to beat the grass and scare the snake!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wasn’t a regional power, but as long as Feudal Lord Hun Tong was forced to react, they could use his strength to conduct a large-scale search and investigate the black gowned magician.

Cang Xu was relying on the Deceptive Disguise spell.

They used the Pearl Bubble, a god level casting material, making the magic the apex of its type.

If the black gowned magician was a necromancer, he probably wouldn’t be able to escape the search and investigation. But Cang Xu was very sure that his disguise could be maintained.

Cang Xu informed the dragonman youngster of this plan.

After pondering the cause and effect, the young man approved this plan.

Although using a necromancy method was risky, he too believed in the Deceptive Disguise spell and the Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell.

“If Two Eyes Island has a necromancer nestled in it, it would be an immense danger to the masses.”

“Not all necromancers are like Cang Xu.”

“We must seize him; this is the righteous thing!”

The young commander approved this plan.

Thus, Cang Xu began to refine his mask.

He wasn’t able to make an actual ashen death mask, and the one he did refine was a commonly seen magician mask. The magician mask could slightly accelerate magic power recovery speed and was known as a magic recovery mask.

After the magic recovery mask was refined, Cang Xu inserted it into the Deceptive Disguise formation.

After taking on the mask, it now looked exactly like the one the black gowned magician was wearing.

Not long after obtaining the mask, Cang Xu heard that someone was making false charges against and blockading the Wisteria store’s gateway, defaming the Green Hat Potion’s healing potency.

He and Zi Di left together to check up on the particulars.

On the way, Bai Ya found them.

Naturally, Bai Ya knew who they were and informed them that he had noticed a group of people secretly playing dirty tricks and that Xu Ma was already chasing them down.

Cang Xu’s and Zi Di’s hearts shivered with joy, the trail was already there, and there was no need to investigate.

The troublemakers at the scene were mere shilling underlings, not worth to capture. Furthermore, they also weren’t able to act out in the open, they couldn’t let more people say and think the Wisteria store was acting tyrannically and having a guilty conscience, could they?

Therefore, Cang Xu Made arrangements, it was likely that a battle might occur and since the enemy power’s situation was unknown, Bai Ya would continue to stay behind to take care of things in the alley mouth, Zi Di provided support midway, and he, the strongest, would approach the target. If something occurred, there would be a specific response based on the specific situation. Such as what they would do if the enemy was weak, if the enemy was stronger than those deployed, and if the enemy was stronger than the entire Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

This arrangement was quite rational and strict.

Zi Di was just a bronze level, and based on currently available information, the thin person was a bronze level fighter, a threat to Zi Di. But to the iron level Cang Xu, such a person was a far lesser threat.

In addition, if the enemy power was large and Cang Xu was in an unfavorable situation, Zi Di would retreat without delay, and transmit a request for aid.

After the three acted according to the arrangement, Cang Xu quickly reached the destination.

It wasn’t hard to find.

Xu Ma had left behind secret marks along the way.

This was standard for all veteran mercenary corps. As a mercenary commander, Tripleblade helped Zong Ge and played an important role in plugging such deficiencies.

Cang Xu saw the guards out in the open and also knew the place likely concealed stakeouts.

Since he didn’t see Xu Ma or any traces of battle, Cang Xu determined that Xu Ma was still safe and was currently hidden somewhere inside.

The necromancer was in no rush to act. He thought that if Xu Ma snuck back out and provided more accurate information, it would undoubtedly be more appropriate to act after he verified the risks.

Of course, the necromancer didn’t place all of his hopes on Xu Ma; as he stood on the street, he began to mobilize scouting puppets.

The survivors had made large quantities of these alchemy puppets on Snake Rat Island.

After coming to Two Eyes Island, they released a batch into it.

Although some were recalled, many were still placed in crucial locations.

The Wisteria shop, of course, was a key place. Therefore, there were a few alchemy puppets nearby.

Cang Xu had the authority to control these scouting puppets.

Under his spiritual control, three puppets sneaked towards the building.

But before the puppets could enter, something strange happened.

The sound of fighting could be heard from the courtyard, and someone shrieked, “Capture him!”

Soon after, Cang Xu saw Xu Ma’s sorry figure running away, while at the same time, sensing the intense aura of a silver level caster!

Cang Xu hesitated for a moment, then decided to act!

He put on his magic recovery mask; he had been wearing a black armored gown. In order to conceal his necromancer identity, he chose to use Fireball scrolls.

The scroll was activated, causing it to shoot a fireball.

As Xu Ma rushed towards the courtyard gate, he tumbled to the ground and was hanging by a thread between life and death.

Cang Xu didn’t save him directly, rather he detonated the fireball next to the latter.

The faceplanted Xu Ma was the least harmed, while his pursuers were blown up by the fireball, leaving them in a pitiful state.

Xu Ma was able to escape with his life.

At the same time, Zi Di in the middle section, responsible for providing support and detection, immediately sensed Xi Dai Zi’s life aura.

“A silver level caster!?” Zi Di knew the situation had suddenly changed, the enemy’s strength surpassed their own, thus, they immediately carried out their corresponding response plan.

She instantly summoned Bai Ya, then had Bai Ya find someone to indirectly inform the city guard.

She then hurried elsewhere so she could take out the ghost Lan Lu and set up the ambush.

Cang Xu and Xi Dai Zi fought.

The entire fight was filled with danger.

At first, Cang Xu used several magic scrolls in a row, one fire ball after the other to suppress Xi Dai Zi in the early stage.

But Xi Dai Zi was a silver level, and his strength exceeded Cang Xu’s greatly, while also having powerful magic equipment too.

Through his magic item and higher strength, he was able to resist the fireball bombardment and turn the tables.

Cang Xu didn’t dare to zealously fight further. In addition, Zi Di told him that the ambush was set.

Cang Xu chose to retreat.

Xi Dai Zi drove the three headed water snake and pursued relentlessly.

Cang Xu arrived at the ambush site, and with a storm of fire ball scrolls, attracted Xi Dai Zi’s attention.

Zi Di immediately had the ghost Lan Lu launch a surprise attack.

The silver level ghost’s sneak attack was successful and achieved a crucial battle result.

Xi Dai Zi was heavily wounded, causing him to change his plan to retreat.

The ghost Lan Lu was battered by divine power, causing him to become unstable and unable to fight further.

Zi Di was even more on the verge of collapse, barely able to control Lan Lu.

Cang Xu joined back with Zi Di, and decided not to pursue Xi Dai Zi, but instead to dispose of the thin person and his people.

Xi Dai Zi was barely able to withstand the necromancy, while the thin person and others were unable to resist them at all. Most of them were killed on the spot, while those who didn’t sank into a dying unconsciousness.

By the time the city guard commander and his subordinates arrived on the scene, Cang Xu, Zi Di, Xi Dai Zi, and the others had already vanished without a trace.

Only a messy battle aftermath remained.

After Xu Ma returned to the ship, he reported everything he had heard.

The survivors rewarded him to a certain extent and gave him a chance to heal and rest.

The survivors soon after held a meeting to discuss this matter.

What was the origin of the thin person and his group? They worked without a bottom line or principles, they were actually colluding with a fishman pirate group and agreed to pave the way for the fishman pirate group’s invasion of Two Eyes Island!

That was something the dragonman youngster did not want to see.

Zong Ge abhorred these traitorous people even more!

The survivors made a unanimous decision; they would dig out these people and eradicate them.

Not only were they retaliating against a different race and protecting the righteous humanity, but these people had also wickedly discredited their Green Hat Healing Potion and severely hindered the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ plan to acquire capital.

Cang Xu proposed, “I have already disguised myself as another, and exposed myself as a necromancer. After the city guard investigates, they will surely discover some things.”

“In order to prevent the other party from covering this information up and for insurance, we should disguise our features and spread some rumors.”

“Since this matter involves a necromancer, Hun Tong will be unable to cover it up, there is bound to be an all-out investigation and a speedy report to the Blood Light Punishment Institution.”

“The Blood Light Punishment Institution’s dispatched personnel will tidy up Two Eyes Island. The mysterious black gowned magician will have nowhere to hide and will have to prove their identity. Even if they aren’t a necromancer, that group of people will also be exposed and the fishman pirate group’s place to attack the island will be completely destroyed.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was a newcomer, and it was difficult for them to sniff out the culprit. But Cang Xu’s scheme allowed them to draw support from the natives’ and the empire’s strength.

Wisdom was a type of power.

Cang Xu’s necromancer identity was originally his weak point. But it could ingeniously be put to use and be treated as a type of weapon that could solve the difficult problem before them.

After understanding the particulars of the battle, Zong Ge secretly had a new level of respect towards the necromancer, “That old codger knows the hearts of men and is an expert of crafty plots and machinations.”

“He obviously didn’t have armor, but during the fight, through his flurry of attacks and fearless attitude, he was able to force Xi Da Zi onto the defensive.”

In order to find the third party and find that damned necromancer, Feudal Lord Hun Tong and the imperial secret spy genuinely cooperated fully for the first time.

One was the feudal lord of Two Eyes Island, the other was a master intelligence officer, an imperial secret spy, and there were almost no hindrances to their investigation.

The imperial secret spy led the investigation.

This was his specialty.

Most people would be baffled and at a loss at where to start, because those well acquainted with what happened, namely the thin person and the others, were all missing. But in the imperial secret spy’s eyes, there were many clues in his hands.

Chapter 319: We are Innocent

Firstly, even though the necromancer that came to Two Eyes Island was well hidden, they would eventually leave behind some traces.

As long as a large-scale investigation is launched, new clues will always be found.

Before their deal, the imperial secret spy couldn’t search the entire island with their strength on hand. But in coordination with Hun Tong’s full strength, he now had the ability to do so.

But the imperial secret spy didn’t decide to do an extensive search.

To him, this was the option of last resort.

A large-scale search would cause pirates to be deterred and the auction to lose a great number of customers. The secret spy didn’t care about the damage it would cause Hun Tong, but he did care that it might cause the fishmen to dispel their plan to storm Two Eyes Island.

“With my talent, can I really not find an iron level necromancer? Hehehe.”

The imperial secret spy was confident in himself.

He precisely grasped the other party’s identity.

“A necromancer!”

“Why did he come to Two Eyes Island?’

“Even if he came for some secret mission, doesn’t he desire those necromantic materials?”

Necromancy was a new school of magic.

New schools have some advantages and some disadvantages.

Among the greatest disadvantages was that due to not being widespread, related resources were difficult to develop and hard to acquire.

The imperial secret spy audaciously surmised that the mysterious necromancer was likely searching the marketplace for necromancy materials!

Following this point, he mobilized his personnel to inquire about that.

Very quickly he obtained a trail.

The Dark Rainbow Store!

Like the rumors said, it was the pirate’s largest booty disposal site and its backer behind the scenes was Hun Tong.

By Hun Tong’s command, the Dark Rainbow Store cooperated fully with the imperial secret spy’s investigation.

“Ice blade fish bones contain a death qi magic material.

“This caused two magicians to bid for it.”

“One is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ Lao Zhong, the other was a masked black gowned magician.”

“In the end, the black gowned magician bought the ice blade fish bones at a premium.”

“Isn’t the ice blade fish bones that contained death qi a necromantic material?”

“Both of them are suspicious!”

The imperial secret spy quickly made a portrait that depicted the mysterious black gowned magician’s appearance.

He gave the inquiry into the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to Hun Tong.

As a gold level cultivator and Two Eyes Island’s feudal lord, Hun Tong wouldn’t get up and conduct the search personally. He instead dispatched the city guard commander with a temporary search team.

The search time immediately searched for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps at port and came clean in their purpose of coming.

As the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ representative, Cang Xu welcomed them, “We are willing to cooperate actively, necromancers are public enemies of everyone. The sooner we find and eliminate that hidden danger, the better it will be for everyone on Two Eyes Island.”

The city guard commander asked about what happened at the Dark Rainbow Store.

Cang Xu revealed a look of astonishment, “Are you saying that the mysterious magician I was competing against for the ice blade fish bones was actually a necromancer?”

Soon after, he analyzed to himself, “If that is so, that might be possible.”

“At the time, I did feel somewhat strange.”

“The other party was very determined to buy the ice blade fish bones. But that material in my opinion had an immense blemish.”

“Now that you reminded me, upon careful reflection, that material was filled with death qi, and to a necromancer, that was where its value lay.”

The city guard commander couldn’t help but give a weird look.

“Cough, cough.” The city guard commander was somewhat embarrassed, “If you can, please display your magic. As far as I know, you are a fire magician, right?”

Cang Xu was startled, then pursed his brows in displeasure, “Are you suspecting me?”

The city guard commander shook his head, he then solemnly stated, “We suspect anyone, even the Feudal Lord’s personal maid. The sooner we wash you clean of suspicion, the better for both you and us, no?”

Cang Xu coldly snorted.

Although he was an iron level, when confronted with the silver level city guard commander, he still displayed a magician’s haughtiness.

“Then you better keep your eyes open and observe.”

After saying that, Cang Xu began to chant in front of everyone.”

After the chant was completed, a magical light glistened.

Everyone saw a fireball condensing above Cang Xu’s palm.

Cang Xu threw the fireball, and it streaked in an arc across the air before hitting the seawater and exploding loudly, lifting a jet of water from the ocean.

“Any other questions?” Cang Xu asked.

“Not for the time being.” The city guard commander nodded, “As far I know, you aren’t the corps’ only magician, there is also a female bronze level magician called Hong Teng.”

Hong Teng was Zi Di’s pseudonym.

Cang Xu nodded, “Hong Teng is my corps’ pharmacist. Although she is a bit weaker, I greatly admire her medical skills.”

“She has been inside the ship researching medicinal recipes.”

“I will call her over at once.”

Soon after, Zi Di also came to see the investigation team.

Like before, she too used fire magic.

“If there isn’t anything else, I’ll take my leave first.” Zi Di showed a disagreeable attitude, “Don’t interrupt me so trivially again, my research is in the middle of a critical phase.”

“Hold on.” Another important person on the investigation team walked over.

It was the Wealth Church’s priest, a silver level clergyman.

The priest smiled to Zi Di, “In order to not waste your time, let me investigate you now. Please understand this slight offense.”

Zi Di sighed with frustration, “If you have a divine spell, then just use it. Make it quick, my time is precious.”

The priest thereupon used the divine spell – Detect Necromancer.

Nothing happened to the two magicians.

They had both the Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell on them. With both combined, an insignificant silver level divine spell was not enough to see through them.

Of course, Cang Xu and Zi Di couldn’t use fire magic, however, they were wearing the fire path rings they had refined.

This equipment was also covered with Deceptive Disguise.

In front of ten investigation team members, the two had pretended to chant magic, while in reality they were activating their rings’ magic, yet no one noticed anything amiss.

The two necromancers had all suspicions of them cleared.

Soon after, the investigation team cast the detection divine spell on all members of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

They most certainly did not find any marks of a necromancer.

Before leaving, the city guard commander apologized to the survivors and stated that he was just doing his duty.

The priest reminded them, “If you find anything, please let us know. We will reward you generously based on how valuable the information is.”

This caused Zi Di’s heart to move, and after quietly asking, he discovered that both the government as well as the Wealth Church had issued a bounty.

The bounty was the same, if any island resident found anything on the necromancer, they could report it to the authorities.

A reward would be given based on how valuable the information was in retrospect.

The reward was quite ample.

After sending off the investigation team, the survivors once again held a private discussion.

“Like we anticipated, the necromancer’s appearance has touched everyone’s sensitive nerves. Now, Hun Tong and the Wealth Church have joined hands to carry out a full investigation. Even if Two Eyes Island needs to be flipped upside down, they will find the necromancer’s trail.” Said Cang Xu.

Zong Ge suddenly stated, “The auction will be held earlier.”

The dragonman youngster faintly smiled, “Since Hun Tong is doing this, perhaps he is now worried that a large search will severely damage the flow of customers. He is probably afraid that the Blood Light Punishment Institution’s dispatched person that will completely blockade Two Eyes Island and prevent the auction from taking place.”

Zong Ge replied, “This plan has a systemic problem. We came here to participate in this auction.”

He wanted to restore his arm and needed a gold level healing potion.

Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had the recipe, the tower spirit was damaged and didn’t have the ability to refine that level of potion.

“Not a big problem.” Said Zi Di, “In fact, the benefits are greater than the harm.”

“News of the necromancer has caused everyone to become tense, both inside and out, of Two Eyes Island. Prices on the black market and the discount market will tumble greatly.”

“According to imperial law, Hun Tong must report this to the higher ups.”

“If the Blood Light Punishment Institution’s people come to Two Eyes Island, they are bound to do a big clean up and search, many deals will no longer be conducted.”

“Therefore, many people chose to cut their prices instead. They will rush to sell their goods before the Blood Light Punishment Institution comes and withdraw their funds.”

“We now have a lot of money; this is a good opportunity for us to go on a shopping spree and stock up on our alchemy materials!”

“I am only worried about the auction.”

“Getting back to that now, Hun Tong is very thoughtful to have moved up the auction date.”

The survivors didn’t think that Hun Tong would delay his report. Because they didn’t know about the imperial secret spy and didn’t anticipate that the imperial secret spy’s people and Hun Tong were conspiring together.

Cang Xu then said, “If Hun Tong finds the necromancer before the Blood Light Punishment Institution comes, he will either capture or kill them, causing the Blood Light Punishment Institution to likely let Two Eyes Island off.”

“If I was Hun Tong, I would surely strive for this to happen.”

“Because Two Eyes Island’s commerce won’t be able to withstand the Blood Light Punishment Institution’s wanton shakeup.”

“In fact, the current search has already harmed Two Eyes Island’s business ambience.”

“That is not only Hun Tong’s interest, but also the Wealth Church’s interest too.”

Two Eyes Island was a Wealth God parish.

If business was cracked down here and the income of believers were greatly diminished, then the offerings to the church would also likewise decrease.

Seeking wealth in of itself was also one of the Wealth God’s major teachings.

“We also have a weak point.” Said Cang Xu, “That is Xu Ma.”

“Both Zi Di and I were disguised, even if we were seen, nothing will come of it. But if Xu Ma is discovered, it will be easy to find that he is from our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Hun Tong’s searching capabilities are formidable, especially with the Wealth Church releasing the same bounty.”

“Almost all of Two Eyes Island believe in the Wealth God, with the twin power of belief and benefits, they will be reporting vigorously.”

“Xu Ma is only a bronze level thief, he was stabbed the first night on the island, and he was caught eavesdropping. It will be difficult to guarantee that his entry and exit from the courtyard will go undiscovered.”

“Especially after I injured him before he could escape.”

Chapter 320: I Figured It Out!

Zong Ge pursed his brows, “Don’t tell me we need to hand over Xu Ma? That can actually be explained. According to Xu Ma, the thin person was the one who stabbed him, no?”

Cang Xu chuckled, “I have a better way that can both prove ourselves while at the same time also earning us some money.”

Zi Di’s eyes flashed, and she smiled, “I know, you are thinking of communicating with Hun Tong and telling him about the fishman attack?”

“Yes.” Cang Xu nodded.

After the dragonman youngster realized that he clapped his hands, “That is a good method. Before Hun Tong finds the necromancer, they won’t be able to learn of the fishman’s planned attack of the island. Xi Dai Zi escaped.”

“But the feudal lord and the Wealth Church aren’t aware of his existence.”

“Everyone’s attention is on the necromancer that appeared.”

“This would be a terrible time for the fishmen to assault Two Eyes Island.”

“For humanity’s sake, we should warn Hun Tong.”

“The only worry is being implicated with the necromancer. But since the investigation team had just stopped by, this is the most opportune moment to send Hun Tong a message.”

Cang Xu profoundly added, “Hun Tong is an intelligent person, and we have cleared ourselves of suspicion. We need to give some clues so that they hypothesize and authenticate.”

City government building.

The study.

Hun Tong was having a discussion with the imperial secret spy through an alchemy device.

Hun Tong said, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has been investigated, nothing was found.”

At first, the imperial secret spy was silent, but shortly after said, “Even so, the suspicion on them cannot be completely eliminated. This world holds many concealment and disguise methods. The investigation team’s main detection method was the silver level divine spell, and if their disguise method surpassed the silver level, they could remain hidden.”

“According to current intelligence, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has a gold level cannon. During the Snake Rat naval battle, that cannon mysteriously vanished. Therefore, it is possible they have a gold level storage device.”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is newly established and is likely a front for a group of arms smugglers. According to this layered conjecture, them having a disguise method is quite rational. It being gold level isn’t strange either.”

Hun Tong nodded, “Makes sense. But what other clues have you seen? The black gowned magician who bought the ice blade fish bones at a premium is far more suspicious!”

“Apart from that, there is another trail you should be clear about. Two level seven elders suddenly came to the Wisteria Store. The two popular potions came from them. Those two are elusive and very dubious.”

Hun Tong continued to analyze, “The Wisteria Merchant Alliance was a grass roots organization, born from the lower class. After being annexed and broken up, the survivors of that crave power and influence even more. Under those circumstances, it is normal for them to renounce their bottom line and be infiltrated by the corruption of necromancers.”

The secret spy replied, “I have some inside information on those two. In fact, I have already investigated those two. Besides discussing the matter with Xi Dai Zi, I also assigned some people to slander the Green Hat Healing Potion’s healing effects. If there was a problem with the potion, I believed that the two elders would show themselves.”

“Interesting.” Hun Tong smiled.

But soon after, his smile dwindled and his brows creased, “After carefully pondering all of the clues, I have a strange feeling about this. Furthermore, this feeling is getting stronger.”

“What is the origin of this third party power? How did they suddenly emerge without any prior indications?”

“Why were they eavesdropping on the negotiation? Were they shadowing Xi Dai Zi? Were they hired investigators from the merchant alliances who were robbed? What other goals do they have?”

“The eavesdropping was also odd.”

“Since there was a powerful magician capable of doing battle with Xi Dai Zi, why wasn’t the magician eavesdropping? Wouldn’t that be more dependable?”

“Why would they first dispatch an inferior eavesdropper that people would notice?”

When the secret spy heard this, he immediately expressed that he had the same feeling, “There is another strange area, Feudal Lord, do you remember? Someone notified the city guard. When the necromancer and Xi Dai Zi fought, there indeed were constant explosions. But the notifier did it really fast. The timing is amiss. Another irregularity, the notifier did not do it in person, instead they used a beggar to convey it. Why did that person want to hide themselves?”

The study sank into silence.

Hun Tong and the secret spy on the other side of the line were both contemplating.

The more the two pondered, the more questions they found.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was looking for the third party; they were also looking for the third party.

“Hold on, I have some new information!” The imperial secret spy suddenly interjected; it was very difficult for him to conceal the joy in his voice upon receiving the latest information.

“While the two magicians were fighting, someone saw a wounded person running from them in a panic.”

“According to what the information describes, this man is known as Xu Ma, a member of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“Is that so?” Hun Tong immediately flipped to the information on his desk and found Xu Ma’s record, “There is indeed such a person, moreover, he is recuperating.”

“The specific reason for his injuries?” The secret spy instantly questioned him.

“Didn’t ask. Who would think he got it from something related to the necromancer?” The feudal lord coldly snorted.

The study quickly lingered with killing intent.

The feudal lord growled, “Based on this, the third party is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. The investigation team didn’t notice anything, your conjecture might really be true. They have an advanced disguise technique.”

“How should we deal with them?”

Hun Tong’s first notion was to act personally and annihilate everyone in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!

But after reflecting on it, he, with some astonishment, noticed that this matter was comparably thorny, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would not be easy to deal with.

This new mercenary corps had formidable military successes.

They had been a major force in the defeat of Rou Zang’s pirate group.

Their commander, Long Fu, directly vied with gold levels, something many people witnessed during the Snake Rat naval battle.

Hun Tong had met Long Fu once. Upon recalling that meeting, from the onset, he could confirm that Long Fu had abundant battle talent.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps also had a gold level cannon, and it would be unpleasant if Hun Tong was hit by it.

What restrained Hun Tong more was that if he made a move and didn’t immediately control the situation, the cannon would bombard Two Eyes Island, a terrible consequence.

Especially during the preceding period of the auction, when the necromancer was making everyone anxious, and the large search was harming the commerce ambience. If he started a battle recklessly and caused a bombardment, that would be even worse.

Hun Tong didn’t want his foundation to be damaged.

The communication device transmitted the imperial secret spy’s voice. While the feudal lord was fixated on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he was not and maintained his cool, “That might not be so. There is no evidence that proves the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is the third party power. Currently, we haven’t even confirmed the information on Xu Ma.”

“Then let’s verify it. This time I will go myself!” Hun Tong impatiently said.

This time, he would find them himself.

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was the one, conflict would likely break out.

At this moment, a subordinate came to the study door to report.

The subordinate brought considerably urgent and important information.

A warning letter.

The letter stated that the writer of it learned of something endangering Two Eyes Island and Hun Tong’s rule. Furthermore, this information had to do with the necromancer!

“Who sent this letter?” Hun Tong was intrigued.

After receiving the answer, his brows wrinkled deeply. “A beggar?”

“This trick again?” Hun Tong couldn’t help but associate this with the city guard incident when a beggar had warned them too.

The warning letter didn’t detail the contents of this information; however, it indicated the place and time to negotiate the transaction of this information.

The other party made it known that in order to ensure the reliability of this information, Hun Tong’s side could use divine spells, magic, and other methods to verify it.

Through the communication device, the imperial secret spy also found out what had happened on Hun Tong’s side.

“Wait a moment.” The secret spy suddenly said, “I figured it out! Could this be the truth of this matter?”

“If the wounded man was really Xu Ma…”

“Perhaps our initial guess was wrong, the eavesdropper and the necromancer are unrelated.”

“What do you mean?” Hun Tong was puzzled.

“Feudal Lord, who do you think this letter is from?”

“Of course it is from that damned third party power.” But as Hun Tong said that he paused.

He sensed that his logic had a grave error.

The secret spy laughed, “Correct, it seems you too, Feudal Lord, realize it. If it was the third party power who sent this letter, they wouldn’t have disclosed their identity in front of us for such a small reward. Conversely, I surmise that those who want to claim the reward should be innocent and know the inside information on this matter.”

“Xu Ma…the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?” Hun Tong soliloquized.

“You are wise, Feudal Lord! I have always felt that the truth of this matter was never that simple. But this time, I really do need you to go out yourself.” Said the secret spy.

Two Eyes Island, the old island.

A hidden chamber.

The necromancer Chi Yu sighed to himself, “The outside investigation is getting more and more rigorous.”

“The mask will instead make me more suspicious.”

“That necromancer who made a move was truly a fool. Why don’t they just drop dead? They left me with so much trouble.”

Since Cang Xu and Xi Dai Zi fought, Chi Yu had been sheltering here to painstakingly refine his magic item.

He didn’t have much talent in alchemy, and even though the refinement threshold for necromancy magic items was lower than other magic schools, Chi Yu had suffered unspeakable misery during it.

“Perhaps I should rest and not be so tense.”

“It wasn’t me who did it!”

“This place has been very safe.”

Chi Yu fretfully comforted himself.

This hidden chamber was deeply concealed in the underground abyss, in a small space between the branches of the coral ball cage tree, it was concealed well.

Chi Yu discovered it by accident and treated it as his base of operations.

For the last several years, he had been sneaking into Two Eyes Island and living here.

With his arrangements, this hidden chamber became even more covert.

Chapter 321: Paying to Listen to a Murderer’s Explanation

After meditating for a while, Chi Yu recovered somewhat.

“Alas, I truly have bad luck to come across such shit.”

“I planned on selling soul crystals on the black market, now that is impossible.”

“That necromancer, don’t let me find you. If I do, I will give you a stern lesson!”

Chi Yu had sold soul crystals on the black market several times and had earned a huge profit.

“I don’t know why, but things feel a little off.”

“My own safety comes first!”

“Think prudently, first I must refine the pale bone wings.”

After cursing Cang Xu, Chi Yu once again immersed himself in the painstaking refinement of the flying magic item.

The fishman Xi Dai Zi was groping his way forward.

He was well acquainted with the coral ball cage tree’s topography, including most of the secret paths and hidden chambers.

This was what he relied on to sneak into Two Eyes Island.

The coral ball cage tree had been grown by the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan.

Humanity was the invader who had expelled the fishman clan. Although Hun Tong and humanity occupied the surface of the coral ball cage tree and renamed it Two Eyes Island, none of them had a deep understanding of its internal structure.

Because Two Eyes Island was a living being.

Inside the coral ball cage tree, there were secret paths and hidden chambers among the small cracks in its branches.

The growing vines and branches meant that these secret paths and hidden chambers were constantly disappearing, appearing, or relocating.

The coral ball cage tree’s life level was also high and could resist many detection methods.

Of course, the longer Hun Tong reigned here, the greater humanity’s control of Two Eyes Island became.

The secret path was dark, for Xi Dai Zi didn’t create a light.

He was a deep sea fishman, and his big fish eyes could catch deep sea prey, thus, he had dark vision.

He wasn’t in a good state.

After being ambushed by the ghost Lan Lu, his soul had been injured. This injury was very troublesome to deal with, far more difficult to heal than flesh wounds.

“If I could get a soul crystal, I would have the right medicine for this.”

“Otherwise, the standard method of healing is simply waiting for my soul to restore itself.”

“That takes too long. The clan will soon launch its counteroffensive, and I will be in a terrible state when it happens.”

“What am I thinking?” Xi Dai Zi bitterly laughed, “The entire island is searching for the necromancer, and the Blood Light Punishment Institution will also dispatch a cultivator here. The clan’s plan to recapture our home will be run aground!”

“I must find a chance to leave this place and tell the captain and clan what happened here.”

Xi Dai Zi grew ever more impatient.

Suddenly, he stopped walking.

He faintly sensed an abnormality ahead.

Thus, Xi Dai Zi reached out to touch the secret path and joined his spirit with the coral ball cage tree.

He closed his eyes, and an image of the scene ahead gradually appeared in his mind.

Ahead of this secret path, there was a relatively hidden chamber.

Upon leaving the hidden chamber, one could go to the old island’s slums on the surface.

But now, this hidden chamber concealed a single person.

Xi Dai Zi detected this person, causing both alarm and anger to suddenly flare as his eyes unconsciously flew open.

My God!

It’s that damned necromancer!!

He had been hiding here and was doing alchemy!

“Good, very good.” Xi Dai Zi tenaciously bit his jaw, blood filling his eyes.

“So, you were hiding here!”

“Let me run across you.”

“Hehe.”

“Hehehe.”

The alchemy engrossed Chi Yu sensed an inexplicable nip of chillness.

“That feeling again.”

“It’s been very frequent recently.”

“Am I fussing about nothing?”

Under a bridge, a masked Cang Xu was hiding in the darkness.

Footsteps could be heard.

The city guard commander and the Wealth God priest were walking together.

“Stop, don’t go any further.” Cang Xu’s voice came from the darkness.

He had deliberately lowered the tone of his voice.

The city guard commander’s eyes flashed, “We have come to trade in information. Come then, won’t you tell us what you know?”

“Did the Feudal Lord come?” Cang Xu asked.

The city guard commander coldly snorted, “Did the Feudal Lord say he would come? He is a gold level cultivator and has many affairs to attend to every day. This type of thing falls to me to authenticate first.”

Cang Xu was silent for a bit, then suddenly threw a scroll, “This is a contract scroll, please look at the clauses, and if there aren’t any problems, please sign the contract first, then we’ll talk.”

The city guard commander caught the scroll and after carefully unrolling it, he and the priest attentively studied its clauses.

The clauses were very open and transparent. It mainly guaranteed that both parties would deal in good faith and prevent either side from acting against each other.

“Fine, I will sign this contract.” Said the city guard commander.

But Cang Xu stressed, “Lord Priest, please sign the contract too. After all, you are a silver level caster.”

The priest nodded, “I can.”

“No need!” At that moment, Hun Tong’s voice came from behind Cang Xu.

Cang Xu turned pale with fright, he didn’t sense Hun Tong approaching at all.

By the time he turned around, it was already too late.

Hun Tong seized him by the neck.

“Don’t do it!” Cang Xu said at once, “I sincerely want to work with you. I have real information. I guarantee it!”

“Lord Hun Tong, Lord Priest.”

“You were the ones who issued the bounty, I also considered the needs of all the humans on Two Eyes Island, I am on your side.”

Hun Tong dragged Cang Xu by the scruff out of the shadow of the bridge.

Before Hun Tong tore off Cang Xu’s mask, he coldly snorted, “You think I don’t know anything? You are the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ magician, Lao Zhong!”

Cang Xu went stiff, “You, what do you know? No, how much do you know?”

Hun Tong sneered, and upon believing that he had intimidated Cang Xu, let him go.

Cang Xu regained his freedom but seemed dazed.

He bowed his head in a moment of pondering, then took off his mask to reveal his agonized “true face”, “Lord Hun Tong, Lord Priest, and Lord City Guard Commander, good afternoon to you all.”

“Please forgive me, three Lords. We really offended you inadvertently, there have been many misunderstandings. Lord Hun Tong, you are a gold level cultivator, this place is your domain. We were really worried about our safety, but this information is really important and hiding it will have grave consequences.”

“Therefore, after we negotiated, we decided to do this. Please believe us, we have no relation with that necromancer!”

“Whether or not you have our trust depends on your explanation. Old magician, you will value this chance to explain yourself properly. “Hun Tong pressured.

But Cang Xu shook his head slowly, “I have come on a mission. I can guarantee to your Lordships that this information is extremely valuable. If you overlook it, Two Eyes Island might suffer catastrophic damage. This is no alarmist talk. Before I say this information, can we discuss the matter of remuneration?”

Hun Tong wanted to slap Cang Xu down.

What information?

He didn’t know.

Isn’t this about the fishman pirate group attacking Two Eyes Island?

Hun Tong looked forward to the fishman pirate group and fishman clan attacking the island.

But this enticement plan had been conspired by him and the imperial secret spy, neither’s underlings knew about it.

The city guard commander was the same. The Wealth Church’s priest even more so.

Hun Tong endured the indisposed feeling, “You won’t lack the pertinent reward.”

Cang Xu replied, “No, your esteemed Lordship. Compared to the generous monetary reward, we would rather purchase some rare materials. We are also very interested in what will be on offer at the auction. Is it possible to purchase some surreptitiously ahead of time?”

Hun Tong’s eyes narrowed as he suppressed his rage, “It seems your Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps has some ideas. As for you, Lao Zhong, you are very loyal to your mercenary corps. You do know that your life lies in my hands. You came alone this time, where are Long Fu and Shi Qi?”
