Cang Xu shook his head and forced a smile, “It is precisely because I just joined that I need to work even harder.”

“As for the commander and auxiliary commander, they trust your Lordship’s reputation and stayed behind on the ship, waiting for news of me.”

Hun Tong coldly snorted.

Long Fu and Shi Qi staying behind on the ship was a secondary preparation.

If they noticed the situation went amiss, they might force their way through the custom ships and out to sea.

Hun Tong didn’t despite the other party’s determination.

To him it seemed that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were arms smugglers, dangerous people who took risks out of desperation. If they were forced to, they would fire their gold level cannon and shell Two Eyes Island!

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps only dispatching Cang Xu did seem irresponsible, but in actuality it was the greatest deterrent to Hun Tong and the others.

Two Eyes Island was Hun Tong’s foundation. It was also his weak spot.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ strategy had come from Cang Xu.

Hun Tong gradually curbed his grandeur and waved to Cang Xu, “Follow me, this is not the place to speak in detail.”

Cang Xu relaxed, most of his plan had already succeeded.

He followed Hun Tong and returned to the city government building.

The feudal lord sent back the city guard commander and had the priest use the Detect Lie divine spell before letting Cang Xu into his study for a private discussion,

Hun Tong did not want information on the fishman’s attack on the island to get out to the priest and the others.

He and Cang Xu carried out a negotiation in the study.

Even faced with a gold level cultivator, Cang Xu still strived for the most benefits.

After a spell of wrangling, the two had come to a final proposal.

Hun Tong would give some rare resources and permit the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to purchase some things from the auction early. But the selection was restricted, and they were to be sold at 30% above market price.

Hun Tong found it hard to breathe.

He obviously knew the fishmen were going to attack the island, but he had to purchase the information.

He just had to.

Because he pretended not to know about it and through this transaction, he sealed the lips of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and prevented them from divulging this information.

Of course, Xu Ma knew the facts and had personally witnessed what the necromancer looked like. This important clue was also an important bargaining chip to Hun Tong.

Hun Tong did not know that the one before him, Cang Xu, was the real murderer, the necromancer he should hack into pieces.

This necromancer was not standing in front of him, explaining himself.

Hun Tong, nevertheless, focused and earnestly listened.

Because he had bought this opportunity with money.

Chapter 322: “The Truth”

Inside the city government building study, Cang Xu began to give “the truth” to Hun Tong.

“One of our corps’ members, Hei Ya, was procuring some equipment at the Dark Rainbow Store when he ran into another member, Xu Ma.”

“Xu Ma and Hei Ya went together to continue purchasing things, and while doing that, they saw the disturbance at the Wisteria Store.”

“They wanted to simply watch the excitement, but as a result, Xu Ma accidentally discovered that the disturbance was organized, and that the ringleader of it was his personal enemy – the guy who had stabbed him.”

Cang Xu then briefly recounted that Xu Ma had a grudge against the thin person, and what had happened to Xu Ma when he first entered the island.

Hun Tong frowned, he wasn’t interested in this and impatiently urged, “Get to the important stuff.”

Cang Xu faintly smiled, “Xu Ma followed his trail and wanted to ambush the thin person. This ended up with him sneaking into the courtyard and accidently hearing an important secret!”

“This secret is precisely what my corps must tell you, Feudal Lord. It is very significant and concerns the safety of Two Eyes Island!”

Cang Xu deliberately flaunted the climax.

The corner of Hun Tong’s eye invisibly twitched.

Of course, he knew what this secret was – the Vinegar Jar Pirates wanted to buy defensive charts and an insider so that they could invade Two Eyes Island!

He not only knew this secret, but he also knew even more. For example, the Vinger Jar Pirates’ backer, the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan.

But to not divulge this weak point, Hun Tong still decided to cooperate with Cang Xu, “Oh? What is this secret?”

Cang Xu became very solemn, “Feudal Lord, there is a third party power on your Two Eyes Island. This power is very mysterious and has deeply infiltrated it. Xi Dai Zi disguised himself and came to the old island to hold a discussion for their help.”

“They not only traded away Two Eyes Island’s defensive layout, but he also agreed to help the fishman pirate group, the Vinegar Jar Pirates, attack Two Eyes Island, the mysterious power has become their insider.”

“If we don’t take preventive measures, when both sides join hands and coordinate inside and out, Two Eyes Island will be in danger!”

“That’s right…” Hun Tong sighed to himself. But outwardly, he went faintly agape, his eyes glared wide open, and he used a stupefied tone, “Oh? Is that so? They have the guts to dare attack my Two Eyes Island?!”

“It is indeed so!” Cang Xu affirmatively nodded.

Hun Tong said to himself, “I still hope that they will attack me.”

But outwardly, he was stewing with alarm and fury, “This mysterious third party, who are they? Do you know anything about them? Out with it now!”

Cang Xu deeply sighed, “My corps just ran across them. We don’t know much at all about them.”

It would never have occurred to the necromancer that the feudal lord before him knew far more of what was going on behind the scenes than he did.

Hun Tong’s acting was exceptional, and Cang Xu didn’t notice any flaws in it.

Hun Tong’s tone sank, “I will investigate this power with all my might. What other information do you have, continue on.”

Cang Xu continued, “After Xu Ma eavesdropped on some information, Xi Dai Zi noticed him. Luckily, he was quick-witted and escaped Xi Dai Zi’s attack.”

“While he was about to be gutted by an encirclement, a masked, black gowned magician suddenly appeared.”

“He used many fireball scrolls and fought Xi Dai Zi, causing immense chaos.”

“Xu Ma took advantage of the chaos to escape the scene.”

“Not long after, Hei Ya used a beggar to inform the city guard, and they hurried to the scene of the battle.”

“As for the situation there, I believe the Lord City Guard Commander knows more than I do.”

Hun Tong nodded his head inquisitively, “That black gowned magician…”

Cang Xu took out a parchment from his clothes.

He handed the parchment to Hun Tong and said, “Feudal Lord, I had Xu Ma describe them and drew a likeness of the black gowned magician for you. It should show an approximation of his figure and appearance. Please take a look.”

Hun Tong took the parchment and the moment he opened it, his eyes instantly contracted.

He had a similar sketch of this person too.

It was another clue.

He had obtained it from the Dark Rainbow Store.

The black gowned magician had purchased the ice blade fish bones at a premium.

“The necromancer…should be him!” Hun Tong’s heart was growing more certain of this conjecture.

Anyways, he quickly restored his calm demeanor and gazed at Cang Xu, “I will still need to validate your information.”

“Of course.” Cang Xu promptly nodded, “It is how things should be done!”

Later, Hun Tong and the priest personally came to the port to see Xu Ma.

Xu Ma was lying in a bed, recuperating.

The dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, Hun Tong, and the priest intruded into his cabin, causing him to jump up in fright and scramble away.

As Xu Ma got up, the others saw something green.

Xu Ma’s hair had turned completely green. Due to his bloodline giving him thick hair, its green strands were more conspicuous.

As for why it was green, of course it was because he had used the Green Hat Healing Potions the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had issued him to heal his injuries.

“Don’t be nervous, sit back down on your bed. Your merits aren’t small, and you are still injured.” The dragonman youngster smiled at Xu Ma.

Xu Ma, green of hair, stammered in terror, “Your Lordships, I…”

Zong Ge interrupted him, “Tell us again what happened to you. Don’t hide anything and don’t lie. Understood?”

“Under, understood!” Xu Ma replied at once.

Hun Tong didn’t speak, instead he looked at the priest.

The priest cast the divine spell – Detect Lies.

As the light of the divine spell enveloped him, Xu Ma narrated his experiences.

His tale was no different from the one Cang Xu had told to Hun Tong in the study, aside from odd embellishments of particulars.

After Xu Ma told his story, Hun Tong immediately asked, “So, you didn’t see the black gowned magician truly act.”

“You only saw him using fireball scrolls, is that right?”

Xu Ma pondered it, then nodded, “Correct.”

After telling his tale, his anxious heart relaxed a lot.

Then Hun Tong asked a few more questions.

Xu Ma replied honestly to what he knew and what he didn’t know.

When everything was finished, Hun Tong nodded to the dragonman youngster with a satisfied look, “Not bad.”

The priest coughed, and the divine spell, Detect Lies, was dispelled.

Everyone filed back out.

The dragonman youngster was the last out and consoled Xu Ma, “Rest well.”

“You gave us information on the necromancer and the pirates.”

“That is no small merit.”

“Two Eyes Island’s feudal lord and the church have given a bounty, they are yours.”

“Furthermore, the mercenary corps will reward you too.”

“But the information on the Vinegar Jar Pirates, in accordance with Lord Hun Tong’s wishes, must be kept strictly secret. It cannot affect the auction.”

“Oh, I understand, I understand Lord Commander!” Xu Ma hastily nodded.

After everyone left, Xu Ma sat at his bedside for a good while before reacting.

“Hehehe…” He began to giggle, “I’ve made a great profit!”

Although he was wounded again, his reputation had been completely redeemed. When news of this merit began to propagate, he would be able to hold his head high in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Not only would he be famous, but there was also a considerable monetary reward too.

“I didn’t expect the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to not take a share of the reward for the information.”

“This mercenary corps really is quite different; they are so just and fair!”

“Commander Long Fu was also concerned about me, which was quite different from what I remember about him. Compared to him, Auxiliary Commander Shi Qi acts so coldly. It’s no wonder Long Fu is the commander.”

Xu Ma had a rich experience of society.

Despite that, because of the dragonman youngster’s concern for him, he felt a trace of warmth.

“Ah…” Xu Ma let out an exceedingly deep sigh of satisfaction. He put both hands on the back of his head and slowly stretched out on his bed and looked at the ceiling, “These wounds were worth it. Next time, if there is such an opportunity, ugh…”

As he thought freely of the future, he carelessly pulled his wounds, causing him to instantly let out a cold gasp of pain.

“Next time…hiss, forget about it.”

“My small arms and legs can’t take such risks again.”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had benefited beyond Xu Ma’s imagination.

After Hun Tong and the priest left the port, Zi Di and Cang Xu studied the list of items Hun Tong left behind.

The survivors’ next plan was to set up a bloodline detection drug database.

Only by testing their members’ bloodlines could they take the appropriate steps so that after they built the Bloodline Purification Formation, they could evaluate their members’ talents and limits.

Therefore, although there were many things on the list, Zi Di and Cang Xu were most interested in medicine and drugs.

“They have a snowflake catalyst; this is an outstanding catalyst rarely seen on the market. Take them all first.”

“There is 30kg of raw nut copper, so much? It is very difficult to produce and is often a byproduct of large scale magic tests. Generally, a large quantity of high level life material and metal material is used to make it. Although it is expensive, it’s absolutely worth it.”

“This is delaying time grease; we also need to buy that. Look. This is the product of the Diamond Box alchemy group. That’s right, that’s the one. It is said that this alchemy group’s product, the delaying time grease, is so gentle in its medicinal effect that it can even be applied to human skin and apply its delaying effect to it. Many nobles privately have a strong demand for it.”

“Look at this. Bird’s nest mercury, this is the main alchemy material for sharpening potions. The empire has long restricted this material; I didn’t expect this auction would be selling 500kg of it. We need it all! Many bloodline drugs can be made from it.”

Zi Di and Cang Xu grew more excited as they studied the item list.

Two Eyes Island’s auction listings was far richer than the two had anticipated.

Hun Tong had once said to the dragonman youngster that every auction on Two Eyes Island exceeded the previous one.

It was very possible that after Hun Tong and the imperial secret spy collaborated together, they wanted to profit as much as possible with the old business model before the situation on Two Eyes Island changed.

Chapter 323: You’ve Found the Wrong Person!

Since the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and Hun Tong had traded information, the survivors had a chance to buy some things ahead of time.

These things rarely appeared in the marketplace, either because the output was low, the empire regulated them, or perhaps because the source of them was rare.

Zi Di and Cang Xu soon saw a tree mushroom.

This tree mushroom never grew on the Sheng Ming or Wilderness Continent, but an entirely different one far away,

One item after another was annotated.

The survivors wanted to buy many things.

Establishing a bloodline detection database was an enormous undertaking, because innumerable bloodlines existed in this world.

However, the survivors could only buy 30 items ahead of schedule.

Hun Tong couldn’t allow them to run away with everything ahead of time, if he did that, he couldn’t hold the auction at all.

It is worth mentioning that there weren’t that many things on the list.

Such as a gold level healing potion.

To a gold level cultivator, this kind of potion was likely a second life.

Therefore, it was very precious.

Hun Tong wanted to save it for the auction and increase Two Eyes Island’s business effect and wouldn’t sell it to a mercenary corps ahead of time.

The city government building.

A gold level communication device was on the desk; its surface was glowing brightly.

Hun Tong was asking about Xu Ma’s story through the device.

The device transmitted the response of the imperial secret spy, “Shadow”. “That is correct, the black gowned magician only used magic scrolls when they attacked the courtyard.”

“The scene there only had water path and fire path traces and aura present.”

“Only on the second battlefield was there a large amount of necromancy residue aura.”

Shadow continued, “Like we expected, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was just dragged into this. Does this Xu Ma believe in the God of Misfortune?”

“He was stabbed by my subordinate’s blade, leading him to notice the latter, following his trail, and getting caught up in a greater crisis. He risked life and death in doing so.”

Hun Tong replied, “He should consider this a blessing in disguise.”

“I heard that the information reward wasn’t confiscated by the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ leadership, instead they gave it to him. Furthermore, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps will also reward him for his merit.”

“Oh?” The imperial secret spy was somewhat surprised, “It seems this group of arms smugglers are quite interesting.”

Hun Tong approved of it, “Although this group has an unclear origin, for the sake of money, they were willing to take the risk to inform me of this information. They sit straight with me.”

After their meeting, the Two Eyes Island’s feudal lord now had a favorable impression of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

If only he knew the truth. Who knows what he would think if he knew Cang Xu was the necromancer.

Hun Tong continued, “Next matter, we will search for that black gowned magician with all haste.”

“Good.” The imperial secret spy didn’t object, “Currently, we have determined for sure that he is the necromancer!”

Just as he said that the imperial secret spy’s voice came to a spontaneous stop.

Then, Hun Tong heard a burst of peculiar static coming from the communication device.

The sound quickly ceased when the imperial secret spy relayed the latest urgent information.

His voice was both surprised and joyous, “Good news, my side has found where the necromancer is.”

“Eh?” Hun Tong was surprised.

The imperial secret spy laughed, “Take a guess, who gave us this significant information?”

Feudal Lord Hun Tong’s mind flashed, “Was it Xi Dai Zi?”

“That’s right, it was Xi Dai Zi.” The imperial secret spy confirmed.

As it turns out, after Xi Dai Zi was ambushed by the ghostly two headed ogre, he retreated from the battle.

While sneaking through the secret paths, he happened upon the necromancer, Chi Yu.

Because of Cang Xu’s performance, Xi Dai Zi thought that Chi Yu was the necromancer he fought and to him, he was the enemy he had snuck up on.

Although Xi Dai Zi had wrathful and killing intent towards Chi Yu, he endured them and didn’t attack.

He was still wounded, and the other person was immersed in painstaking alchemy refinement and was also in excellent condition. In addition, Xi Dai Zi had some misgivings.

It was the environment here.

Two Eyes Island was not the boundless ocean.

If battle broke out, it would attract the attention of Hun Tong and the priest.

After Xi Dai Zi carefully assessed things, he found that attacking was too risky.

He planned to use another’s blade to do the dirty work.

Although the thin person and his people were all dead, he still had a way of communicating with the spy.

Thanks to that, Xi Dai Zi could secretly contact the imperial secret spy’s subordinate and indicate where the necromancer, Chi Yu, was hiding.

Feudal Lord Hun Tong sneered, “We don’t want the necromancer to draw in the Blood Light Punishment Institution, the fishmen are the same. If the Blood Light Punishment Institution comes, there’s bound to be a strong cultivator that will seriously hinder their plan of seizing Two Eyes Island.”

“We couldn’t find the necromancer, but Xi Dai Zi did it with ease.”

“He must know how to control Two Eyes Island, along with where many hidden rooms and secret paths are.”

The imperial secret spy replied, “To ensure absolute security, you should do it yourself, your Lordship. Although Xi Dai Zi requested that we act, I told him this: it is necessary to do something so troublesome, we can let a borrowed knife do the dirty work. We believe that the Feudal Lord is more worried about this than us.”

Hun Tong chuckled, “So, you are biding your time while I, the grand feudal lord, will personally settle this.”

The imperial secret spy chortled back, “My Lord, you also know my strength is incomparable to yours.”

Hun Tong shook his head, “If you want me to act, my fee won’t be small.”

The imperial secret spy was silent for a bit, then said, “You can take half of the 50,000 coins, worthy Lord!”

The imperial secret spy was talking about the 50,000 gold coins he had gotten from Xi Dai Zi.

At the time, he and Xi Dai Zi’s insincere negotiation was fruitful, and he agreed to become the Vinegar Jar Pirates’ insider. Therefore, Xi Dai Zi gave him 50,000 gold coins as an advance payment.

Afterwards, the black gown magician appeared and battled Xi Dai Zi.

The imperial secret spy’s subordinates then vanished without a trace.

Hun Tong and the imperial secret spy believed that the black gowned magician killed the thin person and the others. Naturally, the 50,000 gold coins the thin person had on him was now in the black gown magician’s hands.

50,000 gold coins was no small amount.

Even Hun Tong was thinking about it, the imperial secret spy more so.

But to work together, the imperial secret spy wanted to divide the spoils equally with Hun Tong.

Hun Tong secretly considered this and felt that an equal division was good enough and decided to follow this distribution plan.

Neither knew that this large sum of gold coins was not with Chi Yu, instead it was with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ leaders Cang Xu and Zi Di were deciding what to buy with this sum of money.

“Finally, my refinement is done!” In the hidden chamber, the necromancer Chi Yu focused his eyes on a finished alchemy product, his eyes couldn’t contain his excitement.

The pale bone wings were floating in the air.

It was a pair of bone wings, symmetrical on both sides, and its fine joins looked like delicate bamboo. Between the bones, the badly damaged light purple skin looked like a crude cleaning rag.

Chi Yu took a deep breath, and took off his upper garments, exposing his emaciated body.

He took a step forward towards the pale bone wings, then poured his spirit into the magic item.

The pale bone wings moved by themselves and like a sharp awl, the pale bone wings dug into Chi Yu’s back.

Chi Yu gasped miserably.

The severe pain caused his face to warp.

His eyes flashed with a determined light, and he tenaciously clenched his jaw in perseverance.

After the pale bone wings inserted themselves into his body, green-black veins traced across Chi Yu’s back like wrinkles and emitted a straggly sinister aura.

“Hu hu…” Chi Yu panted, “I didn’t expect that even after being remade by necromancy that accepting this item would still be so difficult.”

“No, it should be because I’m in a terrible state.”

“To refine it, I spent so much energy.”

“I should rest well. After I recover, I use the curtain of night to fly away from Two Eyes Island.”

Bang!

Suddenly, there was a loud sound.

A gold battle qi wreathed Hun Tong smashed into the hidden chamber.

Chi Yu: ??!!

Hun Tong growled, “Necromancer, I finally found you.”

At that moment, Chi Yu wanted to roar, “I was minding my own goddamm business! You found the wrong person!”

But he had been prepared for this mentally and knew that refuting would do nothing.

In this moment of crisis, he activated his arrangement in the hidden chamber.

Boom!!

With a detonation, dust covered the area and pieces of wood flew everywhere.

Within the smoke and dust, Chi Yu spread his wings, and like a swallow, flew into the skies.

He was running like his life depended on it!

“You’re not going anywhere.” The priest gently called out.

A moment later, the light of a divine spell appeared above Chi Yu.

Chi Yu miserably bawled, and his wings broke, causing him to fall.

On the ground, Hun Tong calmly extended his hand.

Gold battle qi bubbled out and turned into a rope that wrapped itself around Chi Yu.

“If you want to capture me, you shall pay the price!!” Chi Yu knew he was a spent force and, with no hope at all in his heart, brazenly self-detonated.

Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh!

His bones all became spurs and shot down in all directions.

The city guards surrounding him that were hit lost their lives.

Hun Tong, the priest, the city guard commander, and the other cultivators were unmoved by it.

“Is he dead?” The city guard commander was somewhat incredulous.

The priest smiled, “What else could he do? He was a mere iron level.”

Xi Dai Zi was hiding in the shadows. When he saw Chi Yu die, his heart was both delighted and somewhat unsatisfied, “To refine that magic item, he spent too much time and energy, straining his condition.”

“When Hun Tong attacked, he was caught unprepared.”

“He could stand against me, but against a gold level, it was not enough.”

“What a pity, he didn’t damage Two Eyes Island’s defenses significantly.”

Also watching in secret, Cang Xu and Zi Di were also feeling sorry.

“That necromancy bone aspect magic has astonishing might!”

“Truly regretfully, those spurs are casting materials.”

We cannot get them. Even if there is some left, the priest will cleanse them with divine spells.”

“Alas, I still hoped that the necromancer could have escaped and given us a chance to attack him.”

“Hun Tong is the feudal lord here, and his control of this place is very deep. It hadn’t been long since we located the target in hiding.”

“But when I think about it, the mysterious third party power can actually hide under Hun Tong’s nose, it can be seen from this that they are quite strong. I hope that after we warned Hun Tong, he will be able to dig out this power too.”

Chi Yu’s death to Cang Xu and Zi Di was a minor regret.

As the instigators, they weren’t worried about Chi Yu being taken captive.

Even if torture revealed that Chi Yu wasn’t the necromancer who had attacked, would it matter?

Would Hun Tong believe a necromancer’s words?

Even if he did believe them, would he search for the second necromancer with great fanfare?

Positively not!

This was his Two Eyes Island.

This was his foundation.

Especially with the auction about to start.

Chapter 324: Where is My Money?

To everyone’s delight and satisfaction, Chi Yu was dead.

Xi Dai Zi needn’t worry any longer and could proceed with the fishman clan’s planned attack of Two Eyes Island.

The imperial secret spy Shadow also needn’t worry about the Blood Light Punishment Institution sending a cultivator over for an inquiry.

Hun Tong could finally let out a breath. Without the Blood Light Punishment Institution, his transition of Two Eyes Island now had abundant time and space to be conducted.

The port blockade ceased.

When the inhabitants of Two Eyes Island heard the necromancer was executed, one after the other, all of them cheered for Feudal Lord Hun Tong’s abilities.

This included merchants, pirates, and more, all were overjoyed.

The only fly in the ointment was that the 50,000 gold coins were missing.

The necromancer Chi Yu was dead and had exploded into bony dregs, leaving not even a corpse behind.

When the battle began, the arrangement in his hiding place triggered a large explosion.

Hun Tong didn’t give up hope, and he surmised, “The money shouldn’t have been on the necromancer himself, if it was, there would be a trace or two of those 50,000 gold coins. Therefore, this money was likely hidden elsewhere.”

Since the necromancer could find one hidden chamber, then it was reasonable to think that he knew of other ones too.

The imperial secret spy also agreed, “We can continue investigating and follow the necromancer’s trajectory of action, perhaps there will be a valuable trail that will allow us to find where the money is hidden.”

He didn’t want to give it up, it was a lot of money!

Meanwhile, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ Cang Xu and Zi Di finally ended their discussion.

They had issued their final decision and chose 30 items.

These things were almost all related to medicine, not finished products, but instead precious alchemy materials. The only comparatively unusual thing was an intact spurting smoke dragon corpse.

After this, Cang Xu came to Feudal Lord Hun Tong again and reached a deal.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would pay 3000 gold coins to purchase 30 items ahead of time.

“At present, we have a lot of capital.” Zi Di thought to herself.

The 50,000 gold coins Hun Tong and the imperial spy were persistently searching for was in their hands. This purchase didn’t even use a tenth of the gold coins.

“The auction remains the focus.”

“We must compete to buy many things.”

Zi Di had already taken aim at the auction, ready to launch a heavy attack.

As her potion sales, the sales volume had steadily deteriorated, and she no longer paid it any heed.

Of course, this was the primary way the group had gained capital. None of them thought they’d capture such a find!

“These arms smugglers have money.” Hun Tong’s impression of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps improved somewhat.

Remember, these items were 30% higher than the market price.

Hun Tong didn’t make a loss; he had earned quite the profit.

The banker usually never loses.

What he didn’t know was that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was using the money he had been desperately searching for.

Xi Dai Zi wanted to leave.

He was standing at the exit of a secret path.

This secret path was located on the bottom of the old island and only opened up for a dozen minutes in the wee hours.

Saltwater intruded into the secret path, and it was pitch dark.

But to Xi Dai Zi, the environment wasn’t terrible to him. He was a deep sea fishman, he could breathe underwater from childhood and his big fish eyes could glean the barest light to see in the darkness.

But he was still injured and couldn’t change his appearance.

Before leaving, he took out a simple communication device and poured magic power into it.

It was a single-use type.

After the imperial secret spy obtained the communication request, he immediately responded.

This communication device was one that he had his subordinate give Xi Dai Zi.

Xi Dai Zi contacted the imperial secret spy of his own volition, “I’m about to leave the island.”

The imperial secret spy stared blankly, “No need to worry, I just got the latest information on soul crystals being sold on the black market.”

He wanted to strive to gain more trust and learn the exact time Xi Dai Zi planned to attack.

But Xi Dai Zi had already decided, “Although there was an accident, that wretched necromancer has gotten his deserved end.”

The imperial secret spy laughed, “That was the result of our joint collaboration. We’ve always worked well together, no?”

Xi Dai Zi couldn’t refute that.

The fishman was silent for a bit, then said, “It is precisely because of that that my group sought your cooperation. The next step is planning, I hope there won’t be any mishaps.”

“The imperial secret spy replied, “I’ll do my best, but I can’t make guarantees, my fishman friend. Be aware, the world does not revolve around you. Two Eyes Island’s defenses are still very strong, not only is there Hun Tong’s faction, but there are also the five great merchant alliances. If you want to attack the island, you’d better come with sufficient strength.”

“And money too of course.”

“As for us, making money is the most important thing there is.”

When money was mentioned, Xi Dai Zi laughed, “Was the 50,000 gold coins returned?”

Cough.

That sentence was like a formless dagger piercing the imperial secret spy’s heart.

The imperial secret spy felt his heart aching again.

Hun Tong still had his foundation and industry, while he had neither.

Due to the death of the thin person and the others, not only had he lost manpower, but he also had to pay compensation and other expenses.

The imperial secret spy was looking forward to this money more than Hun Tong; he needed it to recruit a new staff!

He couldn’t tell Xi Dai Zi the truth.

He still had to win the trust of the fishman shaman.

The imperial secret spy could only laugh and resolutely answer, “Of course! The money is back in my hands.”

“That’s good.” When he heard that, Xi Dai Zi showed a profound smile.

He crushed the communication device, then with a running start, dove into the water.

He swam the rest of the way through the dark and lightless seawater.

After swimming 2 nautical miles alone, a fishman knight riding a 6 meter long ribbonfish came to his aid.

There was a line of seats on the ribbonfish’s back.

The fishman knight sat at the front and with both hands, grabbed the “reins”.

Upon careful inspection, the “reins” were the ribbonfish’s fish whiskers.

A long lance lay next to the fishman’s foot.

Xi Dai Zi sat in the seat behind him.

The long whiskered ribbonfish roamed through the sea, its speed growing as the two fishmen gradually distanced themselves from Two Eyes Island.

After roughly 3 hours, they arrived at a coral reef on the seabed.

The coral reef on the onset looked nothing special, but inside it, it had been remodeled into a simple and crude camp.

This was one of the Blue Green Coral Fishman Clan’s vanguard camps.

The fishman shaman Xi Dai Zi entered the camp to rest.

The person in charge of the camp was an iron level fishman warrior.

“How is the situation? Are you wounded, your Lordship?” The fishman warrior expressed their concerns.

“Something unexpected happened.” Xi Dai Zi nodded, then detailed what had happened.

The fishman warrior took note of it, he needed to report this to the clan.

“So that’s what happened!”

“A necromancer? It’s good he’s dead. It’s also convenient that he didn’t die by ours too.”

The fishman didn’t have a good opinion of necromancers.

In fact, all races and lifeforms loathed necromancers with disgust and hostility.

“How are you doing with things here?” Xi Dai Zi was concerned about the fishman warrior’s tasks.

The fishman warrior lifted the bone flute in his hand, “I can now play ‘Fish Hometown’ proficiently, every day I can attract a gigantic school of fish together.”

“The Holy City is worthy of being the Holy City, everything from it is good.”

“I now know why Two Eyes Island was locked down. They were searching for a necromancer, so we seized the opportunity to gather many schools of fish without any disturbances!”

“But these schools of fish cannot stay in one place forever, our control of them is breaking.”

Xi Dai Zi nodded, “Then don’t control them, release them and let them form a beast tide!”

“We need them to attack Two Eyes Island, deplete their defenses and supplies ahead of time, and probe their defensive methods.”

“Understood, Lord Shaman!” The fishman warrior immediately accepted the order.

After Xi Dai Zi rested for a short bit, he departed.

Before leaving, the fishman warrior hesitantly asked, “My Lord, is it really feasible to have a human power serve as our insider? After all, they are humans.”

“Don’t worry.” Xi Dai Zi revealed abundant confidence, “As long as they take the coins, it will work.”

“That money is not so easily taken.”

“They are the Devil Gold Coins!”

Upon hearing that secret, the fishman warrior’s eyes suddenly went round with pleasant surprise and cleared misgivings.

Chapter 325: Spurting Smoke Dragon Transformation

Wisteria Store.

Yan Yi was lying on the sales counter, his lightless eyes gazing at the store’s door.

There was barely anyone there.

Inside the store, the salesclerks were also idle and the stocked medicine shelves were of no interest to anyone.

Due to the Green Hat Healing Potion and Sweet Blood Potion, the shore had a great resurgence, but now, things have grown dark again.

The biggest cause was the ruckus that slandered the Green Hat Healing Potion, saying it had serious side effects.

Reputation was always important, especially for a new medicine.

Regardless of whose idea this was, it was a sinister trick equivalent to a serpent striking a vulnerability with a fatal blow.

The salesclerks were depressed and grieved.

They knew there were no issues with the Green Hat Potion.

All of them were speculating whether a great merchant alliance was behind this. Against such excessive methods, one didn’t have any chance to rise.

A salesclerk was unable to hold themselves back and he went to Yan Yi, “Store manager, I heard those two potions were given to you by the elders. Can you not contact them and have them think of something to do?”

“The potions have an expiration date; things can’t continue like this.”

“Right, if we don’t act, the outside will really think our Green Hat Healing Potion has a problem!”

The salesclerk echoed one after the other.

Yan Yi coldly snorted, “Do it yourself!”

He chased away the salesclerks.

Wouldn’t he have contacted them due to such a big matter?

But he got nothing back.

Besides the genuine potions before him, Yan Yi was in a state of misconception, was everything a dream?

The two level seven elders appeared mysteriously and disappeared suddenly.

Originally, he thought they wanted to seize market share; these two potions were part of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance’s leadership’s strategy to use Two Eyes Island as the first step towards their reemergence.

But in the end…was everything ruined?

You won’t budge even after being defamed?

This is but a minor setback!

In Yan Yi’s eyes, there was a way to solve this completely.

Wasn’t it just reputation?

Two Eyes Island had a silver level alchemist; they should seek his endorsement.

If he didn’t want to, they would beseech the feudal lord and work with Hun Tong!

If neither was possible, then there was still one big option left. Cutting the price.

These two potions had a profit margin to spare.

There is plenty of space to discount the price.

If the price was cut, a low cost and quality product, despite rumors of side effects, will generally be used by people taking a risk out of desperation.

As time went on, everyone would find no problems with it.

The rumors about side effects would be discredited.

However, the level seven elders didn’t do anything and took no action.

They just vanished!

Hey, we were just playing around.

Yan Yi had a profound feeling that he had been toyed with.

What happened beforehand?

Why did you toss me these two potions?

The two potions sold so remarkably at the beginning!

It gave people hope, then made them despair.

Yan Yi had a question for the Wisteria Merchant Alliance elders: what are you up to? What do you really want?

Isn’t this conduct a bit crass?

Yan Yi did not know that as a matter of fact, Cang Xu and Zi Di had gone to the shop personally, and wanted to resolve this problem.

But something unexpected happened during it, leading to a series of events beyond their expectations.

Nowadays, Zi Di and Cang Xu temporarily abandoned the medicine business to the back of their minds.

The reason why the two potions were given was to make a profit.

It incidentally helped the Wisteria store too.

50,000 gold coins was a huge sum. With that money, the medicine business problem appeared insignificant.

Cang Xu and Zi Di now have more important things to do.

That was the medicine database!

As long as the medicine database was constructed, they would be able to test their members’ bloodlines.

After bloodline testing, they could use the Bloodcore to absorb the corresponding lifeforms then make use of them with the Bloodline Purification Formation…

When that happens, the survivors will begin to develop rapidly!

Those whose potential and talent are raised will improve and turn the heavens and earth upside down.

With potential and talent, those stagnant will be able to continue cultivating and the shackles that bound the survivors’ limits will completely disappear.

With time, the survivors would become gold levels, saints, and legends!

When that happens, who would dare slander the survivors’ potions?

In the end, this was the world of the strong.

The strong were respected, this was the world’s greatest rule.

Before that was impossible, but with the Bloodcore, with the Bloodline Purification Formation, the survivors had hope.

The dragonman youngster possessed admirable tolerance. He candidly gave the secret of the Bloodcore out, and those that knew about it worked to their limits with the greatest energy and passion they could muster towards the completion of this plan.

Medicine and formations was set aside for magicians to work on.

The dragonman youngster now found himself alone in a ship cabin sizing up a dragon corpse before him.

The dragon corpse was 5 meters long, 4.2 meters wide, and 3 meters tall. The dragon’s feet were shaped similarly to a pterosaur, its dragon skin was red, it didn’t have dragon scales, and its dragon eyes were orange yellow.

This was one of the items Zi Di and Cang Xu had bought ahead of time – a spurting smoke dragon corpse.

The spurting smoke dragon lived near volcanic craters and often lived in large groups. Their life level went from bronze to gold level.

This spurting smoke dragon was silver level.

The spurting smoke dragon’s most prominent exterior feature was the ring mounds across its body. These mounds slightly bulged above the skin and were of unequal height.

Whenever a spurting smoke dragon accelerated in flight or did battle with a powerful enemy, these ring mounds would spurt out dense black smoke with an explosion of splattering flames.

It was precisely because of this trait that scholars named it the spurting smoke dragon.

Why did they purchase this dragon corpse?

The reason was very simple.

According to bloodline relationships, the spurting smoke dragon’s bloodline was descended from the Flame Dragon King.

In other words, the spurting smoke dragon’s ancestor was the Flame Dragon King.

Therefore, by absorbing the spurting smoke dragon, the dragonman youngster could acquire the Flame Dragon King’s bloodline.

Of course, this was just a theory.

Specifically how much could be absorbed depended on many practical factors.

After determining for sure that he was alone and that his surroundings were secure, the dragonman youngster activated the Bloodcore in his heart.

The Bloodcore emitted a red light.

The red light bubbled out and covered the dragonman youngster’s entire body. Soon after, the red light launched into the dragon corpse.

A moment later, the dragon corpse faded into a pile of fine ash.

The red light swelled into a ball, then went back into the dragonman youngster’s body.

The red light increased the bloodlines in the Bloodcore.

Spurting Smoke Dragon bloodline concentration – 35%

Flame Dragon King Bloodline concentration…1.03%!

After the dragonman youngster detected this, he quickly flashed a smile.

Before, his Flame Dragon King bloodline concentration was almost 30%, but now it is above 30%.

1.03% seemed like a low number, but it had actually exceeded the dragonman youngster’s estimations.

He knew that when he absorbed the legend level Flame Dragon King, he only got around 30%, that’s all.

The spurting smoke dragon was only a silver level lifeform. Of course, in light of this, the dragonman youngster now calculated, “Since it had this kind of Flame Dragon King bloodline concentration, that indicates that its potential was very high, it still had room to grow and might have grown into a gold level spurting smoke dragon in the future.”

The increased bloodline concentration caused the dragonman youngster’s height to grow by 5 centimeters and his body to bulk a bit too.

Although his appearance didn’t change too much, the Life Scorching Dragon Breath’s cast time had been reduced by 0.5 seconds.

Before, when the dragonman youngster had trained this racial magic, he had shortened its cast time to 3 seconds.

Now that another 0.5 seconds was gone, the racial magic’s casting time was now 2.5 seconds, and its actual combat value had dramatically risen.

“Hold on, I can also do a hybrid transformation.”

The dragonman youngster’s spirit stirred and the Bloodcore activated again.

Red light flashed across his body, then faded away.

The dragonman youngster’s elbow had a pair of bulging ring mounds.

These two circular mounds were analogous to the ones on the spurting smoke dragon.

The dragonman youngster tried to use these brand new organs.

Very quickly, the temperature inside the ring mounds soared and they began to emit orange red flames. Then, with a crack, a strand of black smoke spurted from them.

The dragonman youngster sensed that hidden within his elbow, there was the power of a restless warhorse ready to stride forth with its great legs and hooves!

The powerful strength in the backside of his elbow was brewing.

The dragonman youngster carefully released this strength, and a moment later, his elbow suddenly swung upwards.

His fist nearly hit his face.

At the same time, his upper arm ferociously smacked his cheek, bruising the flesh slightly.

The ring mounds exploded with such strength that the dragonman youngster couldn’t control it with his first attempt.

The dragonman youngster staggered back, slowly relaxed, lowered his arm, and loosened his muscles and bones.

“Every time the ring mounds are used, they erupt with motive power.”

But this kind of organ structure fits a spurting smoke dragon very well. To use this in human form I need constant trial and error, along with a lot of training to make use of it properly.”

“Wait a minute, can I use the Surge battle skill with the ring mounds as my base?”

The principle behind the Surge battle skill was that by using Bursting Explosion battle qi to produce micro explosions to produce thrust, a fighter could push their body forward.

Nowadays, the dragonman youngster had trained the Surge battle skill to the point where he had perfect command of it, like it was a fundamental instinct to him, and he could direct the intensity of its power as he pleased.

What would happen if the Surge battle skill’s thrust was added to the spurting smoke dragon’s round ring mound organ?

The dragonman youngster’s expectations increased the more he thought about it.

He decided to try it.

A few minutes later.

Bang.

With a loud noise, ship planks were blown up.

The dragonman youngster’s figure crashed outwards and landed into the seawater around the port.

The commotion immediately attracted a circle of onlookers.

“What just happened?”

“Did someone attack us?”

“No, it’s the commander. The Lord Commander fell into the water!”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was shaken.

The dragonman youngster awkwardly swam back up to the surface and climbed back on deck, “No need to panic, I was just trying a new battle skill.”

“Get someone over here.”

“I need to mend a hole in my ship.”

“Understood, Lord Commander!!” Mu Ban loudly responded as he lifted a set of carpenter tools and ran over.

The dragonman youngster returned to the captain room and was about to continue his research when he received urgent news from the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

In the seafloor depths, a massive school of fish was headed towards Two Eyes Island!

An oceanic beast tide was about to roll up on Two Eyes Island.

Chapter 326: Oceanic Beast Tide

“A beast tide?” The dragonman youngster was stupefied.

Every time the rising and falling of Two Eyes Island happened, the surrounding ecosystem would change and be displaced, attracting many predators to a gluttonous feast.

“But to say it is a forming beast tide is somewhat ridiculous.” The dragonman youngster scanned the information, inwardly speechless at the scale of this beast tide.

He was not unfamiliar with beast tides.

On Mystifying Monster Island. He had experienced many.

This was the first one he had experienced after leaving Mystifying Monster Island.

Electric sharks, ghost face fish, noble flying snappers, seafloor mountain snails, narwhales, venomous jellyfish…it really looked like a large exhibition of seabed creatures.

The scale of the school of fish far exceeded any beast tide on Mystifying Monster Island, and although the majority of it was composed of ordinary ocean fish, there were still many magic beasts in it.

There was no magic prohibition domain here, thus the magic beasts could use racial magic.

Therefore, the beast tide was far mightier.

Even the dragonman youngster felt anxious about this information.

“Fortunately, although the beast tide has formed, the schools of fish are scattered and aren’t under a unified command.”

It would have been even worse if a beast king appeared.

“According to the information, this beast tide doesn’t have a gold level magic beast in it yet.”

“Two Eyes Island’s defenses shouldn’t be bad either.”

The dragonman youngster assessed it to himself.

Two Eyes Island’s commerce was flourishing and Hun Tong definitely had money.

Everyone looked up at Two Eyes Island’s defenses. Even during normal times, Two Eyes Island did not have many externally visible defenses.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection abilities still exceeded Two Eyes Island’s. After all, it was War Merchant’s gold level magic ship.

A dozen minutes later, an alarm suddenly rang through Two Eyes Island’s port.

Not long after, a port official ran over to notify, “This is the order directly from Feudal Lord Hun Tong. A beast tide has appeared, he hopes your corps can immediately depart and cull it down!”

This was the mercenary mission given by the feudal lord himself, and the rewards were generous.

The dragonman youngster had no reason to refuse.

Besides the mission, he also wanted to provide support to Zong Ge and the others.

Half the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships had gone out before this. These troops were being led by Zong Ge and were doing training,

Due to the port blockade because of the necromancer search, Zong Ge’s training plan had been delayed by a lot.

At once, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ remaining ships all swung into action and left the harbor at the same time under the dragonman youngster’s leadership.

As they traveled across the ocean, the dragonman youngster noticed that other ships were leaving port too.

Most were large fishing boats, and some were merchant alliance transportation ships.

Leaving aside the fishing boats, merchant alliance transportation ships had decent combat capabilities. After all, voyages were very dangerous these days, with pirates running amuck, one needed battle strength to guarantee the safety of the merchandise.

What most attracted the dragonman youngster’s attention was the two other mercenary corps’ fleets.

On Two Eyes Island, there were two mercenary corps stationed at all times.

One was the Iron Club Mercenary Corps, and the other was the Bandage Mercenary Corps.

To live as a mercenary corps on the ocean, they naturally had their own fleet.

The mercenary corps clearly received the mission and were departing at similar times.

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps was a bit bigger at 14 ships, and their flagship was a dark iron ship. The iron ship’s planks had a half sphere sticking out of every one of them.

The Bandage Mercenary Corps had 12 ships, its flagship was made out of wood, but regardless of whether it was the hull, deck, or mast, all of them had a white bandage tied to it, a very visible characteristic.

Each fleet’s flagship was a bronze level magic ship.

In comparison, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ fleet was a bit smaller and their flagship was of ordinary level.

Anyways, if one wanted to calculate everything in the comparison, the two mercenary corps fleets combined would not even be worth half the Deep Sea Monster Fish.

After the dragonman youngster sized them up for a bit, he returned to his captain’s room.

Amongst the ear-piercing alarms, numerous ships left port and scattered from each other.

The beast tide was coming from all directions, and each ship was headed towards a different front.

After the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps advanced for 20 minutes, they came to their first destination.

The dragonman youngster received Zong Ge’s transmission.

When Zong Ge heard about the beast tide, he decided to fight where he was, and did not plan to return back to converge with the other group. He believed that this was a rare and beneficial opportunity to drill his troops.

“This guy…” The dragonman youngster was a little exasperated.

He trusted the half beastman.

Since Zong Ge acted like this, he must be sure of it.

But as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ foremost leader, he wanted to think things through more.

At once, the dragonman youngster immersed himself in the ocean map on his desk, his finger slowly moving across it.

“The route has to be altered.”

“Deal with this side, then hurry over here, then we will break towards where Zong Ge is.”

“When we reach the third location, we will be close enough. Even if something unexpected happens, we will be able to support Zong Ge’s squad on time.”

Although the Deep Sea Monster Fish had scouted out a lot of intelligence, the beast tide was not completely transparent.

No one could say precisely what was hiding in it.

“A large school of fish ahead, prepare for battle!” The dragonman youngster ordered.

On the deck, mercenaries proficiently pulled sails and restrained rope.

Some mercenaries were on the sides of the ships, preparing fishing nets.

In the hull’s midsection, cannon holes were being opened up.

Three to five mercenaries worked together to push cannons forward so their mouths were sticking out of the hull.

Bai Ya was one of the gunners.

“This is the first time I’ve seen a beast tide in the ocean.” He swallowed his saliva nervously.

Bang, thump, boom.

While smoke puffed from cannon mouths.

After a burst of bombardment, the ocean surface in front of the fleet was mixing with blood.

Countless ocean fish turned on their bellies and floated to the surface. Many severed limbs jumbled in the waves, sinking or floating in the seawater.

The first round of bombardment had scattered the school of fish.

The remaining quarter of the ocean fish were horrified and fled in all directions.

The mercenaries began to throw nets and fish out the spoils of war.

“We will sail in fifteen minutes.” The dragonman youngster commanded.

“But Lord Commander, that isn’t enough time to gather the spoils.” A sailor raised an objection.

The dragonman youngster smiled, “Throw away these fish, there are better things waiting for us.”

He had first-hand intelligence on hand.

He knew there were more valuable schools of fish waiting for him to harvest.

In the early stages of the beast tide, many schools of fish were dispersed about, thus, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps sallied out and routed one after the other.

To the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, this was a profitable hunting opportunity.

Not long after, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps hurried to their second destination.

This school of fish was unlike the previous one.

The water under them was as dark as the night.

Normal cannon shells couldn’t hit them.

But the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had many kinds of alchemy shells in their armory.

The dragonman youngster chose to use sonic shells.

This kind of artillery shell was specialized for shooting into underwater depths. Their original purpose was to target nagas or fishmen. After the shell detonates, a strong sound wave will be produced, capable of destroying the hearing of seafloor lifeforms, vibrating them to death or unconsciousness.

Using the sonic wave shells on deep sea fish felt like using a sledgehammer to crack a nut, the effects were really outstanding.

Countless ocean fish floated up, and for a moment, they filled the ocean surface for several hundred meters in a spectacular sight.

The mercenaries were constantly throwing nets and catching them as best they could.

Large quantities of fish filled their holds.

“We don’t have enough fishing nets.”

“We need more fishing nets.”

“This is only the second location, and our hold is already a third full.”

The mercenaries were happily worried.

“Perhaps we can use alchemy puppets to remodel the ships to have more storage space.” Cang Xu proposed.

“Unnecessary.” Zi Di calculated, “We can return to port early and sell off these fish to clear out the holds before setting out again.”

“When the beast tide strikes, there will be numerous catches.”

“The price of this in the market for these fish will be constantly falling. We want to dump them as soon as possible so we can make the most money.”

“As for afterwards, when the beast tide becomes more compressed and schools of fish crowd around each other and attack Two Eyes Island together, if we set out again, it will be too dangerous.”

Zi Di’s business abilities had long been approved of.

When the dragonman youngster heard this, he immediately adopted her proposal.

Something unexpected happened in the third location.

The school of fish in the information was annihilated by a group of electric sharks.

The original target was gone, replaced by the group of electric sharks.

The beast tide had gone through a violent change.

The electric sharks were undoubtably a bigger challenge.

But at the same time, the spoils of war were more valuable.

Zi Di said, “We need the electric sharks’ electrode vertebrae. We can refine them into scout shark models. To build the medicine database, we also need some electric shark fat and oil.”

“Understood. I will act myself.” The dragonman prepared to fight in light of that.

The electric sharks were in the depths and couldn’t be threatened by ordinary cannons. Even if sonic shells were used, the magic beast sharks would only be tickled by them.

Zi Di tore an underwater breathing scroll and casted it on the dragonman youngster.

Plop.

The dragonman youngster jumped into the water and dived down to face the shark group.

With magic boosting him, he could freely breathe in the water.

“It just so happens I can use actual combat to familiarize myself with my new body structure.” The dragonman youngster thought as he transformed his body.

All around his body, ring mounds appeared.

The spurting smoke dragon transformation was added to the dragonman youngster’s body.

Suddenly, a burst of icy coldness wrapped around him entirely.

The spurting smoke dragon didn’t like water as much as the Flame Dragon King did.

It didn’t have gills and couldn’t breathe underwater. However, if it lacked food, it could act like a seabird and dip into the sea to catch fish in the shallows.

Battle skill – Surge!

With the spurting smoke dragon transformation, the Surge battle skill activated, instantly accelerating the dragonman youngster.

After two seconds, he exploded into the shark group like an artillery shell.

An unlucky electric shark was smashed by him.

All things considered, electric sharks were magic beasts with rough skin and thick flesh, unfortunately, they ran into the dragonman youngster, an inexplicable monster beyond common sense.

The dragonman youngster also felt dizzy when he collided with and killed the iron level electric shark.

The other electric sharks were alarmed and angry, and one after the other, they encircled and attacked the dragonman youngster.

Chapter 327: Electric Sharks and Gun Lobsters

The dragonman youngster was surrounded by at least twenty electric sharks.

As long as an electric shark grew to adulthood, they would become a magic beast.

The strongest electric shark leader was a silver level, the youngster’s equal.

Strands of lightning appeared across the electric sharks’ bodies, and quickly, electric currents condensed into sharp fishbones similar to javelins.

The dragonman youngster found himself in the firing zone but showed no sign of dodging them.

Racial magic – Dragon Prestige.

Dragon Prestige only had a cast time of one second, allowing the racial magic to show its true worth in real combat!

A moment later, a formless undulation engulfed everything, and the electric sharks that bore the brunt of it were broken and scattered by the shockwave.

Of the formerly coherent electric sharks, the vast majority of them became slack, with only a few barely able to shoot.

The electric spears shot out were also very inaccurate, and the dragonman youngster didn’t need to move at all to perfectly avoid them all.

The dragon roar continued to spread through the seawater and even reached the ships.

The mercenaries on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships all felt panic from the bottom of their hearts, and their legs went soft, causing them to kneel on the spot.

The dragonman youngster had dove to the seafloor and was some distance below the ships.

Within the dragon roar’s attack range, it did not distinguish between friend or foe, this was why the dragonman youngster seldom used this battle skill when teams were in combat.

Taking advantage of the swaying electric sharks, the dragonman youngster swung his dragon tail and straightened his back.

With four spurting smoke ring mounds transformed into his back, he used the Surge battle skill to push himself.

The spurting smoke ring mounds produced dense smoke, and within the thick smoke, silver battle qi created micro explosions that formed a brilliant silver blaze.

Every explosion could produce a strong driving force that sharply accelerated the dragonman youngster little by little.

The dragonman youngster rushed out of the electric sharks’ encirclement, smashing through another electric shark in the process.

He barely managed to turn around and sprint back.

In the seawater, he looked like a young knight urging an exceptional warhorse into a charge towards a large group of enemies.

With every charge, he took away the life of another enemy.

But his charge speed was too fast, causing him to appear somewhat clumsy.

Although the electric sharks were ocean magic beasts, they were unexpectedly far slower than the dragonman youngster.

But they had found a way to respond, using their other innate racial magic – Electric Net Shell.

An electric current constantly flowed across their bodies, forming meshes of varying size of blue electric webs on them.

This electric netting combined attack and defense, it could affect the surrounding seawater and paralyze enemies. At the same time, it could also raise their defenses.

The dragonman youngster was pleasantly surprised again and again.

The electric nets restricting his speed, on the contrary, allowed him to more easily familiarize himself with his charge speed.

Using the electric sharks to temper himself was a great gain from actual combat.

He quickly decreased the amount of spurting smoke ring mounds from 4 to 2.

Although his speed dropped by half, his control went from barely to his pinnacle.

At the same time, he combined the Demolition Fist battle skill and a spurting smoke ring mound on his elbow together to astonishing effect!

Every time a fist hit something, it would strike with the sound of thunder and pulverize the unlucky electric shark.

Every fist could explode an electric shark to bits.

This pinnacle feeling of violence caused the dragonman youngster’s smile to appear malevolent, and his heart felt quite refreshed at the sight.

The silver level electric shark leaders were also smashed to death by the dragonman youngster’s fist.

When the leader died, the shark group crumbled, and the few sharks left fled in all directions.

The dragonman youngster chased after them relentlessly.

With his astonishing speed, he caught up and killed one shark after the other.

Even though he endured many Lightning Spears, it didn’t matter.

He had dragon scales and dragon flesh; offense and defense were an integrated whole. They had already been tested in the Snake Rat naval battle, they could withstand Rou Zang’s gold level battle skills, these racial magics were nothing.

Cleaning up the battlefield was a bit troublesome.

Hitting with Demolition Fist was quite satisfying, but the shark corpses were blown to bits and were difficult to gather.

Fortunately, the Deep Sea Monster Fish came to the rescue, and under Zi Di’s control, it quietly let out alchemy puppets.

As the dragonman youngster returned to the ship deck, he took back a gigantic fishing net, inside it were the relatively intact shark corpses.

“The Commander is formidable!”

“All of the silver level electric sharks are dead!”

“The Commander isn’t missing a scale.”

The mercenaries cheered.

This was a good opportunity for the dragonman youngster to raise his prestige and Zi Di didn’t let it slip by.

Sure enough, he gained much adoration, respect, and admiring gazes.

The strength of a person plays an influential role in any organization.

Don’t be fooled by the fact that he was a single person, he could accomplish things impossible for many others.

For example, if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ordinary members had to deal with the shark group, they would be sending them to their deaths.

It would result in total defeat and wouldn’t injure the electric sharks in the slightest.

But when the dragonman youngster deployed alone, he conversely wiped out the entire shark group while he came back unharmed.

This world belonged to the strong.

Through battle strength, one could easily obtain prestige. Even if one’s subordinates died, one could easily get another troop together.

To become a strong cultivator, there was one prerequisite, one’s bloodline.

“He has grown stronger!” Cang Xu was both gratified and delighted.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish had clearly shown him how the dragonman youngster had performed this battle.

Cang Xu was happy from the bottom of his heart.

The dragonman youngster trusted him fully, and likewise, he trusted the dragonman youngster fully.

The experience on Mystifying Monster Island had been their chance to get to know each other and recognize each other’s innate character.

Cang Xu was special to the dragonman youngster. There were many necromancers in the world, and the dragonman youngster was vigilant and fearful of all of them except Cang Xu.

With regards to Cang Xu, the dragonman youngster was the most extraordinary person ever. The world despised necromancers, and only here, at the dragonman youngster’s side, could Cang Xu gain trust and kindness. It was a praiseworthy treasure!

Not only that, but Cang Xu also endlessly admired the dragonman youngster’s frighteningly noble tolerance.

The dragonman youngster took a risk to share the secret of the Bloodcore, which formed an even more tremendous chain of benefits that bound him and Zi Di in inseparable solidarity.

Therefore, every time the dragonman youngster got stronger, Cang Xu would be delighted.

If he was like this, Zi Di would be even more so.

The ghost girl’s heart belonged to the dragonman youngster.

She gazed from afar at the dragonman youngster, her eyes warm and supple as water.

At the same time, she also transmitted to Cang Xu, “It seems that buying the spurting smoke dragon corpse was the right thing to do.”

“He is not like us.”

“He has the Bloodcore.”

“We need to find the corresponding bloodline and build the Bloodline Purification formation, while he can absorb any lifeform.”

“As long as we can buy more in the future, we shall.”

Cang Xu expressed his complete approval, “Zong Ge is unreliable.”

“The Commander is our leader, and our most reliable comrade. The stronger he gets, the better it will be for everyone.”

“Anyways, excessively seeking other lifeform bloodlines is not worth it.”

“First, we need more money to buy them, and even if we use military force to capture and kill them, we engage in the risks of battle.”

“Second, if we excessively pursue that, it will provoke external attention and suspicions, making it harder to hide our identities.”

“Third, the Commander needs to digest and assimilate too. He requires a lot of practice to turn his new abilities into true battle strength.”

“This time, he used real combat to temper himself, and his progress through the fight was very clear.”

The necromancer analyzed with continued calmness.

In this regard, Zi Di was still a distance from him.

The young lady treated the youngster with ardent love. During that, rationality often conceded to emotion.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps set sail once again, and not long after, they successfully converged with Zong Ge’s squad.

Zong Ge was at a sandbar. It was a barren island with no vegetation, it was just a long curved strip of sand and soil.

During high tide, this sandbar would sink.

When the tide receded, this sandbar would peep out of the ocean again.

Zong Ge’s ships were revolving around the beach.

Kuai Tui and the others formed a small formation and had already come ashore to deal with the ocean beasts on the sandbar.

They already formulated a method of advancing or retreating and had cultivated good cooperation and tacit understanding with each other.

Their first opponents were gun lobsters.

These lobsters at the ordinary level could grow up to the height of a person’s knee, while at the bronze level, they could grow up to the height of a person’s waist.

They could grow past that.

The Gun Lobster bloodline foreordained their growth limit to the bronze level.

Their weapon was their biggest claw.

The claw couldn’t open and close on things, instead they blended together.

There was a round hole at the foremost point of the claw, which was the gun lobster’s shooting hole.

When gun lobsters were confronted, they would shoot pellets from these claws. A bronze level lobster leader with a pair of claws was a natural bronze level alchemy firearm.

Anyways, the lobster leader had no way of dealing with Kuai Tui and the others.

Instead, after evading the gunfire, the dogmen would whistle and advance to swing for a few seconds, landing more than a dozen cuts in that time, hacking the lobster leader to death.

After being provided sufficiently good equipment, Kui Yui now headed the young form of a battle squad!

The dragonman youngster observed for a while, exclaiming in admiration for what Zong Ge’s training could do.

“Throw Sweet Blood Potions.” Zong Ge ordered.

Thereupon, Sweet Blood Potion jars were thrown out and crashed into the seawater, attracting even more gun lobsters to shore, creating constant pressure on Kui Tui and his squad.

“That group of beastmen might be a battle squad!”

Because Zong Ge had deliberately tempered them, along with Kuai Tui and the others fighting like this, most of the mercenaries saw some clues of that.

“Our corps actually has a way to organize battle squads?”

“Should be.”

“With a battle squad, our Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is a match for many famous pirate groups and mercenary corps! Neither the Iron Club Mercenary Corps nor the Bandage Mercenary Corps have a battle squad, yet we do.”

For a moment, the mercenaries had more faith in the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ future prospects.

Chapter 328: Helping the Iron Club Mercenary Corps

“Be careful!”

“Turn the rudder left, rudder fully to the left now!!”

“It’s too late, prepare to meet the enemy! Boys, stand by my side and defend the deck.”

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps’ fleet was sinking into a panic.

They had just cleaned up three schools of fish, but halfway en route, they met something unexpected.

A school of ghost face fish broke from hiding and ambushed them.

Ghost face fish looked like flatfish, their bodies were very flat, and when fully grown, their bodies were asymmetrical, their eyes were both on one side, and their mouths were bent. Like flatfish, ghost face fish also were naturally disposed to sitting on the seafloor in perpetuity.

They had a concealment racial magic.

When they were hunting, they would frequently use this innate skill en masse. After “disappearing” from the seafloor, they would flow with the ocean currents to shallow waters and go hunt the more abundant food there.

When prey got close, they would unleash their other racial magic – Soul Absorption.

The prey’s soul would usually be frozen by this racial magic for a short time, allowing the school of ghost face fish to easily nibble them clean.

The ghost face fish school that ambushed the Iron Club Mercenary Corps was silver level. Their leader was a silver level ghost face fish.

When the mercenary corps was enveloped by the soul type magic, the ordinary members fell to the ground rigid, the bronze levels were barely able to stand, and the iron levels were still able to fight.

Even worse, ghost face fish schools usually had ghosts accompanying it.

This involved the ghost face fish’s third racial magic – Soul Summoning.

These ghosts came from the ghost face fish’s prey.

When a soul summoning was successful, the ghosts produced would follow the school of fish.

When the school of fish attacked, the ghosts too would appear to attack simultaneously.

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps’ flagship was a magic ship, its hull was nearly all made of iron, its ship hull was thick and had many semicircle lumps in it. The magic ship’s name represented this trait and was called the Iron Lump.

In the past, the Iron Lump was known for its defense.

But now, against the ghostly assault, this defense was an empty shell.

Three iron level ghosts and twenty four bronze level ghosts penetrated the hull and stormed the hold.

Commander Tie Bang was a silver level fighter, and instantly had to deal with three iron level ghosts.

For a moment, he was surrounded and unable to escape.

The other mercenaries mostly concentrated on the deck in a defensive battle. Because fighting inside the hold was disadvantageous for the sailors.

Humans would be yoked by the narrow space.

The stairs, ship doorways, as well as other installations were not conventional bunkers because the ghosts could penetrate them at will.

Therefore, after the ambush occurred, almost all of the Iron Club Mercenaries ran to the deck.

But the situation on the deck was also far from good.

Not only did they have to face ghosts penetrating from below, but they also had to deal with ghost face fish flying at them.

Ordinary ghost face fish were the size of a carriage. When one leaped, they looked like they were floating and could swoop over the sides of the ship and dive down onto the deck.

Usually when a ghost face fish landed onto the deck, they would open their sinister fish mouth, bite a hapless person, then take advantage of the deck’s friction, jerk and flop away.

If there weren’t that many people on deck, the mercenaries could dodge this.

But now, because the ghosts made the ship hold too detrimental, all of them were concentrated above deck. As long as ghost face fish were diving down, the large swath of mercenaries would be hit, and the unluckiest guy would be dragged into the ocean.

Commander Tie Bang brandished his fists, blocking left and right.

Although the three ghosts he was fighting were only iron level, they could penetrate through obstacles, making the Commander Tie Bang very annoyed.

The Iron Lump’s interior was also mostly made of iron and had a thick and strong defense.

But now, they greatly interfered with Commander Tie Bang, he couldn’t even use his iron club.

As a silver level, his battle qi couldn’t leave his body, he lacked that qualitative change.

“I can’t go to the deck.”

These three iron level ghosts will cause an immense slaughter.”

“I have to entangle them here!”

For a long time, Commander Tie Bang could only fend off the ghosts tearing at him.

The negative energy consequently assailed his body, however, compared to his body, his heart was growing even colder.

Commander Tie Bang fought more recklessly.

“The mercenary corps is in a terrible situation!”

“If my flagship is in a bitter struggle because of the ghost face fish ambush, then the situation on the other ships must be worse.”

“Damn it, even if we drive back these ghost face fish, the mercenary corps will suffer disastrous damage!”

Then at that moment, Commander Tie Bang suddenly heard a subordinate foraying over to report, “Commander, ships are approaching, it is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?” Commander Tie Bang’s eyes lit up, and he blurted out, “Send a distress signal now!”

The mercenaries on deck also saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships.

They shouted loudly, while still being on edge.

“Are they here to save us?”

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were unrelated, they were merely strangers coming together by chance.

In all fairness, if it was within one’s abilities, they would act.

But this was a group of ghost face fish!

Although it wasn’t big, its combination of water path and necromancy was very troublesome.

If they fought, they would likely suffer serious damage.

Unless they had a cleric that could use divine spells on the ghost face fish.

But casters were always precious, clerics even more who usually waited upon the gods inside their churches, who among them would sail with them?

Normal mercenary corps and caravans didn’t have such people.

Without divine spells, it was very difficult to deal with necromancy.

When it came to battle qi, only gold level battle qi and above could leave the body and attack.

Using magic against ghosts wasn’t particularly effective either.

Commander Tie Bang was fighting inside the hold and couldn’t see the situation outside, causing him to worry about personal gains and losses.

“The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps set out earlier.”

“Why are they returning from the front line so soon? Don’t tell me they encountered a formidable fish school, and the damage forced them back to the port to rest and reorganize?”

“I cannot overestimate them too much. After all, they are a newly established mercenary corp.”

“It seems that the key to this bitter struggle is still me!”

Commander Tie Bang tried to steady his mind and did his best to fight on bravely.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ flagship.

“Commander, a battle has been detected ahead, one of the combatants is the Iron Club Mercenary Corps.

“They have sunk into a hard battle against a group of ghost face fish.”

“Their luck is quite terrible.” The subordinate reported.

The dragonman youngster was standing on the side of the ship. He nodded. He had already received the pertinent information from the Deep Sea Monster Fish protecting them from the abyss.

The young man took out a telescope and gazed into the distance.

He first observed where the battlefield was the fiercest.

He didn’t see Commander Tie Bang.

However, the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s detection method included sound, light, and life aura.

Therefore, the dragonman youngster knew that Commander Tie Bang was fighting inside the ship hold. The ghost face fish leader was also concealed near the Iron Lump.

The young man then observed the deck.

A glance could make out that the Iron Club Mercenary Corps had suffered high casualties.

Every time a ghost face fish leaped and dived onto the deck, they frequently crashed into a mercenary. They were quivering, their flat fish bodies were very vigorous, their fish scales were also very tough and ordinary weapons found it difficult to cut into.

“The main thing severely restricting the mercenaries is still the racial magic, Soul Absorption.”

The dragonman youngster changed his attention to the hull.

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps’ ships were scarred greatly, many of the ordinary ships’ hulls were damaged and had been temporarily repaired with planks.

It was very clear that these holes hadn’t been created by this battle.

Before encountering the ghost face fish school, the Iron Club Mercenary Corps had gone through other fierce battles.

On the flagship, Iron Lump, the dragonman youngster found the terrible tool that made those holes.

There were several “cavalier pikes”. These were in fact the spiral horns of narwhals. By its length, it was estimated that the horn belonged to an iron level narwhal.

Narwhal groups weren’t often big, and they usually roamed from place to place with only a few individuals in a group.

The narwhal’s bloodline was a bit more superior.

The highest life level they could reach was the gold level.

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps indeed had gained much.

They had annihilated a narwhal group and captured the narwhal’s most valuable part – their spiral horns.

But that harvest absolutely couldn’t compare to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’!

After the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge converged back together, they eliminated several fish schools.

Their holds were crammed with fish. So much so that many sailor quarters had been cleared out to make more storage space.

There weren’t enough fishing nets, and because there was so many fish that weighed so much, many broke as a result.
