Contrary to Commander Tie Bang’s imagination, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was returning from a rewarding journey and had to empty their holds, because they couldn’t hold any more spoils of war.

This included the Deep Sea Monster Fish on the seafloor, which had stealthily stored a portion of the spoils earlier.

“We have the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s intelligence; it is far more exhaustive than what others have. Not only can it determine the scale and location, and weak points of the beast tide, it can also monitor the real time trend of nearby fish schools, effortlessly seize the occasion in battle, and avoid the strong while attacking the weak.” Zi Di sighed ruefully.

Cang Xu looked at the ghost face fish group, “It seems that the Iron Club Mercenary Corps was ambushed by the ghost face fish school. We won’t. We have detection methods that can find them ahead of time. Right now, I am more concerned about those fish. You know, ghost face fish skin is a top-notch necromancy material.”

The ghost face fish’s name came from its alternating black and gray fish scales that when put together, produced a twisted ghost face.

Although the risks in this ocean beast tide were immense, the gains were likewise immense too.

The dragonman youngster put away his telescope, the distance between the two parties had closed enough.

He firmly transmitted his order, “The other party has already given a distress signal, we will act immediately, no hesitation.”

“Throw the Sweet Blood Potions.”

Complying with the order, the mercenaries took action and began to hurl the potions.

Among the most that performed this task outstandingly was the big guy. He was tall, strong, and was simply a humanoid catapult. Normal people could only throw one jar, while he could throw a bundle.

Chapter 329: Gains

The Sweet Blood Potions washed the seas red when they hit the water.

The potions caused more and more ghost face fish to turn around and abandon the difficult to bite Iron Club Mercenary Corps and instead pour into the blood red sea.

The ocean surface was ceaselessly boiling, the ghost face fish school was strongly stimulated and were crazily tearing and splashing the water.

Most of the ghost face fish withdrew.

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps’ members resolutely took a breather as the pressure on them plummeted.

“Open fire!” The dragonman youngster had been calmly watching, and when he saw that the ghost face fish school was gathered in one place, he shouted the order.

For a moment, the rumbles of explosions didn’t seem to stop.

The ghost face fish were scattered by the artillery shells and blown apart, causing them to screech miserably again and again.

Commander Tie Bang eliminated two ghosts and repelled the third. He didn’t dare to rest for even a moment as he rushed to the deck, stopping on the side of the ship.

He saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps slaughtering the ghost face fish school.

“Isn’t that the Sweet Blood Potion?” Commander Tie Bang’s eyes narrowed.

The first time the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps left port, this potion was subsequently exposed.

Therefore, Commander Tie Bang had known about this potion, but didn’t care about it too much.

Before the Wisteria Shop was slandered, they too had purchased some Green Hat Healing Potions and Sweet Blood Potions, a small contribution to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ collected funds.

Since they were ambushed, they didn’t have a chance to use the Sweet Blood Potion.

Even if they did use them, they didn’t have many potions on hand and wouldn’t be able to replicate what the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was doing.

“A concentrated usage of Sweet Blood Potion has such a strong medicinal effect! When I return to Two Eyes Island, I must buy much more!” Commander Tie Bang made up his mind on the spot.

The war situation when one was active on the battlefield, compared to when one was passive, was entirely different.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had inferred from behind them, exploited the fish school’s lower intelligence, and after a violent bombardment, had secured a narrow victory.

Anyways, the remaining ghost face fish still had their vicious nature stimulated, and one by one, they rushed out of the bloody seawater and charged at the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Cang Xu and Zi Di had been prepared for this.

They superficially chanted, when in reality, they were using the magic rings on their fingers.

Despite the public eye on them, no one noticed any flaws and they really thought these two were fire path magicians.

Fireball spells shot out and blew up flying ghost face fish.

Fire Walls surrounded the hull, and when the ghost face fish took in the flames’ might, they charged at other ships and didn’t dare to jump over the blaze.

Finally, the few remaining ghost face fish chose to flee.

Among them was the only silver level ghost face fish leader.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish was already stealthily moving.

Commander Tie Bang couldn’t help but reveal his envy.

“Oh, that’s right, they have magicians.”

“One iron level, one bronze level.”

“They were just established on Snake Rat Island; they have such great luck for magicians to join them willingly!”

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps didn’t have a single caster.

That was their shortcoming.

As for the Bandage Mercenary Corps, they had one, a mere bronze level. They paled in comparison to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ lineup.

Recruiting magicians was hard.

Because magicians not only had battle strength, but they could produce things too. The latter point was more crucial and important than the former.

Magicians could make potions, magic items, magic scrolls, etc., they could easily provide for themselves. Even if that weren’t their strong suit, a magician’s foundation of scholarly knowledge could allow them to serve as an aide or an adviser to a feudal lord or other nobles. Alternatively, they could also go into management and serve in administrative roles.

Why should one of them join a mercenary corps and experience a drifting existence?

Of course, the Iron Club Mercenary Corps did have some casting capabilities.

They had some alchemy equipment and magic scrolls.

They also had an external magic adviser.

It was a magician, but they were only moonlighting.

“After I return to Two Eyes Island, I’ll make our magic adviser join us.”

“Even if his price is high.”

“Due to the beast tide, my corps urgently needs casters!”

The battlefield was already tranquil when Commander Tie Bang began to ponder.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ people tossed out fishing nets and lowered landing boats to gather the scattered spoils of war.

“That iron level ghost I repelled didn’t trouble the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Commander Tie Bang felt somewhat disgusted.

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps contributed a lot to this battle, and their corps, including the commander, had taken quite the beating, yet in the end, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had easily taken the peach.

But Commander Tie Bang didn’t say anything.

Without their timely assistance, their losses would’ve been greater.

Furthermore, they set up a distress signal and according to the rules, they had to render the other party a reward.

“Assign people to clean up the battlefield. Pay attention to not clash with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.”

Commander Tie Bang issued the order, then asked the helmsman to sail the Iron Lump near the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ flagship.

Upon being welcomed by the dragonman youngster, he climbed onto the other person’s ship.

The two commanders carried out a simple discussion.

“All these fish belong to you.” Commander Tie Bang treated the ghost face fish as the reward.

The dragonman youngster glanced at Zi Di, and with it, he knew what Zi Di wanted.

Mystifying Monster Island had given the two an unusually close tacit understanding.

Of course, Zi D was the best had haggling, but since Commander Tie Bang had come to converse himself, the one who had to act on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ side was the dragonman youngster.

Anyone else doing it would show contempt for Commander Tie Bang.

In terms of apparent strength, even if the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was stronger, they were at the same level, after all neither were gold level.

The dragonman youngster shook his head, “The ghost face fish are necromantic and water path fish, undead path is troublesome, therefore, these fish corpses aren’t worth much.”

Zong Ge couldn’t restrain himself and said to the dragonman youngster, “We need cannons.”

Commander Tie Bang glanced at the half beastman, then nodded to the tall and sturdy human sitting opposite him.

Zong Ge was a silver level like them and was entirely entitled to join the conversation.

The issue Zong Ge brought up was one the dragonman youngster was very familiar with.

Although the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had a gold level cannon, they had no iron level or bronze level ones. Those levels of alchemy cannons were not actually particularly needed.

Because without a transcendent gunner, a compatible magic power reservoir, and other installations, higher level cannons couldn’t be used.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ greatest deficiency was ordinary level cannons.

The dragonman youngster proposed that the Iron Club Mercenary Corps transfer them some cannons, provided they were ordinary level. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would even buy them.

But Commander Tie Bang refused without the slightest hesitation.

With the beast tide imminent, they both needed cannons to handle the beast tide.

The ghost face fish school’s ambush damaged the Iron Club Mercenary Corps greatly. If they traded away their cannons, then their battle strength would plummet. Commander Tie Bang would be braindead to agree to such a suggestion.

After some deliberation, the two corps reached an agreement.

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps’ freshly hunted narwhals would be the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ reward for aiding them.

The most important part of them was their spiral horns, they were very beautiful and looked like cavalier lances.

Commander Tie Bang repeatedly sighed, forced to give them away before he could warm up to them.

After going through an ambush, he still had to take out most of his capital as compensation. When he thought about that, he couldn’t help but have a headache.

Finally, the dragonman youngster and Commander Tie Bang exchanged some information with each other.

The dragonman youngster didn’t divulge his methods too much, because if he did so, then their powerful information scouting ability would be revealed, subsequently increasing the likelihood the Deep Sea Monster Fish would be exposed.

He could only express his worry about the beast tide as much as possible, guess at how big the beast tide would be, and his anxiety about the future.

“No need to be so worried.” Commander Tie Bang didn’t heed the dragonman youngster’s ambiguous warning, and instead comforted the latter, “There have been beast tides in the past, it is very normal.”

“If there wasn’t a necromancer around this year, and if the port wasn’t locked down, we would have noticed this beast tide earlier and have been able to respond better.”

“We have never been the main force that resists the beast tide.”

“The main force is Two Eyes Island’s master and his city guard.”

The dragonman youngster nodded, hiding another misgiving within him.

He thought about Xi Dai Zi and the Vinegar Jar Pirates, suspecting whether this beast tide had fishmen playing tricks behind the scenes.

It was only a possibility, and he couldn’t warn an outsider like Commander Tie Bang of it.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had already informed Hun Tong of the Vinegar Jar Pirates’ attempted invasion of Two Eyes Island. After Hun Tong learned of it, he and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps signed a magic confidentiality agreement, which stated that both sides would guard this secret tightly and not allow any leaks of it whatsoever.

To the dragonman youngster, it seemed that this was done because Hun Tong chose to temporarily maintain secrecy so that the auction wouldn’t be negatively affected.

Both parties left, and the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps returned from their rewarding journey.

They were the first fleet to return to port.

When they entered the harbor, an official came over to ask about their situation. Soon after, Thief Guild and other information merchants came to purchase first-hand information from the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps began to unload cargo.

Temporary workers were convened from large groups of fishermen and laborers.

Everyone that saw the massive harvest of fish were stupefied.

What happened to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps?

Was there really a beast tide?

Don’t tell me they ransacked a fish den?

The dragonman youngster left some people behind at the port, and with Fei She in charge, they were responsible for linking up with the marketplace and selling off these fish.

After doing some ship repairs at port, he and his ships all left the harbor once again.

If they hurried up, they could catch some more.

Good chances like this came seldom.

With the Deep Sea Monster Fish providing information, they could stay clear of the main force and strike at weak points, collecting the fruits of victory.

After several days, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was the harbor’s star.

Every haul they brought back was their maximum capacity.

The profits were gigantic and made many people jealous.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ information gathering abilities were unavoidably revealed.

To deal with this matter, after the dragonman youngster and the others discussed, they deliberately tossed out some alchemy scouting puppets to resolve the suspicions the other powers had of them.

The same day that was revealed, Hun Tong dispatched a trusted aide to board the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ship and discuss arrangements of purchasing the alchemy puppets.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps selectively sold a few to Hun Tong alone.

These alchemy puppets had been modified somewhat and there are already similar types to them in the marketplace. The risk of selling them was minimal to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

As for why the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had these alchemy puppets?

Everyone independently recalled their background.

Arms smugglers.

Thus, this was all reasonable.

Chapter 330: Handling the Sweet Bood Potions

Inside the Wisteria Shop, the salesclerks were awfully busy.

There were many customers coming through.

After a long time, a salesclerk excitedly raised the account book and showed it off to Yan Yi, “For the last several days, our potion sales have been very good, the estimated market demand still remains high. How much is still in stock? If there is not much, shouldn’t we raise the price?”

Yain Yi looked at the account book.

The listed data had already made plain the immense change that occurred several days ago.

It was all because of the beast tide.

Yan Yi clearly remembered the first evening when the alarm was raised. The Iron Club Mercenary Corps had run into the store and purchased a lot of Sweet Blood Potions.

It was like a watershed moment, and from that purchase, more and more people came to buy the Sweet Blood Potions. Even now, customers were still streaming in endlessly.

No one knew that the Sweet Blood Potion would be so useful.

If one encountered a powerful fish school, as long as they weren’t like the ghost face fish school, as long as people tossed out some Sweet Blood Potion, they could attract the fish school elsewhere in the sea and allow their ships to easily escape.

When they encountered other fish schools, they could pour out Sweet Blood Potions and gather the fish school at the surface. Then, they could bombard them continuously with cannons. Although the cost of shells wasn’t low, when the mercenary corps put it into practice, they found that their hunting efficiency had enormously improved, and although their usage of gunpower and artillery shells increased, at the same time, they saved a lot on manpower, physical resources, and time, as well as reducing the battle risks. The harm to squads was lowered, battle strength was preserved, and additional compensation payment didn’t need to be paid out.

The Sweet Blood Potion really was sweet!

In the account book in Yan Yi’s hand, the Sweet Blood Potions’ sales had already exceeded its section and became the Wisteria Store’s new hot item.

The other section was the Green Hat Healing Potion, still affected by the prior disturbance.

“Don’t worry about stock, our warehouse has ample reserves.” Yan Yi’s eyes tore from the account book and back towards the salesclerk.

The day the beast tide alarm rang, Zi Di and Cang Xu had mysteriously appeared again and privately met with Yan Yi to replenish his merchandise.

In the days after, they would appear nearly every day to replenish the potion stock.

The Sweet Blood Potion was 90% of the replenishment, while the Green Hat Potion was the remaining percentage.

Yan Yi continued to speak, “The Sweet Blood Potion sells great because a relatively rare beast tide has broken out and because there is a gap in the market. The market here lacks something like the Sweet Blood Potion.”

“Therefore, we cannot raise the price. Next year, no, as long as some time passes, a competing product with the same effect as the Sweet Blood Potion will appear in Two Eyes Island’s market. Don’t underestimate our rivals, the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance is a colossus, they are one of humanity’s six great merchant alliances!”

“If we raise the price, we will only profit and benefit in the short term but create a negative effect on our buyers. If we were the only ones with the Sweet Blood Potion, then this wouldn’t be a problem. But in the future, when rival products hit the market, our current market share will be attacked, rapidly reduced, and pushed aside by the rival products.”

“Sweet Blood Potion…this will be our main product from now on. You know we have to take the long view, right?”

“Understood, my Lord.” After the salesclerk received instruction, he altered his opinion and went back to work with satisfaction and hope.

These last few days, the salesclerks worked with surging enthusiasm.

The Wisteria Shop’s business was flourishing, their income improved, and their future looked more beautiful and hopeful.

Yan Yi thought of Zi Di and Cang Xu, the two level seven elders.

What he just said was not his idea, rather it was Zi Di’s urging that made him rigorously work without the slightest slip-up.

Yan Yi expressed his opinion of the Green Hat Healing Potion to the two elders.

He wanted to contact Hun Tong’s subordinate, the silver level alchemy master and have him analyze the potion so that he could inform everyone the healing potion had no major side effects. Then the prior disturbance would be slandered back.

But Zi Di refused.

This confused Yan Yi.

“We have been framed; the Green Hat Healing Potion is an excellent potion. Since we can reduce the negative effects of the disturbance, why don’t we do so?”

Zi Di saw Yan Yi’s misgivings but didn’t explain them away.

To Yan Yi it seemed that the two level seven elders seemed to be scared of contacting Hun Tong and other people, and it also seemed that they never took the trivial matter of the potion slandering to heart at all.

“Anyways, if this continues, as long as the Sweet Blood Potion sales continue, our new island store position can be saved.”

The Wisteria Shop’s new island position was impressive, and the Flint Merchant Alliance once dispatched their manager to buy it in advance.

At the time, the manager was very haughty.

But now, with the Wisteria Store’s sales figures becoming considerable and greatly making up for their prior gaps, he now had hope he could save his store location.

The Sweet Blood Potion was a hot item and had already caught the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance’s attention.

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance’s manager had valued the two potions and had once asked the merchant alliance’s pharmacist to determine a part of the potions’ recipes.

“The Wisteria Store hasn’t raised the Sweet Blood Potion’s price; it seems their supply of goods is both stable and sufficient.”

“Strange, they don’t have a medicine workshop.”

“That would be terrible.”

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance manager was gloomy.

According to the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance’s rules, every marketplace situation must be reported to the higher ups at the end of the month.

When the higherups saw the situation here, they would think the manager was neglecting their duty for not noticing the gap in the market there and allowing a competitor’s hand to fill the gap.

Therefore, on the third day the Sweet Blood Potion was wantonly sold, the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance manager appealed to the higherups: he hoped that he could acquire stock of a magic potion similar to the Sweet Blood Potion so he could fill in the gap in Two Eyes Island’s medicine marketplace.

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance had a huge volume of medicinal products and recipes. Picking one out from within that and producing more of them was not challenging.

Therefore, in the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance manager’s opinion, he had long prepared to utilize this potion to attack the Sweet Blood Potion’s market share it had for the last few days.

But looking at it currently, the Wisteria Store acted steadily, it didn’t raise the price, and it didn’t have any flaws.

If this continued, when he attacked the market in the future, it would be relatively difficult.

If the rival product didn’t suppress the Sweet Blood Potion, then when his merit was evaluated, it would decline again.

The Secret Medicine branch manager didn’t want to see this happen.

“I must think of something else I can do.”

Naturally, the Secret Medicine branch manager thought of the Flint Merchant Alliance.

“The Flint branch wanted to buy the Wisteria Store’s new island store location, but now that purchase plan has perhaps come to a premature end. The Wisteria Store can rely on the Sweet Blood Potion to save their store location and won’t offer it for sale again.”

“Previously someone caused trouble, leading to the Green Hat Healing Potion’s sales plummeting.”

“If the Sweet Blood Potion has a mishap, then there is hope for the Flint branch’s purchase to go through. In that aspect, my and their benefits are in alignment.”

The Secret Medicine branch manager went to the Flint branch, and during their discussion, the two managers discovered that both intended on collaborating together.

They clicked together on the spot.

The Flint branch manager vocalized his thoughts, “Perhaps we can find people to continue causing trouble. Didn’t you guys do a good job with the Green Hat Healing Potion previously?”

The Secret Medicine branch manager shook his head and denied, “That wasn’t my doing.”

The Flint branch manager laughed mischievously and didn’t believe the Secret Medicine branch manager’s words. Swapping places, if he had done that, of course he wouldn’t say he did, such clear proof would increase the information that could be used against him.

The Flint branch manager continued on, “We can continue doing that. The last disturbance’s outcome was very ideal, and to date, the Green Hat Healing Potion’s sales volume hasn’t improved.”

When the Secret Medicine branch manager heard the other person faintly praising him, he couldn’t help but force a smile, “I really didn’t do it. The method was really effective, but also not impossible to resist. It’s very odd that the Wisteria Shop didn’t counterattack in any way.”

“It is a little odd.” The Flint branch manager nodded, “Anyways think about Yan Yi, he is too young and his abilities aren’t too high, perhaps he didn’t think of any.”

The Secret Medicine branch manager shook his head, “Even if he didn’t think of anything, the two mysterious level seven elders most certainly would have.”

“Perhaps this time when we act, the two level seven elders will respond.”

The Flint branch manager’s brows pursed, they had obtained information of the two level seven elders, but they didn’t understand much about what they were up to.

Ever since they appeared, the two level seven Wisteria Merchant Alliance elders had been a mystery, they came and went without a trace, and they couldn’t make sense of what they wanted to accomplish, it just so happened that the two potions they tossed onto the market had a big impact.

“If we want to bring false charges against the Sweet Blood Potion, it will be far more difficult than the Green Hat Healing Potion.” The Flint branch manager sighed. The two level seven elders were a psychological pressure on him.

The Flint branch manager’s words made sense.

The Green Hat Healing Potion was consumed by people, while the Sweet Blood Potion was not.

Buyers would undoubtably be more relaxed when using the latter than the former.

At the same time, the Sweet Blood Potion’s effect was obvious to all, and was very difficult to discredit.

Anyways, concerning this point, the Secret Medicine branch manager already had a plan.

He stated, “Our merchant alliance’s magic potion is coming soon.”

“I don’t believe my merchandise stock is smaller than the Wisteria Store’s!”

“Let me display my strength and discipline in competition.”

“You want to undercut them?” The Flint branch manager suspiciously asked.

“No.” The Secret Medicine manager shook his head.

He had already asked someone to calculate the Sweet Blood Potion’s recipe. Although only some medicinal ingredients were identified, it was sufficient enough for him to budget its manufacturing cost.

The Sweet Blood Potion’s recipe was utterly superior, and its production cost wasn’t high.

As expected of one of War Merchant’s products, the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance couldn’t pull out a better magic potion recipe to it. Of course, the required production cost was similar.

If the Secret Medicine branch manager chose to cut the price to attack the marketplace, it would indeed rock the Sweet Blood Potion’s position. But if that happened, he would undoubtedly make a loss, and in the long term, it would greatly damage the merchant alliance’s higherups’ evaluation of the manager.

Chapter 331: Four Kinds of Wine

“I will adopt the same price. But unlike the Wisteria Store, their connections cannot compare to ours. Who are we? We are of the six great merchant alliances, what is his Wisteria Merchant Alliance to us?” The Secret Medicine branch manager stated.

“I understand what you mean.” The Flint branch manager immediately understood and expressed his active cooperation.

Since the six great merchant alliances were all the top dogs in the Sheng Ming Empire, and also because their business areas did not conflict, this was not the first or second time these two had worked together.

“Do we need to unite the other merchant alliances?” The Flint branch manager asked.

“No need. With our combined efforts, why are we afraid of dealing with a minor Wisteria Store?” The Secret Medicine branch manager declined.

The Flint branch manager loftily shrugged his shoulders and approved of the other’s decision.

The Secret Branch manager’s plan was very simple, there were other ways of attacking the market besides slandering the other party’s potion or cutting the price.

Wouldn’t a more direct method be to stop buyers from using the Sweet Blood Potion?

In light of the six great merchant alliances’ influence and strength, achieving this on Two Eyes Island was not difficult.

The most important target was the city guard.

Currently, the Sweet Blood Potion’s biggest consumer was Feudal Lord Hun Tong’s troops. With One Eye heading the patrol fleet, they were the ones who fought the hardest to hold back the beast tide.

The Secret Medicine branch manager found the military supply official himself.

He proceeded to bribe them with a large sum.

The military supply official also saw the Secret Medicine branch’s magic potion sample and found that it indeed had a nearly identical effect.

The Secret Medicine branch manager quickly accomplished his first objective.

He and the Flint branch manager then acted together.

The second objective had three targets, Two Eyes Island’s three mercenary corps.

Besides the city guard, these three were the biggest users of the Sweet Blood Potion.

Compared to the city guard, dealing with the mercenary corps was even easier.

First the easy, then the hard.

The Secret Medicine and Flint branch managers found Commander Tie Bang and Beng Dai and discussed the matter of the magic potion with them.

Beng Dai and Tei Bang of course had collaborated with the island’s merchant alliances many times.

In fact, of the missions in the island’s Mercenary Guild branch, the five great merchant alliances’ mission rewards were the most generous.

In normal times, the two mercenary corps also emphasized protecting their relationship with the merchant alliances. The five great merchant alliances had an intense employment demand, and the mercenary corps were their big long term clients.

The negotiations went very smoothly.

The Bandage and Iron Club Mercenary Corps quickly agreed to change their potions and no longer use the Sweet Blood Potion.

The two managers also asked if the two commanders could join them to discuss this with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Commander Beng Dai’s tone was somewhat complex, “The one most in the limelight on Two Eyes Island is the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. Speaking of which, when I conversed with Commander Long Fu for the first time, I knew that dragonman was quite remarkable!”

Commander Tie Bang also agreed, “That mercenary corps’ intelligence gathering abilities is very strong, that is why during the beast tide’s early stage, they were like a fish in water. It seems they were also the first to use large quantities of Sweet Blood Potion. I fear that their usage amount of the Sweet Blood Potion exceeds both of ours combined.”

The two managers got the hidden meaning in the two commanders’ words.

It wouldn’t be simple to reach a deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

After all, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was a newcomer.

The two branch managers knew the Iron Club and Bandage Mercenary Corps inside and out, however, they didn’t have such a clear understanding of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Although they knew a lot about them, such as the fact that Long Fu and Shi Qi were the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ strongest people, the corps had two fire path magicians, one iron and one bronze, this information was just about surface level things.

The two branch managers especially cared about one thing, that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ original identity should be arms smugglers.

Concerning this public image, the survivors had begun to craft it on Snake Rat Island.

When they arrived at Two Eyes Island, they were no longer groundless accusations, instead they became the tacit consensus others had of them.

As far as that kind of identity was concerned, Hun Tong had some misgivings about it, while the two branch managers were even more fearful of it.

When dealing with arms smugglers, the two branch managers had to be gentler, they couldn’t be too tough.

Anyways, from the beginning, they never planned on being tough.

Merchants tastefully believed that amiability made one rich. If one could solve things peacefully, why use violence?

Taking advantage of the time of rest at night, the managers and commanders went together.

They were the local bullies here, and effortlessly scouted out that the dragonman youngster was drinking in a tavern right now.

When they arrived at the tavern’s doorway, the four heard the clamor inside, there was music, clinking wine cups, and hearty laughter.

There were two Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps members outside the door on guard duty.

“Did your commander reserve all the seats today?” Commander Beng Dai asked.

When the mercenaries saw the four silver level lords, they promptly replied, “Yes, your Lordship. Today, everyone inside drinks for free, our commander will foot the bill.”

Commander Beng Dai and Tei Bang were both envious.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were the biggest beneficiary of the beast tide.

This was something the entire port, no, all of Two Eyes Island knew.

“We want to see Commander Long Fu, please announce it for us.” Said the Secret Medicine branch manager.

A guard immediately went in to report.

The other guard remained at his post at the tavern door, and he didn’t relax whatsoever. Even though he was only an ordinary person and couldn’t stop the four people in front of him whatsoever.

The guard who went to report soon returned, “The commander invites your Lordships inside to have a detailed talk.”

The tavern door opened, and when the four people entered, the passionate celebratory atmosphere immediately hit them.

The tavern was the biggest one on the port, but because of how many people were inside, it seemed crowded.

But no matter how many people there were, none could match the dragonman youngster’s elegant bearing.

His physique and appearance attracted many gazes.

The first thing the four noticed was that the dragonman youngster was at the front of the stage, with a magician by his side.

The latter had thick long black hair, and seemed fresh out of the shower, for their hair was still moist. He had heavily puffy eyes and his pupils were purple.

“That is the card magician, Shi Fa.” The four immediately recognized who the magician was.

Shi Fa was a silver level magician, and well known across Two Eyes Island.

His bloodline was quite rare and peculiar, every day, moisture would secrete out of him and flow onto his hair.

Therefore, his hair had always been damped year in, and year out.

Shi Fa was a magician but was also fond of wine.

This interest seemed incompatible with being a magician, but Shi Fa held himself together well.

Shi Fa made magic scrolls and possessed unusual mastery at doing it. He mastered an ancient scroll manufacturing technique and could concentrate magic scrolls into the shape and side of a playing card.

Every playing card could be considered to be a small magic scroll, and although the might was smaller than normal magic scrolls, the casting speed was faster.

When Shi Fa fought,,, besides routine chant casting, he also used his stashed playing cards to unleash high frequency and speed attacks., creating a wave of magic attacks in a short time.

Shi Fa’s battle strength was quite powerful, especially in terms of bursting ability.

The four headed towards the counter, the mercenaries along the way automatically letting them pass.

When the dragonman youngster saw them, he nodded to them, then turned around to look back at the magician Shi Fa to continue his discussion with the latter.

Commander Tie Bang had keen hearing, and what he heard caused his heart to skip.

Shi Fa was a silver level magician, if he joined the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, then the latter would have three silver levels. More crucially, one of them was a caster.

If that happened, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ strength would undoubtedly be greatly increased and surpass both the Iron Club and Bandage Mercenary Corps by a distinct margin.

Shi Fa slowly shook his head, “Compared to joining you guys, I much rather be a free man by myself.”

Shi Fa was a hedge magician, a loner.

Hun Tong once actually tried to recruit him, but he was rejected.

When Commander Tie Bang heard this, he relaxed, somewhat happy to see the dragonman youngster kitting a wall.

Commander Beng Dai besides him was the same.

Contrary to them, the two branch managers didn’t have strong feelings for the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

When the dragonman younger was refused publicly, he laughed out, “Then we shall continue drinking wine.”

Shi Fa laughed too, “Commander Long Fu, you are different. Although I’ve only interacted with you for a few days, you have a commendable demeanor. Actually, it isn’t impossible for me to join your corps, as long as I get enough wine to drink.”

The dragonman youngster blankly stared.

Tie Bang and Beng Dai both slowed their steps somewhat, their hearts somewhat bewildered.

“What’s going on?”

“Did that come out of Shi Fa’s mouth?”

“Why were you so firm and candid when you refused me?”

“Where is your personal integrity?”

“What’s so good about Long Fu? He isn’t even a human!”

Shi Fa continued, “You know I love to drink, Commander Long Fu.”

“If you can find Spear Wine, Artemisia Wine, Spider Red, and Spring Wine, those four types, I will join you, how about it?” Shi Fa laughed.

Commander Tie Bang and Beng Dai glanced at each other and let out a breath.

Although Shi Fa’s demand seemed very simple, it was anything but.

Of the four wines that were raised, the two commanders only knew of Artemisia Wine and Spring Wine.

Artemisia Wine sounded like a commoner drink (cider), but in actuality, it was a famous elven wine that was brewed with the fruits of magic plants.

Spring Wine was a legend.

Legend said that in some corner of the ocean, there was a spring on an uninhabited island. The spring dried up, and only when the sun and the moon appeared in the sky at the same time, did the spring flow with water. The water for Spring Wine was the world’s rarest liquor, and after drinking it, one would forget all of their worries.

Commander Tie Bang and Beng Dai knew Shi Fa was joking with Long Fu, however, the dragonman youngster nevertheless earnestly listened and replied, “I have never heard of those four wines. But I will be mindful, and if I gather them in the future, I will give them to you.”

It was Shi Fa’s turn to stare blankly, but soon after he burst into laughter, “Then it’s a deal, Commander Long Fu!”

Chapter 332: Cannon Transaction Part 1

Two Eyes Island’s scale was incomparable to Snake Rat Island. Before the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps arrived, there were 27 silver levels on the island. Of these silver levels, there was no shortage of strong ones. Such as the city guard commander, some merchant alliance managers, and don’t even mention the Wealth God priest.

These people could not be recruited.

Of the silver level cultivators, there were a few free agents, and of them, three were the most renowned.

They were the wandering ronin Ku Feng, the treasure hunter Hong Zhu, and the card magician Shi Fa.

The dragonman youngster had spent the last few days contacting these three.

When the beast tide appeared, the Mercenary Guild branch immediately issued missions. These beast tide confrontation missions had considerable rewards, because the feudal lord and priest were both backing and pushing for them.

The rewards moved everyone, and the silver level cultivators were no exception.

For one, eliminating the beast tide would protect their safety and benefits.

If the beast tide was allowed to spread, and wasn’t stopped, Two Eyes Island’s auction was assured to fall through.

It wasn’t known how many tons of people were awaiting this auction.

Almost everyone was acting around the beast tide.

Ku Feng, Hong Zhu, and Shi Fa didn’t have ships, and to eradicate the ocean beast tide, they had to join a ship.

Since that is so, their options for that were few.

The first option was that these free cultivators join up with each other and charter a ship for sailing. This option had many systemic problems, such as if they had a lack of qualified and well-coordinated sailors, they would find it difficult to control the ship and even be each other’s enemy during the voyage.

The second option was to join the city guard and patrol fleet. But these organizations were not easy to join, and they wouldn’t rashly admit outsiders into their ranks.

Two Eyes Island was a commerce island, not a fishery island and didn’t have large ocean fishing fleets.

The third option was choosing a merchant alliance fleet.

The final option was choosing a mercenary corps.

Unlike mercenary corps, merchant alliance fleets emphasized the transportation and protection of merchandise.

Therefore, Shi Fa and the others were inclined towards joining a mercenary corps.

Mercenary corps were also not the city guard and were more open to outsiders. Even in normal times, many silver level cultivators were external members of the mercenary corps.

Since mercenary corps were the choice, then the three most precedented options were Two Eyes Island’s three biggest mercenary corps.

The Bandage and Iron Club Mercenary Corps were deeply ingrained in the hearts of the people.

But after the beast tide came, the most in the limelight was nevertheless the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Every time the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps returned to port; they returned with full holds. Perceptive people could see that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was powerful, especially their information gathering abilities. They could determine where the beast tide’s weak points were and powerfully struck there.

Which mercenary corps would be more appropriate to join?

Therefore, as if by prior agreement, Ku Feng, Shi Fa, Hong Zhu, and others chose to depart on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships.

During the fights against the beast tide, they would contribute their power in exchange for a profitable mission commission.

The dragonman youngster enthusiastically welcomed these people.

For the last few days, he tried his best to rope them in and enhance his relationship with them.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was always expanding.

Regardless of whether it was the dragonman youngster or Zong Ge, both were recruiting new manpower and strengthening themselves.

However, with respect for ordinary personnel, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had reached its limit, and it was inappropriate to expand that aspect.

Because expanding that aspect wasn’t profitable enough.

Besides increasing supply consumption, it wouldn’t improve the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ strength by much.

Before on Snake Rat Island, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had press-ganged many pirate captives. Not enough time had passed, and these people hadn’t been completely converted to their side yet, while at the same time, they had a large equipment deficiency.

The most serious one was cannons.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps lacked ordinary level cannons.

There were many deficiencies.

Many ships’ cannon holes were empty, and if they encountered a normal fleet, in respect to their long range firepower, they would be found lacking.

There were also deficiencies with their ships.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ ships (not including the Deep Sea Monster Fish, because it couldn’t be treated as a conventional weapon) were all ordinary ships, they needed a magic ship.

Only with a magic ship could hold their position in fierce naval battles.

But although Two Eyes Island’s trade was booming, it didn’t have a shipyard, a large harbor, or sell cannons.

Of the bronze level and iron level cultivators, Lan Zao, Tripleblade, and others received equipment, became well-known figures among them, stabilized their positions, and helped the survivors control the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

But in truth, it was still tenuous at best.

Before the lower level cultivators truly acknowledged the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, the dragonman youngster didn’t dare recruit more lower level cultivators to the corps. For it would destabilize the survivors’ control of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ lower ranks.

Expanding in those two aspects was out of the question, but they could still attract silver levels.

This was the world of the strong.

One bronze was better than 10 ordinary people. An iron level was usually as strong as a hundred people, and a silver level’s battle strength could shake off several iron levels. So long as one had abundant battle qi, a thousand people couldn’t stop a silver level.

As far as the survivors were concerned, the requirements for recruiting a strong silver level were relatively ripe.

First of all, they had an open identity – The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Secondly, they had abundant capital for the time being. For example, to solicit Shi Fa, a magician, the team had to be able to satisfy his many resource requirements. Currently, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ material storehouse is very full.

Once again, the survivors had strong people. Although the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge were fighters, they were powerful silvers, especially the dragonman youngster who had firmly fought Rou Zang during the Snake Rat naval battle. With this kind of military success, in all honesty, one could completely command other silver levels.

Cang Xu too was in favor of recruiting other silver level cultivators. He believed that it could hedge the risks in response to Zong Ge going semi-independent.

Finally, there was one other condition, a well hidden one, it concerned the Bloodcore.

Conventional organizations would find it difficult to admit outsiders. Especially cultivators.

Why? Because it involved power, position, and the distribution of benefits.

To bring in a strong person, one had to give them resources and a place.

The internal organizational framework was already fixed.

Under normal circumstances, if a corps like the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, who had an iron level magician, went out to attract a silver level magician, the iron level magician would most positively be dissatisfied. Unless there was immense external pressure demanding the urgent need to improve team battle strength.

But Cang Xu was quite happy to welcome them in.

It wasn’t because he was selfless. In fact, the necromancers were well acquainted with the path of power. Even when he faced the gold level Hun Tong, he still argued for what was his and gained a great profit from it.

The reason he was welcoming rested with the fact that it wouldn’t jeopardize his most central benefit.

The Bloodcore.

Or in other words, the method of bloodline alteration.

If he could improve his bloodline, he would completely change his future.

Any outsider joining the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps would never touch this core benefit.

Perhaps in the future they would, but only after their loyalty was validated.

By the time that happened, Cang Xu and Zi Di would have already altered the bloodlines, raised their strength substantially, and not fear that this core power was being spread.

With all of this combined, the dragonman youngster decided to brazenly solicit silver level cultivators.

Not just Shi Fa, but also Ku Feng, Hong Zhu, and others.

However, none of these people accepted the dragonman youngster’s offer.

They had always been free agents, accustomed to their liberty and liked it that way, or perhaps for another reason.

The dragonman youngster’s offering did not move them.

The one hardest to attract would undoubtably be Shi Fa.

He was a magician!

But of all that were given an invitation, he was nevertheless the only one who didn’t refuse on the spot.

The failed solicitations were something the dragonman youngster had prepared for. He wasn’t frustrated at all as he secretly memorized the four wines then turned to look at the four people coming towards him.

Commander Beng Dai and Tie Bang, as well as the Secret Medicine and Flint Merchant Alliance managers.

The dragonman youngster was somewhat surprised to see them here. He guessed as to why.

“Hello, Commander Long Fu, can we speak with you alone?”

“We can go upstairs.” The dragonman youngster nodded and led the four to the upper floor.

The first floor of the tavern had many eyes mixed in and wasn’t a good place for talks.

When they reached the room on the second floor, the four exposed their purpose in coming.

That also validated the dragonman youngster’s conjecture.

But outwardly, he revealed his astonishment and frowned with a sigh, “You don’t want my corps to continue using the Sweet Blood Potion?”

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance manager nodded, “Our merchant alliance can supply a perfect alternative.”

The Flint branch manager then took over, “It’ll be offered free of charge, it won’t cost your corps a cent.”

“Free?” The dragonman youngster mulled it over for a bit with a weird expression, “Will it always be free?”

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance manager awkwardly laughed, “Only for a time.”

“So, you’ve come to ask for my help? Am I understanding that right?” The dragonman youngster leaned back on his chair in a calm manner.

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance manager deliberated for a bit, then said, “Indeed. We have come in good faith and hope we can work together. If we can reach a collaboration, then we will owe your corps a favor. You know, we are two of the six great merchant alliances.”

“That’s not quite accurate.” The dragonman youngster shook his head, “If I help you, you guys will owe me a favor, not the Flint or Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance. The difference between the two is quite vast.”

The two merchant alliances managers glanced at each other, both equally perceiving the dragonman youngster’s unreasonableness.

They simply didn’t know of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ intrinsic connection with the Wisteria Store.

To find trouble with the Wisteria Store, they had to ask the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps for a favor.

Wasn’t this like seeking the help of the victim’s family to deal with the victim?

If they knew the truth, they would have considered themselves thoroughly stupid.

When they saw the discussion was becoming difficult, Commander Tie Bang and Beng Dai tried to offer their assistance. They used their own experience to tell the dragonman youngster that cooperating with the two great merchant alliances and maintaining a good relationship with them would always benefit the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“Of course, if the Wisteria Store works with your corps, then pretend we were never here.” Finally, Commander Beng Dai seemed to somewhat probe what was really going on with the dragonman youngster.

The dragonman youngster shook his head and denied it, “My corps just came to Two Eyes Island, how can I have a relationship with them? Commander Beng Dai, you should know when I began to use these two potions. It was at the Mercenary Guild branch; you saw it with your own eyes.”

Commander Beng Dai nodded.

With him bearing witness, the two managers’ already sparse suspicions lessened to nothing.

Chapter 333: Cannon Transaction Part 2

The dragonman youngster denied they had a relationship with the Wisteria Store.

It was undoubtably wise.

Before they redeemed themselves, they had to reduce the connection between them and their past relations to prevent exposing flaws and causing people to investigate them.

This clever arrangement was already working.

The two merchant alliance managers didn’t detect the truth and had unexpectedly sought the dragonman youngster to deal with the Wisteria Store.

This had allowed the dragonman youngster to firmly hold the initiative from the beginning of these talks.

The dragonman youngster said, “The Wisteria Merchant Alliance cannot compare to the six great merchant alliances, let alone two managers joining hands.”

“Since you’ve found me and have also convinced the other two commanders, you must be determined to win.”

“But as far as I know, the city guard is the biggest consumer of the Sweet Blood Potion. But I believe that since you are speaking to me right now, you have already resolved that problem.”

“Therefore, I am the last one left.”

“Am I to believe that if you don’t reach an agreement with me, your strenuous efforts will be in vain? Won’t that mean you’re running the enterprise for the sake of one basketful?”

The two managers glanced at each other, their features very grave.

They felt somewhat regretful and blunderful. By bringing Commander Beng Dai and Tie Bang with them, instead of looking strong, they divulged their weakness.

But if they didn’t do that, they were worried that during talks, they would be suppressed by the dragonman youngster’s grandeur. After all, Commander Long Fu’s martial fame had already spread throughout Two Eyes Island. These last few days, more people have seen the dragonman youngster dive into the sea and wipe out entire schools of magic fish alone with their own eyes.

In the end, even with two commanders on their side, the dragonman youngster still held the advantage.

Unlike what they feared, the dragonman youngster didn’t flaunt any martial skills, instead he used words to hold the initiative.

The hearts of the two merchant alliance managers was heavy, and they began to raise their offer.

The dragonman youngster smiled and was noncommittal toward the new offer, expressing that he needed to discuss it with the other members of his corps.

He got up to leave the room, telling them to wait for him to return.

The four people left in the room, Commander Beng Dai and Tie Bang, along with the two managers, didn’t look too well.

The Secret Merchant Alliance manager coldly snorted, “What is there still to discuss?”

“This is obviously an excuse.” The Flint Merchant Alliance manager pursed his brows in vexation, “It seems we need to raise the offer a bit more.”

The dragonman youngster went to a lonely corner, and with an alchemy communication device, contacted Cang Xu and Zi Di who were on the ship at port and informed them of the situation.

Cang Xu sighed, sensing this would be challenging, “The Wisteria Store on Two Eyes Island is obviously being suppressed, the other powerful party has already succeeded, and it will be difficult for us to stop it.”

Zi Di deeply sighed, “So money is just secondary, and without power to protect it, there will be no impartial transactions.”

Power came from strength, and strength was intimately related to bloodlines.

At that moment, Cang Xu and Zi Di were further motivated to build the Bloodline Purification formation.

The dragonman youngster replied, “I’ll listen to your ideas, are you trending towards agreeing with cooperating with them?”

Cang Xu nodded, “Non-cooperation will worsen the relationship between the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and two great merchant alliances. The Wisteria Store’s future is still dark, and our main focus has never been the Wisteria Store. But if we work with them, we can purchase a large amount of alchemy materials from the Flint Merchant Alliance and medicine from the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance.”

Zi Di frowned, “I agree to working with them. But I believe that trading with two great merchant alliances is risky. I would rather buy from the black market and the discount sale to decrease exposure risk. Don’t underestimate the two great merchant alliances, they can make out much non-public information from the transaction list.”

The dragonman youngster suddenly interjected, “I have a better idea.”

“We need cannons, remember?”

“For the last several days, the massive beast tide has been pressing towards Two Eyes Island. It will be difficult for us to continue leaving the harbor, because the surroundings won’t have enough space to sail our fleet while we fight.”

“Next, we need to defend the island.”

“We experienced that type of battle on Mystifying Monster Island.”

“We need to meet force with force, unlike before, where we could move around the enemy and calmly put distance from them.”

“Under those circumstances, cannons are undoubtably more important.”

Since establishing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they had a deficiency of ordinary cannons.

Although Two Eyes Island’s business was bustling, it wasn’t an arms manufacturing location. In fact, in the conventional market, weapons were very difficult to buy.

Although the marketplace didn’t have any, the two big mercenary corps, along with the city guard and patrol fleet had them.

When the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to buy cannons from Commander Tie Bang, the latter rejected them.

What about this time?

The dragonman youngster’s analysis and suggestion caused Cang Xu’s and Zi Di’s eyes to light up.

Cang Xu voiced his thoughts, “I support the Lord Commander’s idea. Replenishing our cannons will boost the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps substantially. If we fight the beast tide in close combat, our ordinary members will incur high casualties. We will have to make numerous compensation payments. By replacing them with artillery shells, we will greatly reduce casualties. Currently, we have enough financial resources, and enough artillery shells.

Zi Dia also agreed, “This is indeed a good opportunity. I have a premonition, although I can’t be sure about the city guard, the two commanders will agree.”

After a proper discussion, the dragonman youngster returned to the room and told the four his demand.

Of course, this caused the two merchant alliances to look heavy, and the two commanders to turn pale.

The two commanders rejected it on the spot.

“Soon, we will need to withstand the beast tide coming to Two Eyes Island.”

“Cannons are always important.”

“We cannot sell off our weapons at this juncture!”

The dragonman youngster smiled and stated, “When it comes to defending the island, we aren’t the leads. Could it be that you commanders haven’t figured out how the wind is blowing? This responsibility should be the city guard’s and the patrol group’s to assume, no?”

The two commanders’ brows wrinkled, and one shook his head.

It’s a bit ridiculous.

They came here to just help the two managers in their negotiation, yet it resulted in them not getting what they wanted and instead becoming the target themselves.

Especially when they saw the two merchant alliance managers, the two commanders’ hearts sank.

Although their refusal was very firm, with the current situation, they were no longer confident.

Because the situation was out of their control.

The dragonman youngster’s mouth twitched into a smile, gazing at the two commanders, then at the two managers.

The two merchant alliance managers looked at each other, then both bitterly laughed.

The Secret Medicine manager looked at the two commanders, wanting to say something, but hesitated.

The dragonman youngster understood tact, and withdrew again at his own accord, “I will go first, you guys can chat privately.”

He left the room, went to the end of the second floor’s corridor, and through a window, appreciated the evening streets.

Before waiting too long, the door opened and the four walked over to tell the dragonman youngster the outcome, “Commander Long Fu, we agree to your conditions!”

Just as Zi Di had calculated, the mercenary corps and merchant alliances were inseparably close, leading to the latter finding it hard to refuse.

Because it concerned the two mercenary corps’ future livelihood.

Weapons were very important for the beast tide right now.

But now that the deal was struck, it only felt a bit bad presently.

The two merchant alliance managers gave their guarantee and set the pact that they would definitely work more with the two mercenary corps in the future.

The two commanders understood that they were using this moment in exchange for the future, thus, they reluctantly agreed.

The two managers were in a similar situation, their sunk costs were quite high.

The two commanders had already agreed to stop using the Sweet Blood Potion and helped the two managers in the deliberation with the dragonman youngster. After investing so much, and traveling to this degree, if something was rejected, the consequences would be unacceptable for them. The past investments and upheld relationships would be gone.

The next dawn, both sides completed their deal.

Through this, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps finally got a lot of cannons and finally filled in this deficiency.

Although their cannon holes weren’t completely full, there was still some empty, but 80% of them were filled.

The dragonman youngster took charge and mainly equipped Zong Ge’s troops first.

The reason was his troops were more elite and would benefit the most from them!

“We unexpectedly got a hold of these?” Zong Ge was surprised, but pleasantly surprised at that.

After asking about the whole story. The half beastman felt admiration.

Cang Xu and Zi Di were able to handle it on their own because they could see the situation clearly, understand the structure of the strong and the weak, and acted rationally and calmly.

And how the dragonman youngster handled things made Zong Ge even more certain that he hadn’t misjudged the young man.

“He puts the big picture first, such an outstanding leader.”

Chapter 334: The Defense of Two Eyes Island (1)

Black clouds covered the sky, it was obviously the afternoon, but it was dark, and the seas were rough.

As waves slapped the shore, they too slapped everyone’s hearts.

Two Eyes Island was already on high alert.

The beast tide had persisted for many days, and despite the efforts of the city guard, mercenary corps, and other organizations to stop it, it still rolled up to Two Eyes Island.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ defensive position was on the northeast of the old island.

The port was empty, and immense iron panels, stakes, and coral pillars lined the coastline as temporary fortifications.

All ships had sailed into the island.

To accomplish this, Hun Tong had displayed boldness – he had ordered many places on the old island to be blown up to crudely make internal docks for ships to take refuge in.

There was no other option.

If the ships were still berthed at the port, the ships would bear the brunt when the beast tide approached.

Of course, Two Eyes Island was not an ordinary island, it was a coral ball cage tree.

It was a living being that could restore itself and grow back.

Therefore, for the next year, when the old island gradually sank to the seafloor and slowly grew back, these temporary cavities from explosives would slowly be filled in.

By the time the old island floated back up next year, these cavities would all be gone.

The constant alarm reminded everyone that the beast tide was about to hit.

The dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, and the others on the fortified coast saw many vertical fins suddenly emerging from the white waves.

“They’re coming!” Bai Ya’s heart sank, his lips were dry, and he was tense.

The dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, and the others nevertheless remained motionless. Their every movement influenced the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ morale.

“It’s like the intelligence said, it is a sailfish school.” After Zong Ge realized that he relaxed.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had the Deep Sea Monster Fish supplying them with information, and their control over the battle far exceeded that of others.

The sailfish were fast swimmers; therefore, they were the first of the beast tide to attack the defensive line.

“Fire!” The dragonman youngster ordered.

Bang, bang, bang.

A line of alchemy firearms fired into the waves.

Whoosh, swish, whoosh.

A line of alchemy crossbows also fired arrows soon after.

The sailfish were hit, but even though they died in an instant, because of their high inertia, they still ferociously slammed into the embankments.

Bang, bang, bang.

The sailfish school was very wild, and when they struck the embankments, the wall of coral pillars set up violently shook.

They also dented the iron panels upon impact.

The sailfish also paid the price for this in blood, and numerous ordinary level sailfish were struck down dead, only the sailfish with cultivation survived.

But soon after, before the surviving sailfish had time to move, the sailfish behind them rammed into them, seriously injuring or even killing them.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps suffered no casualties, but none of them relaxed.

This was only the first wave, and everyone could sense how frenzied the school of fish was.

This was only the first course.

The dragonman youngster not only paid attention to his defensive line, but he was also receiving news of other places. The alchemy puppets constantly monitored the other defensive lines, passing on the situations there to the survivors.

If one of Two Eyes Island’s defensive lines collapsed, the beast tide would attack from the flank, putting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps in a dangerous situation.

If that happened, they would have to risk death to break out of the encirclement.

Since this concerned their life or death, the dragonman youngster was forced to be prudent.

Through this information, he found that he was the first line, while the Iron Club Mercenary Corps was the second.

A group of ghost face fish attacked the defensive line.

The racial magic – Soul Absorption, attacked.

But this time it was different.

They had Wealth Church priests protecting them.

The priests had prepared divine spells, and when those activated, gold light glittered, a characteristic of the Wealth Church.

Divine spells dispelled the racial magic, Soul Absorption, and it didn’t suppress them into stillness like it did the Iron Club Mercenary Corps previously.

They were all inspired and with the ghost face fish before them as regarded as their target of vengeance, they fought with high morale.

The dragonman youngster quickly withdrew his attention.

Their defensive line was welcoming the second wave of beasts.

It was their old friend, the lightning sharks.

The lightning sharks charged, and also suffered disastrous losses, however, the remaining lightning sharks didn’t repeat their disastrous mistake and instead withdrew back into the seawater and shot the embankment with racial magics.

For a moment, condensed electric spears and Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps arrows and bullets crisscrossed through the air as they fired at each other.

The mutual firing didn’t continue for long.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps obviously had superior firepower, and quickly shot the sharks dead, leaving only shark bodies bobbing in the waves until they eventually sank to the seafloor.

“The embankment’s defenses are about to collapse!”

“This is only the second wave, should we start using alchemy puppet?”

The dragonman youngster directly ordered, “Deploy all basic maintenance puppets!”

They had 8 basic maintenance puppets; they were from the Deep Sea Monster Fish. They could carry out basic maintenance and emergency repairs for ship damage.

This was maintenance puppets’ first public appearance.

Their operational tools had been replaced with hammers, rivet guns, glue tubes, and other such tools.

The depressed and deformed iron panes were struck flat with their hammers, causing the cracks in the coral pillars in the wall to spread, some pillars even crumbled and formed gaps in the wall.

Three other maintenance puppets worked together. One was transporting new coral pillars, and another was cleaning out the debris. The pillars were put in, and upon filling the gaps in the wall, rivet guns and glue tubes activated simultaneously to blend the city wall back together.

The third wave of the beast tide quickly arrived, but the network of basic maintenance puppets worked even faster than them, they had accomplished their maintenance task a step ahead and had withdrawn already.

With the city defense and occupying the commanding position, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had a thick bunker to secure the advantage with.

But the other defensive lines were doing far worse in comparison.

After the fourth wave of the beast tide, the vast majority of defensive lines had already collapsed, forcing the defenders to retreat to the second defensive line.

“Only the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps is still at the first defensive line?” Commander Tie Bang was also gathering all of the information on the island, and after he noticed this, he was flabbergasted.

“Is that so? They surprisingly have such convenient alchemy puppets?” One Eye was envious, and he muttered to himself, “Those arms smugglers truly have many good things.”

One Eye was the leader of the patrol defense fleet and was a silver level fighter. When the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps first arrived, he welcomed the mercenary corps to the island.

Currently, One Eye was the overall commander of the old island’s defense.

Hun Tong and the Wealth Church’s head priest, along with the main force of the city guard were defending the new island.

The new island was the beast tide’s actual target, while the old island was merely collateral damage to the disaster.

Therefore, Two Eyes Island’s feudal lord’s faction concentrated most of its forces on the new island. Most of the old island’s industry had already been relocated, thus, the merchant alliances and the other powers were protecting it too.

It wouldn’t do to not garrison it.

If the damage was severe, and the old island was destroyed, unable to grow back and recover, then Two Eyes Island would only be a single entity.

As time passed, the beast tide’s main force would eventually batter against the old island.

At that time, everyone on the island wouldn’t be confronted by single beast groups, but rather an integrated mix of lightning sharks, ghost face fish, sailfish, and other aquatic lifeforms frothing to attack them.

Defensive lines everywhere were destroyed.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps too was forced to withdraw to the second line, for the first defensive line was thoroughly battered, and no longer worth maintaining.

The Bandage Mercenary Corps was in the worst shape.

They had already retreated to the third defensive line.

When they were at the second defensive line, they were ambushed by a group of ocean frogs.

These frogs were excellent jumpers and all of the ordinary level ones could jump over the ad hoc walls and into the mercenary corps to wreak havoc.

It was because of these ocean frogs that the Bandage Mercenary Corps’ front line sank into chaos, causing their fighting spirit to violently drop until finally causing the defensive line to collapse.

The Bandage Mercenary Corps was forced to defend the third defensive line to the death.

The third defensive line was located where Hun Tong demolished the island to create the temporary dock.

The dock had 12 of the Bandage Mercenary Corps’ ships.

If these ships were destroyed, the losses would be disastrous.

When the Bandage Mercenary Corps arrived at ships, they displayed a stronger fighting spirit and battle intent, because this was their corps’ property!

The 12 ships were crowded together in a line. The flagship White Bandage was at the front, with the other ships bound together with iron chains, forming a stable high platform.

Some of the ocean frogs jumped onto the ships but were quickly wrapped up by an immense white bandage and rendered immobile.

The other mercenaries swarmed over and beheaded the ocean frogs as fast as they could.

These white bandages were an alchemy treasure that not only strangled enemies but could also act as temporary ship hole pluggers and be woven into a net that could bind artillery shells, they were very flexible.

With the White Bandage keeping watch, the Bandage Mercenary Corps finally stabilized themselves.

“Rib, ribbet, ribbet!” The frogs’ croaks echoed like gigantic drums.

A dozen ocean frogs launched into the sky like cannonballs, then in a parabola, smashed into the rear of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ second defensive line.

“Ocean frogs!”

“Don’t panic, stabilize your position.”

“Follow the plan!!”

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps reacted with lightning speed. The cultivators quickly mobilized and charged at the ocean frogs.

The dragonman youngster, Zong Ge, and the others had been collecting all kinds of information on the other defensive lines, and after they learned of what the Bandage Mercenary Corps had encountered, Cang Xu and Zong Ge quickly discussed, then issued the emergency response measure.

Lan Zao charged at an ordinary ocean frog.

The ocean frog lowered its head and charged at him.

Lan Zao shouted, and urged his body’s battle qi, suddenly changing the direction of his charge and brushed past the ocean frog.

The ocean frog howled, a massive blade cut across its body, and blood ejected from it like a fountain,

Lan Zao immediately turned around to slash the ocean frog’s back.

His battle strength had increased greatly!

He had an entire set of bronze level equipment.

The blade wound had been caused by the cutlass in his hand.

He had used almost all of the money the dragonman youngster had given him to buy this equipment.

He was thankful for the dragonman youngster from the bottom of his heart.

Chapter 335: The Defense of Two Eyes Island (2)

“If it was a bronze level ocean frog, I would need a helper to surround it.”

“But ordinary ocean frogs are not my match!”

“I must kill it as soon as possible, the faster the better.”

With strong battle intent, Lan Zao quickly finished off the ocean frog.

He looked around, then rushed to another place.

“Kill!”

He fought bravely and purposely looked for ordinary ocean frogs to attack.

Ocean frog skin was tough, and those of similar level would usually get in a deadlock with them.

But the battlefield was a race against time.

“Good, I killed another!”

“That’s the second one.”

“But it isn’t enough.”

I need to do better, get more battle merits, and stabilize my position.”

“Then I’ll be able to help the commander, and not disappoint his expectations of me!”

Lan Zao’s battle intent stood tall and unafraid.

Situations similar to his was taking place everywhere.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had formulated the best strategy. They dealt with the ocean frog attack by having their bronze levels deal with ordinary ones, their iron levels deal with bronze level ones, and their silver levels deal with the iron level ones.

Thus, in every contest level suppressed all and allowed them to quickly clean up the ocean frogs and stabilize the rear.

As time dragged on, the greater the damage the ocean frogs would cause.

The Bandage Mercenary Corps’ second line of defense had collapsed because their higher-ups had failed to respond adequately and hesitated.

Soon after, the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge returned to their command post.

The highest level in this ocean frog group was only iron level. This allowed them to swiftly eliminate the ocean frog group.

Such good luck won’t persist for long.

Before long, they suffered a second ocean frog group jumping in.

This time, the frog leader was silver level.

But Zong Ge and the dragonman youngster fought together and swiftly beheaded the silver level leader.

“Say, these ocean frogs could become one’s mounts. They can be domesticated.” Said the dragonman youngster.

He had always been interested in establishing cavalry.

Mystifying Monster Island was like so.

But Zong Ge shook his head, as a general, he knew more, “Ocean frogs are unsuitable for humans. Frogmen have a more mature taming method. Frogmen can breathe underwater, steer ocean frogs across the seafloor, then jump ashore. That is one of the frogmen’s most common tactics.”

The two took a moment of leisure to chat a few words, then once again sallied out to attack.

The beast tide brought increasingly more pressure, and eventually, one of the walls collapsed, throwing a dozen people from it, followed by them being submerged by the beast tide. This was the first time the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps took major casualties.

Due to the dragonman youngster blocking the gap, that section of wall was swiftly repaired.

The basic maintenance puppets displayed their immense impact.

They soon withdrew, and under Zi Di’s control, they entered an alchemy formation built beforehand and filled up on their respective magic power.

“Moo!” With a prolonged growl, a turtle slowly climbed onto the old island.

“What is that?” Someone cried out in alarm.

“Gasp…” Someone drew in a cold breath.

When a merchant alliance store manager in charge of the defensive line, a silver level fighter, saw the turtle, his face greatly changed, “That’s a bramble turtle group. Quickly, send a distress signal, we can’t stop them!!”

Bramble turtles were slow moving, however they were massive, like gigantic elephants.

Every time they took a step, they would leave behind deep footprints in the ground.

The surrounding beast tide surged around them, but it couldn’t stop the turtles’ advance. Regardless of whether it was ghost face fish or lightning sharks, the turtles could easily crush one with its foot.

Walls and iron panels collapsed with a loud rumble.

All were toppled by the turtles.

As a silver level fighter, the merchant alliance manager stayed on the front lines and tussled with the turtle leader.

After a few rounds, the silver fighter felt powerless.

He was the same level as his opponent, but the latter’s defense, regeneration, and bodily essence far surpassed a human’s, he couldn’t even bite at them.

The others besieging it also suffered grave complications.

The bramble turtle had sharp brambles growing all over their back.

The brambles whipped or tied up the people attacking it from the front, allowing the turtle to continuously move forward at a smooth pace.

Every bramble turtle was like a moving fort.

With them taking the lead, the defensive line was completely broken through. A gap in the old island’s defense appeared, and the beast tide surged into that gap.

One Eye immediately transferred manpower to help the defense.

Three clergymen and six silver levels rushed to provide aid.

Two Eyes Island had at least 27 silver levels, not counting the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

These fresh troops rapidly displayed their impact and for the first time, the bramble turtles stopped moving.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps didn’t have bramble turtles, but they did welcome a large amount of narwhale.

The narwhals outnumbered the turtles by several-fold, and their charge was like a battering ram at a city gate.

The spiral horns on their heads extended out a few meters, and with incomparable sharpness, they penetrated the iron panels and walls. Some unlucky Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps mercenaries were skewered by the horns and died on the spot.

“It is no longer essential to continue defending the second defensive line.” Said Zong Ge.

“Retreat!” The dragonman youngster ordered, and the mercenaries began to conduct an orderly retreat.

The cultivators brought up the rear, while the ordinary members withdrew first.

When two fighters responsible for gathering information witnessed this, they couldn’t help but sigh.

“This is the most combat coordinated mercenary corps I’ve ever seen.”

“Not just mercenary corps, I believe the city guard is unimpressive in comparison.”

One Eye and the others didn’t have detection type alchemy puppets, they had to dispatch scouts to gather information on the defensive lines in a timely manner.

Therefore, the two fighters saw a lot.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps gave them an amazing feeling.

Not only did their teams coordinate, but their leaders also assumed their duties.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ powerful members fought with all of their strength and at the head of their troops. The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was not hasty or disorderly, their various dispatchments were tight and smooth.

This information was handed over to One Eye.

After One Eye looked at it, his gaze tightened, “The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps really is the strongest of them.”

“Excluding the city guard, they are second. They are even stronger than my patrol fleet!”

“Their defensive position was historically the most heavily attacked part of the island.”

But now, they only just abandoned their second defensive line.”

“Until now, they have never displayed their full strength. They haven’t used the gold level cannon yet either.”
