“Once you reach the third defensive line, I don’t believe you can still endure that temptation!”

One Eye looked forward to seeing the gold level cannon being used.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was a true newcomer and had no foundation on Two Eyes Island.

Although Hun Tong thought favorably of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, under the pressure of the beast tide, Hun Tong’s defense plan still struck a nerve on how he used the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps reached the temporary dock.

Their 12 ships were stored here. All of them were ordinary ships. The dragonman youngster’s and Zong Ge’s flagships were medium sized ships, while the rest were small sized ships.

After the main force arrived on deck, the beast tide followed at their heels.

Silver level narwhals crashed into the enormous clearing, followed by lightning sharks, ghost face fish, and other beasts swarming in.

For a moment, pressure on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps increased dramatically.

Ocean frogs jumped on deck, breaking a mast after continuous strikes.

Mercenaries used alchemy crossbows and firearms to return fire.

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge had been prepared, but they were unable to rout the beast tide’s offensive.

The beast tide suffered disastrous casualties, but due to their enormous scale, they could suffer even more.

“Should we use the cannon?” Cang Xu asked, “We cannot protect our ships.”

Zi Di shook her head, “No need!”

“Using the gold level cannon will consume too much magic power.”

“We need to use at minimum, a silver level artillery shell, an expensive proposition.”

“Before the battle, Hun Tong did not pledge to compensate for these expenditures.”

“What does it matter if a few ships are lost? These are ordinary ships and are cheaper than artillery shells.”

“This is Hun Tong’s domain, not ours.”

Zi Di understood this cost calculation clearly.

Actually, on this battlefield, if the two magicians displayed their genuine abilities, that is necromancy, would yield a strong battle result, and would get even stronger as the battle went on.

But it couldn’t be used!

Ordinary mercenaries would panic and flee.

News would leak, and the survivors’ outward identity would be ripped to pieces.

The Sheng Ming Empire cracked down on and hunted necromancers with constant vigilance.

“We need a magic ship! If we had one now, even a lowly bronze level one, it would be much better.” Zong Ge sighed.

When they arrived at the third line of defense, the pressure on the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was greater than the other two mercenary corps.

The main reason was that besides sustaining heavier beast tide attacks, they didn’t have a magic ship.

Both the Iron Club and Bandage Mercenary Corps had a bronze level magic ship that served as their flagship.

In this forceful defensive battle, having a magic ship was the backbone of the battlefield.

“If things don’t work out, then we’ll have to retreat first.” Said the dragonman youngster.

Zong Ge replied, “Perhaps we can continue blowing up passages to allow the ships to follow our retreat.”

The dragonman youngster shook his head, “That will damage the old island even more, and that will influence our relationship with Hun Tong. Don’t forget, we still need to rest and reorganize on this island, along with participating in the auction.”

As the dragonman youngster was planning their retreat, reinforcements arrived.

“Commander Long Fu, I have come to lend you a hand!” The wandering ronin Ku Feng brandished his long sword, shouting as he killed his way into the battlefield.

On the other side, treasure hunter Hong Zhu swam through the sea water, occasionally jumping out, and occasionally diving back in, leaving fish corpses behind.

Not long after, the card magician Shi Fa also rushed over.

His hand was constantly moving.

The cards he threw out turned into flames, ice, thunder balls, and so on.

The dazzling magic bombardment greatly restrained the beast tide’s offensive.

Chapter 336: The Defense of Two Eyes Island (3)

The dragonman youngster smiled.

He didn’t officially ask for help, yet these three aided him of their own accord. As free people, they weren’t bound by One Eye’s orders and were free to act as they pleased.

For the past few days, the dragonman youngster accepted them onto his ships, helped them deal with the beast tide, and allowed them to profit from mission rewards.

Although his recruitment of them failed, what was happening now was undoubtably proof that the dragonman youngster’s investment had paid off.

One Eye had veiled criticism of this.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps still hadn’t used the gold level cannon, and they still had strength in reserve, yet these three decided to assist them.

This formidable strength should be provided elsewhere to benefit the overall situation more.

But One Eye couldn’t voice his critiques.

That Ku Feng and the others even stayed on the island to defend it already made him glad. Besides his troops, he had no right to truly command such people.

Even if One Eye was deployed, everything depended on whether Ku Feng and the others were willing to join.

Ku Feng’s and the others’ help allowed the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps to pass through their most difficult juncture.

After Shi Fa’s ice magic cards froze the venomous jellyfish surging poison waves, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps launched a comprehensive counterattack.

The beast tide was already thinly spread.

They sailed their ships and nearly made it through the thick layers of fish corpses.

There were still sporadic combat across the old island, and the battles on the other defensive lines had ended earlier.

Shi Fa went to the dragonman youngster and whispered to him, “The outsiders might not know, but as a magician, I have read Two Eyes Island’s records. In the records, your position was where the beast tide most heavily attacked in the past.”

The dragonman youngster’s eyes shrank, and he nodded to Shi Fa.

As a matter of fact, he didn’t need that warning, as Zi Di and Cang Xu had already informed him of this contrast.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had faced the strongest part of the beast tide.

Now, they had 7 ships remaining.

5 were completely lost, almost half their number.

But due to the silver levels exerting themselves, personnel loss was actually quite small, and far less than what the Iron Club and Bandage Mercenary Corps suffered.

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish cannot be exposed.”

“If we had a magic ship to serve as our flagship and assume the most pressure, we wouldn’t have lost so many ships.”

The dragonman youngster had high hopes for a magic ship.

However, Two Eyes Island didn’t have a shipyard, and all of their docks were temporarily formed through explosives.

As the beast tide attacked, the Deep Sea Monster Fish was hiding on the seafloor disguised as a reef, completely still as it did its best to supply information to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

“Commander Long Fu, please marshal your force and follow me to support the Feudal Lord.” One Eye requested this time.

The old island’s beast tide was at an end, but the new island was still being vigorously flooded by it.

“I will.” The dragonman youngster nodded his agreement.

They stayed to defend this place partially because of the generous reward.

In fact, besides the mission monetary reward, the mountainous heaps of fish corpses left behind on the battlefield were also worth a lot.

Since they had already accomplished this much, the dragonman youngster naturally wouldn’t decline to continue exerting themselves.

Of course, before leaving, he left behind some people.

It just so happened that 5 of his ships were destroyed, and many mercenaries needed to tend to the wounded and clean up the battlefield.

After thinking on it, the dragonman youngster left Cang Xu and Tripleblade behind to preside over the old island’s situation.

Both were iron levels, Tripleblade was a fighter, but very reliable and worth trusting. Cang Xu was better, a magician and an expert schemer.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ defensive line had the most fish corpses on it, including many magic ocean beasts.

To ensure their benefits, leaving Cang Xu and Tripleblade was appropriate.

The dragonman youngster left behind a ship for them to return with, then soon after, he, Zong Ge, and the others steered the 6 ships away from the old island and towards the new island to support it.

Together with them was the Bandage and Iron Club Mercenary Corps, as well as merchant alliance ships and One Eye’s patrol fleet.

The new island was not far from the old island.

But the support fleet found it difficult to advance.

They were obstructed by bramble turtles.

“A gold level!” One Eye took in a cold breath.

The bramble turtle leader was a gold level.

Its brambles were especially lush, like a jungle. The brambles were spread wide across the ocean surface and became a stumbling block.

The support fleet wanted to go around, but the bramble turtles were not slow in the water.

If they really wanted to get around, it would waste too much time.

“Commander Long Fu, please use your gold cannon to kill that bramble turtle!” One Eye asked through an alchemy communication device.

The dragonman youngster’s voice sank, “What will be the price for doing so? Captain One Eye, you must understand that to kill this gold level turtle leader swiftly, we not only need to use the gold level cannon, but also gold level artillery shells!

“One shell won’t even resolve the problem. The bramble turtle’s defense is thick; it will take at least two.”

One Eye took a deep breath, “Don’t worry, Commander Long Fu. Before the battle started, the Feudal Lord instructed me that I could decide. Your corps’ wear and tear will be compensated by Two Eyes Island according to the market value! Your contribution for blowing up the gold level magic beast will also be computed in and be remunerated as charged.”

But the dragonman youngster was not satisfied.

Due to how the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was set up on the old island’s defensive line, if Hun Tong had been candid about it beforehand, things would have been fine, but now, the dragonman youngster realized they had been used somewhat.

Since this was the case, why would the dragonman youngster be courteous?

He barked back, “I am not satisfied with that price.”

He thought back to when the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps made a private deal with Hun Tong. There were able to buy items from the auction ahead of time, but they had to pay 30% above the market value,

Thus, the dragonman youngster followed up with, “Our artillery costs need to be compensated at 30% above market value.”

One Eye greatly frowned with annoyance, he wanted to haggle, but the silence indicated the dragonman youngster’s firm position.

Soon after, One Eye couldn’t persevere.

He was assigned to defend the old island, then to organize a fleet to reinforce the new island.

Every second delayed meant a second that he was procrastinating his heavy military duties.

When he thought of the dragonman youngster’s request, he realized it hadn’t exceeded Hun Tong’s bottom line, thus One Eye bit his jaw, “Fine, I agree to your terms.”

Boom!!

The gold cannon was personally fired by Zong Ge once again.

The gold level artillery shell smashed into the brambly turtle shell and erupted with a massive explosion and triggered the appearance of a small mushroom fire cloud.

The bramble turtle leader was heavily damaged, and after it miserably howled, it sank into the ocean..

It didn’t choose to counterattack, instead it sank deeper into the ocean, it was fleeing!

“That really is a gold level cannon.”

“Its might is indeed formidable!”

“With the corresponding gold artillery shells, it can directly take care of a gold level bramble turtle.”

“No, it isn’t dead. It fled!”

“It’s halfway dead, if it didn’t run away, it would have really died.”

Everyone was stunned, and their tongues wagged.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ members were all proud. Look, this is our mercenary corps!

One Eye asked, “Why don’t you press the attack and fire a second shell to kill that gold level bramble turtle?

“Our gold cannon isn’t attached to a ship, and without a magic power furnace supplying it, we have to do it ourselves. It is difficult to fire a second shot in succession.” The dragonman youngster explained.

Meanwhile, hidden on the seafloor, the Deep Sea Monster Fish began to quietly move.

One Eye repeatedly sighed, “What a pity, a true pity.”

Then, using this opportunity, he led the fleet towards the new island.

The new island was not a good situation.

Two gold level beasts were already ashore.

There was a noble flying porgy circling in the skies. The other gold level magic beast was a seafloor mountain snail that was slowly moving across the new island.

The noble flying porgy had its community around it; these fish had flying racial magic empowering them and could fly as long as a flock of birds through the sky.

All seafloor mountain snails were massive. They always dwelled on the seabed, and even ordinary level mountain snails were the size of mounds. This gold level seafloor mountain snail truly looked like a small mountain.

Because it came ashore, the entire new island had a distinct tint to it and was trending towards sinking.

Hun Tong was already active.

The main target was not the noble flying porgy, but rather the seafloor mountain snail.

He had to kill the mountain snail as soon as possible, otherwise the entire new island would suffer a calamity.

However, when Hun Tong’s gold battle skills smashed into the seafloor mountain snail, it didn’t yield any clear battle results, they only slowed the snail’s pace somewhat, nothing more.

The seafloor mountain snail had extraordinary resistance, its snail shell was incomparably thick, its soft body was considerably firm, and its restoration ability was outstanding.

The mountain snail secreted a slippery corrosive liquid wherever it went.

The new island was a coral ball cage tree, and its branches were being increasingly corroded, the surface layer of the island was already rotted away.

Upon seeing this, Zi Di smiled and summoned One Eye, “We can shoot the cannon at the seafloor mountain snail.”

But One Eye hesitated, “What if you miss and hit the new island?”

Zong Ge gave his assurance, after all the mountain snail was gigantic.

But Zi Di didn’t want to shoulder that risk and said to One Eye, “We cannot guarantee absolute safety, however, if we let the seafloor mountain snail continue to wreak havoc, the damage will be hard to estimate.”

One Eye too didn’t want to carry this risk, thus, he contacted the new island and hoped that Hun Tong would give him directives.

When Hun Tong heard the quoted price, he coldly snorted to himself.

His battle skills were oriented towards condensed and focused battle qi and his fighting style was not suited for open warfare, instead he was an expert at stealth, ambush, and assassination.

Therefore, the seafloor mountain snail was an opponent that restrained him.

“Don’t let them fire, even if they hit the mountain snail, the resulting explosion will impact the new island.” Hun Tong declined the suggestion of using the cannon.

Chapter 337: The Defense of Two Eyes Island (4)

It wasn’t that Hun Tong couldn’t afford the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ quoted price.

Money was not the issue.

Similarly, he also wasn’t worried the new island would be affected by the residual impact of the shell.

Rather it was reputation!

If he let an outside mercenary corps help him out of trouble, then his reputation as feudal lord would greatly suffer.

That was out of the question.

Regardless of whether it was the impending auction, future consolidation of the island, or developing a transportation lane, all of those required reputation above all else.

A high reputation could not only make his administration of his domain easier and make his control over it deeper, but it could also deter foreign enemies.

Such as pirates and those fishmen.

Hun Tong understood he had to use all of his strength here.

He took a deep breath, and his murky eyes flashed sharply.

A moment later, his life aura suddenly erupted, forming a qi wave that swept in all directions.

Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh…

A golden flame surged from his body, a raging inferno.

This was what it looked like when a gold level fighter’s entire battle qi burst out from themselves.

At that moment, Hun Tong’s aura was incomparably tempestuous and easily surpassed Rou Zang’s.

A split second later, he took another deep breath.

Like a bird returning to its nest, all of the gold battle qi around him faded back into his body, causing the elated gold level aura to also vanish, as if the prior scene had been an illusion.

When the dragonman youngster saw this from afar, his eyes inevitably contracted slightly.

Right now, Hun Tong seemed even more formidable!

Because all of his power had been condensed together and restrained.

Not every gold level fighter could do this.

This was Hun Tong’s special battle strength. He was an expert at condensing his power to deliver a fatal blow.

Battle skill – Silence.

·

Battle skill – Extreme Speed Stage Four.

Hun Tong moved with such speed that it looked like he vanished.

Even the dragonman youngster only saw a perfectly straight golden ray of light cutting across their field of view.

When Hun Tong attacked, he would inevitably leak some battle qi. He wasn’t able to achieve complete restraint and condensation.

The golden ray of light thrusted towards the seafloor mountain snail.

Battle skill – Bone Penetration!

The seafloor mountain snail was enormous and its movements sluggish. It didn’t even have time to react before the golden light penetrated its snail shell.

The snail shell was a deep cyan, it was thick, heavy, and very firm, and these all combined surely gave it magic resistance.

But the golden light directly penetrated it as easily as a needle through tofu.

The seafloor mountain snail was as large as a hill, and when Hun Tong cut his way into it, his field of view was dark.

But a moment later, he used another battle skill.

Battle skill – Mortal Blow!

This battle skill altered and increased his perception substantially.

For a split second, Hun Tong sensed where the seafloor mountain snail’s 7 fatal points were.

His spirit firmly locked onto the nearest one.

A large amount of gold battle qi condensed into his dagger.

This fine dagger was a gold level weapon!

The dagger shot out of his hand.

Less than a half second later, the dagger struck the fatal point Hun Tong’s spirit had locked onto.

The gold level dagger pierced the seafloor mountain snail’s heart, and because it was too sharp, it continued to travel through the snail, until it flew out of its shell and inserted itself deeply into the ground.

The seafloor mountain snail’s heart ruptured and was soon torn to pieces by the gold battle qi.

The gigantic seafloor mountain snail trembled, and it raised its head high in what seemed to be a silent howl.

Thump.

Soon after, the mountain snail’s head smashed into the ground and no longer moved.

Deep blue blood quickly flowed from the hole Hun Tong made, spraying out like a fountain and pooling underneath the dead mountain snail.

Hun Tong drilled his way out of the mountain snail and stood on top of the snail shell like a spear.

Regardless of whether it was the defending city guard or the reinforcing mercenaries and patrol fleet members, when all of them saw this, they cheered and praised Hun Tong’s might!

“Good show!”

“He killed that gigantic magic beast so quickly.”

“Lord Hun Tong truly is worthy of his reputation.”

The dragonman youngster and Zong Ge both looked surprised.

The battle strength Hun Tong displayed from a certain point of view was more threatening than Rou Zang’s.

He was an expert assassin, and his battle skills were all single targeted ones. Perhaps the dragonman youngster’s dragon scales could withstand it. But without gold level armor, Zong Ge would find it hard to withstand such an attack.

Hun Tong heard the joyous shouts and couldn’t help but smile. But then his gleaming eyes darkened further than what they were before.

A strong feeling of exhaustion attacked him.

“I used up 70% of my battle qi.” Hun Tong evaluated.

The eruption of energy not only expended a frightful amount of battle qi, but also his stamina and spirit too.

Because his full strength had burst out, much of his muscles were injured, his body felt sticky and in pain, he was covered in the seafloor mountain snail’s bodily fluids.

“There is still another gold level magic beast.”

Hun Tong raised his head towards the sky.

In the sky, a large group of noble flying porgies were soaring through the air like a murder of crows and a swarm of bats.

Although these fish lived on the seabed, they had flying racial magic.

The noble flying porgy leader was an impressive gold level magic beast.

Hun Tong hesitated and didn’t attack the gold level noble flying porgy, instead he gave the order to have the light cannon tower shoot it.

The new island had two light cannon towers.

One was complete, it had begun construction when the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps arrived at the island.

The second was quite short and small in comparison, but it still had a firing platform. It was half as tall as the first tower and was the result of hasty work after the beast tide was detected.

Anyways, the component at the top of the two light cannon towers was the same. The 3 meter long silver white cubes had no sign of sloppy work on them.

When the beast tide attacked the new island, the two light cannon towers had started firing.

Under Feudal Lord Hun Tong’s commander, the light cannon towers’ cubes immediately turned around and aimed towards the sky.

The cubes swiftly absorbed the surrounding light elements, and after a dozen seconds, one of the eye runes suddenly opened, and from the depths of its pupil, a light ray fired outwards.

The pure white dazzling ray of light shot into the sky with rapid speed.

The gold level noble flying porgy leader didn’t have time to react and was hit by both light rays at nearly the same time.

The noble flying porgy shrieked, and in a flash, its floating body began to fall.

But after falling 4 meters, it stabilized itself and once again rocketed upwards into the sky.

When many people saw this, they felt grieved.

But the dragonman youngster was not surprised, “The light cannon tower’s strength lies with the speed of its light ray projectile, it is almost impossible to evade. It can only be predicted and be evaded or defended against ahead of time.”

“Its might is only silver level, yet its penetration performance is quite outstanding.”

“It being unable to shoot down a gold level magic beast is not strange.”

Hun Tong’s complexion was grave, on the one hand, he wanted to hurry up and rest and restore his battle qi, while on the other hand, he had to give orders, “Begin a strafing firing pattern!”

The two light cannon towers began to emit a more tempestuous white light ray.

The white light concentrated, causing the cubes to glisten ever brighter.

All four of the cube’s eyes opened at the same time.

The cube began to slowly rotate.

White light shot out from the eyes.

Unlike before, the four beams of light persisted, and the cubes did not stop shooting.

In the wake of the rotating cubes, the two cannon towers’ eight beams of light swept across the sky.

When it swept across the noble flying porgies, the flying porgies were easily pierced or cut open. Only the silver level ones and the gold level flying porgy were able to remain alive through this.

The eight light rays looked like sharp long blades that wreaked havoc across the sky.

In but a moment, the noble flying porgy group suffered disastrous casualties.

The sky began to rain with dismembered bodies and blood.

“They actually had such an attack stance?!”

Everyone was pleasantly surprised, and morale was greatly raised.

More than half of the massive flying porgy group had died by the strafing light cannons.

The surviving flying porgies sank into a rage, and one after the other, they threw themselves at the light cannon towers.

But this was precisely what Hun Tong wanted to see happen.

Around the light cannon towers were strong and prepared defenses.

Not only was Hun Tong there, the priest and others were personally keeping watch over them.

When the flying porgies pounced, the light cannon cubes turned even faster, and the constantly strafing light rays caused many flying porgies to fall from the sky midway to them.

There were only three truly able to approach the light cannons.

Those would be taken care of by the defense’s cultivators.

When Hun Tong saw an impending opportunity, he suddenly jumped at the flying porgy leader.

As the flying porgy leader rose into the air, Hun Tong followed firmly behind and fiercely attacked it.

The flying porgy shot its racial magics at Hun Tong.

Gold battle qi emerged from Hun Tong’s body, stiffly resisting the close ranged attacks and doing its best to close in on the flying porgy.

Both sides grappled with each other for a good while, the flying porgy’s rough skin couldn’t compare to Hun Tong’s comprehensive equipment set.

The flying porgy leader finally fell into the sea with a tremendous splash.

Hun Tong jumped up onto a nearby ship.

He was also a sorry figure, but his leather armor and inner armor were both glisteningly brightly with a gold level aura.

This made the observers on the sidelines all envious.

From a surface glance, one could see Hun Tong had at least two gold level protection equipment and a gold level dagger.

He really was wealthy!

“Dredge it up for me.” Hun Tong ordered.

The subordinates all cheered, and morale once again rose dramatically.

To have a feudal lord this formidably greatly stimulated their battle intent.

In contrast, the beast tide had lost their two gold level magic beasts. Despite there still being a lot of beasts in the new island’s waters, the outcome of this battle had already been determined.

The reinforcements, including the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, first cleaned up the beast tide in the nearby waters, then calmly made landfall.

The new island’s devastating beast tide had nearly been whittled away completely.

Finally, the two forces coordinated both inside and out to surround the remnants of the beast tide and eliminate them speedily.

“The beast tide has ended.”

“Lord Hun Tong is divinely powerful!!”

“We are victorious!”

“Speaking of which, this beast tide was the most intense ever recorded.”

Everyone sighed sorrowfully ad nauseum as they cleaned up the battlefield.

Zong Ge came to the dragonman youngster and whispered to him, “Did you notice that this beast tide was not chaotic, the many beast groups were distinctly divided up and there was even obvious levels to the beast tide’s attacks.”

The dragonman youngster muttered to himself, “It was indeed strange.”

This strangeness was not something most people could detect, but due to experiencing Mystifying Monster Island’s beast tides, all of the survivors more or less could feel that peculiarity.

In the ocean depths.

Fishmen were gathering together.

“The beast tide is finished, and our task has been accomplished.”

“The new island’s defenses have been exposed, especially those two cannon towers.”

“Hun Tong’s strength hasn’t visibly increased, however, he has much gold level armor on him.”

“Hm, go on then.”

The fishmen left the secret camp together to prevent Two Eyes Island from following their races and to not expose themselves.

“Soon…”

“Soon, we will return!”

Chapter 338: The Defense of Two Eyes Island (5)

Six ships formed a fleet that sailed across the dark blue sea.

The dragonman youngster stood at the side of the ship and felt the sea wind on his face.

The red dragon scales reflected the sunlight and emitted a copper luster.

“A school of fish detected ahead!”

“A school of ghost face fish!”

“No, it is a school of ghost face fish and noble flying porgies fighting each other.”

The lookout pointed and shouted to pass on the information.

After the mercenaries heard this, they expressed a trace of excitement.

“What great luck!”

“When coming across fish schools fighting, we only need to pick up the ones left behind.”

“A pity, I wanted to enjoy a fight.”

After the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ mercenaries got through the beast tide defense battle, the mentality of a number of them more or less changed.

The entire mercenary corps’ internal atmosphere suddenly changed for the better.

It was precisely because of their mutual cooperation and dependence on each other during battle that some comrades-in-arms were formed.

The secret competition and internal instability between members during the early stage of establishment had come and gone.

The mercenaries had managed to institute a hierarchical relationship.

After this battle, the entire mercenary corps seemed to have been cleansed inside and out.

The dragonman youngster felt that commanding the mercenary corps was now much smoother than before.

“Reduce speed, approach slowly.”

“All personnel be on your guard.”

“Be alert for fish school attacks.”

The dragonman youngster issued a series of orders.

The mercenaries began to push up cannons, rope in some sails, inspect hulls, and wait in certain places.

The ships gradually approached the fish school battlefield.

The dragonman youngster calmly gazed into the distance.

The fighting between the schools of fish was very fierce.

This was normal.

The beast tide had reached and attacked Two Eyes Island, but because of Hun Tong’s performance, it was utterly routed.

After the beast tide collapsed, many remnants of it sojourned in the waters around Two Eyes Island.

Because Two Eyes Island’s rising and falling attracted many ocean predators, all of the fish schools in the beast tide were essentially carnivores.

When the beast tide passed, these different types of predators began to match their strength against and battle each other.

The young man’s face was tranquil, his heart too.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish gave him information far soon and with more detail than any lookout could.

It was precisely because of this scouting that the dragonman youngster had intentionally adjusted his course to guide the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps towards discovering these fish schools.

The dragonman youngster patiently waited for a bit until the outcome of the battle between fish schools was decided.

The winner was the ghost face fish school.

Their ghosts troubled the noble flying porgies greatly.

The flying porgy school didn’t have clerics to solve this problem.

Therefore, despite the ghost face fish school being smaller than the flying porgy school, they were ultimately the winner.

“Attack!”

Seeing the opportunity mature, the dragonman youngster led his troops into the battlefield.

The ghost face fish were almost depleted, and the victorious survivors were wounded and dispirited.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were a tiger setting upon sheep, and after a slaughter, the battlefield became calm.

They immediately began to clean up the battlefield.

A large number of harvested fish was crammed into the holds.

The mercenaries were quite practiced at doing this.

During the day of the beast tide invasion, they had captured a large quantity of resources.

It could even be said they filled their coffers.

If this harvest continued for one or two months, the mercenary corps could purchase a manor in an important imperial city.

The survivors not only gained money, but also advanced their main project – the bloodline medicine database had consequently made a lot of progress.

Zi Di was in charge of establishing the bloodline medicine testing database. She and Cang Xu had already begun to prepare the materials needed for the Bloodline Purification Formation.

Anyways, the most valuable alchemy materials from the beast tide was in Hun Tong’s hands.

Two gold level magic beasts were slain by his hands.

Whether it was the seafloor mountain snail or the noble flying porgy, both gold level leaders were full of gold level alchemy materials.

The Bloodline Purification Formation just happened to need some choice morsels of mountain snail flesh.

Thus, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps asked Hun Tong for a deal.

But Hun Tong’s reply was that he would sell these spoils at the auction.

By doing this, he would undoubtably be the one who benefited the most.

After all, he had sold some items ahead of time to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps. But now, after the beast tide, he now had even more materials of even higher quality.

It is always said that when there were risks, there were usually profits.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was left helpless and could only wait for the auction.

Fortunately, it was a few days away.

During that time, besides needing to repair their ships and treat their wounded, they also took Mercenary Guild missions related to cleaning up the beast tide.

After spending half an hour, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps finished cleaning up the battlefield. Then, following the dragonman youngster’s lead, they rushed to the next location.

Night was falling.

The mercenaries were both exhausted and content.

Today’s fish catch was abundant, and although it couldn’t compare to when the beast tide was most turbulent, the combat pressure and risks were far fewer.

It was a wonderful experience that many mercenaries were reluctant to part with.

Many people ruefully sighed: if such fish harvests continued, they wouldn’t need to live dangerously as mercenaries, and instead take money and become wealthy.

Some people even proclaimed: since the mercenary corps had such good detection puppets, it would be better to become a hunting group and specialize in ocean fishing.

Such an opinion indicated that the vast majority of those who lived as mercenaries were only in it for their livelihood.

Of course, such calls for this were merely superficial.

Most of their mercenaries were pirates or lived on islands year round, thus they knew that beast tides were rare.

“Report!”

“A small ordinary ship had appeared ahead.”

A lookout on the mast suddenly shouted and pointed.

“What are you afraid of?”

“We are the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“Go on and kill them, that fish school belongs to us.”

“They were currently pursuing a fish school; it was their last hunt of the day.

Because the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps possessed a formidable information advantage, within a day, they had fished up almost all of the most valuable fish schools from the waters around Two Eyes Island.

The leftovers had low value, low enough that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wouldn’t be going out tomorrow.

The mercenaries all knew this, and despite being beaten, the harvest allowed them to maintain their vigorous spirit.

They had experienced Two Eyes Island’s beast tide defense battle, and also knew the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps position in Two Eyes Island’s hierarchy, therefore, they were unconcerned about a small ordinary ship.

But the dragonman youngster’s eyes flashed gravely.

The Deep Sea Monster Fish transmitted information, illustrating that this small ordinary ship was not so ordinary after all. Or more accurately, the sailors were not ordinary.

“Pursue them.”

“Hold the sails at full.”

“All personnel on guard!”

The dragonman youngster ordered.

He didn’t choose to disclose this information.

Scout type alchemy puppets couldn’t investigate to this degree.

“A group of ice blade fish is charging towards us?” On the small ship, a young fighter’s brows were raised when he heard this news.

“Captain.” His first mate analyzed, “It should be a remnant of the beast tide.”

“Ice blade fish have considerable value, many of their parts can be used in alchemy.”

“Correct.” The youthful captain nodded repeatedly. Every strand of his blue hair was vertically sticking up from his scalp and formed the shape of an explosion.

“Hehe.” The youthful captain laughed heartily, “Pretty good luck to have a fish school approaching us as we are coming to Two Eyes Island’s auction.”

“This is our money!”

“The ship won’t do; I shall take action personally.”

Chapter 339: The Lightning Pirates

The youthful captain’s motivation was very strong, and after he said this, he left his ship cabin and arrived on deck.

Plop.

He jumped into the ocean and swam towards the ice blade fish school.

At this time, the small ship’s lookout issued a warning: a small fleet was bearing towards them at great speed!

When the first mate heard that, his expression slightly changed, he wanted to inform the captain but couldn’t find them.

“Oh dear, the captain went out to fight again.”

“He is too impulsive; there wasn’t enough time to tell him.”

The first mate lamented to himself and promptly ordered that all crew was to take precautions, pull the sails to half, and that all sailors sit at their oars.

The small ship didn’t just have sails, but also 6 oars on each side of the ship, it was a ship with a combination of sails and oars.

He led the leaders and experts onto the foremost part of the deck and emitted their life aura in a display of military force.

The incoming fleet was the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

When the mercenaries closed the distance, they saw the small ship and also noticed the people aboard.

“Something isn’t right.”

“That is no ordinary ship.”

“That ship has many cultivators on it; the other sailors are fierce looking too.”

“I get it, they are pirates!”

Some had suddenly thought of the answer.

Two Eyes Island’s auction was about to be held, and during this time every year, not only would merchants, mercenaries, and individual free cultivators participate in it, but also pirates big and small.

Two Eyes Island was a convenient place to dispose of booty and was also an important resupply point for pirates.

However, Hun Tong was still an imperial feudal lord and couldn’t allow pirates ships to overtly dock at his port.

Therefore, when pirates came to Two Eyes Island, they would change their ships.

Their flagships were magic ships and had distinct characteristics. If they rode them over here, it would provoke Feudal Lord Hun Tong. The other pirates traveling there would also think that they were breaking the rules.

In quite the irony, pirates had considerably strict internal discipline.

From this angle, they were always emphasizing rules.

Therefore, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had actually encountered a pirate group.

They were just about to come ashore to Two Eyes Island and participate in the auction.

“Anyways, which pirate group is it?”

“There is an iron level standing at the head. It might be a small sized pirate group that isn’t well known.”

“No, I feel these people are not so simple.”

As the mercenaries were guessing with each other, the seawater ahead of them suddenly exploded with sparkling and dazzling lightning.

After the lightning dissipated, a fish corpse floated to the surface.

The shadow of a human jumped from the seawater and onto the deck of the small ship.

He was soaking wet, fresh from the water, yet his blue hair was still straight and upright like a hedgehog’s spines.

“Captain!”

“Boss!!”

The pirates called out on the deck.

Due to the youthful captain’s excessively notable feature, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ mercenaries had to stifle their cries of alarm.

“That’s Dian Wan, a silver level pugilist!”

“They are the Lightning Pirates!”

“A silver level pirate group that is very active in Two Eyes Island’s waters.”

But that doesn’t matter, we are the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“That’s right, our commander can fight against gold levels, and our auxiliary commander Shi Qi is a silver level too. We have two silver cultivators, while they only have one.”

The mercenaries quickly compared the strength between them, ensuring their confidence and morale.

In contrast, the Lightning Pirates were somewhat grave and depressed at this.

“It’s the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps!”

“Those guys have recently been in Two Eyes Island’s limelight for being strong, don’t look down at them.”

“The other side’s strength is greater than ours, don’t act without permission. Hun Tong’s auction has been held many times before. We go to participate in it to not only shop, but to also sell things. Which shop on Two Eyes Island is not welcoming? As long as we don’t unconsciously provoke the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, they are unlikely to attack us.” The first mate softly and rapidly spoke his clear and logical analysis.

But a moment later, their captain Dian Wan reproached, “Hey, what do you think you’re doing? I killed all of those fish!”

As it turns out, the mercenaries were already beginning to net the dead fish. They were quite familiar with this type of work.

The dragonman youngster appeared before the pirates, “We were pursuing this fish school…”

Before he could finish speaking, the pirates suddenly changed color as the Lightning Pirates’ first mate shouted, “Stop the captain now!”

But it was too late.

Dian Wan thundered, “Long Fu, I know you! Take this!”

He suddenly jumped, and with thunder and lightning twisting around him, he shot at the dragonman youngster like an artillery shell accelerated by electricity.

After a moment of astonishment, the dragonman youngster bent his right arm and tightened his fist.

Dian Wan dove down, fist brandished.

The dragonman youngster steadied himself on the deck and straightened his body like a spear.

Battle skill – Electric Fist.

Battle skill – Demolition Fist.

Bang!

With an explosion, both unyielding right fists clashed against each other.

The dragonman youngster fell back three steps while Dian Wan flew back. As he was falling towards the sea, the soles of his leather boots suddenly flashed, allowing him to stand firmly on the ocean surface like he was standing on level ground.

This was his alchemy equipment, bronze level water walking boots.

As Dian Wan walked on the water, his body staggered fiercely, and he almost fell.

But in the end, he was barely able to steady himself and stand up straight to look up at the dragonman youngster on the ship above, his gaze filled with battle intent.

“Pirate…” The dragonman youngster didn’t cover the disgust filling his face, “But this guy…isn’t weak.”

The dragonman youngster had seen many other silver levels.

During the beast tide defense, there was 27 silver levels on Two Eyes Island.

Based on their contest just then, the dragonman youngster secretly evaluated that Dian Wan would easily be in the top 5 strongest silver levels on Two Eyes Island.

After all, if a pirate didn’t have enough strength, how could they control the will of the people and plunder fruitfully?

The Lightning Pirates’ first mate shouted, “Captain, don’t forget that we have come here to join the auction!”

Then the first mate yelled to the dragonman youngster, “Commander Long Fu, this is all a misunderstanding. That fish school has always belonged to you.”

“Why?!” Dian Wan turned his head, his eyes ferociously glaring at the first mate.

The first mate did not fear him, and instead resentfully reprimanded, “Captain, save me the trouble. Why do you do this every time? Before I finished analyzing, you were already attacking!”

“Sorry, so sorry!” Dian Wan promptly apologized.

But a second later, he then yelled, “But why are we just giving them these fish? I killed them all.”

“What are you arguing so nosily about pirates?” Zong Ge was on another ship’s bow with his broadsword on his back, his arms crossed across his chest, and a complexion of iron. “This isn’t something fish or an apology can solve. You just attacked us! A battle! Only a battle can resolve the problem between us.”

A second silver level appearing caused the pirates to gnash their teeth.

Everyone had seen the recent confrontation; Dian Wan was somewhat inferior to the dragonman youngster and had eaten a bigger loss than the latter.

In a one on one, they were already at a disadvantage, and if a real fight broke out, it’d be a two against one!

Dian Wan most certainly wouldn’t do well in that scenario.

Pirates were like oceanic wolves; they always knew how to judge the hour and size up the situation.

Especially since they changed to a small ordinary ship, and were at an immense disadvantage in terms of ships.

The dragonman youngster didn’t say anything and instead used dragon eyes filled with battle intent to lock onto Dian Wan, forcing the latter to focus his attention back and not dare expose the slightest flaw.

Zong Ge then said, “Despite you being pirates, we won’t take advantage of your difficulties.”

“After all, this is Two Eyes Island.”

“We are newcomers who also want to follow the local customs.”

“Choose ten people that will fight on my ship. Whoever wins will have this harvest of fish. The losers will leave immediately.”

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps fought Dian Wan’s pirate group with their full strength, they would break the unspoken rules here which would inevitably damage and strain their relationship with Hun Tong.

If Dian Wan’s pirate group was eliminated, who would dare come to join Two Eyes Island’s auction?

Even if it was a large scale pirate group, they had to consider and weigh things.

When the first mate heard this, he immediately let out a breath.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was very powerful and still wanted to attack, however, they had retreated a step, kept some leeway, and didn’t have their leader cross blades with theirs.

“I don’t agree…” Dian Wan shouted loudly.

“We agree!” The first mate promptly interrupted Dian Wan.

The pirates around the first mate also cried out, “Captain, calm down a little, give us some peace of mind.”

“Ugh, you guys.” Dian Wan clenched his teeth indignantly, his tone exposing his frustration and dismay.

“You, you, and also you.” The first mate began to choose people.

“Come out everyone.” Zong Ge smiled as he called Kuai Tui.

The Quick Knife Battle Squad led by Kuai Tui had more than ten members, and although their equipment wasn’t standardized, their capabilities were unified.

Especially through this time of arduous training, a tacit understanding had been fostered among them.

Their appearance caused the first mate to tightly frown, he had a premonition had his opponent was not simple.

As it turns out, Zong Ge’s purpose was that he wanted Lightning Pirates to temper his small Quick Knife battle squad.

During training and even in live combat, the Quick Knife Battle Squad had only fought against the beasts.

They lacked experience in fighting people.

Zong Ge understood thoroughly that this flaw needed to be made up for.

But truly throwing them into actual combat was very risky. The small Quick Knife battle squad had too few people, even small losses would make Zong Ge’s heart bleed a drop.

If a bit too many members of this recently established battle squad die, it would collapse.

Therefore, Zong Ge used the opportunity to cleverly obtain the perfect training environment.

Even if a mistake appeared midway, he could interfere and save this newly created battle squad.

Anyways, although the half beastman’s plan was very good, it couldn’t withstand change.

As ten people on each side stood on the deck, about to battle, a fleet was spotted in the distance.

It was Two Eyes Island’s patrol group.

As its leader, when One Eye clearly saw what the situation was here, he repeatedly shouted, “Desist. Let’s talk things over properly.”

Chapter 340: Admission

One Eye was very nervous, “Lord Hun Ton has already said that after the beast tide, Two Eyes Island needs to recover. Fights between humans are strictly prohibited.

Zong Ge coldly snorted.

The pirates’ eyes shined, each of them pleasantly surprised.

The fist mate turned to shout at once, “We are all good people that solemnly follow Lord Hun Tong’s orders!”

“We don’t want this catch of fish; they will be set aside for you.”

“Return now!”

Although the ten chosen pirates had courage enough to not fear battling on an enemy ship, if they didn’t need to fight, why would they fight?

In the pirates’ plan, Two Eyes Island was a leisurely trip, they weren’t here to make war, but rather to conduct business matters.

The pirates all took a big step back.

This time, it would be harder to start fights.

When One Eye saw this in the distance, he immediately let out a breath and brightly laughed, “Impulse is the devil! Two Eyes Island welcomes every friend that participates in the auction.”

“This really isn’t righteous conduct.” The dragonman youngster sighed softly and gave the order to drop this fight.

According to his temper, he would attack these pirates with all of his strength.

As a believer of the Justice God, pirates who killed and plundered were the dragonman youngster’s natural enemy.

Dealing with them was keeping to the path of righteousness.

But as leader, the dragonman youngster also had to consider the others and the overall situation.

Zong Ge was unable to implement his training plan and coldly snorted with displeasure.

Before leaving, the youthful Dian Wan left behind some ruthless words, “I will remember what happened today. Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, pray you won’t run into me on the seas in the future!”

The first mate yelled at once, “Captain, can’t you talk a bit less?!”

“Don’t provoke trouble, especially on the island.” One Eye warned as he gazed at the departing pirates’ backs.

Dian Wan coldly snorted, “We are leaving!”

The small ship overtly sailed towards Two Eyes Island.

As the countdown to the auction ticked down, more and more people arrived at Two Eyes Island.

They assembled on the old island.

Because Hun Tong had regulated that the new island wouldn’t admit outsiders until the auction day.

Compared to when the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps arrived, the old island had sunken much more. The port was completely packed, and the ships were lined closer together, this was the time of year when there were the most ships berthed.

If it were not because of the beast tide damaging many places, Hun Tong would have ordered people to widen areas to serve as temporary ports. The port typically couldn’t handle this many ships.

Two days after the Lightning Pirates arrived, the equally famous Stench Pirates arrived too.

Before too long, someone at the old island sale saw the frogman “Da Zhang Hu Li”. This silver level pirate was the leader of the Applause Pirates.

The five great merchant alliance ships were also entering port.

If it weren’t for the beast tide, they, like in previous years, would have come here before the pirates.

As if by prior agreement, they all brought their respective merchandise. According to their discussed business agreement with Hun Tong, this merchandise would be sold during the auction.

The merchant alliance members escorting the goods were at least iron level, with some silver levels too.

Each of humanity’s six great merchant alliances were a colossus, and deploying silver levels was not the least bit difficult. Every essential stationed manager needed to have at least a silver level cultivation.

During this time, solitary heroes similar to Shi Fa and Hong Zhu also appeared in succession.

The gunman Mei Ban, a female dancer from the owlman clan Yue Ban, the dwarf Men Shi, and so on.

Following Song Shou paying a visit, the auction floor was in turmoil.

Song Shou was a gold level fighter and a newly promoted imperial noble. Information stated that he planned to copy Hun Tong and choose an ocean island to serve as his domain.

After Song Shou arrived, people began to guess whether the Ten Touch Pirates would come too, and if they did, when they would come.

The Ten Touch Pirates were the only large scale pirate group around Two Eyes Island that had a gold level fighter, their commander Qi You.

In previous years, Qi You had always shown up. But because of Song Shou, would he cancel his trip?

As the old island sank, Hun Tong had no choice but to relocate from his city government building.

People were flowing all over the old island, but since all of the industry had already been located, it looked both prosperous and desolate.

Since the people present was growing increasingly complicated, the problem of law and order on the old island was magnified.

The dragonman youngster constrained his subordinates to the ships as much as possible.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ discipline surpassed the other two mercenary corps, and more so the other pirate groups.

In this respect, Zong Ge’s contributions couldn’t go unnoticed.

In this environment, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, who had once dominated public opinion, gradually faded into the background.

The survivors were all waiting surreptitiously.

They were waiting for the Bai Zhen Clan’s reply.

On the first night, Zi Di had gone to the Wisteria Store’s magic messaging unit to deliver a letter.

Based on the timeframe, if there was a reply back, it would arrive in the next few days.

In addition, they were also waiting for the auction to come.

There were many goods they needed.

They would be used to construct the Bloodline Purification formation in the future.

Some wouldn’t be used for that, but Zi Di and Fei She upon analysis, due to their familiarity with the market, suggested to the dragonman youngster to also seize those goods too.

These goods could be first stocked up on, awaiting an appropriate opportunity for them to be used. Not only could they be used to make money, but many times, such valuable and rare alchemy materials could be used to exchange for other such things.

Upon reaching the gold level, this kind of phenomenon began to appear.

The higher one’s level, the more widespread this phenomenon was.

With everyone looking forward to it, the auction finally arrived.

The new island opened.

The dragonman youngster, the half beastman Zong Ge, Tripleblade, Lan Zao, and Fei She arrived together at the auction meeting place.

The auction venue was guarded by a massive military force.

At the entrance, the five people showed their admission tickets.

After the entrance guards carefully inspected their authenticity, they let them through.

After passing through the hallway, the five people arrived at the main auction venue.

The auction venue was very big and expansively vacant.

The auction stage looked like an arena, and high above, several lights shined downwards, one would believe that any product would be seen clearly in this radiance.

There were many seats for the audience.

There were also a second floor with many private rooms. The private rooms circled around the auction stage in a gigantic annulus.

Upon seeing the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ five people walk in, an attendant immediately came up to greet them cordially, “Lord Long Fu, Lord Shi Qi!”

On Two Eyes Island, the dragonman youngster’s and the half beastman’s reputation had already been spread far and wide.

After seeing the admission ticket in the dragonman youngster’s hand, the attendant grew more respectful and he deeply stooped in salute, “My Lords, you will be sitting in room 14, please follow me upstairs.”

There were a finite number of private rooms, and such seating was a scarce resource, thus not everyone could get one.

With the help of prior relationships, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had made a deal with Hun Tong.

The five people arrived at room 14.

Lan Zao and Tripleblade stayed outside and didn’t enter the room to instead serve as bodyguards.

On the contrary, the ordinary Fei She accompanied the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge to keep watch inside the private room.

With the auction about to begin, they needed Fei She’s business acumen to fiercely compete against others.

The private room faced the auction stage through an immense floor-to-ceiling window.

“That, that is a unidirectional lens, an alchemy item. We can, can clearly see through it, but outsiders cannot see us inside the private room.” Fei She presented.

The dragonman youngster directed his gaze and immediately noticed the advantages of the private room. It had a high location with a broad field of view.

When they came in, admissions had been taken care of and the audience was taking their seats.

Some people were masked, others weren’t.

The masks disguised their identities, for not only were there pirates, but there were also some who were worried that after they bought something, flaunting that they had would provoke trouble.

The dragonman youngster and the others weren’t wearing masks.

Mainly because his body features were too distinct.

Furthermore, they had the Deceptive Disguise spell on them, and it wasn’t necessary to disguise themselves further, besides such a disguise would never fool Feudal Lord Hun Tong.

Since it was so, it wasn’t necessary to be superfluous.

On the contrary, exposing his identity in this situation would benefit him in this auction.

Just as the dragonman youngster and Zong Ge sat down, the attendant gave them prepared tea and pastries.

At this time, a group of dauntless pirates entered the auction venue.

At their forefront was their leader, a man with a head of conspicuous blue hedgehog hair, it was Dian Wan.

Dian Wan hurriedly and excitedly walked towards the second floor.

The attendant receiving him rushed to overtake him, “Your Lordship, your private room is not in that direction, take the stairs closest to us.”

The first mate shrugged and stated that this was how Dian Wan usually behaved. His impulses were too strong.

Dian Wan yelled in displeasure, his voice quite loud.

Many people recognized who he was but pretended not to know him.

Chapter 341: A Substitute Death Puppet!

Dian Wan reproached, “Last year, I was in room 17, why am I going to room 19 today?”

The attendant promptly explained, “My Lord, the room number doesn’t mean anything, don’t be so worried about it.”

Dian Wan raised his head in further annoyance, “Give me a break from your prattle, isn’t Hun Tong in room 1?”

He wanted to speak further, but as soon as the first mate saw he wanted to provoke the feudal lord, he immediately interjected, “Captain, can’t you hold your tongue even a little?”

Dian Wan grumbled as he entered his private room, and the attendant wiped the sweat from their brow in relief.

Like Dian Wan’s, all of the private rooms were arranged by level and status.

This was not only because of their strength, but also because Hun Tong had intentionally arranged the seating based on their identities.

After all, pirates should be out of the public eye as much as possible.

If the auction had a bunch of strong pirates gathered together, an imperial investigation would cause Hun Tong to suffer immense political pressure.

Not long after Dian Wan entered the private room, another group of pirates arrived at the auction.

They were led by someone known as Hu Li, he wore simple and crude captain clothes and most of his body was bare.

He was a frogman.

Not only did he have a frog head, his yellow-green frog skin was both damp and a bit sticky.

Hu Li was a silver level fighter, and his hands were his weapons. A frogman’s palms were webbed and bigger than a human’s. In addition, his palms were wider than a normal frogman’s.

This was because of his bloodline, at the same time, he cultivated the appropriate battle qi and pertinent palm battle skills. These three things combined made him famous for his outstanding fighting prowess.

Hu Li led the Applause Pirates, a pirate group of equal criminality to the Lightning Pirates.

The Wealth Church’s priest appeared soon after.

He also had a private room.

The church was something that needed arrangements.

Not only was the priest a silver level clergyman, but Hun Tong also cared about the church behind him. Even a bronze level, as long as they were the parish leader on Two Eyes Island, would be arranged to have their own private room alone.

This time, the Wealth Church priest was not alone, he had someone accompanying him.

It was an old farming looking priest that came from the Fertile Land Goddess Church. Although his face was wrinkled and his back stooped, their physique was still vigorous. On his waist hung a small hoe. It was a sacred tool, one of the Fertile Land Goddess’ distinctive tools.

The two priests entered room 4.

Right now, the largest stream of people was coming in, and increasingly more people were entering the square.

The dragonman youngster looked down from his private room and saw the treasure hunter Hong Zhu coming in with a fellow silver level.

He saw the card magician Shi Fa was sitting in a corner yet was still attracting the attention of many people. He was a silver level magician after all.

He also saw the female owlman dancer Yue Ban. My goodness, that fat beastman bought three seats, yet after sitting down, she still resented how narrow and crowded the seating was.

The auction stage gradually grew clamorous.

“Did you hear? Ya Ma was attacked and had her private room admission ticket snatched away.”

“No one knows who did it. Ya Ma is a silver level yet was unable to retaliate at all. Hehe, I don’t know if she’ll be able to participate in the auction.”

“Hong Zhu, I finally found you. You wretched woman, return my treasure at once.”

“Why? When that adventure concluded, everyone divided up the treasure. You lacked discerning judgement and gave me that treasure. It is mine, and if you want it, you can buy it from me!”

Even more people came in. Some conversed with each other, and others glared at each other due to both new and old grievances.

The attendants were busily serving or perhaps soothing clients, the pressure growing ever larger on them.

After the auction stage’s seats were nearly full, a ferocious criminal strode into the square with head held high, attracting everyone’s gaze.

“It’s the Ten Touch Pirates!” Someone called out.

Their leader was a middle aged man with curly hair and his melancholy gave him the image of a poet. His gold level aura was undeniable, it really was the Ten Touch Pirate Commander Qi You.

For a moment, the noisy square went quiet and everyone conversing unconsciously lowered their voices.

“He still came.”

“Hun Tong was quite outstanding when he beheaded two gold level magic beasts in a row. But in in a true battle, which of the two would win?”

“This time Song Shou is visiting, but even if Hun Tong and Song Shou join hands to deal with Qi You, the latter might not necessarily lose.”

Someone thought very highly of Qi You’s battle strength.

“Even if one is attacked, can’t they still escape?” Another one muttered.

This gold level was pure strength, and without a word, Qi You ascended the stairs.

Everyone turned their heads and watched his figure disappear behind a stair corner.

As the auction audience packed the seats, the auction master took the stage and Hun Tong and Song Shou showed themselves.

They didn’t go on stage, rather they were softly chatting with each other as they strode towards the second floor.

Someone said hello to Hun Tong, causing Hun Tong to shift his eyes and nod with a smile, expressing his benevolence.

Today, Hun Tong’s power and influence was overflowing.

Recently, his military successes during the beast tide had been propagated, increasing his prestige greatly.

Furthermore, he had a person of equal level, Song Shou, accompanying him.

The two gold levels were working together, and no one was stupid enough to cause trouble.

Hun Tong and Song Shou entered their private room.

“Wait a moment, Qi You is in room 3.”

“Hun Tong and Song Shou are in room one, then who is in room 2?”

Someone couldn’t help but question.

According to the current arrangement, the church priests were in room 4. The five great merchant alliance representatives were in the rooms after room 5.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was in room 14.

Dian Wan’s pirate group was in room 19.

The rooms in the twenties did not have island groups in them, but famous lone heroes.

The most discussed room was room 21.

This room used to belong to Ya Ma, a well-known silver level cultivator. But on the old island, she was ambushed, and her entry ticket was stolen.

The conflict between both sides was brief, and by the time the city guard rushed over, it was a foregone conclusion.

Ya Ma hadn’t shown back up, obviously too embarrassed to show her face.

Then the people inside room 21 were robbers.

No one looked into it.

This was the world of the strong.

Pirates robbed to make a living, and Hun Tong wasn’t going to make a fuss about it. He was most concerned about the auction right now, anything else was pushed to the side! In previous years, such a situation had happened many times. Hun Tong’s resolution doctrine was so long as things didn’t escalate, he would let the two sides resolve it among themselves!

Rooms 30 and up were sold like merchandise by Hun Tong.

So long as someone bid high enough and was one of the top bidders, they would have the chance to obtain admission to a private room.

It had to be said that Hun Tong had plentiful business acumen. By just selling entry tickets to outsiders, he made a big profit.

Like what Dian Wan was complaining about, the numbers of these private rooms, to some degree, represented the strength and status of these visitors on Two Eyes Island.

Qi You was in room 3, Hun Tong and Song Shou were in room 1, then the guest in room 2 was undoubtably a gold level too.

But who were they?

No one saw this mysterious guest’s true colors.

This mystery continued to remain unsolved as the auction master began to speak.

Three magic light beams focused on the first item.

Everyone’s eyes was attracted to it.

It was a toy figurine, made from gloomy green lumber, had withered yellow straw serving as hair, and its eyes and nose were severely askew, giving it a strange and sinister sense of beauty.

Everyone’s enthusiasm was invigorated by this toy figurine.

“That is a live saving treasure that can be used during crucial times!”

“No one would suspect anything.”

Even the dragonman youngster ‘s heart sped up, despite him having dragon scales that could resist gold level battle qi.

It was a substitute death puppet.

It was made by Voodoo Sect shamans.

Thus, manufacturing techniques for substitute death puppets needed both magic mastery and the power of divine spells.

That determined that such a treasure was rare.

With regards to puppet efficacy, the auction master presentation was very detailed: it was a silver level treasure, thus it most suited silver level users. But even if one was gold level, a portion of mortal harm could be transferred to it.

“A pity it isn’t gold level.” In room 3, Qi You’s gaze flickered, even though it wasn’t too applicable to him, he still felt how precious it was.

He decided to act.

In room 1, Song Shou also regretfully said, “If that was a gold level artifact, it would have been much better. As the first item, it would have added such a bright luster to the auction.”

Hun Tong nodded and smiled with approval, but his heart objected, “Fortunately, it is not gold level, this is the most suitable item to start off with.”

If it was a gold level substitute death puppet, then the great majority of cultivators couldn’t afford it, for the price would be too high.

Silver levels were the best user.

Since even bronze level cultivators could also use it, despite it being excessive, compared to life, what was more important?

Silver levels were the most fitting.

Gold levels would also be aroused. It could transfer a portion of harm, and sometimes on the battlefield the slightest difference could be everything that decided the disparity between life and death.

When Hun Tong saw this substitute death puppet, he determined that it would be an excellent item to begin the event with.

As expected, reality matched his expectation.

The substitute death puppet evoked the interest of almost everyone.

Everyone began to quote prices one after the other.

Regardless of whether it was the auction audience seating or the private rooms, everyone was bidding.

For a moment, quotes were up here, down there.

The price started at 100 gold and climbed higher and higher.

It swiftly reached 800 gold, then the price competition began to distinctly slow down.

When it reached 1200 gold, only a few people were left in the competition.

“1500 gold!” Suddenly, someone from room 21 increased the price by 300 gold, the biggest price increase.

That price was like a basin of pouring iced water that woke up many people.

“Someone went so far as to bid such a price!”

“Ah 1500 gold, that is the price of a gold level weapon, an absolutely top quality one at that.”

“I remember Feudal Lord Hun Tong’s gold dagger, back then its auction price was just 900 gold.”

The auction master began to confirm, “1500 gold going once, 1500 gold going twice, 1500 gold going thrice.”

“Good, then congratulations to room 21 for getting the first item.”

“Because this is the first deal, the buyer will also receive a personal gift from Feudal Lord Hun Tong, an iron level fragrant gill tea plant.”

“Once again, congratulations to the friend in room 21!”

For a moment, everyone was looking at room 21.

There were two people sitting in room 21.

One male and one female, one old and one young.

It was Cang Xu and Zi Di.

Chapter 342: Auction Strategy

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ main purpose in coming to Two Eyes Island was to deliver a letter, they had done that the first night they had come to the island.

Currently, they were waiting for a reply.

Apart from this, they hoped to obtain supplies from Two Eyes Island.

Two Eyes Island was a trade island, it was prosperous and had abundant goods, Snake Rat Island could not compare to it.

On this island, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps indeed obtained many benefits. Gold coins, equipment, and even cannons were finally filled up on.

As far as this auction was concerned, the survivors were looking forward to it considerably.

The auction would have gold level medicine, which was currently the best way to treat Zong Ge’s arm injury.

But that was only a small part.

The survivors had learned of many items from Hun Tong earlier. During the information deal, Hun Tong had restricted the amount and kind of goods they could buy in advance.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps wanted to buy many things, but couldn’t, thus they had no choice but to make difficult choices on what to take and what to give up.

They didn’t lack coins, but at the time, they couldn’t use them recklessly. They had a lot of gold coins, but their origin was dubious. Moreover, flaunting too much wealth might provoke the covetousness and greed of Hun Tong and others, possibly leaving behind a flaw that future investigations could find.

The final reason was that unknown items could appear. After the beast tide and pirates appearing in turn, pirate commanders like Dian Wan would usually escort some major treasures themselves to the auction to be sold. These treasures were usually quite valuable or rare, and had to be personally escorted, lest one not feel at ease.

Sometimes, during the auction, when a buyer needed to raise enough money, they would decide to sell one of their own things at the last moment.

For the purpose of this auction, the survivors had crafted a strategy ahead of time.

Combining the ideas of both Zi Di and Cang Xu, the survivors decided to display their superiority.

Their greatest advantage currently was not their two silver levels, the Deep Sea Monster fish, or their medicine manufacturing ability, rather it was the Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spells.

With the help of the divine artifact, they had the present age’s best disguise and deception ability.

They could disguise their identities, enter with many people, exert pressure at many points of the auction, and coordinate with each other.

After careful deliberations, the survivors decided to form two groups.

One was composed of the dragonman youngster, half beastman Zong Ge, and most importantly, Fei She. Tripleblade and Lan Zao were supplementary, used to strengthen their prestige and be the main attraction.

The other group was Zi Di and Cang Xu. Their public identity would be the Wisteria Store’s level seven elders.

Why not do anything else?

First, the auction floor was limited, and it was a cultivator auction, thus entry tickets were hard to get.

Second, Deceptive Disguise was only camouflage and could not increase battle strength. Therefore, when dividing one’s forces, one needed to value one’s battle strength. After all, there were numerous pirates at the auction, and once upon a time, even a murder occurred at an auction.

Third, every group had an accomplished merchant that could make decisions. The survivors had Zi Di and Fei She, both of whom had strong business abilities, thus each was in their own group that conformed with each other.

Finally, because the Deceptive Disguise spell was an important secret, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps only used it on their core members, the survivors.

The dragonman youngster’s entry ticket and private room were from Hun Tong.

Zi Di and Cang Xu had no such thing.

At first, Zi Di and Cang Xu went to the Wisteria Shop to check on whether the Bai Zhen Clan replied, while trying to help Yan Yi in passing.

Yan Yi was in the same clan as Hun Tong and had lived for a long time on Two Eyes Island. But regrettably, he expressed his powerlessness. He did have contacts, but he couldn’t do anything.

He told Zi Di and Cang Xu that entry tickets were rare, because the auction was geared towards cultivators. According to usual practice, Hun Tong would first issue a batch of tickets in advance, then leave the final tickets in his hands. First was to deal with emergencies, and second was that if there was a lack of buyers, to prevent a desolate spectacle, Hun Tong could sell them to ordinary people to earn a profit.

Zi Di and Cang Xu bid farewell to Yan Yi and left the Wisteria Store, then tried to find Hun Tong’s merchant to buy an admission ticket.

As a result, they had a conflict with a cultivator on the way back.

Cang Xu decisively used the fire path rings with Zi Di to push Ya Ma into a disadvantageous position.

Ya Ma wanted to escape but was blocked.

Cang Xu and Zi Di didn’t want to kill anyone, and so after Ya Ma paid with her auction ticket, they let her go.

Who would have imagined that not long after, the news that was spread said that Cang Xu and Zi Di had deliberately attacked Ya Ma for her entry ticket.

Cang Xu analyzed that this was done deliberately by Ya Ma. But with the entry ticket, Cang Xu and Zi Di didn’t need to find Hun Tong.

In light of their identities, going to find Hun Tong had hidden risks.

After changing their identities and returning to the ships at port, during their discussion, the survivors surmised that Ya Ma was unresigned and wanted to get back into the venue, and so, she distributed this rumor so she could stand in the right.

The disgusting and vile thing about rumors was that they were hard to deal with.

The more one denied, the more the rumor would spread unchecked.

If it was Hun Tong, he could rely on his rule to strangle the rumors by force, however, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps were clearly unable to do that.

Everyone decided to first see what would happen.

In the end, when the auction started, Ya Ma hadn’t reappeared.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ two groups entered the auction venue at nearly the same time.

The dragonman youngster’s group entered through the main entrance, while Cang Xu and Zi Di came in from the back door and immediately went up to the second floor without appearing in the first floor’s large reception hall.

After all, how they got their admission ticket was somewhat unclear.

Luckily for the two man group, they smoothly entered the venue. Ya Ma’s ticket also unexpectedly included a private room, which was a pleasant surprise.

When the first item appeared, Zi Di and Cang Xu were both tempted.

Cang Xu immediately said to Zi Di, “Live preserving things are unusually valuable, we have to fight for it. If we miss this chance, it might be a long time before we see another. We fundamentally don’t have any normal channels with the Voodoo Sect.”

“I suggest we seize it and give it to our captain. The captain is our most important person, undoubtedly. We have to prioritize his safety, no matter how far we go, it won’t be enough!”

The dragonman youngster controlled the Bloodcore, and if he had an accident, the Bloodcore would be lost or destroyed. Thus, even if the Bloodline Purification formation was built, without the Bloodcore, it would be a pile of rubbish.

Zi Di nodded vigorously.

When she saw the small puppet, she too immediately thought of buying it for her sweetheart.

Rational analysis was only a small part of Zi Di’s decision, it was mostly driven by emotion.

With the final price raise, where Zi Di suddenly raised the price substantially, she directly and efficiently won the puppet doll.

“Once again, congratulations to the friend in room 21!” The auction master brought the hammer down.

After the auction was decided, an attendant climbed onto the auction stage, and under the public eye, took the puppet doll.

The attendant carried the puppet doll off the auction stage, through the corridor, climbed up some stairs, and vanished through a staircase corner from everyone’s field of view.

The auction was paused.

Regardless of whether it was the buyers or auction master, both needed to patiently wait.

The attendant with the puppet doll rang the doorbell to room 21.

After the door opened and Cang Xu inspected its authenticity, both sides did the transaction.

One hand paid the price, the other delivered the goods.

The attendant brought the gold coins down from the second floor and returned to the auction stage, and under everyone’s gaze, squeezed behind a curtain.

“Good, now we can proceed to the auction of the second treasure.” The auction master laughed, “Ladies and gentlemen, please see our second item…”

This was a necessary process for Two Eyes Island’s auction.

At any auction, transactions would be completed on the spot.

This was something Hun Tong specially formulated.

The main consideration was that the buyers here had complex identities, with many of them being pirates. One had to know that they were unlikely to obediently settle accounts, for they were the best at killing and plundering, thus one naturally had to be on guard against them.

If someone didn’t pay, they would be driven away from the auction. If there was a pillaging thief, the auction would stop and the criminal would not only commit an offense against Hun Tong, but also against everyone at the auction.

The auction continued.

Everyone stared at the second item.

It was an infusion bottle. The infusion bottle looked to be made of refined brass, with the bottom of the bottle on top, while the mouth of the bottle was on the bottom. Something was dripping constantly from its mouth.

The auction master presented, “This is a fresh water infusion bottle, of silver level workmanship. When installed, it can absorb water elements from the air and condense it into fresh water. If we imbue it with magic power or fill the bottle with water element crystals, it can produce a large amount of fresh water. With enough magic power, this small hanging bag can meet the daily water needs of 200 people. The starting price is 100 gold coins. Every bid must not be any lower than 10 gold coins, now let the next auction begin!”

Chapter 343: Feather Snake Sail
