Just as the auction master finished speaking, someone inside the large hall shouted their quote, “100 gold.”

“120 gold.”

“130 gold.”

“150 gold!”

When Zi Di raised the price to 150 gold, the room went silent.

“Congratulations to room 21.” The auction master clacked his hammer, ending this auction.

Both sides did the transaction on the spot once again.

The fresh water infusion bottle was a survival type treasure.

After experiencing Mystifying Monster Island, the survivors had a strong demand for this kind of treasure.

They could make use of it during voyages.

After all, seawater was undrinkable.

Normally, ships stored fresh water in bulk.

Of course, to get more fresh water, there were many good ways of doing so. Such as using a magic formation to make water.

The fresh water infusion bottle was silver level, and for many people, it was too extravagant and an unnecessary and pointless expense.

Silver level lone figures such as treasure hunter Hong Zhu needed such a treasure too. But she hoped to spend her money on defensive tools and storage equipment.

She didn’t prioritize the fresh water hanging back that much.

As Zi Di took the fresh water infusion bottle, an exquisitely small compass was placed onto the auction stage.

“This item is a palm compass, silver level…” The auction master enunciated clearly at a medium pace.

It was a maritime navigation device that could indicate a ship’s orientation.

The dragonman youngster decided to quote his price first.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps lacked a true navigator, and currently the dragonman youngster was substituting for that role. Luckily they had the Deep Sea Monster Fish; the alchemy ship had abundant ocean maps and could precisely orientate its position.

Therefore, that compass was a treasure that would greatly help the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

The dragonman youngster’s bid swiftly suppressed the audience.

The selling price ended up being 130 gold.

The dragonman youngster slightly frowned, he intended on continuing to increase the price.

Such a treasure was not currently necessary, but the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps was doing well as of late.

It’s better to have something than nothing.

Naturally, the dragonman youngster was not wholly determined to win this item.

“If it went over 160 gold without a winner, I would have stopped bidding.” The dragonman youngster thought to himself.

“140 gold.” When Cang Xu saw the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps hadn’t acted, he called out a price.

After knowing who had bid, no one was interested in the item anymore.

When the auction master saw that there were a few in the audience with ideas, he said a few sentences to instigate them, but it didn’t do anything.

“140 gold going once.”

“140 gold going twice.”

“140 gold going thrice.”

“Congratulations to room 21 for getting this compass!”

The situation surprised the dragonman youngster somewhat.

140 gold was still a bit above the bottom line, he didn’t expect things would go so smoothly.

This was a happy occasion.

But Fei She frowned somewhat, “Not good.”

The other buyers also felt surprised too.

Many were gazing directly at or implicitly at where room 21 was on the second floor.

The auction had just begun, yet the first three items had all been obtained by room 21.

“Such a ferocious position.”

“Who are they actually?”

The buyers mentally associated them with Ya Ma, and a layer of faint restraining fear covered room 21.

In room 1, Hun Tong who was smiling as he chatted with Song Shou about some things, wrinkled an eyebrow at this.

The auction played a critical role in Two Eyes Island’s commerce.

First, it was an extensive business event that could bring a great amount of tax money to Two Eyes Island. Second, it could spread Two Eyes Island’s fame and attract more customers to Two Eyes Island for the higher level the auction was, the higher level the outsiders coming to Two Eyes Island were.

Hun Tong had organized many auctions and was very experienced at it.

He knew and researched the item types, the presentation sequence, and so on.

The first item had to warm and energize the audience, while simultaneously being somewhat rare and not be of low level.

The second and third items had a similar function too.

The third item was to set the ambient tone of the auction and was to motivate the buyers to raise the prices.

Although three items had been sold off, the ambience hadn’t reached Hun Tong’s ideal level.

The entire auction stage was still trending towards silence.

The reason was that all three items were bought by a single group.

That was bad!

The depressing atmosphere was not conducive to competitive sales.

Hun Tong didn’t want to see a monodrama; he wanted a hundred flowers to bloom.

But unfortunately, room 21 had shown off their seemingly substantial financial resources.

Hun Tong knew the business with Ya Ma, but he didn’t know who the buyers in room 21 were.

On the one hand, after the beast tide, many people came to Two Eyes Island and mingled together. On the other hand, Hun Tong wanted to restrain himself and didn’t honestly want to investigate these buyers. Investigations needed money and manpower. He was not an imperial secret spy and was no expert in this kind of thing, and after the investigation was complete, it would spoil Two Eyes Island’s image.

If these pirates heard about it, although Two Eyes Island could still be used to dispose of booty, who would want to suffer the feudal lord’s investigation?

Hun Tong thought quickly, then waved his hand.

An attendant immediately walked to his side and leaned over to hear his order, “Feudal Lord, what is your command.”

Hun Tong whispered to the attendant, “Adjust the order of the fourth item and the eighth item for me.”

“Ok.” The attendant left the private room.

After the auction backstage received the order soon after, they quickly did the adjustment.

As the auction master was waiting for the third item’s transaction to be completed, he received a slip of paper from backstage.

When the auction master saw the order on the slip, he couldn’t help but think, “The fourth item and eighth item have been swapped with each other? A silver level item has been replaced with a gold level one.”

Such a sequence was uncommon.

Because gold level items surpassed silver ones in workmanship, the target audience was small.

The most fitting was naturally gold level cultivators, and although rare silver levels could barely make use of them, iron levels could only look at them and sigh.

In fact, although the auction was for cultivators, silver levels were a minority, the most common were iron levels.

In addition, the price of gold level items was also far higher than silver level ones.

There really weren’t many cultivators with such financial resources.

Generally speaking, gold level items were all towards the tail end. This was the most advantageous order for the entire auction.

But now, Hun Tong had issued such an adjustment.

“Why did the Feudal Lord do this?” The auction master thought for a bit, then suddenly realized, “I understand. As expected of Lord Hun Tong. Since the three items were all bought by a single party, this adjustment is in response to these circumstances.”

At that moment, two iron level fighters carried a large wooden chest together to the stage.

The wooden chest was obviously heavy; the two iron level fighters were puffing hard as they strained to carry it.

They opened the wooden chest, showing that it was packed tightly with a neatly and tidily folded sail.

The auction master raised his voice high, “Ladies and gentlemen, pay attention. This magic sail here is gold level. Please let me solemnly introduce the Feather Snake Sail!”

The two iron level fighters held the sail, then pulled, unfolding a portion of the sail.

The magic light beams aptly shined upon it, allowing the sail’s weaving lines to be clearly visible.

The sail appeared to be a mild cyan and it was obviously mainly made out of feathers.

The auction master then detailed the sail’s area, length, width, and other such data about it.

Everyone calmly listened.

The auction became so quiet that the buyers could hear each other’s breathing.

The breathing became heavier.

Feather snakes.

They were a snake type magic beast that was covered in feathers, had wings, and could fly.

Their feathers were mostly green, but it is said that rare ones had rainbow feathers.

This magic life form’s bloodline was high-end, and when young feather snakes broke out of their shells, they were bronze level. Adult feather snakes had slender physiques and traveled the vast sky with vigorous grace.

The feather snake race has even birthed a god!

That was always a remarkable achievement.

This meant that within the feather snake bloodline, there was a god level bloodline.

Speaking of which, the survivors had a relationship with the Feather Snake God.

They had lost then regained the Green Jade Gold Coffin, the Feather Snake God’s divine artifact!

The Feather Snake Sail’s main material was feather snake feathers.

It was a well-known magic sail whose main function was acceleration and flight. If it was fit into a light enough ship, the Feather Snake Sail could even allow a ship to lift off from the ocean surface and fly for a short distance!

For most people, such a momentary flight was an extravagant hope and didn’t consider it too much. But the Feather Snake Sail’s acceleration effect caused them to feel a rush of excitement.

Chapter 344: Expensive Purchase

Because of the Feather Snake Sail’s acceleration effect, straightforward control, and outstanding stability, its magic power consumption compared to similar alchemy items was far less.

The only flaw was that maintaining it later was quite troublesome, for it needed an alchemy master skilled in tailoring.

Mercenary Commander Tie Bang’s heart thudded, Commander Beng Dai was the same, while the dragonman youngster’s eyes slightly lit up when he heard Zong Ge shout, “I want that!”

Maritime mercenary corps all had their own fleet, and this gold level sail suited them well.

In the private rooms, the five great merchant alliances’ representatives also looked interested.

If a merchant alliance fleet had it, transporting merchandise would be far more guaranteed.

“Ohoho, now that’s a great thing!” Dian Wan yelled.

The pirates all felt a rush of excitement.

With this sail expediting them, they could greatly increase their pursuit success rate.

Among the many elements that affected naval battles, ship speed was always a crucial one.

The sea was flat all around, and quite different to dry land. Land battles paid particular attention to the terrain, while the seas rarely cared about such things.

The auction master cried out, “The starting price is 200 gold, every bid must raise it by 30 gold! Let the auction begin!”

“250 gold.” Hun Tong’s voice transmitted from room one.

His voice was calm, but that stimulated the desires of many buyers.

“Look, Feudal Lord Hun Tong was the first one to bid.”

“He too is undoubtedly interested in the Feather Snake Sail.

The people were thinking this way.

In room 14, Fei She suddenly had a realization, causing him to hum and haw, “It seems, seems things are a bit discouraging.”

“What do you mean?” Zong Ge didn’t understand.

In room 21, Zi Di faintly narrowed her eyes.

“I fear this change is in response to me.”

“The original auction order wasn’t like this.”

“The price Hun Tong stated seems to say: do you want to follow up on me?”

This behavior was a little strong.

Zi Di understood Hun Tong’s methods, and if she was the auction host, she would want better sales too.

Hun Tong had adjusted the item order, a clever move done by using Zi Di and Cang Xu to probe what the auction would be like.

On the one hand he felt out their financial resources.

The gold level Feather Snake Sail would not be easy to win.

On the other hand, he was probing who they were.

If Zi Di and Cang Xu were lone figures, similar to Hong Zhu and Shi Fa, they generally wouldn’t try to buy the Feather Snake Sail. But if there was a power behind them, such as a feudal lord, mercenary corps, they would have a huge demand for the Feather Snake Sail!

Finally, even if Zi Di came out on top and won the Feather Snake Sail, Hun Tong was willing to see it.

Because Zi Di and Cang Xu would spend a lot of money on it, which would decrease their joint impact on the auction at an earlier time.

So, should one follow up?

Yes!

Zi Di hesitated a little, then quoted, “280 gold!”

In room one, Hun Tong faintly raised an eyebrow, his expression subtle.

Just as Zi Di spoke, another bid, “310 gold.”

The bidder was Mercenary Commander Tie Bang.

He planned to find a place for the Feather Snake Sail on the Iron Lump after buying it.

Although they weren’t a perfect match, they were a usable pair. The Feather Snake Sail could thoroughly make up for the Iron Lump’s weakness of slow speed.

No, to be more precise, it turned a shortcoming into a strength.

After all, the Iron Lump was only a bronze level magic ship, while the Feather Snake Sail was gold level.

The Flint Merchant Alliance representative raised the price, “400 gold.”

He raised the price by 90 gold, appearing rich and imposing.

But this price was still below the market price for a gold level treasure.

Consequently, soon after, another followed up, “450 gold.”

The bidder this time was the Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance.

Although these two merchant alliances cooperated to deal with the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps and the Wisteria Store, for the sake of winning the Feather Snake Sail, they openly competed with each other.

The relationship between merchant alliances was like that, occasional cooperation, occasional opposition.

The dragonman youngster increased the price, “480 gold.”

As the leader of the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he too was interested in this sail.

He didn’t disguise or conceal his voice, and when some of his competitors heard it, their hearts sank.

The Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps had been shining in the limelight recently.

Everyone knew they had ample financial resources!

The beast tide contained great wealth!

The gigantic volume of fish harvested made many envious. Along with the plentiful mission rewards.

Mercenary Commander Beng Dai grinded his teeth, “510 gold.”

He similarly coveted the Feather Snake Sail. Despite knowing he wasn’t as wealthy as the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps, he still wanted to make an effort.

One wouldn’t be resigned until they tried.

The Flint Merchant Alliance representative continued to bid, “560 gold.”

Not knowing his disposition or his bidding strategy, the two bids both raised the price substantially.

The Treasure Merchant Alliance representative spoke, “600 gold.”

As one of the five great merchant alliances, the Treasure Merchant Alliance was well known for making and selling alchemy devices.

The Scale Merchant Alliance soon followed up with, “630 gold.”

However, inside their room, that merchant alliance representative was disinterested.

The reason why he had bid was merely to exploit the excitement.

The Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance representative calmly shouted, “670 gold.”

Just as they spoke, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance also spoke, “700 gold.”

The five great merchant alliances had astonishing financial resources, and for a moment, they were all competing with each other.

Dian Wan impetuously interjected, “750 gold!”

For the first time, a pirate group had joined the competition.

For a moment, the pressure on many buyers’ hearts increased greatly.

No one knew that the Lightning Pirates’ first mate felt even more pressure than them. He glared at Dian Wan, softly growling, “Captain, we cannot use this sail.”

“You forgot that our flagship has an electrical discharge ability.”

If we discharge the electricity, the electric current will burn the sail.”

“Eh?” Dian Wan rubbed his head, “That makes sense!”

The first mate’s eye twitched, “So, before you bid, why don’t you discuss it with me first, captain? Don’t shout prices so wantonly.”

“In addition, we came here to mainly buy healing potions, don’t you forget that!”

“Oh, I know, I know.” Dian Wan waved his hand frivolously.

Dian Wan’s bid was quickly pushed down by another.

“800 gold.” Said a voice from room 3.

He was one of the Ten Touch Pirates, Qi You’s representative.

As a gold level pirate, the first thing he bid on was the Feather Snake Sail.

Hun Tong laughed in his heart.

Qi You’s response was within his expectations.

When a large pirate group placed their bid, that caused the other buyers to sense an incorporeal pressure.

Especially the merchant alliance representatives.

They even thought: If I purchase this sail, will it provoke the Ten Touch Pirates into robbing me?

The gold level pirate group was like a fierce tiger in the jungle that put the fear of death into the other lifeforms there.

In the wake of rising bids, more and more people gave up.

The most frequent bidder was the Ten Touch Pirates.

The Ten Touch Pirates hadn’t joined the initial bidding, but when they made their move, they pressed hard continuously, revealing their ambitious determination.

The bids rose to 920 gold.

The auction master yelled, “Room 3, 920 gold. Going once.”

“Room 3, 920 gold. Going twice.”

“950 gold, no, 980 gold!” Zi Di cried out.

For a moment, everyone’s gazes once again focused on room 21.

Many people showed their amazement and admiration.

Soon after, everyone looked back at room 3.

In room 3, the pirate underling gave an inquiring look at Qi You.

During the auction, Qi You had told him the reserve price was 900 gold.

The underling slightly exceeded that as he shouted a bid of 920 gold.

Qi You didn’t react.

But if he bid now, it didn’t exceed their bottom line by 20 gold, but by 110 gold.

The disparity was too great.

The underling didn’t dare to act on his own.

The one in charge was Qi You, and only Qi You could make the decision.

Qi You closed his eyes, reclined back, and didn’t respond.

The underling didn’t dare to make a sound.

It was naturally the attitude of one who renounced the bidding competition.

The Ten Touch Pirates withdrawal disappointed many buyers who wanted to watch a good show.

As for the previous bidders, all of them sank into hesitation.

The pressure Zi Di and Cang Xu exuded on them was incomparable to the one Qi You gave off.

This psychological pressure greatly influenced the bidding war between them.

Now that Qi You went quiet, some of these buyers thought to themselves this, should they follow up?

Upon considering it, all of them, as if by prior agreement, gave up.

Each bid needed to raise the price by at least 30 gold.

Now that it was at 980 gold, adding another 30 gold would bring it up to 1010 gold.

Don’t be fooled by the difference of 30 gold, one was a four figure number, and the other was a three figure number, the latter of which gave them a wholly different feeling.

If there still was a bidding war, it might be possible that one of them would be too excited to act rationally and bid.

The crucial thing was that Qi You exerted formless pressure, and even if someone bid, their heart would be on the tenterhooks.

Finally, room 21’s unexpected interference, along with the uncultivated bidding war ambience, caused the unmotivated competitors to all give up together.

Therefore, in the end, the sail was delivered to the second floor.

Chapter 345: Homeward Dolphin

Both sides quickly completed the transaction.

The hammer clacked three times, and the auction master offered his congratulations.

“Investigate who is in room 21.” Hun Tong revealed a curious look, “In this round of the auction, room 21 exploited Qi You. Combined with their prior showings, it is clear they are very skilled at auctions. More crucially, they don’t fear provoking Qi You’s hatred. You know, the Feather Snake Sail is meant to be raised.”

“Now that they bought it, it is likely that in the future, they will be plundered by the Ten Touch Pirates.”

At the same time, in room 3, Qi You faintly opened his eyes a crack and gave the same order to his underling, “I want information on room 21.”

“Understood, boss!” The pirate underling accepted the order at once, exposing an evil grin as he excitedly stood up, “These guys are extremely daring! They even dared to provoke us! Instead of buying the Feather Snake Sail, we can steal it instead!”

In room 21, Cang Xu was examining the Feather Snake Sail’s quality.

The more he scanned, the more satisfied he was, and he couldn’t help but exclaim in admiration, “This workmanship, it really is a work of art! I heard that the famous general, Yi Qing, of the Elf Kingdom’s air force likes collecting all kinds of sails and hanging them up like rugs on his palace walls. Now I understand why Yi Qing does that.”

Why did the survivors want to buy this sail?

Because currently, they weren’t able to refine gold level items.

In fact, having such a capability was both too costly and unnecessary.

Their primary plan right now was to build the Bloodline Inspection Database and the Bloodline Purification Formation.

If they were distracted by other matters, the gains would never make up for the losses.

As Cang Xu examined the Feather Snake Sail, Zi Di was analyzing the auction sale.

“980 gold for the Feather Snake Sail is a reasonable price.”

“Gold level equipment on the market can cost anywhere from 100 to 3,200 gold, for all gold level equipment is different, thus, there is the large price discrepancy. Overall, armor is twice the price of weapons. This Feather Snake Sail belongs to sailing equipment; it needs a ship to be used, or for best usage, a magic ship.”

“But generally speaking, gold level magic ships are usually owned by saint level beings.”

“As far as gold level cultivators are concerned, they mainly spend their money on their own cultivation and gold level weapons and armor, among other things. The demand for sailing equipment like the Feather Snake Sail is always lower in demand compared to other things.”

“From this point, one can comprehend why Qi You gave up on this Feather Snake Sail.”

“Anyways, he is a pirate and is perhaps making discreet inquiries on us, probably looking for a chance to rob us.”

Despite the inquiries, only the outer shell identities of Zi Di and Cang Xu would be discovered.

Level seven elders of the Wisteria Merchant Alliance.

The Wisteria Merchant Alliance didn’t have a trade fleet on the ocean, and if Qi You wanted to rob them, he wouldn’t know where to start.

The Feather Snake Sail would naturally be given to the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps.

Don’t hesitate to use it!

The survivors had the Deceptive Disguise and Anti-investigation and Prophecy spell, they could disguise the Feather Snake Sail, and no one would notice what it really was.

Therefore, no matter if someone wanted to examine who Zi Di and Cang Xu were, those with ulterior motives using the Feather Snake Sail as a clue would fail utterly.

Zi Di thought back to the prior auction sales.

Although the price was above market price, they were still reasonable and not too outrageous.

The purchasing power of gold coins remained formidable!

In fact, within the Sheng Ming Empire, the cost of living for an ordinary person was anywhere from 5000 to 30,0000 copper coins, or in other words, 0.5 to 3 gold coins The housing price in major cities was about 10,000 copper coins for every square meter, or 1 gold coin per meter2. A baron’s annual income was about 4,800 gold coins.

It goes without saying that the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ intercepted booty of 50,000 gold coins was a truly tremendous sum!

Although they had used some of it, they still had about 40,000 of it left, a substantial sum.

“The discussed price for the deal they made with the hidden power was 80,000 gold, the 40,000 was but a deposit.”

“I also don’t know where the fishmen came up with this much money.”

Zi Di had doubts.

But when thinking about shipwrecks and unusually abundant seafloor resources, the fishmen being able to put forth this sum was not unreasonable.

“Ladies and Gentlemen, this next item is a batch of medicine.”

“Underwater Breathing Potions!”

“It can allow people to breathe underwater for a short amount of time.”

“During my voyages, they have been very helpful. No matter if it is ocean battles, treasure hunting, or other applications, it is always practical to use.

“This batch of potions contains 50 bronze level, 30 iron level, and 10 silver level underwater breathing potions…”

After the auction master gave a detailed introduction, the auction began.

The first price shouted out was from One Eye.

He was the leader of Two Eyes Island’s patrol fleet and had some credit in hand. Hun Tong allowed him to attend the auction so he could buy some resources for his subordinates. As for the cost, it would be reimbursed in full.

Zi Di didn’t bid.

They had the Magic Medicine Bathtub as the core of their medicine refinement system and could manufacture large quantities of this type of potion with little production force.

They also had underwater breathing scrolls too.

They were seized from War Merchant’s storerooms and hadn’t been used up yet.

Zi Di was silent, but the dragonman youngster bid twice.

It came down to two competitors.

One was One Eye, the other was Hong Zhu.

Hong Zhu was a treasure hunter; she spent her years hunting for treasure on the seafloor and needed to breathe underwater.

These potions were what she needed.

After two rounds of bidding, Hong Zhu stepped back. One Eye had Hun Tong behind him, and the money he spent was not his own, thus he was far more confident.

This batch of underwater breathing potions was delivered to One Eye.

One Eye was sitting in the audience and handed over the gold coins in front of everyone.

Two iron level fighters approached the auction master once again.

They were moving a large tank filled with water. The water tank was made of transparent crystal; thus, the buyers could see inside and out, allowing them to notice that the jar only had water in it, nothing else.

But soon after, an attendant brought up a necklace.

The auction master himself was an iron level magician, and when he lifted the necklace up high, he successfully attracted the attention of the buyers.

“Everyone, come see. This is silver level beast enslaving equipment; sealed inside is a sea mount. After one instills their authority and sufficient energy, it will be activated.”

“Regardless of whether it is battle qi, magic power, or divine power, all of them can be used with the necklace. Please see.”

Upon saying that, the auction master used the necklace.

The necklace cast a light blue beam of light.

The light beam illuminated the water tank, and within the light and shadow, something formless appeared. Two seconds later, the emptiness turned solid.

A dolphin appeared in the water tank.

The dolphin was very big, and within the water tank, it barely had any space to turn.

The dolphin’s most distinctive characteristic was the blue gem growing from its forehead; it looked like it was inlaid there.

After it was summoned, it immediately wailed and cried, then gazed at its temporary owner, the auction master, and cleverly awaited his commands.

The auction master leaned his body towards the audience in front of him and yelled, “As everyone can see, this is a homeward dolphin.”

“It can penetrate dense fog.”

“Even if the environment is complex, that won’t stop it from finding your way home.”

“With it, we will never worry of losing our way.”

“At the same time, it is also an outstanding ocean mount. Regardless of whether it is speeding across the ocean surface or through the hidden depths of the sea, it will perform well.”

“As a silver level mount, it will often be a great help in allowing us to flee for our lives.” The auction master’s words were full of deep intent.

Many buyers perceived the unspoken implication, and their hearts were stirred.

The homeward dolphin is very fast, far faster than most iron level magic ships. For a short time, its dash speed is quicker than many silver level magic ships.

This implied that if a naval battle was lost, one could use it to escape said battle alive.

Naval battles were cruel.

Because naval battles happened on ships, and even if they were found around islands, the losing party would rarely be able to retreat.

This was unlike dry land.

People could rely on their own two feet to flee.

The ocean was boundless; ships and isolated islands often gave the losers nowhere to escape to.

But with the homeward dolphin, it wouldn’t be like that.

The homeward dolphin bestowed some hope of escape to the losers.

Even though it was just one hope, it was still crucial and precious.

Regardless of whether it was merchant alliances, pirates, or mercenary corps, all of them desired this.

An intense tussle began.

The price clambered up bid by bid.

This included Shi Fa, Hong Zhu, Mei Ban, and other lone figures who were also interested, each of them bidding one after the other.

“A pity that there is only one homeward dolphin,” The dragonman youngster said to Zong Ge, “If there was a pod, perhaps we could acquire them. No need for a silver level one, that is too high a life level, we only need bronze level ones. We could have established ocean cavalry.”

The dragonman youngster perhaps couldn’t sense that he had deep feelings for cavalry.

Zong Ge faintly nodded, and freely stated his thoughts, “I heard there is a type of battle squad known as the Dolphin First Aid Squad. The core mount of it is the homeward dolphin. It needs cavalry that can breathe underwater and an alchemy master to remodel the dolphin’s forehead sapphire. When the Dolphin First Aid Team is established successfully, it will both have battle qi healing abilities and be able to teleport over long distances.”

“Those seriously hurt on the frontline can have their injuries stabilized by the First Aid Squad, allowing them to retain their lives, then be immediately teleported back to the rear so they can receive more comprehensive treatment.”

“Anyways, I don’t have the organization method for that battle squad.”

The half beastman too was always interested in strengthening their troops.

The two had great resonance in this aspect.

When he heard that, he suddenly had a thought, “Since it can teleport, can the Dolphin First Aid Squad serve as auxiliary forces and help teleport some of their allies’ heavy armor directly into the enemy or at their rear?”

Zong Ge shook his head slightly, “The alchemy master that remodeled the dolphin gem has to simultaneously build a teleportation formation too. Only the homeward dolphins can teleport to the destination with this formation.”

“So that’s how it is.” The dragonman youngster sighed.

As they were talking about this, the auction had ended.

The final winner was Hong Zhu.

She was a lone figure but was still a silver level and had family assets.

After completing the transaction, she had obtained the homeward dolphin, however, she had paid a lot of gold coins and even one of her pieces of equipment for it.

It was clear the price was not small.

Chapter 346: Shadow Walker Outfit

“This next item is called the Arrow Avoidance Headband.”

“It is a silver level defensive tool!”

“When wearing it and imbuing it with energy, one can generate a forcefield.”

“Physical shells and arrows will be forced to the side by its power, thereby allowing people to evade barrages of long range fire.”

“Without a doubt, the one wearing it won’t need to worry about being shot at.”

On the stage, the auction master raised the headband as he passionately spoke.

Shi Fa was the first to bid. He was a magician and needed all kinds of protective equipment.

Soon after, Ku Feng also joined the competition.

He used a katana, and relied on close quarters combat, thus he wanted that headband.

Because the headband did not interfere with his normal protection equipment, he just needed to wear it around his head.

Soon after, Mei Ban, Yue Ban, and others were bidding one after the other.

The Lightning Pirates’ first mate suddenly said, “Captain, that protection equipment suits you well. The price is suitable, we can seize it.”

“Every fight, you are always the first one shooting from the flagship, charging at the enemy ship alone.”

“With that, we can be a little more relaxed about that.”

Dian Wan was enthused, he had been bored senseless, and he promptly shouted his bid.

The price rose step by step.

Shi Fa had already shut up and withdrawn from the competition.

Dian Wan, Ku Feng, and a latecomer, the gunman Mei Ban, were the three parties in contention.

All of a sudden, Cang Xu’s voice was heard saying, “240 gold!”

Everyone went silent.

Room 21 acted again and had increased the current bid by 50 gold.

This kind of rich and imposing style made everyone shiver slightly.

Ku Feng clenched his teeth, “240 gold…forget about it. I don’t need that equipment that much.”

Mei Ban rubbed his forehead, “Where did they come from? That’s 240 gold! Who will accompany you into madness?”

He also withdrew from the competition.

Just as Dian Wan was about to speak, his first mate reached out and covered his mouth.

“Mumble, grumble…” Dian Wan struggled.

But the first mate did his best to wrestle with him as he growled. “Captain, don’t bid again. Buying at that price will cause us to suffer great losses, don’t forget, we came here to buy medicine! Save enough money, don’t deplete our strength.”

Dian Wan’s struggle slowly ceased, and his face became depressed, “I’m truly bored.”

“If I had known about this earlier, I would have done more, robbed more ships, made more money.”

“I wouldn’t be as restricted as I am now.”

The first mate consoled him at once.

Dian Wan was still unresigned, “Those guys in room 21 are too vile. Out of the question, only I can be so arrogant, I won’t allow others to be like that!”

The desire for vengeance rose in Dian Wan’s heart.

A short time later, the Arrow Avoidance Headband was delivered to room 21.

Cang Xu put it on to test it. Using his magic power, he activated the alchemy treasure.

Immediately, he sensed an incorporeal force field wrapping around his body.

“That thing’s pretty good.” Zi Di nodded with satisfaction.

In the market, unless it was under unusual circumstances, protection tools were more in demand than weapons.

The Arrow Avoidance Headband was a protection tool, although not a traditional one.

In naval battles, perhaps there will be many situations where it will see usage, after all, long ranged firepower was an integral part of naval battles.

Of course, the headband still had a flaw.

For instance, it is unsuitable for squad based combat.

Because its barrier was only around the user and didn’t cover an area. Therefore, even though the forcefield was active and redirected arrows and bullets, it would only protect its user, while everyone around them would suffer a calamity.

If a teammate on the forefront was wearing the headband, no one could say for sure if the teammates at the back would be hit by those shots.

“This is something everyone should be familiar with.”

“That’s right, a moving map!”

“This is very convenient and fast maritime navigational equipment. It can automatically record a user’s position and route, then plot it precisely on itself.”

“The imperial navy already has it on every ship. It is clear that it has a significant impact on navigation.”

“One silver level moving map starts at the low price of 50 gold. Every bid must be at least 10 gold. Now let the auction begin!”

One Eye was uninterested in this thing; he already had one of them.

Shi Fa, Ku Feng, and others were indifferent, as far as these lone figures were concerned, spending dozens of gold to buy a rarely used treasure wasn’t cost effective.

The merchant alliance representatives in the private rooms ate fruit, while others were dozing off.

The merchant alliances had already provided them with moving maps. Despite not being silver level, they were adequate enough for them.

“60 gold.” Hong Zhu bid again.

She was a treasure hunter, and a moving map would be of great help when she went exploring.

It would be of considerable assistance especially when encountering labyrinths and other twisted topography,

After a moment of hesitation, a bronze level cultivator shouted out a bid of 70 gold.

Hong Zhu turned around to look at him, causing the latter’s eyes to shrink under the pressure.

Hong Zhu was pondering on whether to increase the price.

From room 14, the dragonman youngster said, “80 gold.”

That caused Hong Zhu to bow out.

If she wanted to bid again, it would be almost 100 gold.

“I have always had a strong sense of direction, spending almost 100 gold coins for a moving map isn’t worth it!”

Hong Zhu gave up, and so did the bronze level.

In the end, the dragonman youngster obtained the moving map.

Not bad for the price of 80 gold.

The main reason was that there were few bidders.

The dragonman youngster felt happy.

He had wanted a moving map. He also learned how to be a navigator, such as calculating speed, ship distance, position, and other such things.

Having a moving map made things so much more convenient.

Next up, a dummy was moved onto the auction stage.

The dummy was the same size as a real person and dressed in a dark leather outfit. It comprised leather armor around the upper body, leather pants around its lower body, leather boots, leather gloves, and a mask.

The auction master introduced it, “This is an outfit.”

“Three years ago, it was refined in some no-name alchemy workshop.”

“This alchemy workshop was called the Smell Cobbler Workshop. It has now become one of the empire’s esteemed alchemy workshops and has expanded a hundredfold.”

“Because when this outfit hit the market, it received widespread favorability, and in its second year of sales, became the most popular outfit in the imperial market!”

“I believe that after that introduction, everyone has already guessed what the name of this outfit is.”

“That’s right. It is the Shadow Walker Outfit.”

“The entire outfit has five parts and covers most of the body. The leather armor and leather pants can provide fairly exceptional defense. The leather boots can eliminate excessive sound, the leather gloves make one’s fingers nimbler, and the shadow monk mast can give one dark sight.”

“The entire outfit can dwindle one’s aura and amplifies all dark aligned battle qi to an outstanding degree!”

“The next auction shall begin.”

The auction master declared the price, followed up immediately by someone bidding.

This was the first outfit that had appeared in the auction, drawing the attention of many.

The amount of cultivators bidding was at a record high!

The biggest reason was that this was a bronze level set.

Such sets were not commonly seen.

Because it involved comparatively abstruse alchemy skill – part magic spell connection, short distance magic power conformation, among other things.

Through different components integrating with each other, one could form a set effect. The result was usually quite powerful and very practical.

The bronze level set effect of it greatly surpassed normal iron level goods and could even match silver level gear. It was tantamount to stepping over a level!

Therefore, from this respect, the bronze level set could enter a silver level cultivator’s eye.

Consequently, the bidders were not just bronze levels and iron levels, but also a few silver levels.

In previous auctions, the treasures were either silver or gold level. But the audience was mostly made up of iron levels.

Most of the iron levels joined the bidding.

Adding on the low base price and low price increases, for a moment, a boiling cauldron of voices formed in the auction, a bustling spectacle.

When Hun Tong in room 1 saw this, he couldn’t help but smile.

He deliberately planned for this to happen; he wanted to make this kind of auction ambience.

He had some people bidding on the auction stage.

At some crucial auctions, they would affect the atmosphere.

Xu Ma gazed at the bronze level outfit on stage, his saliva nearly dripping. Oh, how it suited him!

“I must have it!” In the audience, Xu Ma was constantly bidding.

The Iron Club Mercenary Corps also acted.

Their corps had an iron level thief, and if they put that set of equipment on, they could even fight silver levels.

“That set is very practical. We cannot do without information from spying. Buy it for me.” Qi You ordered.

But the Ten Touch Pirates were no match for everyone’s enthusiasm.

After the first round of bidding, people withdrew while others joined in.

The Stench Pirates also joined the competition.

Xu Ma was deathly pale as he gazed at the outfit on stage, his heart filled with laments and regret.

Despite making money from the necromancy information bounty, that money couldn’t compare to the funds the mercenary corps and pirates had.

More and more lone figures were also joining in.

The frequency of bids sharply declined, and every price increase was done with hesitation.

The outfit’s price had already exceeded market value.

“I want that set.” Said room 21.

All of the bidders’ hearts jumped, and they felt things were going to get bad.

Just as expected, Zi Di brought it up to 80 gold!

They were simply spilling money.

In room 3, the underling once again gazed at Qi You, asking for instructions.

Qi You coldly snorted, “Let them have it for the time being.”

As far as the Ten Touch Pirates were concerned, they had a second option.

The gold level pirates withdrew from the competition, to say nothing of the others.

The Shadow Monk Outfit fell into Zi Di’s hands.

Xu Ma gazed at room 21; he deeply sighed with a desolate expression.

Earlier, when room 14 joined the bidding competition, he had been excited. He had hoped the dragonman youngster would be able to claim that outfit and fill up the team’s arsenal. In the future, perhaps he’d have an opportunity to take possession of it.

But in the end, room 21 got it, shattering his hopes!

“Room 21, for what reason did you do this?” Xu Ma was both envious and spiteful.

Chapter 347: Mushroom Dancing Shoes

Not only was Xu Ma looking at room 21, so too were others gazing at it with some thoughts.

“Room 21 is too much in the limelight.”

“They have a lot of money. They even forced merchant alliance representatives to withdraw.”

“How many times has this happened?”

The people whispered to each other.

“Don’t worry, today’s auction has just begun. There are numerous things, and based on the itinerary, we aren’t even 10% done.” The auction master was experienced, and could sense the mood of the audience, thus he decided to console them.

Everyone’s mood gradually stabilized.

This auction was organized by Hun Tong, and its scale and item selection had always been bigger than the last.

Two Eyes Island’s transformation was imminent, and Hun Tong believed that this would be the final time an auction would ever be held here.

Naturally, he did his utmost and had numerous expectations for it.

It was precisely because this auction was so valued that Hun Tong was not very satisfied with the result of the outfit auction.

“Room 21 were the buyers once again.”

“Although at prices far above market value, it is harming the overall auction ambience.”

“They are a little vigorous.”

Hun Tong adjusted the item order again.

This time, he planned to have three gold level items in a row!

The auction master threw himself into the task as he yelled, “This is a magic book, a water path book. Although the magic book doesn’t have many pages left in it, only having the Water Arrow spell, Water Snake spell, and Thick Fog spell, these three spells are all unquestionably gold level!”

Someone unconsciously looked at room 3, and spoke some gossip, “Qi You night raided an Elf Kingdom naval fleet, killed one of their gold level water magicians, and captured a lot of equipment. That magic book should be from Qi You.”

Magic books were a type of casting equipment.

It could be regarded as a set of magic scrolls.

However, magic scrolls were often a single-use consumable, while magic books used more sophisticated magic arts that allowed them to be used repeatedly.

If they were replenished in a timely manner, they could be used for one’s entire life.

The gold level magic book was very valuable, but that item was a spoil of war, it was dilapidated and only had three water magic spells left in it.

This treasure was most suited to magicians.

There weren’t many magicians at the auction, most present here were fighters.

In room 21, Zi Di won the magic book in the end.

With it, they could now easily pretend to be water magicians in the future.

The magic book on stage was then replaced with a pair of shoes.

Gold level shoes.

The shoes had a peculiar shape; they were chubby and looked like spongy bread.

The auction master introduced it, “This gold level equipment is the Mushroom Dancing Shoes.”

“This is dancer equipment.”

“It uses alchemy materials from the elven homeland – Erotic Dance Mushrooms.”

“It is said that whoever eats this highly toxic mushroom will become aroused, then as they approach death, they will do an erotic dance for half an hour, then welcome their end during that dance.”

“Brave elven alchemy masters refine the Erotic Dance Mushroom with alchemy techniques unlike our Sheng Ming Empire ones. From the outward appearance of this pair of dance shoes, one can see that it possesses a distinctly foreign bearing.”

To be more accurate, this pair of shoes looked very ugly.

Elven products were always gorgeous and elegant.

If it wasn’t for professional judgement provided by the seller, Hong Zhu, no one would have thought this was the work of an elven alchemy master.

Hong Zhu was a treasure hunter, and she had discovered many things hidden in shipwrecks on the seafloor.

When it came time for the team to divide the treasure, she chose these dancing shoes. At the time, these dancing shoes were sealed, leaving only its peculiar shape without a trace of gold level aura.

When they returned to Two Eyes Island and the treasure hunting group appraised the treasures, everyone discovered that Hong Zhu had earned the most.

The main reason was because she had gained those dancing shoes.

Many members were envious, and Hong Zhu sagaciously left quietly that same evening, separating herself from this temporary team.

The auction began.

For a moment, there was unexpectedly an awkward silence.

No one bid.

“Please, please!” Hong Zhu couldn’t help but pray to herself; she was inwardly anxious.

If these shoes were sold off, she would have enough funds for her next expedition. She had been preparing for this expedition for a very long time.

“520 gold.” The first bidder appeared.

Hong Zhu promptly turned to look at them and saw the beastman female occupying three seats.

It was Yue Ban.

She was an owlman dancer.

These dancing shoes fitted her well.

Yue Ban’s downy and circular face was expressionless.

“520 gold going once.”

“520 gold going twice.”

The auction master heaved his small hammer.

Hong Zhu was in a complex mood.

Gold level equipment selling for 520 gold was not too high, just a fair price for it. It was not the ideal sale for Hong Zhu.

One must know that she had also paid a commission to the auction.

The remaining coin was not enough to prepare her next treasure hunt. A massive shortfall in funds!

“600 gold.” Zi Di’s voice suddenly transmitted from Room 21.

Hong Zhu’s eyes dilated in both alarm and happiness.

She gazed at room 21, and for the first time, felt that this rich and overbearing bidder still had an adorable side to them.

Hong Zhu looked back at Yue Ban.

Yue Ban had been outbid, yet she still remained expressionless, and even somewhat aloof.

“Although she is a famous dancer, those dancing shoes really do look ugly…” Hong Zhu sighed to herself.

“600 gold going once.”

“600 gold going twice.”

The auction master slowly lifted his small hammer.

“620 gold.” Yue Ban suddenly said.

Hong Zhu felt both alarm and happiness once again, she thought that Yue Ban had given up, and hadn’t expected that at the last moment, she would raise the price.

Hong Zhu looked at Yue Ban.

“640 gold..” Zi Di followed up.

“640 gold!” Hong Zhu couldn’t help but be moved. The increase of 40 gold would allow her to prepare far more treasure hunting supplies.

“Will it go up a bit more?” Hong Zhu unconsciously glanced at Yue Ban.

Yue Ban still remained expressionless and indifferent.

Hong Zhu coldly snorted to herself, “She should be giving up.”

“640 gold going once.”

“640 gold going twice.”

The auction master slowly lifted his small hammer.

“660 gold.” Yue Ban opened her mouth again.

Hong Zhu, for the third time, felt startled and delighted, “Did I mishear?”

She looked at Yue Ban and although the latter was still expressionless, they were now sitting upright.

This time not only was Hong Zhu looking at her, but so too were others.

Hong Zhu’s heart was in disarray, and many people realized that Yue Ban desired these dancing shoes greatly.

From room 21, Zi Di smiled and continued to probe their disposition with bids, “700 gold.”

“700 gold going once.”

“700 gold going twice.”

The auction master unhurriedly raised his small hammer.

As expected, a moment later, Yue Ban, expressionless, said with a flat voice, “720 gold.”

The auction master sighed to himself, feeling tired, “The same pattern every time, waiting until the last moment to bid, you’re kidding me!”

But this time, Zi Did stopped delaying.

She had been leaning back on her chair, but now, she straightened her back and sat completely up.

Her eyes flashed with exuberant interest, like a hunter discovering their prey.

Her voice candidly and nimbly stated, “780 gold!”

Yue Ban’s complexion changed abruptly.

Her carefully managed face finally fell apart.

780 gold, every bid must increase the price by 20 gold.

That meant that her bid would have to be 800 gold.

“800 gold…” Yue Ban lowered her voice, as if biting her teeth when she announced her price.

The auction master’s eyes shined, thinking to himself that the two sides were finally beginning to grapple with each other directly.

The prior probing had a very slow tempo.

But now it was an open battle, and both sides’ bids were unwilling to fall behind. After one bid, the other side would immediately increase the price, giving the people the feeling that a windy rainstorm suddenly appeared.

The price rose to 1200 gold.

Yue Ban was unable to keep going.

With face filled with emotion, she twisted around to glare at room 21 and spat out, “1,300 gold!”

The abrupt price raise by 100 gold was her attempt to scare her competition into withdrawing.

But nevertheless, Zi Di laughed back, “1,400 gold.”

Yue Ban seemed to have been struck, and her face turned gloomy.

Her main goal of coming here was to acquire these shoes.

Some information on the goods here had been disseminated by Hun Tong ahead of time in preparation for the auction.

1,300 gold was Yue Ban’s limit.

She couldn’t come up with any more money.

“Don’t tell me I’m about to miss this golden opportunity?”

“My strength is inferior to that snake, and without those dancing shoes I have no hopes of winning the Dance Festival.”

For a moment, Yue Ban looked both hopeless and defeated.

The auction master eventually clacked the small hammer, making Zi Di the ultimate winner.

In the audience, Hong Zhu covered her mouth in disbelief.

Tremendous joy flooded her heart, a distinct contrast to Yue Ban.

From this, one can see that the sadness and happiness of people are not interlinked.

As far as Hong Zhu is concerned, despite the commission, what was left of the 1,400 gold was still a huge sum!

Not only was the capital shortfall filled in, but there was also a large surplus.

“Today is my lucky day!

Hong Zhu looked at Yue Ban, the latter appeared dejected and defeated, everyone knew how important these dancing shoes were to her.

“Her expression was like that because she wanted to keep a low profile, but room 21 saw right through it.” Hong Zhu thought to herself.

As she mulled that, she glanced at room 21 on the second floor.

She had a somewhat complex expression.

The people inside room 21 were truly heroic and rich!

So far, they have already confidently spilled thousands of gold coins.

“Perhaps I can try to contact them, and in the future, I can sell my found treasures directly to a big client.” Hong Zhu reckoned slyly.

A gigantic platinum colored circle was carried onto the auction stage.

The circle was as thick as a palm, and when it stood upright, the auction master was less than half its height.

The two iron level fighters, one left, one right, held it up.

The auction master gently caressed the platinum circle, “Friends, allow me to introduce this alchemy item, the Sun Halo”

As the auction master introduced it, in a private room on the second floor, Yang Cong Tou laughed heartily, “Wait! That’s it.”

He was the commander of the Stench Pirates and a fallen druid.

He had joined this auction to get the Sun Halo.

The Sun Halo could consume energy and create sunlight of varied intensity. Normally, it was used in high end plantations. Some valuable and rare magic plants needed a particular growing environment.

Yang Cong Tou wanted it not to build a plantation on his ship, but rather his meditation method needed intense light.

Chapter 348: The Grand Arena Medal

“I originally wanted to eat up the Treasure Gold Merchant Alliance fleet.”

“But in the end, the Lightning Pirates robbed it first!”

“Hmph, if Dian Wan didn’t put the Sun Halo up for auction, I would have attacked him.”

“With that halo, my cultivation efficiency will be improved by 20%.”

“I must have it!!”

Yang Cong Tou was determined to win.

A short time later.

Bang.

The auction master’s hammer clacked once, and he loudly proclaimed, “Congratulations to room 21 for obtaining the Sun Halo.”

Yang Cong Tou was ashen, and with a sneer, he growled, “Investigate them for me who is in room 21! I want them to know that my things are not so easily taken!!”

A small box was then delivered to the auction stage.

Ladies and gentlemen. So sorry, a small incident suddenly occurred. Another item has taken its place at the last moment.”

“Let me first see what’s inside.”

The auction master’s words successfully stirred the interest of many buyers.

Under the stares of many people, he opened the small box.

Instantly, everyone sensed a silver level aura emitting from the box.

“What is it?”

“It is a last minute item. Since it passed inspection and was let onto the auction stage, its bound to be valuable.”

“Will it be sold to room 21? They have won so many items; shouldn’t they be running out of money?”

Whispers rippled through the audience.

“Oh!” The auction master exclaimed in surprise with a somewhat exaggerated expression, “What am I looking at?”

“Honored guests, this is actually a medal.”

“A Grand Arena Medal!” He reached into the box, took out the treasure, and raised it high, allowing all of the magic beams of light to focus on what was in his hand.

Everyone was in an uproar.

A Grand Arena Medal was a significant thing!

The Grand Arena was not a building of the main world.

Its master was the Abyss Demon Clan’s Demon God – Serene Fury.

Serene Fury was an ancient war god, far older than many of the elven gods. He established the Grand Arena in the Abyss, then forced or invited all sorts of powers and cultivators from all regions to enter the Grand Arena to impartially test their skills.

The victor would obtain Serene Fury’s medal as a reward and the loser often lost their life.

The ancient war god’s rewards were naturally extraordinary!

The Grand Arena Medal had two main functions.

The first function was to absorb a defeated opponent’s battle experience, skills, and so on, then imbue them into the medal’s wearer.

The second function was that it amplified the owner’s overall growth rate, including strength, speed, defense, attack, recovery, and so on. There was one more characteristic: the more people were watching the medal’s owner fight, the stronger the growth rate amplification was.

“I didn’t expect that Grand Arena Medal would actually appear.” Qi You’s eyes opened, revealing an excited glint, “It’s also a silver level Thousand Victory Medal.”

In room 1, Song Shou also sighed, “Such a good thing is indeed rare.”

Hun Tong chuckled.

This so called last minute placement was just a deliberate trick of his.

This had been a treasure he had collected intentionally and kept secret for several years.

Any information that he’d be selling it this auction was kept tightly sealed. If it was leaked, it might provoke thieves and others to visit the city government building.

“A Thousand Victory Level Grand Arena Medal, even in the Sheng Ming Continent’s Grand Auction, is seldom seen.” Zi Di exclaimed in admiration.

Cang Xu was eager to give it a go.

The Grand Arena Medal not only suited fighters, but its growth rate amplification had a wide application that included a magician’s spiritual toughness, magic power restoration rate, casting range, casting speed, and even casting success rate, among other attributes.

Therefore, it was a marvelous jack of all trades that suited all cultivators.

In room 14, because of the medal, Zong Ge too stated his interest in it, “A thousand years ago, the demons attacked the main world, bleeding the earth for a thousand kilometers and savaging its people.”

“While the alliance of races fought back, the Demon God Serene Fury often chose or invited the strong that sprung up during the war and had them compete fiercely in his Grand Arena.”

“The victorious had a chance to leave the Grand Arena.”

“If they only had one victory, the Grand Arena Medal received would be an ordinary level item and have no extraordinary effects.”

“If one stayed to continue the challenge, and accumulated ten victories, they would leave with a Ten Victory Grand Arena Medal. It would be bronze level.”

“Going to a hundred victories, it would then be iron level.”

“A thousand victories, silver level.”

“Ten thousand victories, gold level.”

“Regardless of whether it was demons, us humans, elves, or dwarfs, all cultivators would enter the Grand Arena. Some successfully escaped and obtained a Grand Arena Medal.”

“Some warriors wanting to fight to the death even prayed to the demon god Serene Fury at their own volition in hopes that they’d be able to fight in the Grand Arena.”

“However, later on, the demon army was routed, and the allied forces sealed the planar passages and many weak regions of space. This has led to few people entering the Grand Arena.”

“In one millennium, the Grand Arena Medal was a common thing, but now it has become rare, especially a Thousand Victory Level like that one.”

The dragonman youngster understood, “Will a silver level Grand Arena help a gold level? If that is the case, its selling price most certainly won’t be small!”

As expected.

The auction master opened the price at 200 gold, and every increase had to be at least 30 gold.

As a silver level item, 200 gold was already a high price for it.

Even so, many people were rushing like ducks at it.

The main reason was that this medal had an immense range of applications. It was also difficult to find, and was equipment that suited everyone, fighters, casters, and clergy.

Therefore, even if one’s life level rose in the future and its practical nature declined, if one considered getting rid of it or needed emergency cash, selling it would be very easy.

The bids had raised the medal’s price to 360 gold, and it was still going up.

Song Shou also joined the bidding.

After he announced his price, he said to Hun Tong, “The demon war a thousand years ago has made the Grand Arena known to everyone in the world.”

“To obtain that silver level medal, it is rare for someone to rely on duels to accumulate a thousand victories, the optimal way would be participating in large scale melees. Becoming the final survivor of a close quarters battle with several hundred people in it.”

“I also heard that there is a faster way to obtain medals in duels. Through winning streaks!”

“Three victories in a row will net a bronze level medal. Five wins in a row can obtain an iron level, ten for a silver, twenty four for a gold.”

At this time, the medal had reached a price of 460 gold.

Song Shou stated, “600 gold.”

Everyone went silent.

Song Shou smiled as he explained to Hun Tong, “This equipment suits the younger generation of my family. The silver level medal won’t help me that much. The medal can gather a defeated opponent’s experience and skills, nourishing the growth of the younger generation.”

Hun Tong nodded, “That’s right. Alas, if it weren’t for the fact that this was a last minute replacement, I would have traded to you ahead of time.”

“Hehehe, perhaps in the future there will be an opportunity to do that.” Song Shou was laughing when he heard a voice.

“1000 gold!”

Song Shou’s smiling expression immediately stiffened.

“It’s that guy in room 21.” Song Shou’s tone was cold.

“1000 gold going once.” The auction master yelled.

Everyone was silent.

“1000 gold going twice.”

Everyone was still silent.

“1000 gold going thrice. Good, let me congratulate the customers in room 21.” The auction master’s small hammer fell.

At that time, all of the buyers seemed to sigh in relief, then began to whisper to each other.

“1000 gold to buy a silver level equipment, that’s simply insane!”

“Even though it’s a Grand Arena Medal, that’s too outrageous.”

It had far surpassed the market value of silver level things.

1000 gold could unquestionably buy a gold level weapon.

The gold dagger Hun Tong assassinated the seafloor mountain snail with only cost 900 gold.

Who would spend 1000 gold to buy a silver level medal?

Room 21 gave everyone an answer.

They didn’t lack money!

Rich!

Before the auction started, some guessed that perhaps room 21 had been the seller, so that they could make up for the cash they spent, but everyone knew those guesses were superficial.

“How much cash do those people have on them?”

“They aren’t emptying their accounts for this auction?”

“Even if it is a power, is it really worth it to spend so much on a single piece of equipment?”

Even the great merchant alliance representatives were also analyzing Cang Xu’s and Zi Di’s financial resources and intentions.

Zi Di’s and Cang Xu’s hearts were awash with joy.

The two had made up their mind, they would not hesitate to purchase the Grand Arena Medal at a premium for the dragonman youngster.

“The captain has the Bloodcore and can theoretically transform into any lifeform. However, in practical use, many systemic problems are discovered in doing so.”

“The amount of energy, time, and labor spent to be able to use each life shape skillfully is always massive.”

“Under these circumstances, the captain limiting himself to humanoid shapes is very sensible.”

“However, with the Grand Arena Medal, things will be different!”

Cang Xu smiled as he said that.

“That’s right.” Zi Di nodded, the girl’s eyes were twinkling with a warm and soft light, “This arena medal can absorb the loser’s experience and skills. That is very crucial.”

“In the future, even if it isn’t a humanoid, the commander can still absorb as much as he likes. He only needs to defeat said type of lifeform as much as possible so the Grand Arena Medal can acquire sufficient experience and skills.”

“With that experience and skills, the commander can save large amounts of time and energy and swiftly master the strengths of every life shape.”

Cang Xu pushed up the spectacles on his nose, “In theory, everything is feasible. I hope that it really works, that the results can satisfy us.”

The world had innumerable alchemy treasures.

Even the same treasure has a different worth to different users.

If the Grand Arena Medal had a great practical result, then the assistance it would bring to the dragonman youngster would be no less than a quasi-divine artifact!

Chapter 349: Balloon Knapsack

The auction had reached the middle section.

The gathering square’s ambience was growing warmer and warmer.

Under three magic light beams, the auction master took out the next item from a flat box.

When the box was opened, the item’s workmanship aura leaked out.

Everyone’s mind was shaken upon suddenly realizing that this was a gold level treasure.

“How many gold level alchemy items are there?”

“I have participated in five of Two Eyes Island’s auctions; this is absolutely the biggest one I’ve ever attended!”

“Even in an imperial metropolis, such an auction is not commonly seen.”

“Hun Tong has truly proved his skill. The influence of this auction will inevitably raise Two Eyes Island’s business status once again!”

Many present gradually realized that after this auction finished, Two Eyes Island’s fame would be elevated substantially.

The auction master began his presentation, “Noble customers, this is a classic treasure.”

“It comes from the Piggy Bank Alchemy Workshop.”

“Eight centuries ago, an alchemy item was produced, and through minute improvements, it reached its final form a century ago.”

“That’s right, it is the Balloon Knapsack!”

Everyone gazed at it with rapt attention and saw that in the auction master’s fingers, there was a thin cloth bag.

The cloth bag was shaped like something between a balloon and a shoulder knapsack.

Its materials seemed to be frail and crude, however, the surface was very brightly colored and looked like multicolored weaved squares and triangles.

“Honored customers, perhaps you are unconvinced, don’t worry, this treasure is at its smallest when it is empty.” The auction master continued his presentation, “The more stuff we store inside it, the bigger its bulk. Everyone please watch the demonstration patiently.”

As the auction master said this, a few fighters were moving twenty casks.

“These are empty casks.” The auction master opened the first ten casks.

Each cask was half a person’s height.

Everyone saw that the casks were indeed empty, nothing was inside them.

The auction master walked to the side of a cask, then pulled the knapsack’s zipper before everyone.

After the knapsack was unzipped, a light leaked from within it.

The white light looked like fog, and within the fog, specks of crystal light glittered like shiny diamonds.

The white diamond lights cascaded down and covered the entire cask.

When the fog light pulled back into the knapsack, the entire cask subsequently disappeared from where it stood.

It was evident that the cask had been stored away.

But the knapsack’s size only minutely changed, almost indistinguishable to the naked eye.

Then after the auction master stored all ten casks, the knapsack got a bit bigger.

“Next will be casks full of fresh water.” As the auction master said this, some people carried over ten water casks.

The auction master stored all ten water casks into the knapsack.

The knapsack grew bigger, becoming a bit bulgier. The dragonman youngster couldn’t help but associate it with Zi Di’s small waist handbag filled with medicinal bottles.

“These ten casks are filled with large amounts of Brain Pebbles.”

Another ten casks.

They were opened, allowing the audience to peer in and see that they were indeed filled with Brain Pebbles.

These white pebbles had strands of bloody wisps, like a person’s brain. They were a type of alchemy material with many uses.

The ten casks were filled with Brain Pebbles, five of them were filled with bronze level ones and the other five were filled with silver level ones.

The auction master first stuffed the five casks of bronze level Brain Pebbles into the knapsack.

The Balloon Knapsack swelled even larger and seemed to be the size of a normal inflated balloon.

The auction master then put the other five casks of Brain Pebbles into the knapsack, causing the Balloon Knapsack to inflate to the size of a normal knapsack. After being packed with silver level alchemy materials, it had swelled to its biggest size yet.

From this, it could be seen that an item’s workmanship had an immense effect on its deformation!

The auction master put the knapsack on his back and raised his voice, “Everyone, please witness the Balloon Knapsack’s greatest merit: it can store a large number of things while still being relatively light.”

“Right now, I feel it is effortless to carry.”

“Of course, I am an iron level cultivator. Therefore, we specially arranged to have an ordinary person for this.”

A common youngster came on stage, and with all of the cultivators watching him, he appeared somewhat nervous.

He placed the Balloon Knapsack onto his own back.

His shoulders consequently sank slightly, but the burden was not strenuous.

Next, he slowly paced around, then walked quickly, then ran and jumped, all of which appeared fast and nimble. The knapsack on his back influenced his movement negligibly.

“Honored guests, your eyes see the truth – an ordinary person can carry this Balloon Knapsack!”

“Of course, this is not without cost. The knapsack must be imbued with battle qi beforehand, and only by expending battle qi can its weight be lowered.”

“Battle qi can be used; magic power will work too. Even some stored magic power crystals or elemental crystal stones in the knapsack can imbue it with energy.”

“At present, this mystical storage equipment is far from reaching its limits.”

“We can still put more things into it!”

Following that, the auction master placed even more things into the Balloon Knapsack, their quality ranging from ordinary to silver level.

The total amount of items in it was now ten times more than previously.

The Balloon Knapsack grew even more in volume.

The balloon gradually grew from the size of a human child, to the size of a human adult, then to the size of the horse, until finally, it had the bulk of an adult elephant.

The elephant sized balloon floated on the auction stage. The bag strap had also become taut, like a strand of hemp rope. One end of the “hemp rope” was connected to the multicolored Balloon Knapsack and the other was held gently by the auction master.

The auction master tugged on it from time to time, causing the Balloon Knapsack to softly sway accordingly.

The auction master then said, “Those familiar with this alchemy treasure should know that it can become even bigger. It can grow to the size of a carriage, and even a house with no issues.”

“As you can see, even though there are so many things loaded into it, it is still very light and portable. I can pull it with one hand.”

“Everyone, this gold level Balloon Knapsack has outstanding storage capabilities!”

“With it, we can store ever more things and pull it ever longer distances.”

“We can store food and water, the most basic insurance of survival while sailing the seas.”

“Think about it everyone, it isn’t easy to run shipping routes, there are many risks! There are pirates, ocean monsters, nasty tempests, and many other innumerable troubles. Our ships are finite, our hold space is finite, and we can only load so much merchandise. With this treasure, we can load so much more! What two voyages could transport can now be completed in one! That is an immense improvement!”

“You won’t regret purchasing this Balloon Knapsack, a gold level Balloon Knapsack.”

“Right, now the auction will begin. The starting price is 1000 gold, and every price increase must be no less than 80 gold.”

“Let the auction begin!” The auction master’s small hammer fell with a crack.

“1,200 gold!” The Flint Merchant Alliance representative bid soon after.

The auction master’s words simply drove themselves into his heart. In this respect, he wanted it quite exuberantly.

“1,400 gold.”

“1,500 gold.”

Quite quickly, the five great merchant alliance representatives took action in succession.

As a pirate commander, Dian Wan was unwilling to be left out, “1,600 gold.”

This time, his first mate didn’t object.

They were pirates, they needed such storage devices. Pirate ships emphasized pursuit speed and agility compared to trade ships, ensuring military strength by sacrificing storage space.

Often when the Lightning Pirates were looting, merchant ships sank, and they didn’t have surplus space to stow the spoils of war. They had no choice but to resign themselves with what treasures they could take and abandon most of the merchant goods, taking only the most valuable things.

With the gold level Balloon Knapsack, they could maximize the profits of each piracy.

Therefore, this time, the first mate supported Dian Wan.

Dian Wan’s bid was the prologue of pirates joining the “war” in increasing numbers.

Very quickly, Stench, Applause, and other pirate commanders all joined the price competition.

Not long after, Qi You’s voice transmitted from room 3, “2,500 gold!”

The Flint Merchant Alliance representative who had always been in contention didn’t back down as he yelled, “2,600 gold.”

Currently, the auction price was still below his bottom line.

The Flint Merchant Alliance primarily excavated and mined ore, along with transporting ore and metal, all of which were both numerous and heavy. The Balloon Knapsack storage device agreed well with the Flint Merchant Alliance.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance representative followed up serenely, “2,800 gold.”

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance’s main business was food and cloth, both of which were rigidly in demand. None could run away from these two requirements, therefore of humanity’s six great merchant alliances, the Clam Cloth was the biggest and had very substantial financial resources.

As for the Treasure and Secret Medicine Merchant Alliance representatives both had withdrawn from the competition.

They were both refiners, one refined alchemy treasures while the other made and sold magic potions. Both these types of products had small quantities and high quality. Therefore, their requirements were far less intense than the Flint and Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance’s.

The playing card magician Shi Fa, the treasure hunter Hong Zhu, the dwarf Men Shi, the gunman Mei Ban, and the other lone figures watched the competition play out from the side lines.

The Balloon Knapsack’s price constantly rose. When it surpassed 3,000 gold, the bids began to become sparse and less frequent.

There were ten competitors left, the most powerful of which were the Flint Merchant Alliance representative, the Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance representative, as well as the Ten Touch Pirates.

After a violent battle, the Ten Touch Pirates were the last one standing.

“Room 3, 3,200 gold, going once.”

The Flint Merchant Alliance representative hesitated.

“Room 3, 3,200 gold, going twice.”

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance’s representative’s forehead was already layered with beads of sweat, he sighed and chose to give up.

Just as the auction master was about to pound his hammer, Hun Tong’s voice transmitted from room 1, “3,280 gold.”

Chapter 350: Zi Di’s Appetite

He hadn’t joined the price competition from the start, and no one expected that he’d stick himself in at the last moment.

Room 3 didn’t react, while the Flint Merchant Alliance representative was provoked and blurted out, “3,360 gold!”

Qi You coldly snorted and opened his own mouth this time, “3,500 gold.”

The voice was steady, but it was like a boulder smashing into the Flint Merchant Alliance representative’s heart.

The Flint Merchant Alliance representative turned red, anger and unwillingness filled his face. His mouth was open, yet he was silent.

The Clam Cloth Merchant Alliance representative’s room didn’t respond either.

The others awaited Hun Tong’s bid.

But Hun Tong in room 1 went silent. His raising of the price was merely a ploy.

Hun Tong accurately gauged Qi You’s ambition for the Balloon Knapsack, along with the Flint Merchant Alliance representative’s innermost struggle.

From the observable result, his price raising ploy was very fruitful.

The Flint Merchant Alliance was provoked and couldn’t restrain himself, while Qi You was forced to one up the former.

The auction price rose from 3,200 gold to 3,500 gold, a difference of 300 gold.

If neither Qi You nor the Flint Merchant Alliance representative bid, the Balloon Knapsack would fall into Hun Tong’s hands, an acceptable result to him.

Because when Two Eyes Island transitioned into a transportation hub, this storage alchemy treasure would be very useful.

“Balloon Knapsacks are not uncommon, but gold level ones are. The price has reached its peak.” Hun Tong said to himself.

“Room 3, 3,500 gold, going once.”

“Room 3, 3,500 gold, going twice.”

“Room 3, 3,500 gold, going three times...”

The moment the auction master’s wooden hammer fell, Zi Di suddenly said, “3,800 gold.”

Everyone’s eyes focused on room 21.

Like Hun Tong, this was the first time Zi Di had placed a bid.

But that sentence stunned everyone else.

She took the peak price of 3,500 gold and increased it by 300 gold, the audacity of it awed the whole audience.

Hun Tong raised his eyebrow in surprise.

Room 21 was currently his main target of interest. But in this auction, Zi Di hadn’t acted and Hun Tong still thought the other party was uninterested in it and hadn’t expected them to wait until now.

“Are they insane? 3,800 gold?!”

“It’s room 21 again.”

“For what reason did they do that, this is the second time they verbally attacked Qi You.”

“So rich and imposing, truly rich and overbearing!”

In room 3, Qi You narrowed his eyes; they were glistening with dreadful cold light.

Just as success was within sight, a misfortune appeared, disturbing his mood.

Competing against such a wealthy competitor made Qi You jittery.

If he wasn’t at an auction, it was likely he would have murdered them on the spot.

This was the second time room 21 intercepted him, which meant they didn’t care about the Ten Touch Pirates, this made Qi You even more enraged and felt he lost face.

“Have you still not investigated their origins?” Qi You coldly questioned.

“This subordinate is incapable of doing so.” The underling responsible immediately knelt, his complexion pale and his voice fearful, “After this one received your order, I have been investigating. However, Two Eyes Island is not our domain, and the people who snatched Ya Ma’s admission ticket are very mysterious, it’s like they suddenly appeared out of nowhere!”

“Regardless of where they appeared from, keep an eye on them for me. Soon, I shall let them know the consequences of opposing me.” Qi You touched his chin, his voice calm as his heart boiled with murderous intent.

He decided not to bid.

The auction had exhausted him mentally and physically, but more crucially, despite how formidable he was, he wasn’t confident that he could compete with room 21.

Up till now, room 21 had left an exceedingly deep impression on everyone.

Everyone knew these mysterious buyers had astonishing financial resources!

Zi Di’s sudden increase of 300 gold awed many people.

That hadn’t been at the beginning of the auction, but rather the end.

An auction price of 3,800 gold, truth be told, spilled far over the normal price.

In room 21, Cang Xu was silent for a bit, then deliberated, “Does this Balloon Knapsack really suit us?”

“Our true core ship is the Deep Sea Monster Fish; when it sails through the depths, it has limited internal space.”

“The Balloon Knapsack’s volume will grow bigger the more things are stored inside it, it will be rather difficult to store it inside the Deep Sea Monster Fish’s holds.”
