“The Balloon Knapsack only suits maritime sailing ships. But if one wanted to sufficiently display this alchemy treasure’s functions, one had to have a ship pulling it through the air.”

“The Balloon Knapsack floats in the air, it has no defense, that flaw is too great!”

“It is also too bombastic.”

“It will clearly tell pirates this: come rob us, we are transporting a lot of goods.”

Since the auction began, this was the first time Cang Xu’s and Zi Di’s opinions differed.

Zi Di nodded, first acknowledging Cang Xu’s analysis, then instead of answering it directly, she instead addressed another subject.

“On our first night on Two Eyes Island, we sent our message, however, the Bai Zhen Clan has not responded.”

“We don’t know what might have happened, we must plan for the worst.”

The experience of surviving on Mystifying Monster Island had burnished the survivors into habitually acting cautiously. Without this habit, they would have died on that island.

Zi Di continued, “Therefore, we must do our best to improve our strength, so that we can deal with the worst circumstances. When we have enough money, we should do our best to purchase equipment, that way we can raise our strength immensely within a short amount of time.”

“The stronger we are, the more able we are to deal with the worst possibilities.”

Cang Xu nodded, “We have already reached a consensus on this.”

Therefore, the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps’ main goal in this auction was to buy, buy, buy!

Gold level medicine was only one of the objectives.

This was a rare opportunity.

Especially in the vast ocean.

Two Eyes Island had been developed for many years, while most islands were like Snake Rat Island, barely developed at all. In the past, imperial power had been centralized on dry land, it was because they wanted to invade the Wilderness Continent and needed supply lines that they made arrangements on many islands.

If the Dragon Lion Mercenary Corps missed this chance, it would be a long time until they could attend such an auction again.

Zi Di explained, “In my opinion, we can purchase this Balloon Knapsack and then remodel it.”

“Remodel it into what?” Cang Xu asked.

Zi Di replied, “The Balloon Knapsack is a classic alchemy treasure, it contains many high level alchemy techniques, not just space storage technology, but also weight reduction ones too. To attain the goal of weight reduction, its core alchemy technique is a floating force field.”

“This alchemy technology is the core technology in magic airships, we can remodel it to make ourselves a second magic vessel.”

Two Eyes Island’s defensive battle had made the survivors realize how much they needed a magic ship.

This was one of the reasons Zi Di decided to bid.

When Cang Xu heard this, his mind seemed to be struck by lightning, “Are you talking about the airship schematic?”

War Merchant’s database had many alchemy blueprints.

Cang Xu and Zi Di had skimmed over many of them.

Among them were several airship schematics, including one that used the floating force field alchemy technology.

Airships were a type of aircraft, similar to hot air balloons.

The biggest difference was that airships possessed propulsion and aerial control equipment. They were mainly gigantic sleek vessels, and under the vessel, there was a suspended cabin that had a propeller in the rear, which controlled stability and propulsion.

The vessel’s air sac was filled with gas, giving it immense buoyancy.

The suspended cabin could load people and things.

The rear propeller could speed up the airship, alter its direction, shift its pitch, and so on.

The airship had first been invented and manufactured by the goblins.

Nowadays, the most universally common designs are almost all goblin airships.

Zi Di wanted to transform something into an airship, naturally she didn’t want common trash, she wanted an alchemy airship.

Zi Di continued, “We need more magic vessels. The Deep Sea Monster Fish cannot be rashly exposed, it must be our hidden trump card.”

“We also have a severe lack of storage space.”

“The Deep Sea Monster Fish has one big flaw; its interior is narrow.”

“Imperial redemption is merely one stop on our journey. We need to develop, expand our power, and acquire more resources and riches.”

“Based on the auction item list, during the late stage, there will be a lot of things related to metal, ores, and raw materials, we have planned to purchase a batch of those.”

“But in that plan, we were mainly buying materials for the construction of the Bloodline Purification formation.”

“Now that a gold level Balloon Knapsack has appeared, I thought, why not go a step further?”

“With this knapsack, we can try to purchase even more raw materials. On the sea, collecting these materials ourselves will be rather challenging and time consuming.”

“We have the tower spirit; we can make a second magic vessel.”

“If that happens, we’ll have a flying stronghold.”

“Not only that, but we will also have stores of alchemy materials. In the future, these materials can not only be used to make magic formations but also can be used to make an alchemy factory.”

“War Merchant’s database has many alchemy factory designs, as long as we can build one, we can have automated manufacturing. With sufficient raw materials, we will have an unending stream of munitions.”

“We can build the alchemy factory on some uninhabited island. We have the Anti-investigation and Prophecy and Deceptive Disguise formation, who would notice?”

Cang Xu couldn’t help but widen his eyes, “You are thinking about supplanting War Merchant’s position and becoming the new tycoon in arms dealing?”

For the first time, the necromancer sensed the ghost girl’s appetite.

Despite being dead, her appetite truly was great!

Zi Di seemed to know what Cang Xu was thinking, and so she pensively sighed, “If a business model has a profit margin of 10%, that guarantees that it will be used everywhere. If it has a profit margin of 20%, it is a hot market. If it has a profit margin of 50%, it will take risks out of desperation. As for a profit margin of 100%, that will trample the laws of the world. For a profit margin of 300%, it will dare to commit any crime, even if it risks the danger of hanging. If turmoil and dispute can bring profits, the business shall encourage turmoil and dispute, smugglers and slave traders is proof of that.”

“That is business, my friend.”
